Abstract: Modern railway perception requires large comprehensive image datasets, but creating them is slowed by manual labeling, especially for track polylines and other complex infrastructure geometry. This paper presents the Active Railway Infrastructure Dataset Annotation framework, a railway-specific semi-automated pipeline that converts instance predictions from a mask scoring instance segmentation network into bounding-boxes, polygons, polylines, and paired polylines. To keep automatic track geometry annotation reliable without ground truth, it applies confidence thresholds and an unique contour-agreement quality score as an acceptance gate. An active learning loop ranks remaining images by uncertainty and adapts the number of manual queries per cycle. This is achieved through a novel quantity control mechanism, in which easy images are auto-accepted and human effort targets hard cases. Experiments on disjoint, diverse imagery and time-sequential, redundant imagery quantify effort reduction and accuracy trade-offs, achieving about two thirds reduction in the disjoint regime and up to 93% in the sequential regime. The study also provides guidance for initial label budgets and stopping to avoid over-annotation.

</details>


### [35] [SVGFaith: A Two-Layer Meta-Evaluation of Faithfulness Metrics for Text-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06048)
*Guotao Liang,Haitao Zhou,Biao Liu,Huanxuan Liao,Lingfeng Qiao,Qian-Wen Zhang,Qian Yu,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两层元评测框架SVGFaith，揭示栅格图像指标难以捕捉细粒度提示忠实度，并提出可解释的逐点指标SVGFaith-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SVG系统常用栅格图像指标评估栅格化输出，但这些分数能否反映细粒度提示忠实度尚不明确，缺少针对性的元评测手段。

Method: 构建SVGFaith-Diagnostic（1842个受控对比对，覆盖8个能力维度，含"一提示两渲染"与"一渲染两描述"两个方向）和SVGFaith-Arena（1327个人工标注的生成器输出比较）；并提出SVGFaith-Score：领域微调分解器将提示转为原子视觉需求的依赖图，用VQA模型逐需求判别满足性，并按前置需求得分对下游需求得分折减。

Result: Diagnostic上CLIP等四个图文嵌入指标在物体身份上成对准确率≥0.789，但空间关系与属性绑定≤0.672（随机基线0.5）；SVGFaith-Score在Arena及两个Diagnostic方向上显著优于全部九个基线（含Qwen3.5-122B-A10B评审模型）。

Conclusion: 栅格图像指标对细粒度忠实度判别力有限；结构化、依赖感知的逐需求评分既提升准确性又提供可解释性（可定位未满足需求及其折减来源）。

Abstract: Text-to-SVG systems are commonly evaluated by applying raster-image metrics to rasterised outputs, yet whether these scores capture fine-grained prompt faithfulness remains unclear. We introduce SVGFaith, a two-layer meta-evaluation framework in which the more faithful candidate of each pair is determined by construction or by human judgment. SVGFaith-Diagnostic contains 1,842 controlled contrastive pairs across eight capability axes, split evenly between two directions: two renderings compared against one prompt, and two descriptions compared against one rendering. SVGFaith-Arena contains 1,327 human-annotated comparisons of real generator outputs. On SVGFaith-Diagnostic, CLIP and three other image--text embedding metrics reach at least 0.789 pairwise accuracy on object identity but at most 0.672 on spatial relations and attribute binding, where chance is 0.5. We further introduce SVGFaith-Score, an interpretable pointwise metric. A domain-tuned decomposer turns each prompt into a dependency graph of atomic visual requirements, a visual question answering model scores whether each requirement is satisfied, and a requirement's score is discounted when its prerequisites score low. In addition to the overall score, the per-requirement scores show which requirements are judged unsatisfied and which inherit discounts from their prerequisites. SVGFaith-Score significantly outperforms all nine baselines, including a Qwen3.5-122B-A10B judge, on Arena and in both Diagnostic directions. Code and data: https://github.com/Yukinonooo/SVGFaith.

</details>


### [36] [Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06728)
*Shuai Dong,Kanghui Tian,Linghan Chen,Wenqi Shao,Hongrui Wu,Haowen Hou,Yongfu Zhu,Xuehui Wang,Ziyue Wang,Wenpu Liu,Weichu Xie,Yuqi Xu,Congcong Wang,Kaiwen Tuo,Caoyuan Ma,Taiqiang Wu,Ji Zhao,Wenzheng Chang,Dianyi Wang,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei,Yu Qiao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TurnWise，将 GRPO 的轨迹级优势改为轮次级优势，用"静默补全前缀打分"做轮级信用分配，并用轮内 token 均值损失平衡"回答/等待"决策，无需 critic 或额外 rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求模型在每一轮反复决定"回答"还是"继续观察"。现有基于 GRPO 的方法侧重奖励设计，却在整个轨迹上共享单一优势，导致：早期已成功之后的冗余回答被强化，而早期失败之后本应继续观察的有益后续被抑制——轨迹级反馈与轮级交互之间存在错配。

Method: 1) 轮级信用分配：采用 silence-completed prefix scoring，用每个决策点"剩余回报"构造组相对优势，将当前决策的学习信号与先前奖励解耦；2) Turn-mean policy optimization：先在每轮内对 token loss 取平均，再跨轮平均，使回答与等待决策不论输出长度均等权重。整体无需学习 critic，也不增加额外 rollout。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在流式视频理解任务上优于先前的强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 将 GRPO 的优势估计从轨迹级下沉到轮级，并配合长度无偏的轮均值优化，可有效提升流式策略学习效果：鼓励有信息量的回答、减少冗余，同时不引入额外模型或采样开销。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to repeatedly decide whether to respond or continue observing. Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been applied to this setting, with existing approaches emphasizing reward design while retaining a single trajectory-level advantage across turns. Such shared credit can reinforce redundant replies after earlier successes and discourage useful follow-ups after earlier failures. We propose TurnWise, a turn-level GRPO framework that aligns learning with individual streaming decisions. TurnWise addresses the mismatch between trajectory-level feedback and turn-level interaction, promoting informative responses and reducing redundancy. Specifically, turn-level credit assignment uses silence-completed prefix scoring to construct group-relative advantages from each decision's remaining return, separating its learning signal from earlier rewards. Turn-mean policy optimization averages token losses within each turn and then across turns, giving response and waiting decisions equal weight regardless of output length. These complementary designs improve streaming policy learning without a learned critic or additional rollouts. Extensive experiments show our method outperforms prior RL approaches for streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [37] [Modality-Aware Expert Pruning for MoE-Based Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05927)
*Donggeun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的模态感知专家剪枝框架MAEP，通过单次前向将专家重要性按模态分解，把约58GB的MoE-MLLM专家权重压缩约27GB，使其可装入单张40GB GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-MLLM中仅少数专家被激活，但专家参数占比超过90%（如InternVL3.5-30B-A3B与Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B在BF16下54GB/58GB），是内存瓶颈。现有专家剪枝方法对所有token统一聚合重要性，忽视了同一专家在视觉与语言处理中承担不同角色。

Method: MAEP（Modality-Aware Expert Pruning），免训练框架，单次前向即完成模态分解的专家重要性评估：(1) ΔH Output，一种考虑再分配效应的度量，刻画移除专家后MoE输出的变化；(2) 以低注意力视觉token作为视觉侧剪枝信号，该信号的专家重要性排序在不同数据集间稳定。文本侧重要性作为保留信号，视觉侧重要性作为剪枝信号，跨层全局排序专家。

Result: 在三个MoE-MLLM的多模态与文本基准上，MAEP在多模态基准多数指标上优于模态无关基线，纯文本任务保持竞争力；BF16专家权重复用占用减少约27GB（约占总内存47%），使约58GB模型整体权重可容纳于单张40GB GPU。

Conclusion: 按模态分离专家重要性是MoE-MLLM压缩的有效方向；免训练、单次前向的MAEP在保持多模态与文本性能的同时显著降低显存需求，实现单卡可部署。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to scale capacity efficiently, but expert parameters dominate memory even though only a few experts are activated per token: they account for over 90% of model size in modern MoE-MLLMs (e.g., 54 GB out of about 58 GB in BF16 for InternVL3.5-30B-A3B and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B). Expert pruning has emerged as an effective compression strategy, but existing methods aggregate importance uniformly across tokens, ignoring that the same expert may serve different roles for vision versus language processing. To address this, we propose MAEP (Modality-Aware Expert Pruning), a training-free framework that decomposes expert importance by modality in a single forward pass. MAEP introduces (1) Delta-H Output ($ΔH$), a redistribution-aware metric measuring the MoE output change when an expert is removed, and (2) low-attention vision tokens as a vision-side pruning signal that is dataset-stable: its expert-importance ranking remains stable across different datasets. Text-side importance serves as a preservation signal while vision-side importance serves as a pruning signal, and experts are ranked globally across layers. Experiments on three MoE-based MLLMs across multimodal and text benchmarks show that MAEP outperforms modality-agnostic baselines on the majority of multimodal benchmarks while remaining competitive on text-only tasks, reducing the BF16 expert weight footprint by about 27 GB (about 47% of total memory) so that the full weight footprint of an approximately 58 GB model fits within a single 40 GB GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/nota-github/MAEP.

</details>


### [38] [LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06856)
*Haoxiang Cao,Yuxuan Zhang,Penghui Du,Bo Li,Jiajiong Cao,Yingying Fan,Zimeng Wu,Yifu Luo,Mingzhe Zheng,Junwen Miao,Huaisong Zhang,Yang Yao,Yu Wang,Dongfu Jiang,Ping Nie,Wenhu Chen,Chaoqun Wang,Changqian Yu,Kelsey R. Allen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongBanana 是首个由专家设计并验证的长上下文多参考图像合成基准，含90个任务、4,670条原子评估项，并配套提出 agent harness 框架 LongBanana-Gen。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 agent 驱动的图像生成时代，缺少衡量"密集视觉证据使用"的可审计测试平台：现有基准难以评估模型从分散参考图中检索证据、拒绝干扰项、绑定实体与关系并合成统一图像的能力。

Method: 构建 3×3 分类学组织的 90 个任务，每任务约 32 张参考图、20 条约束、50 个评估项；采用图接地（graph-grounded）评估设计，将实体-关系图的节点、边与全图义务映射为 Content Fidelity、Relational Binding、Global Constraint Satisfaction 三类清单项；专家标注含 15% 双标子集（完全一致率 89.4%，二次加权 kappa 0.83）。另提出 LongBanana-Gen agent harness，并测试 ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect（RPR）工作流。

Result: 最小化 agent harness 即优于直接生成；RPR 工作流通过增强证据接地与修复带来依赖 harness 的增益。基准共提供 4,670 条专家指定的原子评估项，标注可靠性经双标一致性验证。

Conclusion: LongBanana 为衡量密集视觉证据利用提供可审计测试台，并为发展更强的 agent 驱动图像生成系统提供统一目标；代码与数据已开放。

Abstract: We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.

</details>


### [39] [ChangeJEPA: Predict Before You Compare for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05570)
*Buddhi Wijenayake,Dhananjaya Jayasundara,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChangeJEPA将双时相变化检测重构为潜在后状态预测：用预变化图像与部分后变化证据预测完整后状态潜表示，再通过多源不一致性推断变化，在三个基准上优于解码器对齐的孪生基线最多1.51 IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统孪生特征比较范式中，两幅图像独立编码后由解码器判别差异，但编码器同时保留了真实场景变化与光照、季节、辐射、几何等 nuisance 变化，导致变化与干扰难以区分。

Method: 提出 ChangeJEPA 双时相联合嵌入预测架构。给定预变化图像与部分后变化证据，在两种互补条件下预测完整后变化表示：由预变化场景导出的先验假设，以及以观测到的后变化上下文为条件的更新假设；用联合嵌入预测损失将两种假设与目标潜表示对齐。变化由预变化表示、观测后变化目标与预测潜状态之间的不一致性推断，涵盖变化证据、先验惊讶度、上下文更新与预测可靠性。

Result: 在 SYSU-CD、LEVIR-CD+、CDD 上均取得最佳 IoU 与 F1，较解码器匹配的孪生基线最高提升 1.51 IoU 点。

Conclusion: 潜在后状态预测相较直接特征比较具有明显优势，可抑制瞬态外观变化并提升变化检测的语义一致性。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection is typically formulated as Siamese feature comparison, where two images are encoded independently and a decoder determines which discrepancies correspond to meaningful change. This is challenging because the encoders preserve both true scene changes and nuisance variations such as illumination, seasonal, radiometric, and geometric differences. We introduce ChangeJEPA, a bitemporal joint-embedding predictive architecture that reformulates change detection as latent post-state prediction. Given a pre-change image and partial post-change evidence, ChangeJEPA predicts the full post-change representation under two complementary conditions: a prior hypothesis derived from the pre-change scene and an updated hypothesis conditioned on observed post-change context. A joint-embedding predictive loss aligns both hypotheses with the target latent representation, encouraging semantic consistency while suppressing transient appearance variations. Change is then inferred from the disagreement among the pre-change representation, the observed post-change target, and the predicted latent states, capturing complementary evidence of change, prior surprise, contextual update, and prediction reliability. Across SYSU-CD, LEVIR-CD+, and CDD, ChangeJEPA consistently outperforms its decoder matched Siamese baseline by up to 1.51 IoU points and achieves the best IoU and F1-score on all three benchmarks, demonstrating the advantage of latent post-state prediction over direct feature comparison. Page: https://buddhi19.github.io/ChangeJEPA

</details>


### [40] [From Activation to Intervention: Interpreting Sparse Visual Features through Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05522)
*Jianyang Gu,Jacob Beattie,Mridul Khurana,Julia Chae,Xi Xiao,Cheng Zhang,Anuj Karpatne,Wei-Lun Chao,Sara Beery,Tanya Berger-Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RecSAEI框架，通过沿SAE解码器方向干预表征并重建视觉输出，主动给出特征语义的直接证据，解决基于激活图像/像素区域解释特征时的歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）将视觉表征分解为单个特征，但现有解释依赖激活最强的图像或像素区域，当不同语义特征（如纹理与颜色）频繁在同一位置激活时，证据变得模糊，难以确定特征真实语义。

Method: Reconstructive SAE Intervention（RecSAEI）：从被动观察转为主动视觉重建。沿SAE解码器方向对表征进行干预，并重建视觉输出，以重建结果直接体现特征语义；同时将[CLS]的SAE干预归因到稀疏的patch级SAE解码器方向集合上。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种视觉编码器上成功消歧特征含义；将[CLS]的SAE干预锚定于稀疏patch解码器方向后，不仅获得稳定的目标响应，还揭示了基本视觉模式与高层语义之间的组合关系。

Conclusion: 干预重建应作为解释神经网络的重要互补信号，与被动激活观察结合使用。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose visual representations into individual features, offering a promising basis for explaining model behavior. Interpreting these features typically relies on images or pixel regions that most strongly activate them. However, this evidence becomes ambiguous when multiple features with different semantics frequently activate in the same locations, such as texture and color in the same patterns. We propose Reconstructive SAE Intervention (RecSAEI), a framework that shifts feature explanation from passive observation to active visual reconstruction. By intervening on representations along SAE decoder directions and reconstructing the visual output, we provide direct evidence of a feature's semantic meaning. We demonstrate that our method successfully disambiguates feature meanings across multiple datasets and vision encoders. Furthermore, our framework grounds [CLS] SAE interventions in a sparse set of patch SAE decoder directions. Beyond stable target response, this attribution also exposes the compositional relationship between elementary visual patterns and high-level semantics. Our results advocate for intervention reconstruction as an essential complementary signal for explaining neural networks.

</details>


### [41] [SO(3)-Equivariant Learning on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05449)
*Matteo Ballegeer,Dries F. Benoit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SO3brep——B-rep面邻接图上的SO(3)等变图神经网络，在局部标架计算特征并用Wigner D矩阵映射至共享标架，通过Clebsch-Gordan张量积保留邻接面相对朝向，实现SE(3)不变输出，在随机旋转测试下精度最高且参数量最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAD模型不以规范朝向存储，B-rep学习需对位姿鲁棒。现有方法要么在世界坐标与法向下操作，旋转时性能退化；要么将每个面编码于局部参考系，虽获得不变性，却使面间消息退化为标量、丢失相邻面的相对朝向信息。

Method: 在B-rep面邻接图上构建SO(3)等变GNN：节点特征在局部标架中计算，经Wigner D矩阵映射至共享标架；用Clebsch-Gordan张量积将特征与相邻面间方向耦合，使相对朝向在消息传递全过程得以保留，输出保持SE(3)不变性。整体结构为等变图消息传递加面级读出。

Result: 在三个面分割与两个分类基准（测试部件随机旋转）上，SO3brep在对比的B-rep网络及等变点云网络中取得最高精度，且参数量最少；在标注部件稀少（工业CAD典型场景）时优势最大。

Conclusion: 通过等变消息传递而非局部标架标量化，可在保持旋转不变性的同时保留面间相对几何关系，为小样本工业CAD的B-rep学习提供高效方案。

Abstract: Learning on boundary representations (B-reps) requires robustness to the pose of a part, since CAD models are not stored in a canonical orientation. Existing B-rep networks either operate on world-frame coordinates and normals, and thus degrade under rotation, or encode each face in a local reference frame. Local frames yield invariance, but reduce inter-face messages to scalars and discard the relative orientation of adjacent faces. We propose SO3brep, an SO(3)-equivariant graph neural network on the B-rep face-adjacency graph. Node features are computed in local frames and mapped into a shared frame by Wigner D-matrices. Clebsch-Gordan tensor products couple these features with the directions between adjacent faces, so that relative orientation is preserved throughout message passing and the output remains SE(3)-invariant. Across three face-segmentation and two classification benchmarks with randomly rotated test parts, SO3brep achieves the highest accuracy among the compared B-rep networks and equivariant point cloud networks, with the fewest parameters. Its advantage is largest with few labeled parts, a setting typical of industrial CAD. Code is available at https://github.com/UGent-CVAMO/so3brep.

</details>


### [42] [Annotator-Free Object Detection via Foundation Model Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06195)
*Yasiru Ranasinghe,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需目标数据集人工标注的目标检测框架：利用分割与视觉语言基础模型互补生成伪标签，并用地标聚类分摊LVLM查询成本、自精炼训练环路迭代优化，在COCO等零样本基准上取得显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测受制于高昂标注成本与监督模型的闭集假设，难以扩展到新类别和新域。现有依赖LVLM查询的方案成本高、标签一致性差。

Method: 模块化流程：1）用预训练分割基础模型自主生成分割候选；2）通过多模态视觉语言推理赋予语义标签；3）引入基于地标的聚类机制，将标注在图像簇间摊销，提升标签一致性并降低LVLM查询开销；4）用轻量级开放集检测器构成自精炼训练环路，迭代提升定位与分类；整体与现有检测架构兼容。

Result: 在COCO、LVIS、ODinW-35、Roboflow-100上优于SOTA开放词汇与长尾检测方法；零样本设置下COCO达60.8 AP，LVIS达47.4 AP。

Conclusion: 实现完全未见类别与域的自适应检测且无需目标域人工标注，为标注不可行的真实未知环境提供可扩展的检测基础方案。

Abstract: Object detection research remains constrained by high annotation costs and the closed-set assumptions of supervised models, limiting scalability to novel categories. We present a target-dataset annotation-free object detection framework that eliminates the need for human labeling on the target dataset by leveraging the complementary strengths of pretrained foundation models, including segmentation and vision-language systems, combined with a self-refining training loop. To further reduce reliance on expensive LVLM queries, we introduce a landmark-based clustering mechanism that amortizes labeling across image clusters, improving label consistency while lowering computational cost. Our method autonomously generates segmentation proposals, assigns semantic labels through multimodal reasoning, and iteratively improves both localization and classification using a lightweight open-set detector. This modular pipeline enables adaptation to completely unseen object classes and domains without any human-provided target-dataset annotations, while maintaining compatibility with existing detection architectures. Evaluations on COCO, LVIS, ODinW-35, and Roboflow-100 demonstrate substantial performance gains over state-of-the-art open-vocabulary and long-tail detection methods, achieving 60.8 AP on COCO and 47.4 AP on LVIS under zero-shot settings. Beyond strong benchmark results, our approach provides a scalable foundation for real-world detection systems in unknown environments, where annotation is infeasible.

</details>


### [43] [RDT-3D: Resolution-Decoupled Training for High-Fidelity 3D Geometry Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06141)
*Jiale Zhang,Yang Liu,Chengyang Li,Jin Yuan,Qianxi Jia,Taicheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示粗到细3D生成框架中RoPE条件引导与几何监督存在训练-推理分辨率不一致问题，提出分辨率解耦训练(RDT)与Dual3D-300K数据集以提升高分辨率几何细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到细3D生成框架使用RoPE对局部查询进行条件化，但训练时坐标引导与几何监督分辨率对齐，推理时却由粗空间坐标解码高分辨率几何，导致严重的分布偏移，削弱精细细节生成质量。同时高分辨率配对数据稀缺构成瓶颈。

Method: 提出Resolution-Decoupled Training (RDT)：将RoPE引导锚定在512分辨率，而在1536分辨率上进行几何监督，实现稳健的细节外推；并构建Dual3D-300K大规模配对数据集，包含300K个水密3D点云，显式对齐512与1536两种分辨率。

Result: 大量实验表明RDT显著改善了尖锐几何区域的生成质量，Dual3D-300K为高分辨率3D生成提供了可扩展的基准。

Conclusion: 通过解耦训练时的引导分辨率与监督分辨率，可有效缓解训练-推理分布偏移，配合大规模对齐数据集，显著提升高保真3D几何生成的细节表现。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D geometry generation requires maintaining global structural coherence alongside fine-grained surface details. While recent coarse-to-fine frameworks leverage Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) to condition local queries, we reveal a critical training-inference resolution discrepancy: coordinate guidance and geometric supervision are aligned during training, whereas inference decodes high-resolution geometry from coarse spatial coordinates. This mismatch introduces a severe distribution shift that degrades fine details. To overcome this, we propose Resolution-Decoupled Training (RDT), which anchors RoPE guidance at 512 resolution while supervising geometry at 1536, enabling robust detail extrapolation. Additionally, to eliminate high-resolution data bottlenecks, we present Dual3D-300K, a large-scale paired dataset comprising 300K watertight 3D point clouds explicitly aligned across 512 and 1536 resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RDT substantially improves sharp geometric regions, while Dual3D-300K offers a scalable benchmark for high-resolution 3D generation.

</details>


### [44] [SleuthVLM: Do We Really Need Audio-in-Video for Omni Video Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06083)
*Peiran Wu,Fangfei Gou,Zhaoxi Mu,Changhao Zhang,Jian Liu,Weiqiang Wang,Yuntao Wang,Yuanchun Shi,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SleuthVLM：不直接"听"音频的 VLM agent harness，将语音转写为文本，由 VLM 自主探查视频，在四个全模态基准（最长 90 分钟）上平均准确率 75.0%，超过 Gemini 3.8 Flash 2.6 分，Video-MME-v2 达 77.8%，开源 27B 版本超越所有开源全模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态（omni）模型依赖联合处理采样帧与音频，进展主要靠扩大模型规模与全模态训练数据。作者质疑：模型是否必须直接"听"音频？若不需要，则说明瓶颈不在感知模态对齐，而在于 agentic 能力。

Method: SleuthVLM 是一个构建在 VLM 之上的 agent harness：语音先经 ASR 转写为文本，VLM 不接收原始音频，且不依赖预定义工作流，自主"侦查"（sleuth）视频内容，进行多步推理与信息获取。

Result: 在四个全模态基准（视频最长 90 分钟）上取得最佳平均准确率 75.0%，比 Gemini 3.8 Flash 高 2.6 个百分点；Video-MME-v2 排行榜 77.8% 居首；以开源 27B VLM 为基座时，优于所有开源全模态模型。

Conclusion: 三条发现：SleuthVLM 胜过"瞥一眼"式模型；听觉并非瓶颈，agentic 能力才是；全模态理解需要更好的 agentic 能力而非更多用于模态对齐的全模态数据。该工作为全模态视频理解提供了一个简单有效的起点——需要更好的 agent，而非更大的"会听"模型。

Abstract: Omni models answer questions about videos by jointly processing sampled frames and audio in one turn, with progress driven largely by scaling model size and omni training data. We ask whether a model needs to hear the audio directly at all, and present SleuthVLM, an agent harness built on a VLM that never hears the audio directly: speech will be transcribed into text, and the VLM will sleuth the video on its own without any predefined workflow. On four omni benchmarks with videos up to 90 minutes, SleuthVLM achieves the best average accuracy: \textbf{75.0%}, 2.6 points above Gemini 3.8 Flash, and it also tops the Video-MME-v2 leaderboard with \textbf{77.8%}; with an open-source 27B VLM, it beats all open-source omni models. We draw three findings: SleuthVLM beats a model that glances; hearing is not the bottleneck, agentic ability is; omni understanding needs better agentic ability, not just more omni data for modality alignment. So we provide a simple but effective starting point for doing omni video understanding. Omni understanding needs better agents, not just bigger models that hear.

</details>


### [45] [UniRestorer++: Scalable Universal Image Restoration with Multi-Granularity Degradation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06042)
*Jingbo Lin,Zhilu Zhang,Fan Li,Renjing Pei,Wenbo Li,Hang Xu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的图像复原框架 UniRestorer++，基于多粒度退化建模结合组专属专家与双路由（退化路由+粒度路由），并构建 CDD-50 基准，实现近对数线性的 PSNR 扩展趋势与 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用图像复原缺乏可靠的扩展（scaling）策略：单纯加深加宽骨干易性能饱和；退化感知的专门化依赖精确退化估计，且对分布外与真实输入易出错。

Method: 多粒度退化建模，逐步细化退化分组并引入组专属专家，使容量增长与专门化耦合；基于退化估计的路由选择根到叶路径，基于粒度估计的路由在该路径上选择层级以平衡专门化与鲁棒性；利用空间稠密退化表征图实现区域感知路由以应对空间非均匀退化。

Result: 构建 CDD-50 基准（50 类退化，单退化至五重退化）；模型规模范围内 PSNR 呈近对数线性趋势，分布外与真实数据上的扩展增益强于普通 MoE 扩展；在各基准达到 SOTA，并在真实图像上展现零样本泛化与少样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 多粒度退化建模与双路由机制为通用图像复原提供了一条可扩展且鲁棒的路径，兼顾专门化收益与对未知/真实退化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Universal image restoration aims to handle diverse degradations within a single model, but a reliable scaling strategy remains underexplored. Increasing backbone depth and width can lead to performance saturation, while degradation-aware specialization depends on accurate degradation estimation and is vulnerable to errors on out-of-distribution and real-world inputs. We propose UniRestorer++, a scalable framework based on multi-granularity degradation modeling. It progressively refines degradation groups and introduces group-specific experts, coupling capacity growth with specialization. Degradation-estimation-based routing selects a root-to-leaf path, while granularity-estimation-based routing chooses a level along that path to balance specialization and robustness. Spatially dense degradation representation maps further enable region-aware routing for spatially non-uniform corruptions. We develop CDD-50, a benchmark covering 50 degradation categories from single to penta-degradation. Experiments show approximately log-linear PSNR trends over the evaluated model-size range and stronger scaling gains on out-of-distribution and real-world data than vanilla MoE scaling. UniRestorer++ achieves state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks and demonstrates zero-shot generalization and few-shot transfer on real-world images. Code: https://github.com/mrluin/UniRestorer_plusplus

</details>


### [46] [Chronos: Sequential Identity-Preserving Video Generation by Temporal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05893)
*Mengqi Huang,Wenchuan Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yijing Tu,Zhike Liu,Luoni Wang,Ning Tong,Xiaoxiao Sheng,Tianyu Huang,Xuecheng Qi,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Chronos 框架，统一单段人脸身份保持与多段序列动作生成，通过语义提示锚定、视觉帧锚定与指标引导候选路由，在 ACM MM 2026 身份保持视频生成挑战赛 Track 1 与 Track 2 均获第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖参考图控制信号与身份感知条件，仅提升静态身份对齐，导致身份保真度与时序可控性之间存在根本张力，在大姿态变化和多段序列动作下易出现身份漂移或动作抑制。

Method: Chronos 在统一时序公式下统一单段与多段任务，包含三个模块：Semantic Prompt Anchoring（自适应精炼用户提示词）、Visual Frame Anchoring（将条件转化为视觉接地关键帧锚点以提供视觉引导）、Metric-Guided Candidate Routing（多维评估从异构生成器中筛选最高质量视频）。

Result: 大量实验验证了方法的优越性，在 ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge 的 Track 1 和 Track 2 中均获得第一名，证明其在人脸身份保持与序列动作生成两类设置下均有效。

Conclusion: 缓解身份保真与时序可控性的矛盾是身份保持视频生成的关键；统一时序建模加上提示、帧级锚定与多生成器质量路由的组合策略可同时应对单段与多段场景，并在竞赛中获得最优表现。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to synthesize high-quality and visually realistic videos from textual and visual conditions while faithfully maintaining the subject's identity across diverse motions. Prior methods have extensively introduced reference-guided control signals and identity-aware conditioning to improve facial alignment from reference images. However, improving static identity alignment alone creates a fundamental tension between identity fidelity and temporal controllability, which frequently causes identity drift or motion suppression under large pose variations and multi-segment sequential actions. To address this, we propose Chronos, a framework that unifies single-segment facial identity preservation and multi-segment sequential action generation under a common temporal formulation. Specifically, Chronos integrates Semantic Prompt Anchoring to adaptively refine the user prompt, Visual Frame Anchoring to transform conditions into visually grounded keyframe anchors for visual guidance, and Metric-Guided Candidate Routing to select the highest-quality videos from heterogeneous generators via multi-dimensional evaluation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach, which achieved first place in both Track 1 and Track 2 of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge, demonstrating its effectiveness across both facial identity preservation and sequential action generation settings.

</details>


### [47] [Feature Puzzles: Single-Image Supervision for Multi-View 3D-Aware Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05874)
*Jiahao Ma,Qiang Zhang,Miaomiao Liu,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Chuong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Feature Puzzles框架，仅用单图像语义分割监督即可学习多视图一致分割：通过裁剪与几何变换构造多视图一致伪标签，并接一个轻量即插即用网络利用预训练3D基础模型的多视图一致特征完成分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能轻松跨视角识别同一物体，但训练多视图一致感知模型通常依赖视频或标定多视图采集，成本高且多样性远不及大规模单目数据。

Method: 以简单有效的流程，通过图像裁剪与几何变换生成多视图一致的伪标签；并设计轻量、即插即用的网络，利用现有预训练3D基础模型学到的多视图一致图像特征与上述伪标签进行多视图一致分割。框架支持来自单视图或多视图的多种提示，并可扩展至开放词汇3D分割。

Result: 在单图像监督下，前馈模型在NVOS上取得最佳mIoU；在SPIn-NeRF与ScanNet++上与采用逐场景优化或域内多视图训练的基线相比仍具竞争力；并展现出对开放词汇3D分割的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 低成本单目监督配合伪标签构造与3D基础模型特征，可有效替代昂贵多视图监督；框架灵活支持多视图提示与开放词汇扩展，具备实用性与可推广性。

Abstract: Humans can easily recognize the same objects across different viewpoints of a 3D scene, whereas teaching models such multi-view-consistent perception typically requires videos or calibrated multi-view captures, which are considerably more expensive and less diverse than large-scale monocular data.~In this paper, we introduce Feature Puzzles, a novel framework for learning multi-view-consistent segmentation using only single-image semantic segmentation supervision. Specifically, we propose a simple yet effective pipeline that constructs multi-view-consistent segmentation pseudo-labels through image cropping and geometric transformations. We further introduce a lightweight, plug-and-play network that leverages the multi-view-consistent image features learned by existing pretrained 3D foundation models and the generated pseudo-labels to perform multi-view-consistent segmentation. Importantly, our flexible framework supports various types of prompts provided from one or multiple views and can be readily extended to open-vocabulary 3D segmentation. Under this single-image supervision, our feed-forward model achieves the best mIoU on NVOS, remains competitive on SPIn-NeRF and ScanNet++ against baselines using per-scene optimization or in-domain multi-view training, and demonstrates zero-shot transfer to open-vocabulary 3D segmentation. Visual comparisons are available on our project page: https://featurepuzzles.github.io/

</details>


### [48] [ReTailor: A Residual Feature Tailoring Framework for Real-Time Cross-Sensor Pansharpening Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05811)
*Jing-Ze Liang,Wei-Jie Xiao,Zi-Han Cao,Yang-Dong Li,Tian-Yi Hu,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReTailor 实时序列式跨传感器自适应框架，仅优化轻量残差 Feature Tailor，在自监督物理约束下快速纠正跨传感器偏移，兼顾质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全色锐化模型在训练传感器上表现好，但跨传感器泛化差。已有跨传感器自适应方案受限于：目标传感器重训练需成对数据与专门训练阶段；逐图零样本自适应需对每张测试图优化全网，计算开销大。

Method: 仅在冻结的融合先验上，以自监督、物理感知约束快速优化轻量残差 Feature Tailor；结合内容感知图块选择（每图挑选少量高信息图块，低开销提升质量与效率）与滚动残差自举（沿图像流继承传感器相关知识）。并与 LoRA 微调进行对比。

Result: 跨传感器实验表明 ReTailor 有效且高效地缓解跨传感器性能退化；相较 LoRA 体现优势；在超分辨全色锐化与图像分割两个遥感任务上其核心思想也带来一致提升。

Conclusion: ReTailor 为真实跨传感器退化下的全色锐化模型部署提供了高效策略，并为其他遥感任务的跨传感器退化治理提供启示，代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent pansharpening models achieve strong fusion performance on trained sensors, yet often generalize poorly to unseen sensors. Existing cross-sensor adaptation is limited by target-sensor retraining, which requires target-sensor training pairs and a dedicated training stage, and by per-image zero-shot adaptation, which is computationally expensive due to whole-network optimization for each test image. To address these problems, we propose ReTailor, a real-time sequential cross-sensor adaptation framework. Only a lightweight residual Feature Tailor is rapidly optimized under self-supervised, physics-aware constraints to correct cross-sensor shifts without modifying the well-learned fusion prior. For efficient and effective deployment, ReTailor combines content-aware patch selection, which identifies a few informative patches per image for optimization at negligible cost to improve both quality and efficiency, with rolling residual bootstrap, which inherits sensor-related knowledge across the image stream to further improve quality. Extensive experiments on cross-sensor adaptation settings show that ReTailor mitigates cross-sensor degradation both effectively and efficiently. Moreover, we compare ReTailor with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a widely used method for fine-tuning, to demonstrate the advantages of our approach. We further evaluate the core principle of ReTailor on two additional remote sensing tasks: super-resolution pansharpening and image segmentation, where it consistently improves performance. In general, ReTailor provides a highly effective and efficient strategy for deploying pansharpening models under real-world cross-sensor degradation, while also offering useful insights into managing cross-sensor degradation in other remote sensing tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/JingZe-Liang/ReTailor .

</details>


### [49] [From Pixels to Actions - Towards Native Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06918)
*Weichen Fan,Haiwen Diao,Penghao Wu,Siyuan Hu,Ziqi Huang,Tianqi Liu,Mingze Sun,Ziyi Wang,Zhongang Cai,Hanming Deng,Quan Wang,Lei Yang,Lewei Lu,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无编码器（encoder-free）的视觉-语言-动作框架NEO-VLA，让图像以轻量patch卷积token化并在主干中与语言共同演化，动作专家可从不同深度提取指令条件化的视觉表示，实验表明原生化架构优于编码器架构且更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型默认"视觉先被编码器压缩、之后才开始动作推理"，但操作任务常依赖最易被该抽象丢失的信号（局部几何、边界、深度、细微状态变化），因此质疑动作策略是否应继承固定视觉端点。

Method: 去除独立视觉编码器：图像经轻量patch卷积层token化，与语言在主干中联合演化；flow-matching动作专家从不同深度的指令条件化视觉表示中取用特征。

Result: 控制对比下原生架构持续优于编码器架构；全量微调可缩小但未消除差距，单纯扩大规模也无法弥补；逐层探测显示动作关键信号常在最终视觉表示之前达到峰值；在多种操作场景与分布偏移下取得更强、更鲁棒的控制。

Conclusion: 具身控制更受益于访问计算层级中多层级的视觉表示，而非固定的编码器输出；视觉端点不应被提前固化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models are typically built on a simple premise: perception is first compressed by visual encoders, and action reasoning begins only afterwards. Yet manipulation often hinges on precisely the visual signals most vulnerable to this abstraction, e.g., local geometry, boundaries, depth, and subtle state changes. This raises a fundamental question: should action policies inherit a fixed visual endpoint, or directly access evolving visual computation? We introduce NEO-VLA, a structurally encoder-free vision-language-action framework. Images are tokenized by lightweight patch convolutional layers and evolve jointly with language throughout the backbone, allowing a flow-matching action expert to draw from instruction-conditioned visual representations at different depths. This simple change reveals a consistent pattern. Native architectures outperform encoder-based counterparts under controlled comparisons; full fine-tuning reduces but does not erase the gap, and additional scale alone cannot recover it. More strikingly, layer-wise probing shows that the action-critical signals often peak well before the final visual representation. Across diverse manipulation settings and distribution shifts, NEO-VLA consequently achieves stronger and more robust control. Together, these results suggest that embodied control benefits from access to visual representations across the computational hierarchy, rather than a fixed encoder output.

</details>


### [50] [Motion-guided Contrastive Regularization For Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06901)
*Yiwen Chen,Qiqi Hou,Jisoo Jeong,Yinhao Zhu,Huaizu Jiang,Amir Said*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出JEPA式世界模型存在"静态坍缩"失效模式，并提出运动引导对比正则化来缓解，显著提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA风格世界模型仅靠分布正则化（如SIGReg）无法保证表征保留控制所需信息。作者识别出"静态坍缩"：编码器保留与情节相关但静态的背景内容，却抑制与操作相关的状态变化；这类静态内容在时间上可预测但在不同情节间差异大，故SIGReg等正则项无法消除。受控实验显示随机背景会让OGBench-Cube规划性能大幅下降。

Method: 提出运动引导对比正则化：将预测的潜空间转移与同一情节内候选观测的转移进行对比，降低静态背景身份在区分动作结果中的作用；用光流相似度作软对比目标，使运动相似的候选获得相似监督；与潜空间预测和SIGReg联合构成总目标。

Result: 在OGBench-Cube（随机背景）上规划成功率由LeWM的52%提升至78%；LIBERO-Object上由14%提升至54%；在另外四个视觉控制基准上性能保持相当。

Conclusion: 单纯分布正则化不足以防止静态坍缩，引入运动引导的对比正则化可有效促使表征保留情节内状态区分及相关转移的相似性，从而显著改善控制性能且不牺牲其他基准表现。

Abstract: JEPA-style world models learn action-conditioned latent dynamics without reconstructing observations, but distributional regularization alone does not ensure that their representations preserve information needed for control. We identify static collapse, a failure mode in which the encoder preserves episode-specific static content while suppressing manipulation-relevant state changes. Because static content is temporally predictable yet diverse across episodes, this failure can persist despite regularizers such as SIGReg. A controlled study on OGBench-Cube shows that introducing randomized backgrounds substantially degrades planning performance while leaving the underlying manipulation task unchanged. To address this problem, we propose motion-guided contrastive regularization. Our approach compares predicted latent transitions with transitions to candidate observations from the same episode, reducing the usefulness of static background identity for distinguishing action outcomes. Optical-flow similarities provide soft contrastive targets, allowing candidates with similar motion to receive similar supervision. Combined with latent prediction and SIGReg, our objective encourages representations to preserve within-episode state distinctions and similarities between related transitions. Our method improves planning success over LeWorldModel (LeWM) from 52% to 78% on OGBench-Cube with randomized backgrounds and from 14% to 54% on LIBERO-Object, while maintaining comparable performance on four other visual-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [51] [Semantic Residual Vector Quantization for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06781)
*Chang Liu,Ke Han,Niccolò Biondi,Gloria Isotton,Elisa Ricci,Davide Talon,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRVQ将手语生成建模为"语义→运动学"两级过程，用单一残差向量量化器实现语义编码与姿态细化，并通过词感知的掩码语义生成器+运动学生成器合成SMPL-X动作，R@1显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于姿态的系统与仅做重建的运动tokenizer把"要表达哪些手语"和"如何精确实现（手势形状、轨迹、表情、身体协调）"两类决策纠缠在一起，缺少显式的、与文本对齐的中间目标，导致生成器无法先确定语义再细化运动。

Method: 提出Semantic Residual Vector Quantization（SRVQ）框架：SRV Quantizer学习层级化码本，第一残差层编码手语语义内容，后续残差层细化姿态、手形、轨迹与身体协调，无需额外的语义表示或外部规划模块；配合词感知条件（word-aware conditioning）进行层级码生成——掩码语义生成器预测语义码，运动学生成器补全残差码，最终合成完整SMPL-X动作。

Result: 在共享文本-运动嵌入空间中用检索指标与FID评估，并辅以标准几何指标。CSL-Daily与Phoenix-2014T上R@1分别达82.3和70.3，较此前SOTA分别提升30和46个点。

Conclusion: 将手语生成解耦为语义码与运动学残差码的两级量化流程，可在单一量化器内显式提供文本对齐的中间目标，显著提升手语生成的内容保真度与检索性能。

Abstract: Sign language generation (SLG) maps written sentences into signing motion while preserving both lexical content and fine-grained articulation. This requires a model to decide what signs should be expressed and to realize them through precise handshape, trajectory, facial expression and body coordination. Existing pose-based systems and reconstruction-only motion tokenizers often entangle these two decisions, leaving the generator without an explicit text-aligned intermediate target for what to sign before refining how to sign it. To address this issue, we introduce Semantic Residual Vector Quantization (SRVQ), a framework for SLG that frames generation as a two-level process moving from semantics to kinematics. The newly introduced SRV Quantizer learns a hierarchy in which the first residual level encodes sign content and subsequent levels refine pose, handshape, trajectory, and body coordination, all within a single quantizer and without separate semantic representations or external planning stages. Through word-aware conditioning for hierarchical code generation, a masked semantic generator predicts semantic codes and a kinematic generator completes the residual codes for full SMPL-X motion synthesis. We evaluate using retrieval and FID in a shared text-motion embedding space, alongside standard geometric metrics. On CSL-Daily and Phoenix-2014T, SRVQ achieves R@1 of 82.3 and 70.3, outperforming the previous SOTA by 30 and 46 points, respectively.

</details>


### [52] [CardioDrift: Automatic Cardiac Motion Tracking on a 3D Atlas Mesh via Shape-aware Coherent Point Drift with Slice Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06664)
*Reza Akbari Movahed,Abuzar Rezaee,Shatha Alamri,Edmond S. L. Ho,Fani Deligianni,Ali Gooya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CardioDrift：一种全自动、基于图谱的概率框架，在具有全局对应点的3D图谱网格上表示受试者心脏运动，将CPD推广为多区域联合配准并内嵌切片位移校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏运动跟踪方法分两类：一类不具备跨受试者解剖对应，限制受试者间运动比较；另一类虽具全局对应点，但需耗时且易错的半自动图谱拟合步骤。

Method: 将Coherent Point Drift（CPD）概率框架推广，联合将多个心脏区域的图谱表面配准到由cine CMR分割图提取的解剖对应轮廓（覆盖整个心动周期）；并将切片位移（slice-shift）校正嵌入同一概率模型，估计逐层平移以抑制错位伪影。

Result: 在1301名随机选取的UK Biobank受试者及M&M数据集上评估，LV、Myo、RV的DSC分别为92.23±8.70%、82.11±7.56%、88.69±4.13%，在两个数据集的所有距离与重叠指标上均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: CardioDrift实现了全自动、跨受试者对应的图谱化心脏运动表示，避免半自动拟合，并同时校正切片错位伪影，精度全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: Automatic cardiac motion tracking from cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging is essential for quantifying myocardial motion abnormalities associated with cardiovascular disease. Existing methods fall into two groups: those that represent motion without anatomical correspondence across subjects and therefore limit inter-subject motion assessment; and those that represent motion with globally corresponding points, which typically require a time-consuming and error-prone semi-automatic step to fit a cardiac atlas to the subject space. To address these limitations, this study proposes CardioDrift, a fully automatic, atlas-based probabilistic framework that represents subject-specific cardiac motion on a 3D atlas mesh with globally corresponding points. The Coherent Point Drift (CPD) formulation is generalised to jointly register the atlas surfaces of multiple cardiac regions to the anatomically corresponding contours extracted from cine CMR segmentation maps throughout the cardiac cycle. Slice-shift correction is embedded in the same probabilistic model, estimating slice-wise translations that suppress the misalignment artefact limiting atlas-fitting accuracy. Evaluation on 1301 randomly selected UK Biobank subjects and the M&M dataset, CardioDrift attains Dice similarity coefficients (DSCs) of $92.23\pm8.70\%$, $82.11\pm7.56\%$, and $88.69\pm4.13\%$ for the left ventricle (LV), myocardium (Myo), and right ventricle (RV), exceeding the strongest baseline on every distance and overlap metric of both datasets.

</details>


### [53] [PathoS2VD: From 2D Histopathology to Pseudo Z-Stack Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05974)
*Jiamu Wang,Keunho Byeon,Ji-Uk Kim,Jin Tae Kwak,Heounjeong Go*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出基于I2V潜在扩散模型的生成框架，从单张2D病理切片合成伪z-stack，并设计保先验混合微调策略，将有限z-stack数据学到的z轴形态先验迁移到常规单平面组织病理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断病理长期依赖二维薄切片，但组织形态本质为三维；单切片仅捕获一个焦平面，z轴方向的重要形态线索被丢弃，限制人工判读与计算分析的表达能力。专用容积成像（z-stack采集）成本高、难以大规模临床部署。

Method: 训练图像到视频（I2V）潜在扩散模型（LDM），以单张2D图像为输入合成伪z-stack，旨在生成相邻焦平面间合理（plausible）的形态变化而非重建真实组织体；进而提出保先验混合微调（prior-preserving mixture fine-tuning）策略，使模型在无原生z-stack的大规模临床数据上适配新域的同时保留已学的z轴形态先验。

Result: 在广泛实验上，合成伪z-stack表现出真实的跨平面形态连续性；相较传统2D表示，在前列腺癌分类下游任务上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 从有限z-stack数据学习可迁移的z轴先验，是丰富常规病理图像、补充形态信息的实用途径；以生成式伪体积替代专用容积成像具备成本效益。

Abstract: For decades, diagnostic pathology has relied on thin two-dimensional (2D) tissue sections, despite biological tissues exhibiting inherently three-dimensional (3D) morphological organization. Because a single section captures only one focal plane, important morphological cues along the z-axis are inevitably discarded, limiting tissue representation for both human interpretation and computational analysis. In this work, we propose a generative framework that learns z-axis morphological priors from pathology z-stack data, offering a cost-effective alternative to dedicated volumetric imaging, and transfers this knowledge to conventional single-plane histopathology. Specifically, an image-to-video (I2V) latent diffusion model (LDM) is trained to synthesize a pseudo z-stack from a single 2D image, capturing plausible morphological variations across adjacent focal planes rather than reconstructing physical tissue volumes. To enable deployment on large-scale clinical datasets without native z-stack acquisition, we further introduce a prior-preserving mixture fine-tuning strategy that adapts the model to new domains while retaining the learned z-axis morphological prior. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the synthesized pseudo z-stacks exhibit realistic cross-plane morphological continuity and consistently improve downstream prostate cancer classification compared with conventional 2D representations. Our results suggest that learning transferable z-axis priors from limited z-stack data provides a practical way to enrich routine pathology images with complementary morphological information. Code: https://github.com/QuIIL/PathoS2VD.

</details>


### [54] [A 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06471)
*Yulu Xie,Zilong Li,Shen Xu,Yifan Bai,Liuhong Shi,Jiao Chen,Xianglei Kong,Wenchao Zhu,Na Shen,Xianghua Ye,Yang Yan,Jiaju Zheng,Yunsong Peng,Yanzhou Su,Chaohui Yu,Hao Luo,Fan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HoloCT是一个统一CT解读与三维分割的通用3D基础模型，通过联合建模全卷上下文与细粒度解剖细节，将3D掩膜作为文本发现的证据，并在346项任务及多中心验证中超越专科模型、基础模型及参与研究的放射科医师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT模型将解读（报告/问答）与3D分割割裂为独立任务，导致文本发现缺乏空间可验证性，且未利用临床理解与空间感知之间的协同潜力。

Method: 提出HoloCT通用3D基础模型，联合建模全卷上下文与细粒度解剖细节，在单一模型中统一CT解读与体素级分割，输出3D掩膜作为证据；构建HoloDATA数据集，含73个公开数据集与6个临床队列共195,921例CT、620万条指令对话及280万对CT-掩膜配对。

Result: 在61个内部与12个外部数据集、覆盖分割/病灶筛查/视觉问答/报告生成的346项任务上优于专科与基础模型；5家医院11,455例多中心验证中，病灶分割与报告生成分别领先18.2与15.3分；甲状腺肿瘤与结肠病变筛查平均准确率85.1%，在回顾性阅片研究中超过全部4位参与放射科医师；语言引导分割较领先方法提升22.1分，反之同步生成3D掩膜亦显著提升解读性能。

Conclusion: 临床理解与空间感知可在单一基础模型内共同学习，并呈现双向语义-空间协同，为更准确可靠的CT分析提供路径。

Abstract: Radiologists interpret computed tomography (CT) scans by jointly determining what a finding represents and where it lies in three-dimensional (3D) anatomy. However, CT models have treated CT interpretation and 3D segmentation as separate tasks, leaving textual findings spatially unverifiable and overlooking potential synergy between clinical understanding and spatial perception. Here we present HoloCT, a generalist 3D foundation model unifying CT interpretation and voxel-level segmentation by jointly modelling whole-volume context and fine-grained anatomical detail, providing 3D masks as evidence. We curated HoloDATA, comprising 195,921 CT scans from 73 public datasets and six clinical cohorts, with 6.2 million instruction dialogues and 2.8 million CT-mask pairs. Across 346 tasks spanning segmentation, lesion screening, visual question answering, and report generation on 61 internal and 12 external datasets, HoloCT outperformed specialist and foundation models. In multicentre validation across 11,455 clinical CT scans from five hospitals, HoloCT outperformed competing models in lesion segmentation and report generation by 18.2 and 15.3 points. It also achieved 85.1% mean accuracy in screening thyroid tumours and colon lesions, exceeding all four participating radiologists in a retrospective reader study. Further analyses revealed bidirectional semantic-spatial synergy: language-guided segmentation delineated unseen anatomical structures and lesions as well as diffuse free-text findings, outperforming the leading approach by 22.1 points, whereas prompting the model to generate corresponding 3D masks alongside its textual outputs significantly improved interpretation performance. HoloCT shows that clinical understanding and spatial perception can be learned together within a single foundation model and offers a path towards more accurate and reliable CT analysis.

</details>


### [55] [CrossPressor: Scalable Cross-View Fusion for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06258)
*Yuxi Hu,Wenhao Dong,Jun Zhang,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法CrossPressor，通过coverage-cycled证据路由解决基于锚点的前馈3D-GS压缩中的"表示-融合失配"问题，实现长序列可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D Gaussian Splatting难以扩展到长多视角序列。基于锚点的压缩方法（如ZPressor）通过仅从紧凑锚点特征图解码高斯来抑制表示增长，但锚点数量固定，导致每个锚点需聚合不断增多的支撑视角，形成"表示-融合失配"（representation-fusion mismatch），融合开销随视角数增长。

Method: 提出CrossPressor：一种免训练的可扩展锚点融合方法。保留所有面向解码器的锚点查询不变，仅对随视角增长的支撑序列施加约束——采用coverage-cycled evidence routing，将保留的空间证据分配到各支撑视角与融合层，并保证所有支撑视角均有表示；将路由置于K/V投影之前，并结合joint context packing，进一步降低支撑侧融合成本，且无需重新训练。

Result: 重建质量接近稠密融合，跨视角融合最高提速26.12倍，长输入序列端到端加速1.43倍。

Conclusion: CrossPressor通过免训练的锚点查询保留与支撑视角侧证据路由，有效缓解表示-融合失配，在保持重建质量的同时显著降低长序列跨视角融合计算量，为前馈3D-GS的长序列扩展提供了一种低成本方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) becomes increasingly difficult to scale to long multi-view sequences. Anchor-based compression methods such as ZPressor alleviate representation growth by decoding Gaussians only from a compact set of anchor feature maps, but keeping these anchors fixed forces each one to aggregate an increasing number of support views. We identify this representation--fusion mismatch and introduce \textbf{CrossPressor}, a training-free method for scalable anchor-based fusion. CrossPressor preserves all decoder-facing anchor queries while constraining the view-growing support sequence through coverage-cycled evidence routing, distributing retained spatial evidence across support views and fusion layers while keeping all support views represented. Routing before K/V projection, together with joint context packing, further reduces support-side fusion cost without retraining. CrossPressor maintains reconstruction quality close to dense fusion while achieving up to $26.12\times$ faster cross-view fusion and $1.43\times$ end-to-end acceleration on long input sequences. Our project page is at: https://yuhsihu.github.io/CrossPressor.

</details>


### [56] [CAPE-JEPA - Cross-modal Adversarial Probabilistic Embedding JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06247)
*Aniket Junghare,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAPE-JEPA，用概率化跨模态JEPA在SAR/多光谱/RGB间实现模态不变且语义判别的遥感检索表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态遥感检索需表示既模态不变又语义判别，但SAR与多光谱等异构传感器存在巨大物理鸿沟。现有JEPA式预测自监督（如X-JEPA）将跨模态对齐建模为确定性潜变量预测，隐含假设各patch可预测性一致，且未处理跨模态分布级对齐。

Method: 三项贡献：(i) 交叉注意力高斯预测器，以对侧模态上下文为条件输出逐patch参数(mu, sigma^2)，用负对数似然训练以建模跨传感器预测不确定性；(ii) 对抗式分布正则，将单模态各向同性高斯正则与跨模态对抗变体结合，通过最坏情况Epps-Pulley特征函数检验对齐模态分布，无需显式样本配对；(iii) 训练中对比学习配注意力池化头，实现场景级判别性锚定。

Result: 在BEN-14K与fMoW上跨SAR、多光谱、RGB评估，较X-JEPA在BEN-14K最高提升13.74点、fMoW最高提升15.59点，单模态与跨模态方向均优于SkySense、CROMA等基线，且参数更少（407.30M vs 432.72M）。

Conclusion: 将跨模态预测由确定性改为概率分布建模，并辅以无配对的分布级对抗对齐与场景级对比锚定，可在更小参数下显著提升跨模态遥感检索性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal remote sensing image retrieval (RS-CMIR) requires representations that are simultaneously modality-invariant and semantically discriminative, despite the profound physical gap between heterogeneous sensors such as Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) and multispectral imagery. Recent JEPA-based Predictive self-supervised approaches such as X-JEPA formulate cross-modal alignment as a deterministic latent forecasting task, implicitly assuming uniform patch predictability and leaving cross-modal distributional alignment unaddressed. We propose CAPE-JEPA, a probabilistic cross-modal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture with three contributions: (i) a cross-attentional Gaussian predictor outputting per-patch parameters (mu, sigma^2) conditioned on opposite-modality context, trained via negative log-likelihood to model inherent cross-sensor prediction uncertainty; (ii) adversarial distributional regularization combining unimodal isotropic Gaussian regularization with a novel cross-modal adversarial variant that aligns modality distributions via worst-case Epps-Pulley characteristic function tests without explicit sample pairing; and (iii) in-training contrastive learning with attention-pooled heads for scene-level discriminative grounding. Evaluated on BigEarthNet-14K (BEN-14K) and fMoW across SAR, multispectral, and RGB modalities, CAPE-JEPA surpasses X-JEPA by up to 13.74 points on BEN-14K and 15.59 points on fMoW, outperforming all baselines including SkySense and CROMA across both unimodal and cross-modal directions while using fewer parameters (407.30M vs. 432.72M).

</details>


### [57] [DSRS: Teaching MLLMs to Perceive, Reason, and Segment in Complex Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06160)
*Sicen Guo,Zhengfei Song,Kuanhsun Chen,Xu Huang,Ping Zhong,Wei Ye,Shaoyi Du,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出驾驶场景推理分割任务DSRS并构建基准数据集，进而提出DSRS-Net，通过无缝注入几何特征缓解MLLM仅RGB预训练导致的模态表示失配，在DSRS及多种语言驱动分割任务上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割框架依赖具备广泛世界知识的MLLM进行类人语义解析并生成掩码，但缺乏对特定场景的感知能力（scene-specific awareness），在驾驶场景中难以处理隐式指令、空间关系推断、潜在风险检测、困难条件与交通规则理解。

Method: 定义DSRS任务并建立基准数据集；提出DSRS-Net，在不破坏原有跨模态融合机制的前提下无缝注入几何特征，增强几何、上下文与语言特征的整合，缓解MLLM因仅RGB预训练造成的模态表示失配。

Result: 大量实验表明DSRS-Net在驾驶场景推理分割以及多个公开数据集上的其他语言驱动分割任务中均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了驾驶场景推理分割这一新方向，提供了任务定义、基准数据集与有效架构，验证了几何特征融合对场景特定推理分割的价值；源码与数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Existing reasoning segmentation frameworks typically employ multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with broad world knowledge to extract semantics in a human-like manner and generate corresponding segmentation masks. Nevertheless, these frameworks lack the necessary scene-specific awareness. This paper introduces driving scene reasoning segmentation (DSRS), a scene-specific task that requires models to interpret implicit instructions, infer spatial relations, detect potential risks, handle challenging conditions, and comprehend underlying traffic rules, ultimately generating accurate segmentation masks. Meanwhile, we establish a benchmark dataset to facilitate future research on the DSRS task. To pioneer this task, we propose DSRS-Net, a novel architecture with advanced reasoning segmentation capability for automated driving. Specifically, DSRS-Net seamlessly infuses geometric features for deeper reasoning segmentation, without compromising the original multimodal fusion mechanism. This strategy effectively mitigates the modality representation mismatch in MLLMs caused by RGB-only pretraining, while enhancing the integration of geometric, contextual, and linguistic features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of DSRS-Net in both driving scene reasoning segmentation and other language-driven segmentation tasks across various public datasets. The source code and dataset will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [58] [PTQ4SAM3: Semantics-Preserving Post-Training Quantization for Segment Anything Model 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06143)
*Xi Xiao,Yunbei Zhang,Lin Zhao,Janet Wang,Xinru Jiang,Yanshu Li,Chenrui Ma,Pan Wang,Tianyang Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对SAM 3的PTQ中的"提示对齐漂移"问题，提出语义保持量化SPQ，通过概念敏感参数加权校准、残差特征保留和视频记忆均值误差校正，实现离线、零推理开销的量化，W4A8下RefCOCO Pr@0.5从42.1提升至72.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 3支持文本提示分割与跨视频跟踪，但内存与计算开销大，难以部署。PTQ虽可降本，却会导致量化模型选错目标或跨帧丢失目标，即"提示对齐漂移"。受控实验表明，扰动低方差特征方向会显著影响目标选择，说明量化误差对概念敏感方向尤为有害。

Method: 提出语义保持量化（SPQ），含三个组件：(1) 对概念敏感参数赋予更高校准权重；(2) 保留残差特征；(3) 校正视频记忆中的均值误差。校准由固定文本概念引导，无需任务标注；三组件均离线运行，不增加推理算子。

Result: W4A8图像编码器下，RefCOCO Pr@0.5由PTQ4SAM的42.1提升至72.5，同时保持54.8 COCO mask AP；在LVIS、RefCOCO变体与SA-V上取得一致增益；CLIP研究中图文检索性能亦有提升。

Conclusion: SPQ以低开销的离线手段缓解提示对齐漂移，显著改善量化SAM 3的提示分割与跟踪质量，并具备跨数据集与跨模型（CLIP）的泛化性。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) can segment objects from text prompts and track them across video, but its memory and computation costs make deployment difficult. Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces these costs by using fewer bits. However, a quantized model can select the wrong object or lose track of its target across frames. We call this prompt-alignment drift. Controlled tests show that perturbing low-variance feature directions strongly affects target selection. We propose Semantics-Preserving Quantization (SPQ), which gives more calibration weight to concept-sensitive parameters, preserves residual features, and corrects the mean error in video memory. Fixed text concepts guide calibration without task annotations. All three components operate offline and add no inference operators. With a W4A8 image encoder, SPQ improves RefCOCO Pr@0.5 from 42.1 to 72.5 over PTQ4SAM while retaining 54.8 COCO mask AP. Experiments on LVIS, RefCOCO variants, and SA-V show consistent gains. A CLIP study also shows improved image-text retrieval.

</details>


### [59] [Semantic Prior-Guided Incomplete Cross-Modal Retrieval with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06113)
*Jiali Chen,Ruitao Pu,Yingke Chen,Dezhong Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPIRN框架，以固定语义先验为统一参考，联合解决不完备跨模态检索中的模态缺失与标签噪声双重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多模态数据采集存在动态性与不一致性，导致模态严重缺失；现有不完备跨模态检索方法采用标签驱动范式，依赖类别标注作为语义基准，而标签噪声难以避免且常与模态缺失共存，二者叠加使鲁棒表示学习与监督信号均不可靠。

Method: SPIRN以固定语义先验作为鲁棒表示学习、噪声标签处理与缺失模态补全的一致参考，包含四个模块：语义先验引导（SPG）建立稳定的类别级语义空间；质量感知鲁棒学习（QRL）动态评估样本质量以区分干净/噪声样本，并通过反向学习处理噪声；先验引导全局标签校准（PGLC）修正被污染标注以获得精炼监督；置信度驱动选择性插补（CSI）借助跨模态记忆库恢复缺失模态，并以递增阈值过滤不可靠插补。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的基准数据集上，于同时包含模态缺失与标签噪声的多种场景中，SPIRN优于11种先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作据称是跨模态检索领域首个同时应对模态不完备与标签噪声双重挑战的框架，验证了统一语义先验参考在多噪声/缺失耦合场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-modal retrieval (CMR) aims to bridge the semantic gaps between modalities by learning shared representations. Unfortunately, due to the dynamism and inconsistencies in real-world data acquisition, multimodal datasets frequently encounter severe modality incompleteness. Existing incomplete CMR methods adhere to a label-driven paradigm, relying heavily on categorical annotations as the semantic benchmark. However, label noise is difficult to avoid and often coexists with modality incompleteness. To address this challenge, we propose a novel semantic prior-guided framework for incomplete cross-modal retrieval with noisy labels, termed SPIRN. Specifically, SPIRN uses fixed semantic priors as a consistent reference for robust representation learning, noisy-label handling, and missing-modality completion. Based on this reference, Semantic Priors Guidance (SPG) establishes a stable category-level semantic space, Quality-aware Robust Learning (QRL) dynamically evaluates sample quality to distinguish clean samples from noisy ones and handles noise via reverse learning, Prior-guided Global Label Calibration (PGLC) rectifies corrupted annotations for refined supervision, and Confidence-driven Selective Imputation (CSI) recovers missing modalities from a cross-modal memory bank while filtering unreliable imputations with an increasing threshold. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework to jointly tackle the dual challenges of modality incompleteness and label noise in the CMR community. Extensive experiments on four widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that SPIRN outperforms 11 state-of-the-art methods in various scenarios involving both missing modalities and noisy labels.

</details>


### [60] [XuanJian-DINOSAR: A SAR Foundation Model Based on Global and Local Semantic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06105)
*Chunxiao Wu,Huixin Huang,Jialei Pan,Qiang Liang,Tao Wu,Cheng Wu,Long Huang,Yuanji Li,Qiang Cheng,Aifang Liu,Linghao Xia,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAR基础模型XuanJian-DINOSAR，通过DINO全局语义自蒸馏+iBOT局部掩码建模+KoLeo正则，并新增面向SAR强度的局部语义一致性分支，在36万+ SAR影像上预训练，多数下游分类与检测指标达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR基础模型多采用掩码图像建模（像素重建或结构特征预测），对相干斑噪声敏感且依赖预定义算子；光学-SAR域间差距与斑点扰动削弱表示稳定性；对应空间位置上的跨视角语义与扰动稳定性联合建模仍研究不足。

Method: 构建36万+公开SAR目标切片、检测图像与场景图像数据集，将光学的DINOv2适配到SAR进行自监督预训练。框架融合：DINO全局语义自蒸馏、iBOT局部掩码建模、KoLeo特征分布正则。新增适配SAR强度统计与相干成像特性的局部语义一致性分支：共享几何变换生成空间对齐的教师弱视图与学生强视图，对学生视图施加强度变换与Gamma乘性斑点扰动，用余弦一致性损失对齐对应位置的patch token；与DINOv2多裁剪的跨区域、跨尺度语义一致性互补。

Result: 在多个下游分类与检测数据集上，多数评价指标取得SOTA性能，验证了所学表示的通用性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过在全局与局部语义一致性上联合建模（跨视角语义+扰动稳定性），可有效克服光学-SAR域差与斑点敏感性，为SAR基础模型提供稳定、可迁移的表示。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training on synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery enables transferable representations, but the optical-SAR domain gap and speckle perturbations limit representation stability. Most SAR foundation models use masked image modeling (MIM) through pixel reconstruction or structural feature prediction, which is sensitive to speckle or relies on predefined operators. Joint modeling of cross-view semantics and perturbation stability at corresponding spatial locations remains underexplored. We propose XuanJian-DINOSAR, a SAR foundation model based on global and local semantic consistency. We curate over 360K publicly available SAR target chips, detection images, and scene images spanning diverse targets, scenes, and sensors, and adapt an optically pre-trained DINOv2 model to SAR through self-supervised learning. The framework combines DINO global semantic self-distillation, iBOT local masked modeling, and KoLeo feature-distribution regularization to learn globally discriminative and locally structure-aware representations. We introduce a local semantic consistency branch tailored to SAR intensity statistics and coherent imaging characteristics. Shared geometric transformations produce spatially aligned teacher weak views and student strong views. Intensity transformations and Gamma multiplicative speckle perturbations are applied to student views, while a cosine consistency loss aligns patch tokens at corresponding locations, promoting stability under radiometric perturbations. This objective complements the cross-region and cross-scale semantic consistency of DINOv2 multi-crop training, jointly enforcing global and local semantic consistency. Experiments on multiple downstream classification and detection datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on most evaluation metrics, validating the generality and transferability of the learned representations.

</details>


### [61] [Many Pixels, One Composition: Self-Supervised Spectra for Specimen-Level Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06096)
*Eleftheria Tetoula-Tsonga,George Arvanitakis,Theodoros Giannakas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用高光谱成像预测地物样本的元素组成：先以自监督方式在像素级训练编码器，再冻结编码器并训练轻量级注意力回归头，性能显著优于光谱匹配与解混基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高光谱（HS）成像能否用于预测地表实物样本的元素组成，并将其建模为监督回归任务，以规避传统光谱匹配/解混方法在该任务上的局限。

Method: 使用含HS立方体与元素表征的公开样本数据集；像素级自监督方式训练编码器，冻结后以监督方式训练轻量级注意力回归头，将同一标本的多个表征与其元素标签配对；对比传统光谱匹配、光谱解混、基于平均光谱的同等容量监督模型，以及全模型微调。

Result: 在测试样本上显著优于传统光谱匹配与解混基线；也优于容量相近的基于平均光谱的监督模型；全模型微调在显著更高模型容量下取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 高光谱成像结合自监督编码器+注意力回归头可有效预测样本元素组成，验证了像素级表征学习相对传统光谱方法的优势；性能提升与模型容量相关。

Abstract: Can hyperspectral (HS) imaging be used to predict the elemental composition of physical specimens found on the Earth's surface? We approach this problem as a supervised regression task, using an open dataset of specimens, each comprising a HS cube and elemental characterization. In particular, we train an encoder in a self-supervised fashion that operates on pixel-level; then freeze the encoder and train a lightweight attention-based regression head in a supervised manner, by pairing many representations of a given specimen with its corresponding elemental label. In test specimens, our approach significantly outperforms traditional spectral matching and unmixing baselines. It also outperforms supervised learning models based on the mean spectrum (of similar model capacity), while full model fine-tuning achieves the strongest performance, at substantially higher model capacity.

</details>


### [62] [USI-Brain: Enhancing Underwater Spatial Intelligence via Spatial Data-Driven Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06040)
*Zhiliang Zhu,Zhiqiang Zhao,Xie'an Jiang,Yingxin Lai,Kangye Zheng,Dawei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出水下空间数据引擎构建4.27M空间QA语料，训练USI-Brain并设计UPR模块，在自建USI-Bench及三个公开基准上显著超越Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下空间推理依赖的物体边界与几何线索被散射、颜色失真和低能见度削弱；现有水下数据以图像、标注、深度图为主，对视觉语言模型仅提供碎片化监督，缺乏统一的空间能力训练信号。

Method: 构建空间数据引擎：通过图像增强、2D与几何辅助的问答生成、专家过滤，将视觉表观与物体级几何关联，产出覆盖5类能力组的4.27M QA对；据此训练USI-Brain，并引入水下先验引导矫正器（UPR），以门控方式对视觉特征施加修正；另构建USI-Bench（6,525条留出QA）。

Result: 在USI-Bench的15项任务指标上，USI-Brain与USI-Brain+UPR较Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct平均提升5.76与7.63分（百分制有符号差）；消融显示关闭UPR时几何辅助监督使深度与顺序任务分别提升4.8与6.1分，等量训练数据下UPR使场景描述提升18.0分；评测均用原始图像。在NautData、UWBench、MarineEval共32项指标中28项取得最优或并列最优。模型权重与USI-Bench将开源。

Conclusion: 几何辅助监督与视觉特征先验矫正互补有效，可在不使用增强图像推理的前提下提升水下空间推理能力，并具备跨基准泛化性；作者开源权重与基准以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Underwater spatial reasoning depends on object boundaries and geometric cues that are obscured by scattering, color distortion, and low visibility. Existing underwater resources capture these cues through images, annotations, and depth maps, but provide fragmented supervision for vision-language models. We present a spatial data engine that connects visual appearance with object-level geometry through enhancement, 2D and geometry-assisted question generation, and expert filtering. It produces 4.27M QA pairs across five capability groups. We use this corpus to train USI-Brain and introduce an Underwater Prior-guided Rectifier (UPR) that applies gated corrections to visual features. USI-Bench evaluates the resulting capabilities with 6,525 held-out QA pairs. On its 15 task metrics, USI-Brain and USI-Brain + UPR improve over Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct by 5.76 and 7.63 points on average, respectively, using signed differences on 100-point scales. Ablations show that geometry-assisted supervision improves depth and ordering by 4.8 and 6.1 points with UPR disabled, while UPR adds 18.0 points in scene description under matched training data. Both variants use raw images at evaluation. Across NautData, UWBench, and MarineEval, USI-Brain achieves the best or joint-best score on 28 of 32 metrics. Model weights and USI-Bench will be released at https://github.com/SKY-Zhu-91/USI-Brain.

</details>


### [63] [METRIC-GS: Selective Metric-Scale Estimation for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06020)
*Sooyeun Yang,Jongseong Brad Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: METRIC-GS是一个面向真实室内照片的"选择性"尺度估计器：从3DGS重建自身的语义内容中读取尺度，并在证据不足时显式拒答。在47个ScanNet++场景上，单靠它将启发式方法的平均误差从21.1%降至16.6%（实际作用场景上9.3%），约11秒/场景；仅将拒答样本路由给MASt3R即可在少60%调用量的情况下匹配其全局精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS重建继承SfM的任意单位，需外部提供尺度才能度量或放置AR内容。两种标准做法在真实采集上都不稳健：在5个卷尺实测层高的室内场景中，分位数地板-天花板启发式误差27%，度量深度对齐在三个网络上误差达35-71%。且现有度量尺度模型存在"训练分布内即最优、分布外严重退化"的问题。

Method: 冻结的2D教师模型通过可见性感知投票为高斯基元打语义标签；对建筑与家具类（门、桌、地板-天花板间距）采用实例级尺寸先验，经鲁棒最大似然融合；以内点质量检验将薄证据转化为显式弃答，并作为路由信号调往重量级度量模型（MASt3R）。可见性投票将可作用场景由16个提升至28个。

Result: 47个ScanNet++激光真值场景上（配置在公开评测前冻结）：估计器单独把启发式平均误差21.1%降至16.6%，其实际作用的28个场景上为9.3%，约11 s/场景。MASt3R单独更准（9.9%），但仅路由拒答样本可在调用量减少60%的条件下达到与全局运行相当的精度。在13段自采手机/无人机数据上（MASt3R自身配准门控失败12次），系统对所作用的5段全部改善、无退化。另报告：某前馈度量模型在其训练混合集内的基准上得2.3%，在未见采集上为28-35%。

Conclusion: 论文主张"选择性混合"配置（轻量语义尺度估计+按需路由）。同时强调：已发表的度量尺度数值只是最好情形，不能作为部署估计；度量的可靠性应通过显式弃答与证据质量检验来刻画。

Abstract: A 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstruction inherits arbitrary units from structure-from-motion, so it cannot be measured or used to place AR content until someone supplies a scale. Both standard recipes are fragile on real captures: on five indoor scenes with tape-measured ceilings, a quantile floor-to-ceiling heuristic errs by 27% and metric-depth alignment by 35-71% across three networks. We present METRIC-GS, a selective scale estimator for real indoor photographs: it reads the scale off the reconstruction's own semantic content and, just as importantly, refuses when the evidence is thin. A frozen 2D teacher labels Gaussians by visibility-aware voting; instance-level size priors for architectural and furniture classes (doors, tables, the floor-to-ceiling gap) are fused by a robust maximum-likelihood step; and an inlier-mass test turns thin evidence into an explicit abstention that doubles as a router to a heavyweight metric model. On 47 ScanNet++ scenes with laser ground truth, with a configuration frozen before public evaluation, the estimator alone cuts the heuristic's mean error from 21.1% to 16.6% (9.3% on the 28 scenes it acts on) at ~11 s per scene. MASt3R remains the more accurate standalone estimator (9.9%), but routing only our refusals to it matches running it everywhere with 60% fewer invocations; this selective hybrid is the configuration we advocate. Visibility-aware voting raises the scenes the estimator can act on from 16 to 28 at unchanged deployed accuracy. Applied unchanged to 13 phone and drone captures of our own, on which MASt3R fails its own registration gate 12 times, the system improves all five captures it acts on and degrades none. We also report that a current feed-forward metric model scores 2.3% on a benchmark inside its training mixture and 28-35% on captures it has never seen: a published metric-scale number is a best case, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [64] [FedIA: Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations for Federated Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05994)
*Xuanjing Zhang,Qi Guo,Yanan Qiao,Yong Han,Junge Bo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedIA，将联邦提示学习（FPL）的个性化从客户端级细化到实例级，通过实例感知的跨模态表示生成与自适应全局-局部融合提升异构场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPL方法采用"全局共享提示+客户端局部提示"的设计，仅实现客户端级个性化：同一局部提示应用于该客户端所有样本，且全局与局部提示以输入无关的固定方式组合，忽略了客户端内样本间的视觉差异，也无法针对每个输入调整共享知识与个性化知识的平衡。

Method: FedIA框架包含三步：(1) 以全局文本提示作为客户端不变的语义锚点，为每个候选类别筛选最相关的图像patch，抑制干扰区域；(2) 用筛选出的视觉证据条件化生成实例感知的局部文本表示；(3) 自适应全局-局部融合模块，针对每个图像-类别对校准局部与全局文本表示的贡献。

Result: 在域偏移、病态标签偏斜和基于Dirichlet的标签偏斜三种设置下，FedIA均优于FPL基线，相较最新基线最大精度提升为1.44%–4.37%。消融研究与分析进一步验证了实例感知表示生成与自适应全局-局部融合的互补贡献。

Conclusion: FedIA在保留跨客户端共享语义知识的同时，将FPL的个性化从客户端级推进到实例级，是一种有效的参数高效联邦视觉-语言适配方案。

Abstract: Federated Prompt Learning (FPL) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of pre-trained Vision-Language Models across decentralized clients without exchanging raw data. To cope with cross-client heterogeneity, existing FPL methods typically pair a globally shared prompt with a client-specific local prompt. However, this design personalizes only at the client level: the same local prompt is applied to all samples of a client, and the global and local prompts are combined in an input-agnostic, fixed manner. Consequently, it overlooks the substantial visual variations among samples within the same client and cannot adjust the balance between shared and personalized knowledge to each input. To address these limitations, we propose Federated Prompt Learning with Instance-Aware Cross-Modal Representations (FedIA), an FPL framework that learns instance-aware cross-modal representations. Specifically, FedIA first uses global text prompts as client-invariant semantic anchors to select image patches most relevant to each candidate class, suppressing distracting regions. The selected visual evidence then conditions the generation of instance-aware local text representations. Finally, an adaptive global-local fusion module calibrates the contributions of local and global text representations for each image-class pair. In this way, FedIA shifts personalization in FPL from the client level to the instance level while preserving the semantic knowledge shared across clients. Extensive experiments under domain shift, pathological label skew, and Dirichlet-based label skew show that FedIA outperforms FPL baselines, with maximum accuracy gains of 1.44\%-4.37\% over the latest baseline across the three settings. Ablation studies and analyses further verify the complementary contributions of instance-aware representation generation and adaptive global-local fusion.

</details>


### [65] [Toward an Optimal Configuration for Tree Species Classification from Bark Images: A Leakage-Safe Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05680)
*Maeve Côté*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统消融研究表明，树皮图像树种识别中补丁尺寸是最主要的精度驱动因素：512 px 时图像级准确率约 94.9%；学习式聚合比多数投票最多只高 0.7 pp，主干容量与两阶段训练影响甚微；推荐配置为大补丁（约 512 px）+ nano 主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树皮照片自动识别树种对林业清查与木材加工业有实用价值，但深度学习方案中影响精度的设计选择缺乏系统研究；同时，已有部分结果可能因同一树干图像在训练/测试集间泄漏而被高估，需要泄漏安全的树级划分下重新审视。

Method: 在公开 BarkNet 1.0（20 类）上，采用既定树级、无泄漏划分做系统消融；以 ConvNeXt-V2 为主干，五折交叉验证，逐一变动：补丁尺寸、补丁预测聚合成图像级决策的策略、训练制度（单阶段 vs 两阶段）、主干容量、整图基线、推理时可用补丁数。

Result: 补丁级准确率随尺寸单调上升（160 px 的 75.6% → 512 px 的 89.6%），图像级准确率同步提升并在 512 px 附近以约 94.9% 趋于饱和；学习式聚合较多数投票最多高 0.7 pp，且随补丁增大、注意力趋于均匀而优势缩小；主干容量（pico 至 tiny）影响不足 1 pp，两阶段训练无优势；整图分类 91.9%，低于补丁聚合（缺乏可平均的 bag）；准确率随推理补丁数增加而提升，约 64 个补丁处饱和。

Conclusion: 给出简单推荐配置：在留有足够补丁可平均的前提下尽量用大补丁（本文约 512 px），搭配 nano 主干；同时量化了学习式聚合与模型容量的二阶作用，为从业者与后续研究提供可复现的参照基准。

Abstract: Background: The automatic identification of tree species from bark photographs is of practical interest for forestry inventory and the wood-processing industry, yet the design choices that govern the accuracy of deep-learning approaches have not been studied systematically, and some prior results may be inflated by leakage of images of the same trunk across training and test partitions. Methods: A systematic ablation study is conducted on the public BarkNet 1.0 dataset (20 species) under the established tree-level, leakage-safe partitioning scheme. Using ConvNeXt-V2 backbones, the following factors are varied in turn and evaluated by five-fold cross-validation: patch size, the strategy by which per-patch predictions are aggregated into an image-level decision, the training regime (single- versus two-stage), backbone capacity, the whole-image baseline, and the number of patches available at inference. Results: Per-patch accuracy rises monotonically with patch size (from 75.6% at 160 px to 89.6% at 512 px), and image-level accuracy rises in step, plateauing near 94.9% around 512 px. Learned aggregation exceeds majority voting by at most 0.7 pp, an advantage that shrinks as patches grow and attention becomes near-uniform. Backbone capacity (pico to tiny) alters accuracy by less than a point, and two-stage training offers no advantage over one-stage. Whole-image classification (91.9%) trails patch-based aggregation, since it admits no bag to average over; accuracy increases with the number of patches at inference, saturating around 64. Conclusions: The study yields a simple recommended configuration: patches as large as possible while leaving enough to average over (around 512 px here), with a nano backbone. It also quantifies the second-order role of learned aggregation and model capacity, providing a reproducible reference point for practitioners and future research

</details>


### [66] [Role-based Effectiveness of an Epistemic Uncertainty Estimate in Training Models for Semantic Segmentation of 3D LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05569)
*Krishnakumar N,Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在四个大规模LiDAR点云基准上显式建模认知不确定性，并将其以"输入特征"和"损失权重"两种角色引入分割流程，证明不确定性可提升mIoU、恢复少数类，并在部分配置下改善误差—不确定性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云固有稀疏性、噪声和无序性使可靠分类困难；现有SOTA多将不确定性作为精度提升手段，缺乏对生成式估计不确定性在分割流程中可扮演多种角色的系统探索。

Method: 采用生成式方法显式建模认知不确定性，构建基线ANN及两个不确定性感知变体（不确定性作为额外输入特征、作为逐样本置信度加权训练损失），在ISPRS Vaihingen、AHN3、Paris-Lille-3D、Toronto-3D四个基准上，与PointNet++、RandLA-Net参考模型及其不确定性增强版本对比，采用规范的空间不相交训练/测试划分协议评估。

Result: 不确定性感知建模提升了平均IoU；恢复了基线ANN完全无法预测的少数类；在一半配置下，以Patch Accuracy对Patch Uncertainty衡量的预测误差与估计不确定性之间对齐程度得到改善。

Conclusion: 不确定性在分割流程中可承担多重角色，而不仅是提升精度的工具；应将其视为流程设计中的可复用信号而非单一精度装置。

Abstract: Point cloud segmentation is a fundamental task in 3D data analysis, with applications across robotics, augmented and virtual reality, and remote sensing. However, the inherent sparsity, noise, and unordered nature of point clouds introduce significant challenges, particularly in achieving reliable classification. While the state-of-the-art rely on training models for explicit modeling of uncertainty, there is a gap in exploring different roles that estimated uncertainty using generative approaches can play. This work investigates the impact of deterministic uncertainty in point cloud segmentation by explicitly modeling epistemic uncertainty and using it in different roles, namely, as a feature and in loss function. We evaluate a baseline artificial neural network (ANN) and two uncertainty-aware variants (uncertainty as an additional input feature and as a per-sample confidence weight in the training loss) on four large-scale LiDAR benchmarks spanning airborne and mobile acquisition, namely ISPRS Vaihingen, AHN3, Paris-Lille-3D, and Toronto-3D, together with PointNet++ and RandLA-Net references and their uncertainty-augmented counterparts under canonical spatially disjoint train and test splits. Under this protocol, uncertainty-aware modeling improves mean Intersection over Union, recovers minority classes that the baseline ANN never predicts at all, and in half of the configurations improves the alignment between prediction error and estimated uncertainty as measured by Patch Accuracy versus Patch Uncertainty. Our findings indicate that uncertainty can play multiple roles in the segmentation pipeline rather than as a device for improving accuracy alone.

</details>


### [67] [Lesion Overlap Ranks PET Segmentation Models but Does Not Certify Patient-Level Tumor Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05564)
*Tushar Kataria,Gerald Sabin,Ponnuswamy Sadayappan,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于1,014例autoPET FDG PET/CT研究，检验Dice等重叠指标能否识别个体患者层面不可靠的MTV估计，发现平均排名一致但患者层面仍存在显著阈值错配，建议基准报告患者级MTV误差与漏检病灶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTV用于全身PET疾病负荷的预后分层与疗效评估，自动化MTV只有在个体患者层面可靠才有价值；然而分割模型通常以Dice等重叠指标评价，而非以负荷误差评价，二者是否等价尚不清楚。

Method: 使用1,014例autoPET FDG PET/CT：评估未参与微调的LesionTracer预测；在489例肿瘤阳性患者上比较五个已发布模型；并在597例PSMA研究中复核。开展模型层面排序比较、患者层面（out-of-fold）Dice与相对MTV误差的相关分析、Dice 0.80与MTV误差25%阈值的交叉一致性分析，以及假阳性/假阴性体积的抵消效应与漏检病灶计数分析。

Result: 五个FDG模型按较高平均Dice排序与按较低平均相对MTV误差排序完全一致（ρ=-1.00，精确p=0.017）。但患者层面out-of-fold Dice与相对MTV误差虽强相关（ρ=-0.75），仍有82/489（16.8%）患者落在Dice 0.80与MTV误差25%阈值的相反一侧。训练暴露的已发布模型相关系数为-0.47至-0.74，且Dice区间较窄偏高。out-of-fold中假阳性与假阴性体积相互抵消约三分之一的整体空间误差；236/489例患者中五个模型各自至少漏检一个病灶（且未必为同一病灶）。

Conclusion: 模型平均排名一致并不能保证个体MTV估计可靠，也无法揭示共有的漏检病灶。PET分割基准应在Dice等重叠指标之外，同时报告患者级MTV误差与漏检病灶数。

Abstract: Metabolic tumor volume (MTV) summarizes whole-body PET disease burden for prognostic stratification and treatment assessment. Automated MTV is useful only if its estimate is reliable for the individual patient, yet segmentation models are usually evaluated with overlap metrics such as Dice rather than burden error. We examined whether overlap metrics identify unreliable MTV estimates using 1,014 autoPET FDG PET/CT studies. We evaluated LesionTracer predictions held out from fine-tuning, compared five selected released models on 489 tumor-positive patients, and examined the findings in 597 PSMA studies. Across the five FDG models, ranking by higher mean Dice gave the same order as ranking by lower mean relative MTV error ($ρ=-1.00$, exact $p=0.017$). At the patient level, however, out-of-fold Dice tracked relative MTV error strongly ($ρ=-0.75$), yet 82 of 489 patients (16.8\%) fell on opposite sides of the Dice 0.80 and MTV-error 25\% thresholds. Within the training-exposed released models, correlations ranged from $-0.47$ to $-0.74$ over narrower, high-Dice ranges. Out of fold, opposing false-positive and false-negative volumes cancelled about one third of spatial error in net MTV. In 236 of 489 patients, each of the five models missed at least one lesion, though not necessarily the same lesion. Thus, agreement in average model rankings did not establish the reliability of individual MTV estimates or reveal shared lesion misses.
