PET segmentation benchmarks should report patient-level MTV error and missed-lesion counts alongside Dice and other overlap metrics.

</details>


### [68] [PointNDF: Cascaded Neural Displacement Fields for Point Cloud Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05411)
*Michael Y. Wang,Pingping Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PointNDF，结合块级几何上下文与局部位移预测的点云去噪框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段方法先提取逐点特征再回归位移，缺乏稳健的特征引导与分布感知的几何细化，难以兼顾点均匀性与几何质量。

Method: 用 PTv3 编码器将多尺度几何信息聚合为全局块描述子；神经位移场（NDF）融合全局描述子与逐点邻域特征，经有界向量分支与尺度因子分支预测位移；三个级联 NDF 阶段传播局部特征并渐进细化点位置。

Result: 在 PU-Net 与 ModelNet10 上取得有竞争力的去噪精度与较优推理效率。

Conclusion: 块级几何上下文与分布感知位移场相结合，可提升点云去噪的均匀性与几何保真度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Point cloud denoising aims to recover clean geometry from noisy 3D observations while preserving both fine surface details and the underlying spatial distribution of the points. Many learning-based methods adopt a two-stage pipeline that first extracts point-wise features from the noisy input and subsequently predicts point-wise displacements via simple regressions. However, these approaches often lack robust feature guidance and distribution-aware geometric refinement, causing them to fail at maintaining optimal point uniformity and geometric quality. To address these limitations, we propose PointNDF, a denoising framework that combines patch-level geometric context with local displacement prediction. Specifically, a Point Transformer V3 (PTv3) encoder aggregates multiscale geometric information into a global patch descriptor. Rather than directly regressing an unconstrained displacement vector, a Neural Displacement Field (NDF) fuses this global patch descriptor with per-point neighborhood features. It then predicts each displacement through bounded-vector and scale factor branches, thereby achieving a superior, more uniform point distribution. Three cascaded NDF stages propagate local features and progressively refine the point positions. Experiments on PU-Net and ModelNet10 demonstrate competitive denoising accuracy and favorable inference efficiency. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/peridous/PointNDF.

</details>


### [69] [Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation for Low-textured Scientific Sketch Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05408)
*Zhiqiang Wang,Sheri L. Barton,Jennifer H. Johnson,Yi He,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ART（Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation），将图像增强选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，自适应构建视图库以微调预训练模型，提升低纹理素描图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低纹理素描图像（如野生动物保护、考古绘图、刑侦画像）缺乏现代深度学习所依赖的细节纹理与视觉变化，学习与检索困难；且素描对旋转、翻转、缩放、拉伸等变换高度敏感，随机增强可能引入扭曲、损害表征学习。

Method: 将增强选择形式化为多臂老虎机（MAB）问题，各类变换视为“臂”，通过奖励持续评估其有效性，在自监督微调前自适应构建奖励引导的视图库；后续表征损失不反馈给ART模块。

Result: 在Mote Marine Laboratory提供的佛罗里达海牛素描上，变换疤痕检索Top-1平均准确率71.40%（随机增强选择为68.83%）；位置感知素描检索55.84%（冻结CLIP为18.18%）。

Conclusion: 奖励引导的增强选择能有效适配低纹理素描的检索需求，显著优于随机增强与冻结预训练模型基线。

Abstract: Low-textured sketch images are widely used in scientific applications, including wildlife conservation, archaeological illustration, and forensic composites. Learning from and retrieving such images is challenging because they lack detailed surface textures and visual variations commonly exploited by modern deep learning models. This paper proposes Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation (ART) to fine-tune pretrained models to learn effective representations for accurate sketch image retrieval. Our key observation is that sketches are particularly sensitive to image transformations, such as rotation, flipping, zooming, and stretching. While some transformations preserve their structural integrity, others may introduce distortions that hinder representation learning. Instead of randomly choosing transformations to construct fine-tuning views, ART formulates augmentation selection as a multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem, where transformations are treated as arms and their effectiveness is continuously evaluated through rewards. This enables ART to adaptively construct reward-guided view banks before self-supervised fine-tuning; the subsequent representation loss does not feed back to ART. On Florida manatee sketches provided by Mote Marine Laboratory, ART achieves 71.40% average Top-1 accuracy for transformed-scar retrieval, compared with 68.83% for random augmentation selection, and 55.84% for location-aware sketch retrieval, compared with 18.18% for frozen CLIP.

</details>


### [70] [VAR-Q: Tuning-free KV Cache Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05380)
*Boxun Xu,Jiaji Lu,Yuxuan Yin,Zihu Wang,Ziyue Liu,Yu Wang,Zirui Liu,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉自回归生成（下一尺度/下一帧预测）中KV缓存远超权重的问题，作者分析发现KV缓存存在特征-块二维的巨值离群通道结构，并提出免调优量化框架VAR-Q沿两轴分组量化，最高降低78.5% KV内存、支持6倍大batch且质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归生成（图像/视频的next-scale预测与视频next-frame预测）采用块级而非token级解码，产生大量视觉token，导致KV缓存规模超越模型权重，成为大规模部署的瓶颈；而现有语言模型的KV量化方法并不适配这种KV统计特性。

Method: 先对视觉自回归KV缓存做实证分析，得到三点发现：(1)少数巨值离群通道承载主要功能，剔除后生成崩溃，而剔除同等规模的内点通道无影响；(2)离群值沿特征与块两个维度组织，每个单元内幅值稳定、跨单元漂移；(3)该结构源自投影偏置与RoPE引起的散射两种独立机制。基于此提出VAR-Q：沿相同两轴进行分组量化的免调优框架，直接利用生成循环天然提供的块边界，无需离群检测或校准，并配合位打包kernel实现运行时内存压缩。

Result: 在覆盖两大范式的九个模型上实现低比特量化，KV内存最高减少78.5%，质量退化可忽略，并可支持6倍更大的推理batch。

Conclusion: 视觉自回归KV缓存具有可解释的特征-块二维离群结构，据此设计的免调优分组量化VAR-Q能显著压缩KV缓存并提升推理吞吐，为大规模视觉自回归生成部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive generation spans two dominant paradigms: next-scale prediction for images and video, and next-frame prediction for video. Both decode block-by-block rather than token-by-token, yielding KV caches whose statistics diverge from those of language models. At scale, KV memory exceeds the weights and limits large-scale deployment caused by a large amount of visual tokens. Our analysis of these caches reveals three findings. First, a small set of massive-value channels carries the functional load: ablating these outlier channels collapses generation, whereas ablating a matched set of inlier channels leaves quality intact. Second, the outliers organize along two axes, feature and block, with magnitudes persistent within each cell and shifting across cells. Third, this structure arises from two independent mechanisms: projection bias and RoPE-induced scattering. Guided by these findings, we propose VAR-Q, a tuning-free KV-cache quantization framework that groups along the same two axes. The generation loop provides block boundaries for free, removing the need for outlier detection or calibration. Paired with bit-packing kernels for runtime memory reduction, VAR-Q delivers low-bit quantization across nine models spanning both paradigms, cutting KV memory by up to 78.5% with negligible quality degradation and enabling 6x larger inference batches.

</details>


### [71] [DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05825)
*Lifan Jiang,Yibo Zhao,Senlin Cheng,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualCast：面向数字人电商视频生成的"角色分工"双图条件框架，把商品图与人物图置于DiT序列前后边界实现条件分离注入，并配合区域加权损失与条件dropout，缓解单参考偏置导致的产品失真与身份漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字人电商视频需同时保持产品外观、人物身份与整体风格。现有支持多参考图的模型采用通用条件方案，未显式区分"展示什么"与"谁来展示"的不对称角色，导致生成偏向单张参考，出现产品形变、身份漂移或单参考坍缩。

Method: 提出DualCast框架：1) 双时空槽位注入（dual-spatiotemporal slot injection），分别VAE编码商品图与人物图，将其潜特征置于DiT序列的前、后边界，实现序列级条件分离与定向注入；2) 掩码引导的区域加权损失，加强对商品区域、人脸区域及两者重叠区域的监督；3) 图像条件dropout，使单一模型同时支持双图、仅商品、仅人物三种生成模式；并构建1万条电商视频精选数据集用于训练。

Result: 实验结果表明DualCast性能强劲（摘要仅给出定性结论，未报告具体定量指标）。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模商品与人物的不同角色、在序列层面分离条件并加权关键区域监督，可有效抑制参考间特征竞争，提升数字人电商视频生成中产品与身份的保真度，并支持灵活的单/双图条件生成。

Abstract: Digital human e-commerce video generation requires preserving product appearance, human identity, and overall video style during dynamic demonstrations. Although recent models support multiple reference images, their general conditioning schemes do not explicitly distinguish the asymmetric roles of "what is presented" and "who presents it." Consequently, generated videos may favor a single reference, resulting in product distortion, identity drift, or single-reference collapse. To address this issue, we propose DualCast, a role-addressed dual-image conditioning framework for video generation. By explicitly modeling the distinct roles of product and human conditions, DualCast reduces role ambiguity and feature competition between references. First, a dual-spatiotemporal slot injection mechanism VAE-encodes the product and human images separately and places their latent features at the front and rear boundaries of the DiT sequence, enabling sequence-level condition separation and directed injection. Second, a mask-guided region-weighted loss strengthens supervision over product, face, and product-face overlap regions, improving generation fidelity in critical areas. Third, image condition dropout enables a single model to support dual-image, product-only, and human-only generation. In addition, we construct a curated dataset of 10K e-commerce videos for model training. Experimental results demonstrate the strong performance of DualCast.

</details>


### [72] [C-WFDM: A ControlNet-Guided Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model for Multimodal Brain MRI Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05737)
*Muhammad Nabi Yasinzai,Remika Mito,Mangor Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出C-WFDM——WFDM的ControlNet扩展，用于按受试者缺失模态补全多模态脑MRI，在BrainScape实验中T2w与FLAIR合成效果最优（SSIM 0.9232/0.8904）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共与汇集神经影像数据集中各MRI模态可获得性不均（T1w充足、T2w/FLAIR次之、T1Gd稀缺），参与者的模态组合多样；现有缺失模态基准多从完整四模态病例中人为剔除模态，不能反映真实可获得性模式。

Method: 在既有BrainScape快照（157个公开MRI数据集、45,880例预处理扫描）上，将预训练Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model（WFDM）与目标模态专用ControlNet适配器结合：主干保留目标模态与元数据条件，适配器以同一受试者的其他MRI对比度作为条件引导合成；T2w由T1w生成，FLAIR由T1w+T2w生成，T1Gd由T1w+T2w+FLAIR生成；优先使用已采集的条件对比度，仅在必要时使用生成的中间对比度。

Result: 在BrainScape补全实验中，与ALDM、cWDM、Pix2Pix比较，采用配对SSIM、PSNR、MAE评估；C-WFDM在T2w与FLAIR上取得最优结果，SSIM分别为0.9232与0.8904。

Conclusion: C-WFDM可针对真实存在的模态可获得性模式进行受试者特异性缺失模态补全，多模态条件与ControlNet适配有效提升合成保真度。

Abstract: Multimodal brain MRI provides complementary anatomical, tissue, and pathological information to support downstream artificial intelligence (AI) applications. However, modality availability is uneven across public and pooled neuroimaging datasets: T1-weighted (T1w) MRI is widely represented, T2-weighted (T2w) and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) MRI are less consistently available, and gadolinium-enhanced T1-weighted (T1Gd) MRI is relatively scarce. Pooled datasets therefore contain participants with different combinations of acquired MRI contrasts, reflecting variation in the study designs, acquisition protocols, and clinical or research objectives of the contributing studies. Many missing-modality benchmarks simulate incomplete MRI by withholding contrasts from complete four-modality cases, which may not represent the observed availability patterns in pooled datasets. To address this gap, we introduce C-WFDM, a ControlNet extension of our Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model (WFDM), for subject-specific missing-modality completion. C-WFDM is built on an earlier BrainScape snapshot comprising 157 public MRI datasets and 45,880 preprocessed scans. The pretrained WFDM backbone retains target-modality and metadata conditioning, while target-specific ControlNet adapters use MRI contrasts from the same participant to guide synthesis. C-WFDM synthesizes T2w from T1w, FLAIR from T1w and T2w, and T1Gd from T1w, T2w, and FLAIR. Acquired conditioning contrasts are used when available, and generated intermediate contrasts are used only when required. We compare C-WFDM with ALDM, cWDM, and Pix2Pix using paired structural similarity index (SSIM), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), and mean absolute error (MAE). In the BrainScape completion experiment, C-WFDM achieves the best T2w and FLAIR results among the evaluated models, with SSIM values of 0.9232 and 0.8904, respectively.

</details>


### [73] [Trainspotting: Learning Object-Centric 3D Reconstruction from Found Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06849)
*Hanyu Chen,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trainspotting 数据集（12,068 个物体、311,886 张来自 Wikimedia Commons 的照片），用于研究野外、物体为中心的多视图重建，并揭示现有前馈重建模型在跨环境、跨物理个体场景下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型主要在单次采集数据上训练，图像仅视角变化而物体与环境基本固定；而公共照片档案中的多视图数据形态不同——同一物体或设计由不同人在多年、不同环境、不同相机甚至不同物理个体下独立拍摄。现有模型在这种 in-the-wild 物体中心照片集合上表现很差，亟需相应数据与评测。

Method: 构建 Trainspotting 数据集：从 Wikimedia Commons 收集 12,068 个物体、311,886 张照片，对每个物体重建以获取相机位姿、多视图立体深度和物体掩码；将物体划分为三种情形（固定于单一地点、在多地之间移动、由多个物理个体共享的同一设计）。在此基础上评测近期前馈重建模型，并在 Trainspotting 上微调 VGGT-Ω。

Result: 前馈重建模型在 Trainspotting 上整体失败，其中"移动物体"与"多物理个体共享设计"两种情形失败最为严重；在 Trainspotting 上微调 VGGT-Ω 后，其在留出物体上的相机位姿、深度和三维几何均有显著提升；在独立的 NAVI in-the-wild 基准（同一物体处于不同环境）上也有类似改进，而在传统基准上的性能基本不变。

Conclusion: 以"找到的照片"形式存在的跨环境、跨个体多视图数据是现有前馈重建模型的薄弱环节，且该薄弱点可通过在 Trainspotting 上微调有效缓解，同时不损害传统基准表现，说明此类数据具有独立且互补的价值。

Abstract: State-of-the-art feedforward reconstruction models perform poorly on in-the-wild object-centric photo collections. They are trained largely on single-session object captures whose images vary in viewpoint while the object and its environment remain nearly fixed. Public photo archives contain a different form of multi-view data. They hold found photos of the same object or design: photos taken independently by many people across years, environments, cameras, and sometimes physical units. In this work, we introduce Trainspotting, a dataset of 12,068 objects and 311,886 photos from Wikimedia Commons. We reconstruct each object to recover camera poses, multi-view stereo depth, and object masks. We categorize the objects into three regimes: objects fixed at one site, objects that move between sites, and designs shared by many physical units. Evaluating recent feedforward reconstruction models on Trainspotting shows that they fail most severely in the last two regimes. Fine-tuning VGGT-$Ω$ on Trainspotting significantly improves camera pose, depth, and 3D geometry on held-out objects. We observe similar improvements on the independent NAVI in-the-wild benchmark, which contains photos of each object in different environments. Performance on conventional benchmarks remains largely unchanged.

</details>


### [74] [Context-Aware Autoregressive Image Generation for Emerging Reasoning Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06838)
*Jixuan Ying,Haoyu Liu,Timing Yang,Tingyu Zhu,Predrag Neskovic,Anand Bhattad,Cihang Xie,Zeyu Zheng,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知自回归图像生成框架，让模型基于全局上下文与当前局部信息动态决定下一个token的位置，而非固定或随机顺序，从而提升结构鲁棒性与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归图像生成的生成顺序多为预定义或启发式（如next-patch、随机顺序、特定尺度的一步生成），导致模型忽视图像中的重要结构依赖，限制结构鲁棒性与推理能力。

Method: 提出上下文感知自回归生成框架：模型根据全局上下文与当前局部信息动态确定下一个token的生成位置，使生成过程自适应图像结构与语义依赖，而非遵循固定或随机顺序；该方法可适配多种图像AR范式。

Result: 在多种图像AR范式（掩码自回归、尺度自回归）上显著提升结构鲁棒性；并观察到涌现的推理特性，模型能更好理解复杂语义指令，生成更忠实于给定引导的图像。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是图像自回归建模中关键但被低估的因素，上下文感知解码可释放更强的推理感知视觉生成能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive image generation has recently emerged as a competitive visual generation paradigm, yet its generation order is often predefined or heuristic, such as next-patch prediction, random-order prediction, or one-step generation for specific scales. We observe that these strategies can cause models to overlook important structural dependencies in images, thereby limiting both structural robustness and reasoning capability. In this work, we propose a context-aware autoregressive generation framework, where the model dynamically determines the next token location based on global context and current local information. Rather than following a fixed or random order, our method allows the generation process to adapt to image structure and semantic dependencies. Extensive experiments show that context-aware generation significantly improves structural robustness across different image AR paradigms, including masked autoregressive and scale-wise autoregressive models. Beyond quantitative improvements, we further observe emerging reasoning properties: the model can better understand complex semantic instructions and generate images that more faithfully follow the given guidance. These findings suggest that generation order is a critical yet underexplored factor in image autoregressive modeling, and that context-aware decoding can unlock stronger reasoning-aware visual generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/Hpesojyjx/Saccades-AR.

</details>


### [75] [Dynamical State-Space Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06652)
*Woojun Lee,Hankyeol Kim,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSSD让扩散模型的加噪过程本身具有时间结构：用稳定对角状态空间模型的阻尼振荡模态构造时间核，并以该模型平稳协方差的Cholesky因子作为时间算子，保证因果、可逆、逐帧噪声方差不变、干净端退化为恒等，且等价于Kalman新息形式；去噪器架构不变，仅改为预测时间相关噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间预测扩散模型对每个未来帧施加独立噪声，时间依赖性完全交给去噪器学习。作者希望让"腐蚀过程本身"携带时间结构，从而把多时间尺度与振荡相关性直接编码进前向过程。

Method: 1) 由稳定对角状态空间模型的阻尼振荡模态构建时间核，其学习到的衰减率与频率刻画多时间尺度与振荡相关性；2) 直接使用该模型在有限片段上的问题在于首帧前的初始状态不可观测，零初始状态会使早期帧噪声低于调度规定；3) 因此改用模型平稳协方差的Cholesky因子作为时间算子；4) 理论上证明该算子因果、精确可逆、保持每帧噪声方差与标准扩散一致、在干净端退化为恒等，且正是状态空间模型的Kalman新息形式；5) 去噪器训练目标改为预测时间相关噪声，采样时每步先移除再重新施加时间混合。

Result: 在科学领域数据集与机器人视频数据集上，DSSD优于共享同一去噪器的扩散基线；训练过程中学习到的模态演化表明时间算子能针对各数据集自适应。

Conclusion: 将时间结构注入腐蚀过程本身是一种有效且即插即用的改进：在保持去噪器架构与逐帧噪声方差不变的前提下提升时间预测性能，并具备状态空间/Kalman滤波的理论支撑。

Abstract: Diffusion models for temporal prediction typically corrupt each future frame with independent noise, leaving temporal dependence entirely to the denoiser. We propose Dynamical State-Space Diffusion (DSSD), which learns the temporal structure of the corruption process itself. DSSD builds a temporal kernel from the damped oscillatory modes of a stable diagonal state-space model, whose learned decay rates and frequencies let the corruption capture multiple timescales and oscillatory correlations, while the denoiser architecture remains unchanged. Running this model on a finite clip, however, requires the state before the first frame, which is unobserved; starting from a zero state leaves the early frames with less noise than the diffusion schedule prescribes. We avoid this by using the Cholesky factor of the model's stationary covariance as the temporal operator. We prove that this operator is causal, exactly invertible, keeps each frame's noise variance equal to that of standard diffusion, and reduces to the identity at the clean end of the process, and that it is exactly the Kalman innovations form of the state-space model. The denoiser is trained to predict the temporally correlated noise, and sampling removes and reapplies the temporal mixing at each step. On scientific-field and robot-video datasets, DSSD outperforms diffusion baselines that share its denoiser, and the evolution of its learned modes during training shows that the temporal operator adapts to each dataset.

</details>


### [76] [WayPOP: A Panoramic Open-Set Panoptic Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05801)
*Rohit Mohan,Maximilian Luz,Swarnava Chowdhury,Yao Lu,Florian Drews,Thomas Nürnberg,Yakov Miron,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WayPOP——基于真实Waymo PVPS序列并注入未知类3D资产的环视开集全景跟踪基准，含980条未知目标轨迹、5.3万余掩码标注与21,300张图像，并设计从单视图开集分割到全环视开集跟踪的任务层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开集全景基准仅针对单图或单视角，而全景跟踪基准又限定闭集类别体系，因此缺乏能够评估未知目标在同步多相机序列中一致分割与跨时间、跨视角关联的基准。

Method: 在真实Waymo PVPS序列中，将来自Objaverse的多种类别外3D资产固定放置于3D场景内的确定位置，由标定好的车载相机随自车与周围交通运动进行渲染，从而获得时间与跨视角一致的全景标注及辅助LiDAR测距观测；构建约670个资产实例化为980条未知目标轨迹；设计从视图无关开集全景分割到完整环视开集全景跟踪的任务层级协议，采用已知/未知PQ与(w)STQ指标，并对代表性开集与开放词表分割方法叠加时序与跨相机关联进行评测。

Result: 基准包含21,300张相机图像与53,452条未知目标掩码标注；评测显示现有代表性开集与开放词表方法在真实开集场景下存在显著局限。

Conclusion: 现有方法在未知目标的鲁棒定位以及跨时间、跨视角的身份关联方面能力不足，这是环视开集全景跟踪的核心开放挑战；WayPOP为相关研究提供了标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Holistic perception for autonomous agents requires reliably segmenting and associating scene elements across time and camera views, including objects outside a fixed semantic taxonomy. Existing open-set panoptic benchmarks focus on individual images or views, while panoramic tracking benchmarks assume closed-set taxonomies. Consequently, these benchmarks do not evaluate whether unknown objects can be consistently segmented and tracked across synchronized multi-camera sequences. We introduce WayPOP, a panoramic open-set panoptic tracking benchmark built on real Waymo PVPS sequences augmented with diverse out-of-taxonomy 3D assets from Objaverse. Inserted objects are placed at fixed locations in the 3D scene and rendered using calibrated vehicle-mounted cameras as the ego vehicle and surrounding traffic move, providing temporally and cross-view consistent panoptic annotations and auxiliary LiDAR range observations. WayPOP contains approximately 670 curated assets instantiated as 980 unique unknown-object tracks, yielding 53,452 unknown-object mask annotations across 21,300 camera images. Our evaluation protocol defines a hierarchy of tasks from view-independent open-set panoptic segmentation to full panoramic open-set panoptic tracking, using known/unknown PQ and (w)STQ metrics. We benchmark representative open-set and open-vocabulary segmentation methods with temporal and cross-camera association. Our results reveal substantial limitations of current methods in truly open-set scenarios, highlighting robust unknown-object localization and identity association across time and views as major open challenges.

</details>


### [77] [mmHand: Toward Pixel-Level-Accuracy Hand Localization Using a Single Commodity mmWave Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05404)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Zhengxiong Li,Chenhan Xu,Luchuan Song,Huining Li,Hongfei Xue,Yingxiao Wu,Wenyao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmHand：基于单台商用毫米波设备的无设备、隐私保护的动态手部定位系统，将毫米波数据转为2D图像，实现像素级精度的手部定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部定位应用广泛，但现有技术受数据模态限制：视觉方法有隐私问题，可穿戴方法影响舒适性，亟需无设备且保护隐私的方案。

Method: 提出mmImage生成工具，从原始毫米波数据充分提取空间信息并构造新型2D图像表示；设计含质量评估方法与像素空间标注的框架；提出跨模态空间特征增强模型，在U-V像素坐标系中实现像素级手部定位。

Result: 在三种场景、12名受试者上实验，四项指标结果验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 单台商用毫米波设备可实现无设备、隐私保护的像素级动态手部定位，验证了该方案的可行性。

Abstract: The hand localization problem has been a longstanding focus due to its many applications. The task involves modeling the hand as a singular point and determining its position within a defined coordinate system. However, due to data modality limitations, existing hand localization technologies face several challenges. For example, vision-based localization raises privacy concerns, while wearable-based methods compromise user comfort. In this paper, we introduce mmHand, a new device-free, privacy-preserving dynamic hand localization system with pixel-level accuracy, using a single commodity mmWave device. We first propose a mmImage generation tool to fully extract spatial information from raw mmWave data and introduce a novel 2D image-format representation of mmWave data. Next, we design a framework that provides a new quality evaluation method and pixel space labeling for the mmWave data. Finally, we present a cross-modality spatial feature-enhanced model with high spatial feature extraction capabilities, which can accurately localize hand positions at the pixel level in the mmWave radar U-V pixel coordinate system. We evaluate the system with experiments on 12 subjects in three scenarios, and the results across four metrics demonstrate the effectiveness of our hand localization system.

</details>


### [78] [TRUST-Stereo: Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05689)
*Pouya Sohrabipour,Yihong Feng,Chaitanya Kumar Reddy Pallerla,Tuan Dang,Ngan Le,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRUST-Stereo通过不确定性引导的残差搜索模块修正粗到细立体匹配中传播视差的误差，并结合两阶段师生知识蒸馏将大型基础模型的单目表征迁移到轻量骨干，仅用1.4M合成数据即在多个基准上取得SOTA且推理约50 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到细（coarse-to-fine）立体匹配虽高效，但对传播视差（propagated disparity）的误差高度敏感：一旦传播值偏离真值，真实对应点可能落在后续局部搜索范围之外，导致不可恢复的匹配失败。同时，高效方法通常依赖大规模数据（含真实伪标签）训练，亟需在有限数据下保持泛化与速度。

Method: 1) 提出不确定性引导残差搜索模块（UGRSM）：估计传播视差的可靠性（不确定性），并预测残差偏移量，自适应地重新定位局部代价体（cost-volume）中心，使搜索窗口覆盖真实对应。2) 设计Senior-Junior教师-助教两阶段知识蒸馏，将大型基础模型（foundation model）的丰富单目表征迁移至轻量骨干网络，实现快速立体匹配。

Result: 在KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015、ETH3D和Middlebury上展现实强零样本泛化能力；仅用1.4M合成立体图像对训练，即在多项基准指标上取得SOTA，优于使用更大规模数据（含真实伪标签样本）训练的同类高效方法。具体：KITTI 2015 EPE 0.93，ETH3D Bad 1.0 为2.13%，Middlebury Bad 2.0 为6.79%，推理约50 ms/对。

Conclusion: 以不确定性引导的残差搜索缓解粗到细匹配的误差传播脆弱性，配合两阶段蒸馏引入单目先验，可在小规模合成数据与轻量骨干下同时获得高精度、强零样本泛化与实时级速度，为高效立体匹配提供了精度—效率—数据成本兼顾的方案。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine stereo matching enables efficient disparity estimation but remains sensitive to errors in propagated disparity, which may place ground-truth correspondences outside subsequent local search ranges. We propose TRUST-Stereo (Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching), which addresses this limitation through an Uncertainty-Guided Residual Search Module (UGRSM). UGRSM estimates the reliability of propagated disparity and predicts residual offsets to adaptively relocate local cost-volume centers. Additionally, we introduce a two-stage Senior-Junior Teacher Assistant knowledge distillation strategy to transfer rich monocular representations from a large foundation model to a lightweight backbone to achieve fast stereo matching performance. Experiments on KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, ETH3D, and Middlebury demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization. Using only 1.4M synthetic stereo pairs, TRUST-Stereo achieves state-of-the-art results on several benchmark metrics, outperforming competing efficient methods trained with substantially larger datasets, including real-world pseudo-labeled samples. Specifically, it achieves a KITTI 2015 EPE of 0.93, ETH3D Bad 1.0 of 2.13% and Middlebury Bad 2.0 of 6.79% while maintaining inference at approximately 50 ms per stereo pair.

</details>


### [79] [NitroFusion2: Bringing Adversarial Loss to the Text Stream of Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05648)
*Dar-Yen Chen,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本流对抗蒸馏(TSAD)与方面线索判别(ACD)，并在NitroFusion2中集成，将多模态扩散Transformer的少步蒸馏从4-8步降至1-2步且质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗扩散蒸馏依赖图像特征进行判别，忽略了MMDiT中文本流经联合注意力已读取图像、且与文本条件绑定这一特性，未充分利用该表示潜力。

Method: 以MMDiT中间文本token状态作为新的对抗表示（TSAD）；因文本流受条件驱动而改变从图像中提取的信息，进一步构造Aspect-Cued Discrimination，按排版、物体、风格等不同方面对判别器施加条件以引导强调的属性；在NitroFusion的专家判别器设计基础上集成二者形成NitroFusion2。

Result: 少步蒸馏从4-8步降至1-2步且质量可比；单步可生成清晰渲染文本与丰富细粒度细节，使一步生成具备实用性；两步在评测基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 确立了MMDiT文本流作为对抗扩散蒸馏中强大且可控表示的可行性。

Abstract: Adversarial diffusion distillation has conventionally relied on image features for discrimination. We revisit this choice for multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs), whose text stream becomes image-dependent through joint attention. We introduce Text-Stream Adversarial Distillation (TSAD), which exploits intermediate text-token states as a new adversarial representation. Because the text stream has already read the image while remaining tied to the text condition, changing that condition changes what information it extracts from the image. Building on this property, we introduce Aspect-Cued Discrimination (ACD), which conditions the discriminator on different aspects of the sample, such as typography, objects, or style, to steer which properties are emphasized during adversarial training. Building on the expert-discriminator design of NitroFusion, we integrate TSAD and ACD in NitroFusion2. NitroFusion2 brings few-step distillation from the conventional 4-8-step regime down to just 1-2 steps at comparable quality. At one step, it produces sharp rendered text and rich fine-grained details, making one-step generation practical rather than merely a stress test for distillation. At two steps, it remains competitive across the evaluated benchmarks. These results establish the MMDiT text stream as a powerful and steerable representation for adversarial diffusion distillation.

</details>


### [80] [CAPTOR: text-encoder-free grounding by counting caption co-occurrence over the visual words of a frozen encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05599)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAPTOR 通过视觉词聚类与标题锚定的词-视觉词共现统计，在缺少文本编码器的情况下估计文本词-图像块打分矩阵，实现文本驱动的图像定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本查询定位图像区域需要"词×图像块"打分矩阵，主流视觉-语言模型依赖对齐的文本编码器；但许多图像编码器（如仅用切片训练的病理模型）没有配套文本编码器，无法直接获得该矩阵。

Method: 冻结图像编码器的块级 token 并聚类成"视觉词"；对每张有标题的图像统计各视觉词的块占比；某词的签名定义为"标题提及该词的图像"与"其余图像"在该占比上的标准化差值。打分矩阵可分解为"词×视觉词"表与一个与查询无关的图像因子，因此图像只需编码一次，新增查询词仅需增加一行表项。全程无需梯度、无需文本编码器。

Result: 在 COCO、Pascal VOC、Cityscapes 上优于所有免训练基线；COCO 单物体独占单元格上 mIoU 0.565 vs 0.502；三者上均与同骨干训练出的文本对齐方法持平，而后者需要梯度、文本编码器及数倍标题。留出乳腺癌切片上，较最强病理文本编码器基线提升 0.160 mIoU；以 CAPTOR 评估，仅用切片训练的 UNI 在统一网格上超出同类基线 0.132。

Conclusion: 基于共现计数的统计式接口可为任意缺乏语言训练的编码器赋予文本界面，前提是存在带标题的图像数据；该方法免训练、可复用、可增量扩展。

Abstract: Grounding a text query in an image needs a matrix scoring every term against every patch. Vision-language models compute it with an aligned text encoder, which many image encoders, such as histopathology-related models trained on slides alone, lack. The Caption-Anchored Patch-Term Occurrence Representation (CAPTOR) estimates the matrix without a text encoder. It clusters a frozen image encoder's patch tokens into visual words and describes each captioned image by its share of patches in each word. A term signature is the standardized difference of those shares between images whose captions mention the term and the rest. The matrix factorizes into a term-by-word table and a query-independent image factor, so an image is encoded once and a new term adds one table row. Against every training-free baseline, CAPTOR leads on Common Objects in Context (COCO), Pascal Visual Object Classes (VOC) and Cityscapes. On COCO, scored on the cells one object owns, it reaches 0.565 mean intersection over union (mIoU) against 0.502. On all three, it is also level with a text alignment trained on the same backbone, which needs gradients, a text encoder and several times the captions. On held-out breast-cancer slides, it exceeds the strongest baseline scoring patches with a pathology text encoder by 0.160 mIoU. Read through CAPTOR, UNI, an encoder trained on slides alone, exceeds such baselines by 0.132 on a common grid. Counting can therefore give a text interface to any image encoder trained without language, wherever captioned images exist.

</details>


### [81] [Unaligned Image Guided Denoising via Cross-modal Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06422)
*Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Si-Yuan Cao,Zhu Yu,Lingyu Zhu,Huaqi Zhang,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UGD-FM，基于跨模态条件流匹配的非对齐图像引导去噪框架，处理强噪声与大幅二维跨模态错位并存的难题，仅需1–2步推理即达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导去噪方法多假设输入空间对齐，或仅处理小基线校正配对，无法应对目标图像严重噪声与大幅二维跨模态错位同时存在的场景。

Method: 将引导去噪建模为渐进式流匹配过程，使图像恢复与对齐引导聚合相互促进；引入阶段聚焦且推理一致的训练策略，聚焦任务关键去噪阶段并缓解训练-推理失配；提出自适应稀疏引导传播实现高效可靠的大范围匹配，并以感知与几何辅助损失正则化。

Result: 仅用1–2步推理即可取得SOTA去噪性能，且与现代单图复原骨干网络兼容。

Conclusion: UGD-FM为强噪声、大范围非对齐条件下的跨模态引导去噪提供了统一且高效的少步推理解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents UGD-FM, a novel unaligned image guided denoising framework based on cross-modal conditional flow matching. Unlike previous approaches that assume spatially aligned inputs or only handle small-baseline rectified pairs, we address a more challenging setting where severe target noise and large-range two-dimensional cross-modal misalignment coexist. Specifically, we formulate guided denoising as a progressive flow matching process, allowing image restoration and aligned guidance aggregation to mutually reinforce each other. To support efficient few-step inference, we introduce a stage-focused and inference-consistent training strategy that focuses on task-critical denoising stages, and mitigates the training-inference mismatch. We further propose adaptive sparse guidance propagation for efficient and reliable large-range matching, regularized by perceptual and geometric auxiliary losses. Experiments demonstrate that UGD-FM achieves state-of-the-art denoising performance with only one or two inference steps, and is compatible with modern single-image restoration backbones.

</details>


### [82] [NanoKV: High-Ratio KV Cache Compression for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06377)
*Jiacheng Lu,Haoyi Zhu,Sipei Yi,Enze Xie,Cheng Zhuo,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NanoKV 是一个冻结骨干的、面向生成任务的高倍率 KV 压缩框架，通过跨 token 与注意力头的冗余利用（聚类中心存共享内容、联合头-通道残差编码为低位系数）实现高效压缩，并针对 Key/Value 在注意力中的不同角色采用差异化拟合目标；其 NanoKV-Fast 变体复用压缩系数实现混合维度注意力以降低计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频模型依赖历史 KV 缓存维持长时 rollout 的场景一致性，导致 KV 存储与重复历史注意力成为内存和计算瓶颈。激进压缩会损害生成质量，而重构全维 KV 又几乎不减少注意力计算量，故需一种既高倍率压缩又不明显降低生成质量、且能降低计算的方案。

Method: 冻结骨干，仅在压缩表示层做适配。跨 token 与注意力头挖掘冗余：共享内容存于聚类中心，联合的头-通道残差编码为低位系数。按 Key 与 Value 的不同作用设置差异化拟合目标——Key 解码器以保留下游块输出为目标优化，Value 编解码器通过实际部署的量化路径拟合以实现忠实重建。可选 NanoKV-Fast 变体复用已存系数进行混合维度注意力，在不修改视频骨干的前提下减少计算。

Result: 在三个自回归视频骨干上均一致改善质量-存储权衡。在 HY-WorldPlay 上实现 26.09× 有效 KV 压缩，约为 KVTC 的两倍，同时 PSNR 提升 1.0 dB；NanoKV-Fast 保持 20.53× 有效压缩，并相对 KVTC 获得约 1.15× 端到端加速。

Conclusion: 生成感知的非对称 KV 压缩（Key 面向下游输出、Value 面向忠实重建）结合跨 token/头的联合低比特残差编码，可在冻结骨干条件下显著提升压缩倍率与生成质量；复用压缩表示做混合维度注意力还能进一步带来实际加速，具备良好通用性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video models rely on historical key-value (KV) caches to preserve scene consistency over extended rollouts, making KV storage and repeated attention over history a growing memory and computational bottleneck. Aggressive compression can degrade generation quality, while reconstructing full-dimensional KV leaves attention computation largely unchanged. We introduce NanoKV, a frozen-backbone framework for generation-aware high-ratio KV compression. NanoKV exploits redundancy across tokens and attention heads by storing shared content in cluster centers and encoding joint head-channel residuals into low-bit coefficients. Crucially, NanoKV uses different fitting objectives for Keys and Values according to their distinct roles in attention: Key decoders are optimized to preserve downstream block outputs, whereas Value encoders and decoders are fitted for faithful reconstruction through the deployed quantization path. Building on the same compressed representation, the optional NanoKV-Fast variant reuses the stored coefficients for mixed-dimensional attention, reducing computation without modifying the video backbone. Across three autoregressive video backbones, NanoKV consistently improves the quality-storage trade-off. On HY-WorldPlay, NanoKV achieves $26.09\times$ effective KV compression, nearly twice that of KVTC, while improving PSNR by 1.0 dB. NanoKV-Fast retains $20.53\times$ effective compression and achieves an approximately $1.15\times$ end-to-end speedup over KVTC.

</details>


### [83] [FLINT: Coupling Proximal Initialization and Bounded Stochasticity for Flow-Matching Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05945)
*Junseo Bang,Daewon Choi,Dong Ju Mun,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示流匹配逆问题求解中"轨迹空间方法"与"源空间方法"的本质耦合关系，提出乘性稳定性界，并据此设计两阶段框架FLINT：少量迭代的流形约束近端源初始化 + 有界随机性调度引导采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配后验采样求解器分为轨迹空间（优化采样动力学）与源空间（优化初始噪声）两条独立路线，先前工作多将其视为可独立设计的选择，缺乏对二者相互作用的理论刻画，导致源优化可能冗余、且其收益在采样中被随机性侵蚀。

Method: 1) 推导统一稳定性界，通过乘性包络将"初始化接近度"与"累积采样随机性"联系起来；2) 给出两点经验观察：存在"充分性区间"，适度优化的近端源初始化即可达到性能平台；该优势脆弱，会被引导采样中过量随机性累积抹除；3) 据此提出FLINT两阶段框架：第一阶段用流形约束的近端目标在少数迭代内建立高保真起点，第二阶段在有界随机性调度下进行引导采样以保护初始化优势。

Result: 在多种逆问题上，FLINT取得state-of-the-art感知质量与具竞争力的失真指标，同时保持更优的计算效率。

Conclusion: 轨迹空间与源空间设计并非独立而是内在耦合；通过显式联合控制初始化质量与累积随机性，可在少量源优化迭代下高效实现高质量后验采样。

Abstract: Flow matching models have emerged as a dominant paradigm for generative posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing solvers follow two distinct lines of work: trajectory-space methods, which refine sampling dynamics from random initial noise, and source-space methods, which optimize the initial noise itself. While most prior work treats these as independent design choices, we show that they are inherently coupled. We derive a unified stability bound that links initialization proximity and cumulative sampling stochasticity through a multiplicative envelope, complemented by two empirical observations. First, we identify a sufficiency regime in which a moderately optimized proximal source initialization is enough to attain the performance plateau, rendering exhaustive source-space optimization unnecessary. Second, this initial advantage is fragile: it is eroded by excessive stochasticity accumulation during guided sampling, necessitating a mechanism that bounds the cumulative noise injection. These findings motivate FLINT, a two-stage framework that operationalizes this coupling. FLINT establishes a high-fidelity starting point via a manifold-constrained proximal objective in only a few iterations, then performs guided sampling under a bounded stochasticity schedule that helps prevent erasure of the initialization advantage. Across diverse inverse problems, FLINT delivers state of the art perceptual quality and competitive distortion metrics while maintaining superior computational efficiency.

</details>


### [84] [Open-HumanScale: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06817)
*Juncheng Ma,Jiaxin Li,Jiankai Tu,Bo Liang,Guodong Lv,Yufan Deng,Junzhe Tang,Liangzhu Yang,Chen Bian,Zicong Cao,Baohao Zhu,Jianxin Bi,Tengfei Yan,Yinyuan Zhang,Kewei Zhang,Xinglong Chen,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Xiaotian Tang,Liang Tan,Li Li,Yuan Xu,Haosen Yang,Yuqi Wang,Jingfei Xia,Songyan Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个完全开放的第一人称人类视频预训练规模化方案 Open-HumanScale，构建 5 万小时带手部动作与自然语言标注的语料 Open-HumanScale-50K，发现预训练时长与下游机器人成功率之间的对数线性标度律，并据此训练 HumanWAM-Vanilla 与视觉目标条件化的 HumanWAM-Goal，取得仿真与真机上的领先性能及零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类视频是机器人操作先验的可扩展来源，但原始数据规模并不能自动转化为可泛化的机器人策略，缺乏严谨的数据筛选与训练配方，也缺乏对"纯人类数据预训练能带来多少收益"的定量刻画。

Method: 构建经筛选、密集标注手部运动与自然语言的第一人称视频语料（5 万小时）；通过闭环仿真评估系统研究纯人类数据预训练，拟合数据时长—下游成功率的对数线性标度律；据此训练仅用人类视频预训练的 world–action 模型 HumanWAM-Vanilla，并进一步提出视觉目标条件化的 HumanWAM-Goal 进行人—机混合数据联合预训练。

Result: HumanWAM-Vanilla 在 RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean 达 92.08%、Clean2Random 达 68.88%，RoboDojo 平均成功率 14.17%、平均进度分 19.00；HumanWAM-Goal 经混合数据缩放将 RoboTwin 2.0 提升至 92.70%（Clean2Clean）与 84.60%（Clean2Random），并在仿真与真实基准上实现广泛零样本泛化，真机上表现出无需参数更新的具身上下文学习能力。

Conclusion: 人类第一人称视频预训练可通过数据筛选与训练配方实现可预测的规模化收益；混合人—机数据缩放进一步拓展能力边界并带来涌现的具身上下文学习。全部语料、数据与预训练流水线、模型检查点、推理代码及仿真评测套件均已开源，支持可复现的规模化研究。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable source of manipulation priors for robot learning, yet translating raw data scale into generalizable robot policies demands rigorous data curation and training recipes. We introduce Open-HumanScale, the first fully open recipe for scaling egocentric pretraining to 50,000 hours, anchored by Open-HumanScale-50K---a curated egocentric video corpus densely annotated with hand motion and natural language. Leveraging this corpus, we systematically investigate pure human pretraining via closed-loop simulation evaluations and uncover an empirical log-linear scaling law linking pretraining data hours to downstream robot success rates. Grounded in these findings, we develop HumanWAM-Vanilla, a world--action model pretrained exclusively on 50k hours of egocentric human video. Upon robot post-training, HumanWAM-Vanilla reaches 92.08% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean and 68.88% on Clean2Random, alongside a 14.17% mean success rate and a 19.00 mean progress score on RoboDojo. To push the capability frontier further, we explore joint human--robot pretraining with HumanWAM-Goal, a visual-goal-conditioned world--action model. Hybrid data scaling unlocks broad zero-shot generalization across both simulation and physical benchmarks, propelling post-trained RoboTwin 2.0 performance to new state-of-the-art levels: 92.70% on Clean2Clean and a substantial leap to 84.60% on Clean2Random. On physical robots, HumanWAM-Goal further exhibits emergent embodied in-context learning, dynamically adapting its manipulation behavior to unseen visual goals without parameter updates. To support reproducible scaling research in embodied AI, we open-source the Open-HumanScale-50K corpus, data curation and pretraining pipelines, alongside the pretrained and post-trained HumanWAM checkpoints, inference code, and simulation evaluation suites.

</details>


### [85] [Benchmarking Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning for Remote Sensing in Urban Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06770)
*Jiawei Zhang,Rizhao Cai,Dayan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出细粒度城市遥感基准 UMBench（基于无人机图像），揭示现有多模态大模型依赖场景级先验而忽视细粒度视觉证据的问题，并提出免训练的证据优先思维链方法提升开源与商用 MLLM 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉推理对城市管理中的遥感应用至关重要，但在多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评测中仍缺乏充分探索；现有模型倾向依赖场景级先验，未有效利用细粒度视觉证据。

Method: 构建 UMBench：基于无人机（UAV）图像、覆盖交通监控、城市建设、环境质量、灾害预警等多个城市管理任务的细粒度遥感评测基准；并提出一种免训练（training-free）的"证据优先思维链"（evidence-first chain-of-thought）方法，引导模型先行检查细粒度视觉证据。

Result: UMBench 评测显示当前 MLLM 常依赖场景级先验、忽略细粒度视觉证据；所提方法在开源与商用 MLLM 上均提升了 UMBench 的性能。

Conclusion: 细粒度视觉证据是城市遥感推理的关键短板，证据优先思维链可在不训练的情况下有效缓解该问题，为 MLLM 在城市遥感评测与推理改进提供基准与方法参考。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual reasoning is essential for remote sensing in urban management, yet remains underexplored in the evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). We introduce UMBench, a fine-grained urban remote sensing benchmark built from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) images for multiple urban management tasks such as traffic monitoring, urban construction, environmental quality, and disaster warning. Evaluations on UMBench reveal that current MLLMs often rely on scene-level priors and overlook fine-grained visual evidence. To address this issue, we further propose a novel evidence-first chain-of-thought method that directs models to inspect fine-grained visual evidence in a training-free manner. Experiments show that our method can improve the performance of both open-source and commercial MLLMs on UMBench.

</details>


### [86] [GeoBiaset: A Counterfactual Benchmark for Demographic Bias in World-Level Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06085)
*Niccolò Niccoli,Silvia Dani,Marco Mistretta,Andrew D. Bagdanov,Marco Bertini,Lorenzo Seidenari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoBiaset——首个衡量插入图像前景中人物"表观种族"如何影响地理定位模型的基准（9,541 张图，配对 7 类种族的反事实编辑，真值坐标固定），并引入 POPI 指标量化模型对人口统计扰动的鲁棒性；实验显示在人群不匹配时洲级定位性能下降 16–47%，且模型在有地标时关注地标、无地标时转向人脸特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界级图像地理定位日益重要，但其偏差机制理解不足。已有工作仅指出数据集粗粒度不平衡（如某些城市缺失、富裕地区表现更好），尚未有人研究究竟是哪些视觉线索驱动了这些性能差异。

Method: 构建 GeoBiaset 基准：将带地理标签的背景图与覆盖 7 类种族的反事实前景人物编辑配对，保持真值坐标不变，共 9,541 张图像；用受控遮挡实验排除遮挡与背景漂移的解释；提出 Person-Origin Perturbation Indicators (POPI) 指标，通过测量预测是否被拉向插入群体所关联区域来量化鲁棒性；并对 SOTA 地理定位模型与多模态大语言模型进行评估，结合有/无地标对比分析与梯度加权热图。

Result: 插入与图像地理背景人口统计不匹配的人物时，洲级定位性能下降 16–47%；受控掩码实验表明遮挡与背景漂移无法完全解释性能退化；有/无地标分析与梯度加权热图显示模型在有地标时优先关注地理地标，缺失时注意力转向人脸特征。

Conclusion: 地理定位模型存在由人物表观人口特征触发的显著偏差，且该偏差源于注意力从地标向人脸特征的转移而非单纯遮挡效应；GeoBiaset 与 POPI 为度量并诊断此类偏差提供了可复现的评测框架。

Abstract: World-level image geolocalization is an increasingly important task, yet the biases of current models remain poorly understood. Prior work has identified coarse dataset imbalances noting, for instance, that some cities are absent from training sets and that models tend to perform better in wealthier regions. However, no one has yet examined which visual cues drive these disparities. To do so, we introduce GeoBiaset, the first benchmark designed to measure how geolocalization models are influenced by the apparent race of a person inserted into the image foreground. The dataset consists of 9,541 images, pairing existing geotagged background images with counterfactual edits spanning seven races, holding the ground-truth coordinates fixed. Controlled masking experiments show that occlusion and background drift do not fully explain geolocalization degradation. Alongside the benchmark, we introduce the Person-Origin Perturbation Indicators (POPI), novel metrics that quantify model resilience to these demographic perturbations by measuring whether predictions are pulled toward the region associated with the inserted group. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art geolocalization and multimodal large language models reveals a 16-47% drop in continent-level performance when a subject from a demographically mismatched group is inserted. A landmark versus no-landmark analysis and gradient-weighted heatmaps show that models preferentially attend to geographic landmarks when present, but shift attention to facial features in their absence.

</details>


### [87] [ChanSFormer: A Channel Agnostic Vision Transformer for Multi-Channel Cell Painting Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06015)
*Jingwei Zhang,Srinivasan Sivanandan,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChanSFormer，用解耦的空间-通道注意力与独立通道CLS token替代可学习通道嵌入，实现真正通道无关的多通道视觉Transformer，在生物成像与遥感数据集上兼顾性能、迁移性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高内涵多通道成像（Cell Painting、遥感等）中通道高度异质且实验配置不断演化，现有通道自适应ViT依赖全局自注意力和可学习通道嵌入，只能处理已见通道；应对新通道时要么丢弃嵌入（丧失通道身份），要么逐通道独立处理（失去跨通道交互）。

Method: ChanSFormer：以解耦的空间-通道注意力（disentangled spatial-channel attention）替代刚性通道嵌入，并为每个通道设置独立的CLS token，从而原生同时保留通道身份与跨通道信息流；该设计还提升自监督表征的信息量，并支持基于表征的通道采样，同时保持通道无关性。

Result: 在CHAMMI、JUMP-CP与So2Sat上评测：JUMP-CP分类精度较SOTA提升3.93%–4.27%；自监督设定下跨数据集迁移精度最高提升2.72%；解耦注意力降低了全局注意力中通道×空间序列长度的二次复杂度，吞吐量提升55%–313%。

Conclusion: ChanSFormer以通道无关架构统一解决多通道ViT的通道泛化、跨通道交互与计算效率问题，在生物多通道成像与遥感任务上验证了精度、迁移性与吞吐量的综合优势。

Abstract: High-content multi-channel imaging, from Cell Painting assays to remote sensing, is central to modern scientific pipelines. Because these channels are highly heterogeneous and experimental configurations frequently evolve, a practical vision backbone must be fundamentally channel-agnostic. Current channel-adaptive vision transformers rely on global self-attention with learnable channel embeddings, which can be computed only for previously known channels. Attempts to overcome this limitation of new channels either discard the embeddings, losing channel identity, or process each channel independently, thereby eliminating critical cross-channel interactions. To address this, we introduce ChanSFormer, an efficient Vision Transformer that replaces rigid embeddings with disentangled spatial-channel attention and individual channel CLS tokens. This architecture natively preserves both distinct channel identities and cross-channel information flow. Furthermore, the individual channel class token design unlocks more informative feature representations for self-supervised learning and enables representation-based channel sampling while remaining channel-agnostic. We evaluate ChanSFormer on two biology multi-channel datasets, CHAMMI, JUMP-CP and a satellite dataset, So2Sat. Experimental results show that ChanSFormer outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 3.93%-4.27% on JUMP-CP in classification accuracy. It also demonstrates exceptional cross-dataset transferability under a self-supervised setting, outperforming previous methods by up to 2.72% in accuracy in the same classification tasks. Furthermore, the disentangled attention reduces the quadratic complexity of channel-by-spatial sequence length of the global attention baseline, thus improving throughput by 55%-313%. Code is available at https://github.com/cvlab-stonybrook/ChanSFormer.

</details>


### [88] [BudgetSplat: Budget-Controllable Generalizable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06593)
*Yuxi Hu,Jun Zhang,Wei Mao,Yuchen Rao,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BudgetSplat，一种预算可控的前馈 3D 高斯泼溅框架，可按用户指定的保留比例自适应压缩高斯表示，在保持渲染质量的同时显著降低存储与渲染开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素对齐的前馈 3DGS 方法产生大量冗余高斯基元，表示规模与渲染成本随图像分辨率和输入视图数量快速增长，缺乏可控的紧凑化手段。

Method: 将压缩建模为重要性估计、空间覆盖与跨视图冗余的联合问题；用 Intra-Cross Mask Supervision 提供结构、覆盖与冗余感知的监督以学习高斯重要性；用预算条件特征调制调整不透明度与属性以适配保留比例；再用 Coverage-Aware Budget Selection 结合逐块预选、逐视图不透明度排序与 3D 冗余抑制完成选择。

Result: 在室内重建与跨数据集泛化实验中，不同输入视图数与保留比例下均呈现一致的质量—规模权衡，显著降低存储和渲染成本且保持渲染质量。

Conclusion: 单一训练模型即可支持可变上下文视图数与多测试预算，无需逐预算重训或逐场景压缩，实现可控预算的前馈紧凑高斯表示。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient generalizable novel view synthesis, but existing pixel-aligned methods often generate highly redundant Gaussian primitives, causing the final representation size and rendering cost to grow rapidly with image resolution and the number of input views. We propose BudgetSplat, a budget-controllable feed-forward framework that adapts the final Gaussian representation to a user-specified preservation ratio. Rather than treating compaction as simple importance ranking, we formulate it as a joint problem of importance estimation, spatial coverage, and cross-view redundancy. A single trained model supports varying numbers of context views and multiple test-time budgets without per-budget retraining or per-scene compression. To realize this formulation, Intra-Cross Mask Supervision provides structure-, coverage-, and redundancy-aware guidance for learning Gaussian importance, while budget-conditioned feature modulation adapts Gaussian opacity and attributes to the requested preservation ratio. Coverage-Aware Budget Selection further combines patch-wise pre-selection, view-wise opacity ranking, and 3D redundancy suppression to construct compact yet spatially distributed Gaussian representations. Experiments on indoor reconstruction and cross-dataset generalization demonstrate consistent quality--size trade-offs across different input view counts and preservation ratios, substantially reducing final representation storage and rendering cost while preserving rendering quality.

</details>


### [89] [Spatially and Compositionally Controlled Multimodal Glioblastoma MRI Synthesis with Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06540)
*Steven Glass,Max Van Puyvelde,Marija Pizurica,Mia Meyer,Olivier Gevaert,Willie Brink*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段潜在扩散模型（LDM），可显式控制地生成二维胶质母细胞瘤MRI图像：先由分割LDM按位置、空间范围、轴位层及子区域组成生成肿瘤图，再由ControlNet条件LDM生成符合该分割的T1/T1Gd/T2/FLAIR图像，缓解临床数据类别不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤在不同MRI模态间的形态与组成高度异质，导致临床数据集在患者病理上严重不平衡，亟需可控的合成数据生成方法。

Method: 两阶段潜在扩散框架：第一阶段为分割LDM，以位置、空间范围、轴位层位置和子区域组成为条件生成肿瘤分割图；第二阶段为模态条件LDM结合ControlNet，依据指定分割生成T1、T1Gd、T2、FLAIR四种模态图像。

Result: 在留出测试集上，生成分割的平均质心误差0.842像素、子区域比例误差0.795个百分点；ControlNet在四个模态上均改善FID，合并FID由42.59降至36.12；专家读者研究平均质量评分：真实图像6.60、ControlNet生成图像6.83、全合成图像6.77。

Conclusion: 该两阶段LDM可实现分割与多模态MRI的显式可控生成，分割精度高，ControlNet显著提升图像真实感，合成图像在专家评估中与真实图像质量相当，具备缓解数据不平衡的潜力。

Abstract: Glioblastoma exhibits substantial heterogeneity in tumour morphology and composition across MRI modalities, often making available clinical datasets highly unbalanced in terms of patient pathologies. We propose a two-stage latent diffusion model (LDM) framework for the generation of explicitly controllable 2D glioblastoma MRI scans. First, a segmentation LDM generates tumour maps conditioned on location, spatial extent, axial position, and subregion composition. Second, a modality-conditioned LDM with ControlNet generates T1, T1Gd, T2, and FLAIR images adhering to the requested segmentation. On a held-out test set, generated segmentations achieved a mean centroid error of 0.842 pixels and a subregion proportion error of 0.795 percentage points. ControlNet improved Fréchet inception distance (FID) across all four modalities, reducing pooled FID from 42.59 to 36.12. An expert reader study yielded mean quality ratings of 6.60 for real images, 6.83 for ControlNet-generated images, and 6.77 for fully synthetic images.

</details>


### [90] [3D Banff Glomerulitis Scoring for Kidney Transplant Pathology: Feasibility Study for Volumetric Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05925)
*Yanfan Zhu,Junchao Zhu,Ruining Deng,Shilin Zhao,Yu Wang,Yaohong Wang,Yuechen Yang,Chongyu Qu,Zhengyi Lu,Juming Xiong,Mengmeng Yin,Yihan Wang,Andrew J. Rauch,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将Banff肾小球炎症评分从2D单切片扩展到3D的框架，通过对连续切片配准、跨深度肾小球身份重建与炎症细胞3D计数，实现Compatible、Volumetric与Deduplicated Volumetric三种评分策略，并以g-score为示例验证其一致性与临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Banff评分基于单一全切片、采用"最严重切片"规则量化肾小球炎症，但病变分布本质上是三维的，跨切片炎症差异会导致2D评估不稳定。

Method: 构建3D Banff病变评分框架：配准连续切片、跨深度重建肾小球身份、整合3D炎症细胞计数，并设计Compatible、Volumetric、Deduplicated Volumetric三种评分策略；以肾小球炎（g-score）为示例指标，应用于多切片移植肾活检。

Result: 3D Banff g-score在不同切片间一致性更高；在半自动设置下，Volumetric策略与临床生物标志物的相关性优于传统2D评分。

Conclusion: 3D体积化量化具有潜在增益价值，表明3D感知的Banff评分对肾移植病理具有应用前景；本研究为可行性研究，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Quantifying glomerular inflammation in kidney-transplant biopsies is traditionally performed on single whole-slide sections using the Banff "most-severe section" rule, despite the inherently three-dimensional nature of lesion distribution. This 2D based assessment might lead to instability when inflammation varies across slices. This paper reports a feasibility study examining the technical feasibility and clinical relevance of extending Banff scoring into three dimensions. To this end, we propose a 3D Banff lesion-scoring framework that aligns serial sections, reconstructs glomerular identities across depth, and integrates inflammatory-cell counts in 3D under three scoring strategies: Compatible, Volumetric, and Deduplicated Volumetric. In the experiments, glomerulitis (g-scores) was used as an example Banff metric and applied to multi-section renal allograft biopsies. Our findings indicate that 3D Banff g-scores are more consistent across sections and, under the semi-automatic setting, the Volumetric strategy correlates more strongly with clinical biomarkers than traditional 2D scores. These results show that 3D volumetric quantification offers promising added value, underscoring the potential benefit of 3D-aware Banff scoring for kidney transplant pathology.

</details>


### [91] [DecoupAttn: Decoupled Sparse Attention for Training-Free Acceleration of Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05917)
*Yanjin Li,Bingbing Qiu,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Yanfeng Li,Jian Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Mingda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DecoupAttn解耦稀疏注意力范式：用语义聚类补偿全部key，用位置固定、离线确定的少量块做精确注意力纠错，并配合Distortion Budget与Reassign Cache，实现免训练的少步视频扩散Transformer加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力在少步视频扩散Transformer中存在三大问题：每步寻找关键块的识别开销、统一或基于局部指标的次优预算分配、以及掩码/聚类失效导致的跨步误差累积。

Method: 观察到被跳过块的误差在经过所有key补偿后可相互抵消，据此将注意力解耦：语义聚类补偿所有key，精确注意力仅在位置固定、离线确定的少量块上纠错。引入率失真目标Distortion Budget，在时间步、层、头间分配精确计算以最大化削减最终latent中的失真；并设计Reassign Cache，每步将漂移的key重新分配到缓存质心并刷新补偿，预算块保持固定。方法免训练：少量校准片段离线编译一次计划，推理使用标准block-sparse核与一个融合补偿核。

Result: 在少步视频扩散Transformer上实现加速，同时保持生成质量；消除了逐步关键块识别的开销，并通过率失真预算与质心重分配缓解跨步误差累积。

Conclusion: 解耦式稀疏注意力通过“聚类补偿+固定块精确纠错”的范式，配合率失真预算分配与缓存重分配机制，为少步视频扩散Transformer提供了免训练、开销低且误差可控的加速方案。

Abstract: Sparse attention accelerates few-step video diffusion transformers by computing a few key blocks exactly and compensating for the rest. However, existing sparse attention still faces key challenges, including identification overhead from finding critical blocks at every denoising step, suboptimal budget allocation that sets sparsity budgets uniformly or by local metrics, and cross-step error accumulation as reused masks or clusters go stale. Observing that the errors left on skipped blocks cancel each other once every key is compensated, we propose DecoupAttn, a new paradigm of decoupled sparse attention: semantic clusters compensate every key, and exact attention corrects their error on a few blocks fixed once offline by position. To support this paradigm, we introduce Distortion Budget, a rate-distortion objective for budget allocation that places exact computation across timesteps, layers, and heads where it removes the most distortion that compensation leaves in the final latent. Built on this budget, we further propose Reassign Cache, in which each step reassigns the drifting keys to cached centroids and refreshes compensation while the budgeted blocks stay fixed. Moreover, DecoupAttn is training-free: a few calibration clips compile one plan offline, and inference runs standard block-sparse kernels with one fused compensation kernel. Our code will be released after submission.

</details>


### [92] [Navigational Safety of Marine Surface Vehicles in Restricted Visibility: From Intelligent Vision to Vision-Centered Multi-Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05818)
*Ryan Wen Liu,Fengchuan Song,Tianxiang Wang,Guobei Peng,Youngrong Kim,Xian Zhong,Hooi-Siang Kang,Maohan Liang,Junzhi Yu,Shanliang Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述文章系统梳理了受限能见度条件下智能船舶导航中"从智能视觉到以视觉为中心的多传感器融合"的最新进展，涵盖图像质量退化机理、四项关键视觉任务、多模态融合方案、开源数据集与安全关键应用，并指出开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船载相机性价比高、广泛用于海上水面载具（MSV）的危险态势检测，但受成像平台不规则运动、恶劣天气和长成像距离等受限条件影响，视觉感知性能不可避免地退化；为提升态势感知与航行能力，需借助AIS、雷达、LiDAR等互补传感器，通过多传感器融合在受限能见度下保障航行安全，而现有研究尚缺乏面向智能船舶导航与智能水上运输系统（IWTS）的系统性梳理。

Method: 采用综述/文献调研方法：首先阐述受限能见度下质量退化图像的生成机理；随后围绕四个关键问题回顾智能视觉方法——船载视频稳像、视觉感知增强、目标检测与跟踪、可航行区域分割；继而考察以视觉为中心的多传感器融合方法，包括红外-可见光图像融合、雷达-视觉融合、LiDAR-视觉融合、AIS-视觉融合及其他融合场景。

Result: 汇总并讨论了相关开源数据集与安全关键应用，整理形成海上数据集资源（https://github.com/MAPGroup/Datasets）并承诺定期更新；同时提炼了该领域的开放挑战与未来研究方向，为智能船舶导航与IWTS领域的新入门者及研究者提供参考。

Conclusion: 该文提出"从智能视觉到以视觉为中心的多传感器融合"的综述框架，强调视觉退化问题与互补传感器融合的必要性，为提升受限能见度下MSV的态势感知与航行安全提供了系统性的方法分类、数据资源与研究路线参考。

Abstract: Marine surface vehicles (MSVs) have attracted considerable attention for their commercial, scientific, and military applications. Owing to their favorable performance-to-cost ratio, shipboard cameras are widely used to detect and recognize potentially hazardous situations across diverse operational scenarios. However, visual perception is inevitably degraded by restricted conditions, including irregular motion of the imaging platform, adverse weather, and long imaging distances. To improve situational awareness and navigation capabilities, complementary sensors, such as the automatic identification system (AIS), radar, and light detection and ranging (LiDAR), etc., have become essential shipboard equipment. Multi-sensor fusion can provide richer perceptual information and help ensure navigational safety of MSVs in restricted visibility. This article reviews recent progress from intelligent vision to vision-centered multi-sensor fusion for newcomers and researchers in intelligent ship navigation and intelligent waterborne transportation system (IWTS). After introducing the generation of quality-degraded images in restricted visibility, we will review intelligent vision methods for four key issues, i.e., shipboard video stabilization, visual perception enhancement, object detection and tracking, and navigable area segmentation. We then examine vision-centered multi-sensor fusion methods, including infrared-visible image fusion, radar-vision fusion, LiDAR-vision fusion, AIS-vision fusion, and other multi-sensor fusion scenarios. Open-source datasets and safety-critical applications are also summarized and discussed. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future research directions. The collected maritime datasets are available at https://github.com/MAPGroup/Datasets and will be regularly updated.

</details>


### [93] [ME^2FDiff: Multiscale Expert Enhancing Feature Diffusion in Residual Network for Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05766)
*Afrida Rahman,Bijoy Kundu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ME²FDiff单模型架构，用于MRI脑胶质瘤术后分割，通过瓶颈处的谱域扩散精炼与多尺度专家增强特征融合，在BraTS 2024数据集上以更少参数和更低推理成本取得优于单模型基线与三专家集成的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后胶质瘤MRI自动分割对诊断与治疗规划至关重要，但手术与放化疗导致切除腔、水肿、纤维化等结构改变，成像表现改变并易与肿瘤复发混淆。现有分割架构存在两点局限：一是瓶颈仅来源于最深编码阶段，单一表示须同时服务尺寸与外观差异极大的各子区域；二是单次前向输出、缺乏精炼机制。

Method: 提出ME²FDiff单模型架构，包含两个模块：(1) 注意力条件谱扩散块精炼瓶颈，采用图像条件潜扩散过程，含空间预注意力门与谱融合块，后者用时间步自适应带通滤波器在各噪声水平选择信息量大的频带；(2) 多尺度专家增强特征块，通过专家选择的共享token将中间编码尺度融合进瓶颈，无需辅助损失即保证均衡利用。

Result: 在BraTS 2024术后胶质瘤数据集上，该方法在所有单模型基线中于每个肿瘤子区域均取得最佳结果（全肿瘤Dice 91.23%、IoU 84.52%、HD95 2.13 mm），并在两项重叠度指标上超越三专家集成，推理成本约为其三分之一；与同等条件重训练的最新SOTA方法相比，以竞争模型中最少参数量保持最强性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单个紧凑网络即可吸收通常需借助集成或更大架构才能获得的专门化能力。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of post-treatment glioma from MRI is essential for accurate diagnosis and treatment planning, yet remains difficult. Surgery and chemoradiotherapy cause structural changes in brain like resection cavities, edema, and fibrosis, that alter imaging appearance and mimic tumor recurrence. Contemporary segmentation architectures attain high accuracy with two limitations. First, the bottleneck originates from the deepest encoder stage, so one representation must serve sub-regions that differ widely in size and appearance. Second, they produce the segmentation in a single forward pass with no mechanism for refinement. We propose ME^2FDiff, a single-model architecture, addressing both. An attention-conditioned spectral diffusion block refines the bottleneck. It uses the image-conditioned latent diffusion process with two processes: a spatial pre-attention gate and a spectral fusion block whose timestep-adaptive bandpass filter selects the informative frequency band at each noise level. A multiscale expert-enhanced feature block fuses the intermediate encoder scales into the bottleneck through expert-selected shared tokens, guaranteeing balanced utilization without an auxiliary loss. On the BraTS 2024 post-treatment glioma dataset, ME^2FDiff achieves the best results on every tumor sub-region among all single-model baselines (91.23% Dice, 84.52% IOU, 2.13 mm HD95 on whole-tumor) and surpasses a three-expert ensemble on both overlap metrics at roughly one-third of its inference cost. Against recent state-of-the-art methods retrained under identical conditions, it maintains the strongest performance with the fewest parameters of any competing model. These findings demonstrate that a single, compact network can absorb the specialization, usually sought through ensembling or larger architectures.

</details>


### [94] [Homology Features for X-Ray Hyperspectral Images Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06230)
*Fethi Harkat,Guillaume Gey,Valérie Perrier,Kévin Polisano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种将持久同调拓扑特征与卷积、图神经网络融合的混合架构，用于多能XRT高光谱图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能X射线透射（XRT）高光谱图像存在高维冗余和非线性光谱重叠，传统分类框架难以处理；UMAP等流形学习虽能保留非线性相关性改善光谱表征，但未显式利用嵌入流形的几何结构进行分割。

Method: 在UMAP投影后的超像素点云上计算Vietoris-Rips持久同调，提取编码光谱异质性的H1环状结构作为拓扑描述子；通过广播机制将超像素级图表示传播至全空间分辨率，与像素级CNN特征融合，构建拓扑特征+CNN+GNN的混合模型。

Result: 在XRT多能影像上的实验表明，该混合模型显著优于仅CNN基线和超像素池化融合基线。

Conclusion: 拓扑特征可为局部光谱-空间描述子提供互补的判别信息，持久同调与深度网络的结合是提升XRT高光谱分类性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Characterizing Multi-Energy X-ray Transmission (XRT) hyperspectral images presents significant challenges for traditional classification frameworks due to high-dimensional redundancy and non-linear spectral overlaps. Although manifold learning techniques such as UMAP enhance spectral representation by preserving non-linear correlations, they do not explicitly utilize the geometric structure of the embedded manifold for segmentation. This study introduces a hybrid architecture that integrates topological features derived from persistent homology with convolutional and graph neural networks. The approach computes Vietoris-Rips persistent homology on UMAP-projected superpixel point clouds to capture H 1 cyclic structures that encode spectral heterogeneity. These topological descriptors are combined with pixel-level CNN features through a broadcasting mechanism that propagates superpixel-level graph representations to full spatial resolution. Experimental results on XRT multi-energy imagery demonstrate that the proposed hybrid model significantly outperforms both CNN-only and superpixel-pooling fusion baselines, suggesting that topological features provide complementary discriminative information to local spectralspatial descriptors.

</details>


### [95] [ReactWorld: Structuring Interaction Responses for Embodied Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06120)
*Hanwei Liu,Huijia Zhang,Hanzhang Wang,Guangyu Liu,Hanbei Zhan,Linjie Hu,Bin Xiao,Xuan Zhou,Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReactWorld——一种由结构化交互预判引导的具身视频世界模型，在特征空间预判交互响应并以反馈机制引导对象特征更新与时序注意力检索，RBench均分77.51（超PhysisForcing 3.29），EZS-Bench域得分86.98、均分81.87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身视频世界模型虽能预测机器人动作引起的场景变化，但生成的对象响应常与任务意图和接触动力学不一致；而具身场景中的关键响应往往集中在局部区域与特定时间区间，需要有针对性的建模。

Method: 借鉴思维链思路，在生成骨干网络的特征空间中引入"交互响应场预测器"，显式刻画参与角色、未来运动响应强度与局部运动变化；并设计"响应反馈适配器"：以预测局部动力学输入与置零输入下对象分支输出的差异形成对象特征增量，同时将预测位移转化为时序注意力的位置偏置，分别引导对象特征更新与跨帧信息检索。

Result: RBench 平均得分77.51，较 PhysisForcing 提升3.29分，且在全部九类上均有提升；EZS-Bench 上 Domain 得分86.98，平均得分81.87。

Conclusion: 通过"交互预判 + 生成反馈"的闭环设计，可有效提升具身视频世界模型中对象响应的物理一致性与任务一致性，验证了结构化预判引导生成范式的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied video world models predict scene changes caused by robot actions, yet generated object responses may remain inconsistent with task intent and contact dynamics. Critical responses in embodied scenes often concentrate in local regions and specific time intervals. Inspired by chain-of-thought reasoning, we introduce ReactWorld, an embodied video generation model guided by structured interaction anticipation. In the generation backbone's feature space, an interaction response field predictor anticipates how the interaction will unfold, explicitly describing participant roles, future motion response intensity, and local motion changes. A response feedback adapter forms increments to object features from the difference between the object branch's outputs with predicted and zeroed local dynamics inputs. It also converts predicted displacements into positional biases for temporal attention. These two mechanisms guide object feature updates and information retrieval across frames, respectively. Through interaction anticipation and generative feedback, ReactWorld achieves an average score of 77.51 on RBench, outperforming PhysisForcing by 3.29 points with improvements in all nine categories. On EZS-Bench, it achieves a Domain score of 86.98 and an average score of 81.87.

</details>


### [96] [RouTex: Multi-reference 3D Texture Generation by Reprogramming Rotary Position Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06070)
*Chengyang Li,Yang Liu,Jiale Zhang,Qiang Rao,Wei Zhang,Taicheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RouTex：基于in-context DiT的多参考图3D纹理生成框架，利用原生3D RoPE的空间寻址能力实现参考-视角对应，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D纹理生成已较成熟，但以多视角参考图为条件的纹理合成研究不足；而多参考对于消除未观测区域的表面歧义是必要的。

Method: 基于in-context Diffusion Transformer。核心见解是原生3D Rotary Position Encoding（RoPE）本身具备空间寻址能力；提出Positional Routing（PR），沿空间与语义维度重编程3D RoPE，在不增加参数的前提下建立显式的参考到视角对应关系；并设计Dual-Granularity Attention（DGA），将逐视角局部注意力（保证纹理细节保真）与对去重参考的全局联合注意力（保证跨视角一致性）相结合以高效执行路由。

Result: 大量实验表明RouTex在多参考3D纹理生成任务上取得新的state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 通过复用3D RoPE的寻址特性并以双粒度注意力实现参考与视角的显式对齐，可在无额外参数条件下有效提升多参考3D纹理生成的质量与一致性。

Abstract: While single-image 3D texture generation has advanced significantly, conditioning texture synthesis on multi-view reference images remains largely under-explored, despite its necessity for resolving surface ambiguity in unobserved regions. In this paper, we propose RouTex, a novel multi-reference 3D texture generation framework based on in-context Diffusion Transformers (DiT). Our key insight is that native 3D Rotary Position Encoding (RoPE) inherently possesses spatial addressing capabilities. We introduce Positional Routing (PR) to reprogram 3D RoPE along spatial and semantic dimensions, establishing explicit reference-to-view correspondence without introducing additional parameters. To execute this routing efficiently, Dual-Granularity Attention (DGA) combines per-view local attention for fine texture fidelity with global joint attention over deduplicated references for cross-view consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RouTex achieves new state-of-the-art performance in multi-reference 3D texture generation.

</details>


### [97] [RI-Prune: Relation and Information Aware Visual Token Pruning for Multi-Image Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06047)
*Dongjin Lee,Eunju Lee,Jinhee Jang,Yoonji Lee,Youngbin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免注意力机制的预解码器视觉token剪枝方法 RI-Prune，用投影器输出的三种信号打分，在多图推理场景下取得更好的token压缩与性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM扩展到多图输入后视觉token预算激增，剪枝成为必需；但现有基于注意显著性的单图剪枝方法迁移到多图场景时性能明显下降、存在强位置偏置、且随图像数量增加判别性注意力信号衰减。

Method: 先用两个因果探针（Leave-One-In 与 Leave-One-Deletion）检验注意力选择token的真实重要性；再提出RI-Prune：在projector输出上计算三类信号——查询相关性、与每图紧凑原型的跨图对应性、图内代表性——做z-normalize加权融合，并按图像均衡执行Top-K选择。

Result: 因果探针显示基于注意力的剪枝相较随机选择仅带来边际提升，说明注意力是多图推理中token重要性的弱代理；RI-Prune在多个多图基准与三个LVLM骨干上，token削减与任务性能的权衡优于先前方法，在激进token预算下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 注意力显著性不适用于多图视觉token剪枝；免训练、免注意力的多信号融合与按图均衡Top-K选择是更有效的多图token压缩方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently been extended to handle multiple input images, but this extension dramatically increases the visual token budget, making visual-token pruning not merely an efficiency option but a practical necessity. Existing visual-token pruning methods, primarily designed for the single-image setting, rely on attention-derived saliency signals to estimate per-token importance. However, this attention-centric design transfers poorly to the multi-image regime: it causes substantial performance degradation on multi-image benchmarks, suffers from strong positional bias, and loses discriminative attention signals as the number of input images increases. To test whether attention-selected tokens are truly important for multi-image reasoning, we conduct two complementary causal probes Leave-One-In and Leave-One-Deletion, which measure token importance by restoring individual tokens to a zeroed visual sequence or removing them from the full sequence. Attention-based pruners provide only marginal gains over random selection, showing that attention is a weak proxy for visual-token importance in multi-image reasoning. Motivated by this gap, we propose Relation and Information Aware Pruning (RI-Prune), a training-free, attention-free pre-decoder selector for multi-image visual token pruning. RI-Prune scores each visual token using a z-normalized combination of three signals computed from projector outputs: query relevance, cross-image correspondence with compact per-image prototypes, and intra image representativeness. By integrating these signals, RI-Prune performs balanced per-image Top-K selection to preserve information across input images. Across multiple multi-image benchmarks and three LVLM backbones, RI-Prune achieves a stronger trade-off between token reduction and task performance than prior pruning methods, particularly under aggressive token budgets.

</details>


### [98] [DeepfakeGenome: Toward Next-Generation Deepfake Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06046)
*Shunli Wang,Xinyu Zhou,Yandan Zhao,Wenbin Liang,Taiping Yao,Ke-Yue Zhang,Zhiyuan Yan,Shouhong Ding,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DeepfakeGenome (DFG) 基准，含 100 种人脸伪造算法、200 万图像，规模为已有 DFA 基准的 4–100 倍，并设计 4 种评测协议（含检索式归因范式），经 2000+ 实验探明 Deepfake 归因任务性能上限并给出 10 条发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC 生成的高逼真伪造人脸图像威胁人脸安全。现有 Deepfake 检测与归因研究存在：二分类性能饱和、数据集与算法多样性不足、评测协议不完善，严重阻碍实际落地。

Method: 构建 DeepfakeGenome 基准：覆盖 100 种人脸伪造算法、共 200 万张图像；设计 4 种面向实际应用的评测协议，其中提出检索式归因范式，其指标相比既有开集评测更契合黑名单注册机制下的真实主动防御场景；在此基础上系统考察归因任务性能上限，开展 2000 余次实验评估。

Result: 基准规模较已有 DFA 基准大 4–100 倍；通过 2000+ 实验评测得出 10 条有价值结论，刻画了 Deepfake 归因任务的性能上限。

Conclusion: DFG 为 Deepfake 检测与归因提供统一、大规模且贴近实际的评测平台，其检索式归因协议与系统性实验结论可为 DFA 领域研究提供新视角；代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: In recent years, AIGC technologies are capable of generating hyper-realistic forged facial images, posing severe threats to facial security safety. To tackle these challenges, early Deepfake research primarily centered on real-fake classification. Recently, growing efforts have been devoted to the Deepfake Attribution (DFA) of generated content. Nevertheless, existing research on Deepfake detection and attribution faces saturated performance in binary classification, limited diversity in datasets and algorithms, and imperfect evaluation protocols, which severely impede practical application. To address these limitations, we propose a comprehensive deepfake detection and attribution benchmark named DeepfakeGenome (DFG). It contains 100 facial forgery algorithms and 2M images in total, achieving 4x to 100x larger than prior DFA benchmarks. We further design 4 protocols for practical evaluation, including a novel retrieval-based attribution paradigm. Unlike previous open-set evaluation metrics, the proposed retrieval metrics are more aligned with the real-world active defense situation of blacklist registration mechanisms. Based on these elaborate designs, we investigate the performance ceiling of deepfake attribution task. Over 2k experimental evaluations are conducted, and 10 insightful findings are derived. We believe this work can provide new insights into the DFA research field. The Code and Benchmark of DFG are avaliable on https://huggingface.co/spaces/DeepfakeGenome/DFG_Page.

</details>


### [99] [Whose Values Are Generated? Measuring Visual Value Leakage in Multilingual Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05578)
*Zucan Lyu,Binwei Yao,Rheeya Uppaal,Jing Yao,Xiaoyuan Yi,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多语言基准 VisionValueBench，用 Hofstede 六个文化价值维度衡量 T2I 系统生成图像中的文化价值取向，发现"视觉价值泄漏"：价值中立提示也稳定偏向特定取向，且跨语言、跨地理线索高度同质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 文化表征评测主要关注地标、服饰、活动等可识别元素，忽视了生成图像所传达的文化价值观念，导致系统在不同语言与文化语境下的价值偏向无法被刻画。

Method: 构建多语言基准 VisionValueBench，将 Hofstede 六个文化价值维度（如个人主义—集体主义）操作化，用中性社会情境提示考察 T2I 如何以特定文化取向框定场景；覆盖 960 个社会场景，变化提示语言与地理线索，评测六种 T2I 配置。

Result: 价值中立提示生成的图像系统性偏向特定价值取向（视觉价值泄漏）；六个维度上取向一致，例如平等优于等级（低权力距离）、个人自主优于群体义务（个人主义）、享乐优于克制（放纵）。提示语言影响有限；地理线索使部分维度向对应文化价值偏移，但总体主流取向基本保持。

Conclusion: T2I 系统在全球范围内所传达的文化价值呈现显著同质性；文化表征评测需从可识别元素扩展到价值层面，地理与语言线索不足以有效调节生成的价值取向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems serve users across languages and cultural contexts. However, evaluations of cultural representation focus mainly on recognizable elements such as landmarks, clothing, and activities, overlooking the cultural values that generated images convey. To bridge this gap, we introduce VisionValueBench, a multilingual benchmark that operationalizes six of Hofstede's cultural value dimensions (e.g., individualism versus collectivism) to investigate how T2I systems frame neutral social situations through a particular cultural orientation. We find that value-neutral prompts, which admit diverse cultural interpretations, nonetheless yield images that favor particular value orientations, a phenomenon we call visual value leakage. We evaluate prompts varying in language and geographic cues across 960 social scenarios and six T2I configurations. Generated images consistently favor the same value orientations across all six dimensions, for example, equality over hierarchy (low power distance), individual autonomy over group obligation (individualism), and enjoyment over restraint (indulgence). Prompt language has limited influence on these orientations, whereas geographic cues shift some dimensions toward the associated cultural values while largely preserving the prevailing orientations. These findings reveal substantial homogeneity in the cultural values conveyed by T2I systems worldwide.

</details>


### [100] [SpikePose: Lightweight Video Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06594)
*Yunhao Huang,Kaushik Bhargav Sivangi,Tianyi Yu,Fani Deligianni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikePose：帧重置单步脉冲编码器 + 运动对齐热图记忆（MAM）的轻量视频人体姿态估计器，在MPII与NTU60子集上以约91%的算术能耗下降换取极小精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频人体姿态估计需同时兼顾定位精度、运动质量与能耗；脉冲神经网络（SNN）虽节能，但在大规模、非事件驱动的视频任务上与经典ANN存在性能差距，需在保持脉冲骨干轻量的前提弥补时序建模能力。

Method: 采用帧重置（frame-reset）、单步（single-step）脉冲编码器提供逐帧空间证据；将时序推理置于脉冲骨干之外的紧凑轻量模块MAM中：MAM估计关节位移、对历史热图进行运动对齐，并与当前预测自适应融合。

Result: 在与带MAM的同构ANN对比中，估算算术能耗降低91.4%，PCK_HB(0.5)仅下降1.12个点；在SpikePose内部加入MAM使速度与加速度误差分别降低28.13%与31.46%，定位精度略有提升，代价仅为0.003M参数与0.0023分析GFLOPs/帧。

Conclusion: 将时序推理外置于脉冲骨干的轻量MAM设计，可有效缩小SNN与ANN在非事件驱动视频姿态估计上的差距，实现显著的能效优势且精度损失可控。

Abstract: Efficient video human pose estimation must balance localisation accuracy, motion quality, and energy cost. We present SpikePose, a lightweight video pose estimator that pairs a frame-reset, single-step spiking encoder with motion-aligned heatmap memory (MAM). The encoder supplies per-frame spatial evidence, while MAM estimates joint displacement, aligns historical heatmaps, and adaptively fuses them with current predictions. By confining temporal reasoning to a compact, lightweight module outside the spiking backbone, SpikePose helps bridge the gap between spiking and classical Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) on large-scale, non-event-driven video tasks. We evaluate spatial accuracy, motion errors, and estimated arithmetic energy on MPII and a fixed NTU60 Cross-Subject subset. Against the architecture-matched ANN with MAM, SpikePose cuts estimated arithmetic energy by 91.4% at a cost of just 1.12 points in head-bone-normalised PCK at 0.5 threshold (PCK_HB(0.5)). Within SpikePose, adding MAM reduces velocity and acceleration errors by 28.13% and 31.46% while slightly improving localisation, at a cost of only 0.003M parameters and 0.0023 analytical GFLOPs per frame. Code will be released on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [101] [MKD2World: A Typed Spatial MarkDown World Model For Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06544)
*Zhihao Nan,Sifan Zhou,Yiming Cheng,Jinkai Zheng,Zheng Zhu,Si Shi,Ming Li,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Typed Spatial Markdown (TSM) 状态表示与 MKD2World 桌面网页世界模型，通过两阶段训练实现当前状态描述与动作条件下一状态预测，显著提升 GUI 智能体的前瞻决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体借助世界模型进行前瞻可提升决策，但前提是预测状态能准确反映动作后的界面。现有状态表示无法同时刻画界面布局、UI 语义与显式位置信息，成为世界模型预测精度的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 设计 TSM：一种 Markdown 风格文本状态表示，按空间关系序列化块级与原子级 UI 元素，联合编码类型、视觉语义、显式坐标、可选控件状态及层级归属关系；2) 构建 AtomBlock-WebUI 数据集，通过向 HTML 注入元素类型标签并在渲染后直接提取边界框，避免事后从网页结构推断带来的标注错误，用于训练 screenshot-to-TSM 渲染器；3) 提出 MKD2World，两阶段训练（当前状态描述、动作条件下一状态预测）；4) 推理时智能体按需查询，并执行“digest”步骤，从预测状态中抽取任务相关证据以选择最优动作。

Result: MKD2World-9B 在域外测试集上 EleF1 达 26.50，超过 GPT-5.6-Sol 的 14.98（提升 11.52 分）；在 Online-Mind2Web 任务上，将 Qwen3.5-35B 网页智能体成功率从 22.94% 提升至 37.61%，在开源 GUI 世界模型中具备竞争力。

Conclusion: 将空间化、类型化的结构化文本状态表示与世界模型结合，可有效提升网页 GUI 智能体的状态预测与决策性能；数据集构建方式与 digest 推理机制是方法有效的关键支撑。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents can improve decision-making with world-model foresight, provided that predicted states accurately reflect post-action interfaces. Existing state representations fail to jointly capture interface layout, UI semantics, and explicit locations. We introduce Typed Spatial Markdown (TSM), a Markdown-style textual state representation for desktop web interfaces. TSM serializes block-level and atomic-level UI elements according to their spatial relationships, jointly encoding their types, visual semantics, explicit locations, optional control states, and hierarchical membership. To train a screenshot-to-TSM renderer, we construct AtomBlock-WebUI, a dataset with precise annotations at both granularities. By injecting element-type labels into HTML and directly extracting bounding boxes after rendering, we avoid annotation errors caused by post-hoc inference from webpage structures. Building on TSM, we propose MKD2World, a desktop web world model trained in two stages for current-state description and action-conditioned next-state prediction. During inference, the web agent queries MKD2World as needed and performs a ``digest'' step, in which it interprets each predicted state and extracts task-relevant evidence for selecting the best action. Experiments show that MKD2World-9B achieves an EleF1 score of 26.50 on the out-of-domain test set, outperforming GPT-5.6-Sol(14.98) by 11.52 points.
