Across Online-Mind2Web tasks, MKD2World-9B improves the success rate of the Qwen3.5-35B web agent from 22.94% to 37.61%, achieving competitive performance among leading open-source GUI world models. Code is available at https://github.com/OmniAI-Lab-CUHKSZ/MKD2World.

</details>


### [102] [Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉令牌路由（VTR），联合训练轻量级时空路由器，按需保留动作预测所需的视觉令牌，仅2000步训练即在多个基准上匹配全分辨率模型性能，同时减少2.5–5.8倍视觉令牌，推理成本降低至多3.3倍、prefill加速至多2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）在长时程基准上已达超人水平，但计算成本高，限制其在长时程任务与实时场景（如电子游戏）中的应用。关键瓶颈是把每张截图表示为数千个视觉令牌，在长交互历史中累积成长上下文。作者观察到：多数视觉令牌要么与下一步动作语义无关，要么跨截图时间冗余。

Method: VTR 是一个联合训练框架，学习保留动作预测所需信息的稀疏视觉表示。视觉语言模型与一个轻量级时空路由器联合训练：路由器检查当前及前一张截图中每个图像区域，决定其视觉令牌是否进入语言模型。路由器用自适应损失优化，在最小化性能损失的同时尽量压缩视觉令牌数量。

Result: 仅2000训练步，VTR在OSWorld、CUA-World和视频游戏动作预测上匹配全分辨率模型性能，视觉令牌减少2.5–5.8倍；推理成本降低至多3.3倍，prefill加速至多2.9倍。相较降低图像分辨率和其他丢弃令牌的基线，在OSWorld上最高领先12.8个点。

Conclusion: VTR使计算机使用智能体只处理其真正需要的视觉令牌，从而以极小的计算成本支持长时程任务与实时应用场景。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently achieved superhuman performance on several long-horizon benchmarks. However, their computational cost limits their use in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains such as video games. A key bottleneck is representing each screenshot as thousands of visual tokens, which accumulate into long contexts as agents reason over extended interaction histories. We observe that most of these visual tokens are either semantically irrelevant to the agent's next action or temporally redundant across screenshots. Building on this observation, we introduce Visual Token Routing (VTR), a joint training framework for learning sparse visual representations that preserve the information needed for action prediction. In VTR, we train the vision-language model together with a lightweight spatio-temporal router that examines each image region in the current and previous screenshots and decides whether its visual token enters the language model. We optimize the router using an adaptive loss to keep the number of visual tokens as small as possible while minimizing performance degradation. With just 2,000 training steps, VTR matches the performance of the full-resolution model on OSWorld, CUA-World, and video game action prediction, while using 2.5-5.8x fewer visual tokens. These savings reduce inference cost by up to 3.3x and speed up prefill by up to 2.9x. VTR also outperforms lowering the image resolution and other baselines for dropping tokens, by up to 12.8 points on OSWorld. Overall, VTR enables computer-use agents to process only the visual tokens they need, allowing them to be used in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and models are available at https://pranjal2041.github.io/vtr

</details>


### [103] [Where to Connect? Boosting MLLMs via Dynamic Gated Pathways across ALL ViT and LLM Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06844)
*Yingying Yan,Jiaqi Tang,Wei Wei,Qianzhou Wang,Lei Zhang,Jianmin Chen,Yuyang Xia,Botong Geng,Jinjian Wu,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DGP（Dynamic Gated Pathways）路由机制，在ViT各层与LLM各层间建立全连接，并由每个LLM层基于自身语义状态动态门控其对视觉层的偏好，实现自适应跨层交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流MLLM将固定单层视觉表示注入LLM并把所有LLM层视为单一消费者，忽视了不同层对视觉粒度需求不同；现有多层融合方法或在编码器侧压缩层级特征，或依赖预定义的稀疏/层级化层间连接，无法建模ViT与LLM层级间的自适应跨层交互。

Method: DGP建立每个ViT层与每个LLM层之间的全连接（all-to-all）路由，各LLM层依据自身语义状态动态地门控对视觉层的选择偏好，从而按需获取不同视觉粒度；基于LLaVA-1.5-7B与13B实现。

Result: 相对LLaVA-1.5-7B基线取得一致提升（SQA^I +4.2、MM-Vet +3.3、VizWiz与LLaVA^W各+3.1），并在多数基准上超越先前的多层视觉融合方法。

Conclusion: 逐层路由分析表明不同LLM层确实按需选择不同视觉粒度，验证了动态门控路径的有效性与可解释性；代码已开源。

Abstract: A central yet under-explored question in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is where to connect---how visual information from a hierarchical vision encoder should be wired into the layer-wise semantics of a large language model. Mainstream MLLMs inject a fixed single-layer visual representation into the LLM and treat all LLM layers as a single consumer, ignoring that different layers demand different visual granularities. Recent multi-layer fusion methods either compress hierarchical features on the encoder side or rely on predefined sparse or hierarchical layer-to-layer connections, and therefore stop short of modeling adaptive cross-layer interactions between the ViT and LLM hierarchies. We address this gap with DGP (Dynamic Gated Pathways), a routing mechanism that establishes all-to-all connections between every ViT layer and every LLM layer, with each LLM layer dynamically gating its preference over visual layers conditioned on its own semantic state. Built on top of LLaVA-1.5-7B and 13B, DGP delivers consistent gains over the LLaVA-1.5-7B baseline (e.g., +4.2 on SQA$^{I}$, +3.3 on MM-Vet, +3.1 on VizWiz and LLaVA$^{W}$) and surpasses prior multi-layer visual fusion methods on most benchmarks. Layer-wise routing analyses further show that different LLM layers do select different visual granularities on demand, supporting both the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed dynamic gated pathways. The code is available: https://github.com/YingyingYan1/DGP.

</details>


### [104] [Same Layout, Different Count: What Number Words Do Past Ten in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06840)
*Liang Yue,Eric A. Vance*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了五个开源文生图模型在请求约十个以上物体时，数字词对生成图像中物体数量的控制能力。作者固定物体与起始噪声，逐一改变变量，并让未看到请求的受试者对3,088张图像计数。结果显示：模型对小数量能遵循数字，但存在"可达范围"上限（SD3.5-M与FLUX.1-dev在十余个后失效，FLUX.2 [klein]与Qwen-Image可松散地跟随至二十五个）；超限后，起始噪声的粗尺度部分决定构图但不决定数量；数字词虽仍进入去噪器并在最初几步起作用，但无法给出清晰方向。此外，在起始潜变量中以带噪网格形式呈现数量时，受试者有59%的识别率（仅口头告知时为4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型在数量控制上的能力边界与失效机制尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对"约十个以上物体"这一区间内数字词语义如何被模型处理的系统研究，以及可靠的评测指标。

Method: 在五个开源文生图模型上进行受控实验：固定物体与起始噪声，每次只改变一个变量；招募未看到原始请求的受试者对3,088张生成图像进行人工计数；分析起始噪声粗/细尺度对构图与数量的影响（仅重绘八分之一以下的细尺度噪声）；考察数字词对去噪器的作用时机；对比"带噪网格"提示与"口头告知"两种数量呈现方式；并对精确匹配、相对误差等评分指标进行比较。

Result: 模型对小数量能准确遵循，但存在可达范围上限：SD3.5-M与FLUX.1-dev在十余个后即停止遵循，FLUX.2 [klein]与Qwen-Image可松散跟随至二十五个。超出SD3.5-M可达范围后，重绘细尺度噪声即可保持构图并使计数变动约三分之二。数字词仍进入去噪器并在最初几步作用，但无清晰方向。带噪网格呈现时识别率为59%，仅告知时为4%。超过十个后，精确匹配分数难以区分"松散跟随"与"完全忽略"的模型，相对误差分数甚至会偏向常数猜测16；每物体少量请求的同噪声测试可区分二者。

Conclusion: 文生图模型的数量跟随存在明确的可达范围上限，超过约十个后数字词失去有效方向性；现有常用评分指标在该区间失效，需采用同噪声对照测试来评估模型的真实数量控制能力。

Abstract: Ask SD3.5-Medium for twelve of an object and then, from the same starting noise, for twenty-five. The picture keeps its layout, but the count changes about as much as it would under a new random seed, and in no consistent direction. We study what number words do once a request passes about ten objects, in five open text-to-image models. Holding the object and the starting noise fixed, we change one thing at a time, and people who never see the request count 3,088 of the images. Every model follows the number for small crowds, but only up to a point, its reach: SD3.5-M and FLUX.1-dev stop following it soon after ten, while FLUX.2 [klein] and Qwen-Image follow it loosely up to twenty-five. Beyond SD3.5-M's reach, the coarse part of the starting noise fixes the composition of the picture but not how many objects it holds: redrawing only the noise finer than an eighth of the image keeps the layout as well as a new number does, and already moves the count two-thirds as far. The number word still reaches the denoiser and acts in its first steps, but nothing we tried gives it a clear direction. Shown instead as a noised grid in the starting latent, the count is drawn: people find it in 59% of images, against 4% when it is only told. Past ten, an exact-match score barely separates a model that follows the number loosely from one that ignores it, and a relative-error score can even prefer a constant guess of sixteen. A same-noise test with a few requests per object tells them apart.

</details>


### [105] [VA-TDM: A Unified View for Improving Few-Step Diffusion Models by Velocity Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06796)
*Yihong Luo,Tianyu Pang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出速度对抗蒸馏(VAD)框架及VA-TDM方法，通过对学生采样轨迹上的速度场进行分布匹配监督，实现高效少步扩散/流模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏虽能高效生成，但仅用少数采样步数时难以保持质量。基于score distillation的分布匹配方法依赖准确跟踪学生分布的变化，然而在少步采样的中间生成步骤中，学生score估计困难，常用估计器所描述的分布可能与实际用于更新学生的分布不一致。

Method: 利用flow matching可从学生采样过程构造速度场的灵活性，提出Velocity Adversarial Distillation(VAD)：用学习到的critic比较学生与教师的速度场，将其分歧转化为学习反馈；理论上统一了score distillation、Fisher-based matching与moment matching，并解释现有启发式MMD更新、导出新训练目标。进一步提出VA-TDM，遵循轨迹分布匹配，对学生轨迹上的部分去噪提供监督，并结合transition-aware在线速度学习与双形式VAD监督。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上，VA-TDM于文生图与文生视频任务中达到四步生成的SOTA性能，并可有效扩展至33B的MiniMax-H3；VA-TDM-H3显著优于少步Fast-H3，按人类偏好更接近原始多步H3。

Conclusion: 以速度场为监督对象的对抗式蒸馏为少步生成提供了统一且可扩展的分布匹配框架，兼顾理论统一性与实际性能。

Abstract: Few-step distillation offers efficient generation with diffusion and flow models, but preserving quality with only a few sampling steps remains challenging. Distribution-matching methods via score distillation provide a strong foundation, yet they rely on accurately tracking the student's changing distribution. At intermediate generation steps in few-step sampling, student-score estimation can be difficult, and commonly used estimators may describe a different distribution from the one used to update the student. Flow matching allows a more flexible approach to supervision through velocity fields constructed from the student's sampling process. Building on this perspective, we introduce Velocity Adversarial Distillation (VAD), which uses a learned critic to compare student and teacher fields and turn their disagreement into feedback for learning. VAD unifies score distillation, Fisher-based matching, and moment matching, also explaining existing heuristic MMD updates and delivering new training objectives. We further develop VA-TDM, which follows trajectory distribution matching: providing supervision for partial denoising along the student trajectory. Beyond this, VA-TDM combines transition-aware online velocity learning and dual-form VAD supervision across intermediate student transitions. Extensive results show that VA-TDM is powerful for learning few-step generators, achieving state-of-the-art four-step performance in both text-to-image and text-to-video generation across various backbones and scaling effectively to 33B MiniMax-H3. In particular, our VA-TDM-H3 notably outperforms the few-step Fast-H3 and is closer to the original many-step H3 according to human preference.

</details>


### [106] [Coupled Scene Graph Anticipation: When With-Constraint Recall Hides Structural Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06738)
*Zeno Nongmaithem,Anushttha Srivastava,Anshit Sinha,Apoorv Sisodia,Suman Chowdhury,Prerana Mukherjee,Rajesh P. Barnwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CoupledSGA，用关系交互图+图注意力耦合各关系的 Neural ODE，并加入结构一致性损失；同时从理论与实验上揭示 With-Constraint 评测协议的局限，建议补充 No-Constraint 指标与矛盾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间 SGA 中每个 subject-object 对用独立 Neural ODE 演化，共享实体的关系无法在积分过程中相互影响，且没有任何机制阻止同一对出现互斥谓词（如 sitting on 与 standing on），产生结构不一致的预测。

Method: CoupledSGA：在 ODE 向量场内部引入 Relationship Interaction Graph 与图注意力，显式耦合共享实体的关系；并增加基于互斥谓词矩阵的结构一致性损失 L_SC。此外对评测协议做理论分析（With-Constraint 下 R@K/mR@K 在组内胜出谓词及分数固定时不变）并分析 L_SC 梯度的性质。

Result: Action Genome 上，两轮 warm-up 后启用 L_SC：R@20 由 42.21% 降至 35.18%，No-Constraint 矛盾率由 45.92% 降到 4.07%；匹配预算下 R@20 为 CoupledSGA 40.48% vs. SceneSayerODE 44.78%。理论表明 L_SC 梯度依赖谓词分数而非真值标签：二谓词规则下放大模型已有排序，组内分数相等时对出现在更多互斥规则中的谓词抑制更强，解释了召回下降但一致性提升的现象。

Conclusion: CoupledSGA 是验证结构一致性的测试平台而非 SOTA 声明；With-Constraint 召回只能体现一致性约束的代价、无法体现收益。建议对约束谓词共激活的方法同时报告 No-Constraint 结果与矛盾率。

Abstract: Scene Graph Anticipation (SGA) predicts object relationships in future video frames. Continuous-time SGA models evolve each subject-object pair with an independent Neural ODE, so relationships sharing an entity cannot influence one another during integration, and nothing prevents mutually exclusive predictions, such as "sitting on" and "standing on", for the same pair. We introduce CoupledSGA, which links such relationships through a Relationship Interaction Graph with graph attention inside the ODE vector field, and adds a Structural Consistency Loss ($\mathcal{L}_{SC}$) based on a matrix of mutually exclusive predicates.
  Developing CoupledSGA revealed a limitation of the evaluation protocol. With-Constraint evaluation keeps one predicate per group for each subject-object pair, so its R@K and mR@K cannot capture the within-group violations targeted by $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$: we prove they are unchanged whenever each group's winning predicate and its score are held fixed. We further show that the gradient of $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ depends on predicate scores rather than ground-truth labels: for two-predicate rules it amplifies the ordering the model already holds, and when all scores in a group are equal it suppresses predicates in more exclusion rules more strongly, explaining why the penalty can lower recall while improving consistency.
  On Action Genome, activating $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ after two warm-up epochs reduces R@20 from 42.21% to 35.18% while lowering the No-Constraint contradiction rate from 45.92% to 4.07% (matched-budget R@20: 40.48% CoupledSGA vs. 44.78% SceneSayerODE). CoupledSGA is a testbed, not a state-of-the-art claim: With-Constraint recall captures the cost of enforcing structural consistency but not the improvement. We recommend reporting No-Constraint results alongside a contradiction rate for methods that constrain predicate co-activation.

</details>


### [107] [Agent-Driven Generative Priors for Pathology Image Synthesis and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06701)
*Sicheng Yang,Rui Tang,Haipeng Zhou,Hongqiu Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Jianda Ma,Lie Dai,Guang Yang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架：病理智能体筛选数据构建TCGA-200K，训练生成模型学习可迁移表征，再用轻量头适配语义分割，并发布PathSeg-380K数据集，取得SOTA分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理深度学习受限于大规模像素级标注的稀缺与高成本；现有合成数据流程缺乏可靠质量控制，且与下游任务衔接薄弱。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 构建病理智能体系统评估图像质量并过滤样本，形成TCGA-200K（217,030个patch）；(2) 在筛选数据上训练生成模型，其骨干同时学习可迁移的结构与语义表征；(3) 将预训练生成骨干通过轻量任务专用头适配到语义分割。

Result: 在多个病理数据集上取得SOTA分割性能；并构建PathSeg-380K语义分割数据集，含12类细胞、约38万细胞专家标注。代码开源。

Conclusion: 自动化数据筛选+生成表征学习+轻量适配的组合可有效缓解标注瓶颈，生成模型骨干可作为病理下游任务的通用预训练表征。

Abstract: Deep learning methods for pathology are constrained by the limited availability and high cost of large-scale pixel-level annotations. Synthetic data can alleviate this limitation, but existing pipelines often lack reliable quality control and remain weakly connected to downstream analysis. To address these issues, we propose a three-stage framework that integrates automated data screening, generative representation learning, and efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. First, we develop a pathology agent system to assess image quality and filter samples, resulting in TCGA-200K, a dataset containing 217,030 pathology patches selected from the public TCGA repository. Second, we train a generative model on the screened data. In addition to image synthesis, its backbone learns structural and semantic representations that can be transferred to downstream tasks. Third, we adapt the pretrained generative backbone to semantic segmentation using a lightweight task-specific head. We further introduce PathSeg-380K, a semantic segmentation dataset with expert annotations for approximately 380,000 cells across 12 cell types. Experiments on multiple pathology datasets show that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/script-Yang/Agent-GenPath.

</details>


### [108] [Sekai: Incremental Event Memory for Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06685)
*Zhengtao Liu,Jinrong Xu,Shiu-Hong Kao,Huixin Sun,Yicong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出广义推理视频分割任务GRVS（目标集合随时间演化），构建Isekai基准，并提出Sekai框架（EMR+MGD），以50.33 J&F超越最强基线3.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线推理视频分割（Online RVS）假设视频全程目标集合固定，忽略了流式推理的本质特性：随着新事件出现，满足查询的对象可能随时间变化（从无到单个或多个）。

Method: 提出GRVS任务，目标集合依赖已观测历史中累积的查询相关事件；构建Isekai基准（60视频、197查询、11种时序查询族、12569个评分点，目标集随时间因果标注）；提出Sekai在线框架：Event Memory Reasoner（EMR）在结构化记忆中维护查询相关事件与演化目标状态，Memory-Grounded Decoder（MGD）从记忆中识别有效目标并在当前帧定位。

Result: Sekai显著优于现有在线RVS方法，达到50.33 J&F，较最强基线提升3.2点。

Conclusion: 将在线RVS从固定目标范式推广到时序演化目标范式，所提基准与框架验证了持续维护目标有效性的必要性，为流式推理分割提供新方向。

Abstract: Online reasoning video segmentation (Online RVS) requires a model to segment query-referred objects as a video streams in, using only the frames observed so far. Existing formulations largely assume a fixed target set throughout the video, overlooking a fundamental property of streaming reasoning: as new events occur, the objects that satisfy a query may change over time. To address this, we propose a new task, Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation (GRVS), where the target set depends on query-relevant events accumulated over the observed video history. As new evidence accumulates, the target set can evolve from empty to one or multiple objects, requiring a model to continuously maintain target validity while localizing the valid objects currently in view. To evaluate GRVS, we build Isekai, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 videos and 197 queries across 11 temporal query families, with 12,569 scoring points whose target sets are causally annotated over time. We further propose Sekai, an online framework with two complementary components: an Event Memory Reasoner (EMR) that maintains query-relevant events and evolving target states in structured memory, and a Memory-Grounded Decoder (MGD) that identifies valid targets from this memory and localizes them in the current frame. Experiments show that Sekai significantly outperforms existing online RVS methods, achieving 50.33 J&F and improving over the strongest baseline by 3.2 points. Code, example annotations, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/zenastangbi/Sekai.

</details>


### [109] [DreamRealm: Exploring and Materializing Hallucination Worlds for Trustworthy Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06433)
*Benlei Cui,Chunyang Chai,Yiliang Zhang,Xiaoqian Xia,Chengwen Yao,Song Li,Longtao Huang,Yuwen Zhai,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DreamRealm后训练框架：通过挖掘目标模型自身的（潜在）幻觉响应并对其原图做最小化编辑使其"成立"，再用图像—响应匹配任务（RealmMatch）训练模型，从而将监督信号对齐到模型真实的感知混淆，提升细粒度视觉感知并缓解幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在依赖细微视觉细节时易产生视觉幻觉。现有两类方法各有局限：(1)基于图像检索/生成/编辑构造视觉对比，但对比不围绕模型自身幻觉，难以保证监督命中其真实感知混淆；(2)在固定图像条件下通过响应纠正或偏好学习抑制幻觉，但图像输入侧的监督信号设计不足。受人类纠正误解方式启发，需以模型自身幻觉为中心构造图像侧监督。

Method: 1) 数据构建管线RealmForgePipe：从目标模型中挖掘已观察到的与潜在的幻觉，并通过图像编辑将其"实体化"（对原图做最小编辑使幻觉响应变为有效），构建Realized Hallucination Dataset (RealmSet)（基于Qwen3.5-9B挖掘）。2) 训练目标RealmMatch：在图像—响应对上构建似然得分矩阵，结合监督学习与双向对比损失，强化正确匹配、抑制错配。

Result: 相较原始Qwen3.5-9B，在六个视觉感知与幻觉基准上均提升：HallusionBench准确率+7.26个百分点，MMVP成对准确率+11.34个百分点；并通过数据构建与训练方法的消融实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 以模型自身幻觉为核心、通过最小图像编辑构造"幻觉—图像"匹配监督的"幻觉归因（hallucination assignment）"范式，是提升视觉感知与缓解幻觉的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain prone to visual hallucinations when correct responses depend on subtle visual details. One line of post-training methods constructs visual contrasts through image retrieval, generation, or editing to improve fine-grained perception. However, these contrasts are not built around the model's own hallucinations, making it difficult to ensure that supervision targets its actual perceptual confusions. Another line suppresses hallucinated outputs through response correction or preference learning under fixed image conditions, but leaves the design of supervision signals on the image-input side underexplored. Inspired by how humans correct misconceptions, we introduce DreamRealm, a post-training framework that explores diverse latent hallucinated responses, minimally edits original images to make them valid, and trains the model to assign each response to its supporting image. Our data construction pipeline, RealmForgePipe, mines observed and latent hallucinations from a target model and materializes them through image editing. Using hallucinations mined from Qwen3.5-9B, we construct the Realized Hallucination Dataset (RealmSet). Our training objective, RealmMatch, constructs a likelihood score matrix across image--response combinations and combines supervised learning with bidirectional contrastive losses to reinforce correct matches and suppress mismatches. Compared with the original Qwen3.5-9B model, DreamRealm improves performance across six visual perception and hallucination benchmarks, including gains of 7.26 percentage points in HallusionBench accuracy and 11.34 percentage points in MMVP pair accuracy. Together with ablation studies on data construction and training methods, these results demonstrate that hallucination assignment is a promising paradigm for improving visual perception and mitigating hallucinations.

</details>


### [110] [Entropogram-guided Visual Realignment Decoding: Capturing the Rhythm of Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06415)
*Jiaqi Deng,Zhe Fu,Yakun Chen,Dichang Zhang,Zonghan Wu,Xiaoshui Huang,Huan Huo,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文从推理生成动力学视角重新审视MLLM幻觉，发现两种与幻觉相关的特征熵模式，并提出熵感知自反思框架，在解码中监测熵动态并在可疑幻觉状态触发视觉重对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在跨模态推理上进步显著，但在长链推理中仍存在幻觉。作者认为熵不仅是静态不确定性度量，而是反映解码过程中模态信息如何整合的结构化信号，据此重新刻画幻觉的生成机制。

Method: 系统分析识别两类熵模式：（1）整体高置信度区间对应过早收敛到不当的多模态交互；（2）局部熵突变对应推理不稳定与幻觉发生。基于此提出熵感知自反思框架：解码时持续追踪熵动态，检测到可疑幻觉状态时执行针对性视觉重对齐，促使模型在关键推理阶段重新审视显著视觉证据。

Result: 在六个多模态推理基准、九种评估设置的广泛实验中，方法在推理准确性与幻觉鲁棒性上均取得稳健提升，并为MLLM失败的生成动力学提供新见解。

Conclusion: 熵动态可作为幻觉的早期可观测信号；解码期监测+条件性视觉重对齐是提升MLLM推理可靠性的有效且通用策略，并为后续推理动力学研究提供新视角。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress on cross-modal reasoning tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations in long-chain reasoning processes. In this work, we revisit hallucination in MLLMs from the perspective of reasoning generation dynamics. We argue that entropy is not merely a static measure of uncertainty but a structured signal that reflects how modality information is integrated throughout decoding. Through systematic analysis, we identify two characteristic entropy patterns associated with hallucination behaviors: (1) a generally high-confidence regime corresponds to premature convergence to inappropriate multimodal interactions; and (2) local abrupt entropy transitions are associated with reasoning instability and hallucination onset. Building on these observations, we propose an entropy-aware self-introspective framework for MLLM reasoning. Our method continuously tracks entropy dynamics during decoding and performs targeted visual re-alignment when suspicious hallucination states are detected, encouraging the model to re-examine salient visual evidence at critical reasoning stages. Extensive experiments across six multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning nine distinct evaluation settings demonstrate that our approach delivers robust improvements in reasoning accuracy and hallucination robustness, while providing new insights into the generation dynamics underlying MLLM failures. The code is available at: https://github.com/jden4524/SelfRetrospectiveDecoding.

</details>


### [111] [FlashRelight: Portrait Video Relighting with Dynamic Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06355)
*Jiahui Sheng,Le Li,Limin Lin,Jiahao Li,Wansen Feng,Hong Gu,Shuhan Chen,Xiaorun Li,Rui Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlashRelight两阶段框架，先"去光照"再在光照中性视频上合成动态目标光照，支持envmap序列、光照文本、光照参考视频三种控制模态，并用静态OLAT数据与生成的动态光照视频做低成本混合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态光照下的人像视频重打光需同时满足照片级真实感与时域稳定性；光照方向、强度、颜色随时间变化使问题显著变难，现有方法难以在真实运动下实现可控且连贯的动态重打光。

Method: 两阶段分解式框架：阶段一去光照（delighting）抑制源光照，得到光照中性人像视频；阶段二在该中性视频上合成目标动态光照。提供三种互补控制模态：显式逐帧控制的envmap序列、语义编辑的动态光照文本、视觉迁移的光照参考视频（支持跨视频迁移）。训练采用低成本混合策略，结合静态OLAT（one-light-at-a-time）数据与生成的动态光照人像视频，兼顾受控光照变化与真实人像运动。

Result: 实验表明FlashRelight实现时域连贯、照片级真实的动态重打光，并具备灵活的跨视频光照迁移能力；代码已开源（github.com/vivoCameraResearch/FlashRelight）。

Conclusion: 将光照解耦的"去光照—再光照"两阶段设计配合多模态控制与混合数据训练，是处理动态人像视频重打光的有效方案，可在保证时域稳定的同时提供多粒度光照编辑接口。

Abstract: Portrait video relighting aims to modify the illumination of a portrait video while preserving photorealism and temporal stability. The task becomes particularly challenging under dynamic lighting, where illumination direction, intensity, and color vary over time. To tackle this problem, we introduce FlashRelight, a novel two-stage framework for dynamic portrait video relighting. FlashRelight first suppresses the source illumination through delighting, and then synthesizes the target dynamic lighting on an illumination-neutral portrait video. FlashRelight supports three complementary control modalities: envmap sequences for explicit frame-level lighting control, dynamic lighting text for semantic illumination editing, and lighting reference videos for visual lighting transfer. Training follows a low-cost hybrid strategy that combines static one-light-at-a-time (OLAT) data with generated dynamic-lighting portrait videos, covering both controlled illumination variation and realistic portrait motion. Extensive experiments show that FlashRelight achieves temporally coherent and photorealistic dynamic relighting while enabling flexible cross-video lighting transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/vivoCameraResearch/FlashRelight

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Scene Shortcuts: Action-Centric Video-Text Alignment for Fine-Grained Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06302)
*Zhiliang Zhu,Jiazheng Huang,Dawei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STARR框架抑制"环境锚定的动作幻觉"(EGAH)，构建FineActionR&R数据，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B得到FineActionVLM-R1，MotionBench平均提升3.01个百分点，HAU-MCQ-Bench达91.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作理解要求视觉语言模型在时空证据中定位"做什么、怎么做、为何做"。但更丰富的监督反而可能适得其反：微调后模型倾向基于静态场景与显著物体而非运动生成详细动作描述，即环境锚定的动作幻觉(EGAH)。

Method: STARR（Staged Attention Rectification and Reinforcement）：以动作为中心的视频-文本对齐框架，通过Action-Environment Dissociation切断场景-动作捷径，并在GRPO中引入Attention Consistency Reward强化动作聚焦的grounding；同时构建FineActionR&R数据集（时序定位字幕＋分层与时序因果推理数据）。

Result: 基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B的FineActionVLM-R1在MotionBench平均准确率较基座提升3.01个百分点，HAU-MCQ-Bench达91.50%；定性比较显示细粒度动作描述更忠实、环境诱导幻觉更少。

Conclusion: 显式解耦动作与环境并施加注意力一致性约束，可有效缓解EGAH，验证了以动作中心的对齐与强化策略在细粒度动作理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-grained action understanding requires vision-language models to ground what an agent does, how it acts, and why it acts in spatio-temporal evidence. However, richer supervision can backfire: fine-tuned models may generate detailed action descriptions grounded in static scenes and salient objects rather than motion, a failure we call Environment-Grounded Action Hallucination (EGAH). To mitigate EGAH, we propose Staged Attention Rectification and Reinforcement (STARR), an action-centric video-text alignment framework that breaks scene-action shortcuts through Action-Environment Dissociation and reinforces action-focused grounding with an Attention Consistency Reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. We further construct FineActionR&R, combining temporally localized captions with hierarchical and temporal-causal reasoning data. Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, FineActionVLM-R1 improves average MotionBench accuracy by 3.01 percentage points over its base model and achieves 91.50% on HAU-MCQ-Bench. Qualitative comparisons further indicate more faithful fine-grained action descriptions with fewer environment-induced hallucinations.

</details>


### [113] [STARE: Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06089)
*Kento Tokura,Ryosuke Korekata,Daichi Yashima,Kei Katsumata,Kanon Amemiya,Miyu Goko,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STARE，一种融合视觉特征与场景文本的多模态检索方法，用于服务机器人依据自由形式指令检索并搬运日常物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需根据可能涉及视觉特征与场景文本的自由形式指令检索和搬运日常物体；难点在于需同时理解物体的视觉特征、空间关系以及环境中嵌入的场景文本，实现多模态联合理解。

Method: STARE 利用视觉与文本线索响应自由形式指令，引入 Crosslingual Visual Prompt 增强场景文本解读，并对指令中命名实体与对应物体之间的复杂关系进行建模；在两个新基准 GoGetIt 与 TextCaps-test（含真实室内/室外图像及涉及物体操作与导航的指令）上评估。

Result: 在标准多模态检索指标上优于所有基线方法；在零样本迁移设置下的物理实验中取得 80% 的总体成功率。

Conclusion: 融合场景文本与视觉线索的多模态检索可有效支撑基于自由形式指令的机器人物体检索与操作，并在真实零样本部署中具备可行性。

Abstract: In this study, we focus on service robots that retrieve and transport everyday objects based on free-form instructions that may refer to visual features and scene text. This is challenging because it requires a multimodal understanding of the visual features and spatial relationships of objects, as well as scene text embedded in the environment. To address this, we propose Scene Text-Aware multimodal Retrieval for Everyday objects (STARE), which leverages visual and textual cues in response to free-form instructions. STARE introduces Crosslingual Visual Prompt to enhance scene text interpretation, and models the complex relationships between named entities in the instructions and their corresponding objects. We evaluate STARE using two novel benchmarks, GoGetIt and TextCaps-test, both of which consist of real-world indoor/outdoor images and instructions involving object manipulation and navigation. The results show that STARE outperforms all baseline methods on standard multimodal retrieval metrics. Furthermore, in physical experiments conducted under a zero-shot transfer setting, STARE achieves an overall success rate of 80%. Our project page and code are provided in the Supplementary Materials.

</details>


### [114] [From Tokens to Trajectories: Parameter-Efficient Continuous Adaptation of Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05451)
*Gayatri Deshmukh,Onkar Susladkar,Adheesh Juvekar,Halil Kilicoglu,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowShift 是一种参数高效的后训练框架，将预训练的离散式统一多模态模型（UMM）转化为连续 rectified-flow 生成器，通过连续隐空间投影替换离散视觉接口、对注意力层施加 LoRA，并用双目标速度向量蒸馏（DOVD）对齐高保真连续教师与真值连续轨迹，在显著提升生成画质的同时保留语义理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散 token 化虽赋予统一多模态模型强语义推理能力，但引入严格的量化瓶颈，损失细粒度视觉细节，导致生成图像结构正确却伪影明显。作者希望在不下滑语义先验与理解能力的前提下突破该瓶颈。

Method: 提出 FlowShift 后训练框架：将离散视觉接口替换为连续隐空间投影；对注意力层采用 LoRA 进行参数高效微调；提出双目标速度向量蒸馏（DOVD），同时对齐高保真连续教师模型与真实数据（ground-truth）的连续轨迹，以抑制语义漂移。

Result: 在主流离散 UMM 上应用后，COCO-30K FID 与美学评分显著提升，同时保留超过 99.5% 的多模态理解能力；消融实验表明 DOVD 是生成质量提升的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: FlowShift 在保持预训练语义基础与提示词遵循、推理能力的同时，实现了高保真连续生成，验证了"离散语义骨干 + 连续生成头"融合路线的有效性。

Abstract: While discrete tokenization in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) establishes strong semantic reasoning, it introduces a strict quantization bottleneck that degrades fine-grained visual details, often resulting in structurally accurate but artifact-heavy images. We introduce FlowShift, a parameter-efficient post-training framework that bypasses this bottleneck by converting pre-trained discrete UMMs into continuous rectified-flow generators. Instead of discarding the backbone's semantic priors, FlowShift replaces discrete visual interfaces with continuous latent projections and applies LoRA to the attention layers. To efficiently boost visual quality without semantic drift, we propose Dual-Objective Velocity Vector Distillation (DOVD), which aligns the model with a high-fidelity continuous teacher and ground-truth continuous trajectories. This approach drastically improves perceptual quality while preserving prompt adherence and reasoning. When applied to leading discrete UMMs, FlowShift significantly improves COCO-30K FID and aesthetic scores, while retaining over 99.5% of multimodal understanding capabilities. Ablations confirm DOVD drives generation quality. Ultimately, FlowShift achieves high-fidelity continuous generation without sacrificing pre-trained semantic grounding.

</details>


### [115] [Visual Forensic Detection of Fabricated Bangla News Photocards using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05403)
*Anindya Kundu,Kabya Mithun Saha,Md. Muhaiminul Islam,Fayek Ahmed,Aminul Kader Bulbul,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究孟加拉语"photocard"图文假新闻检测，将其重构为视觉图像伪造检测任务，构建969张真实与1,077张伪造卡片数据集，经16种变换扩增到32,736样本；用全批量归一化四块CNN（约49万参数）在无泄漏分组五折交叉验证下达到87.45%±0.53%准确率，ImageNet预训练YOLOv8n-cls达97.36%±0.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国的本地化虚假信息以photocard（模仿知名新闻机构品牌、以单张图片呈现新闻摘要的卡片）形式传播，误导信息存在于像素而非可读文本中，基于文本的核查工具无法识别，因此需要从视觉伪造检测角度切入。

Method: 将photocard假新闻检测重构为图像伪造检测；从公开Facebook页面采集真实卡片（17家国家级电视广播机构认证页面）与伪造卡片（仿冒广播机构页面、匿名账号、公开群组），构建969真+1,077假数据集；每图施加16种光度与形态学变换扩增至32,736样本；对比全连接ANN、全批量归一化四块CNN（489,954参数）与ImageNet预训练YOLOv8n-cls（1.44M参数），采用无泄漏分层分组五折交叉验证（同一卡片的所有增强版本归入同一折）。

Result: ANN仅约52%准确率、损失停滞于ln(2)≈0.693，证明展平图像会破坏伪造检测所需的空间线索；紧凑CNN从头训练达87.45%±0.53%，分类头由ANN热启动达88.75%±1.55%；YOLOv8n-cls微调达97.36%±0.42%，显示紧凑模型与预训练骨干间的性能上限空间。另报告各类别precision/recall/F1与汇总混淆矩阵，诊断并修复了未归一化分类头导致的单类坍缩问题。

Conclusion: 视觉空间线索对photocard伪造检测至关重要，全连接结构不适用；紧凑CNN虽远小于预训练模型但存在明显欠拟合（偏差-方差诊断证实），预训练迁移可显著提升性能，为低资源语言的图像型假新闻检测提供了基线、评估协议与误差分析。

Abstract: In Bangladesh, much localized misinformation spreads as photocards: single-image news summaries that imitate the branding of well-known news organizations. Because the misleading message lives in the pixels rather than in machine-readable text, text-based fact-checking tools cannot see it. We recast photocard fake-news detection as visual image forgery detection. We build a dataset of 969 authentic and 1,077 fabricated Bangla photocards scraped from public Facebook pages: authentic cards from the verified pages of seventeen national television broadcasters, fabricated ones from pseudo-broadcaster pages, anonymous profiles, and public groups. Sixteen photometric and morphological transformations per image grow the corpus to 32,736 samples. A fully connected ANN reaches only about 52% accuracy, its loss stuck at ln(2) ~ 0.693, showing that flattening the image destroys the spatial cues forgery detection needs. A compact, fully batch-normalized four-block CNN with 489,954 parameters, about 1,644x smaller than the ANN, is evaluated with leakage-free stratified group five-fold cross-validation, which keeps every augmented version of a card in one fold. Under this protocol the CNN reaches 87.45% +/- 0.53% pooled accuracy from scratch and 88.75% +/- 1.55% when its classifier head is warm-started from the ANN. An ImageNet-pretrained YOLOv8n-cls backbone (1.44M parameters) fine-tuned on the same folds reaches 97.36% +/- 0.42%, marking the headroom above the compact model. We report per-class precision, recall, F1, and pooled confusion matrices, diagnose and fix a one-class collapse caused by an unnormalized classifier head, and give a manual error analysis and bias-variance diagnosis showing that the compact CNNs under-fit.

</details>


### [116] [Decompose the Distillation: Interpretable Single-Pass Guidance for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05755)
*Wuyang Zhang,Xiaolin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CtrlFlow 是一种单次前向即可实现可调分类器无关引导（CFG）的方法：通过两个轻量 LoRA 适配器在一次冻结骨干前向中同时输出基础流与引导方向，配合多尺度蒸馏损失及推理期调制，保持 CFG 的可分离接口与单次成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 CFG 每个采样步需条件与无条件两次前向（N 步需 2N 次），成本高；现有单次替代方案多将 CFG 两个分量坍缩为不透明的引导预测，丧失可分离的基础流与引导方向，难以支持逐步控制与诊断，即使引导尺度仍可调。

Method: 在冻结骨干上挂接两个轻量 LoRA 适配器（各增约 2.5% 参数），由共享冻结骨干的一次前向预测基础流与引导方向；引导尺度在forward后仅乘于引导方向，故单次评估可给出任意尺度下的引导速度，单一检查点推理时可调。训练采用统一多尺度蒸馏损失，使训练误差与推理时按尺度放大的复合误差对齐，而非等权分量损失。针对极端尺度下残差引导误差被放大的问题，引入尺度自适应调制抑制速度场误差，并采用时间网格warping将求解器评估集中于轨迹变化最快处。

Result: 摘要未提供具体量化指标；方法层面实现单次前向等价于 2N→N 的计算节省，同时保留可调尺度与可分离的 base flow/引导方向输出，并通过尺度自适应调制与时间网格warping降低极端尺度下的推理误差。

Conclusion: CtrlFlow 在单次前向成本下恢复了 CFG 的可分离引导接口，兼顾效率、推理期尺度可调性与诊断能力，并通过训练-推理误差对齐及推理期优化缓解高尺度误差放大问题。

Abstract: Classifier-Free Guidance is a widely used inference-time guidance rule for conditional image generation with diffusion models, which exposes a guidance scale that adjusts conditioning strength after training. This flexibility requires conditional and unconditional model evaluations at every sampling step, so an N-step trajectory takes 2N forward passes. Single-pass alternatives reduce such cost, yet most collapse CFG's two components into one opaque guided prediction, losing the separable base flow and guidance direction that support per-step control and diagnostics, even when the scale remains adjustable. This work introduces CtrlFlow, a single-pass guidance method that preserves CFG's separable base-flow and guidance-direction interface. To preserve both components at one-pass cost, CtrlFlow attaches two lightweight LoRA adapters to a frozen backbone, each adding 2.5% parameters, and predicts the base flow and guidance direction from one pass of the shared frozen backbone. Because the guidance scale multiplies the guidance direction only after the forward pass, one evaluation yields the guided velocity at any scale, so a single trained checkpoint remains adjustable at inference without retraining. Preserving two outputs introduces a training mismatch: inference amplifies guidance-direction errors by the chosen scale, while naive component-wise losses weight both outputs equally. To align training with this composed inference-time error, CtrlFlow uses a unified multi-scale distillation loss. Residual guidance errors can still persist at extreme scales, where even small guidance-direction errors are amplified by the guidance scale. To reduce this inference-time error, CtrlFlow applies scale-adaptive modulation to damp velocity-field errors and time-grid warping to concentrate solver evaluations where the trajectory changes most rapidly.

</details>


### [117] [Mitigating Spurious Invariance in Contrastive Learning: A Probabilistic Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05734)
*Wei Wei,Yading Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像中对比学习的不变性可能抹除细微关键特征的问题，将特征嵌入编码为概率分布而非固定向量，并提出统一概率框架整合多种自监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像人工标注成本高昂，自监督学习（SSL）因此备受关注，其中对比学习为主流方法，通过鼓励模型在不同增强视图间编码不变语义信息来学习表征。但医学图像中细微而关键的医学特征可能在增强过程中被抑制，导致这种不变性成为"虚假不变性"（spurious invariance），从而损害学到的表征质量。

Method: 不再使用固定向量表示特征嵌入，而是将其编码为概率分布，从而捕获更丰富、更细致的信息谱；并在此基础上提出一个统一的概率框架，将多种SSL方法无缝整合。

Result: 在多个数据集的多项下游任务（包括医学图像分割与分类）上优于现有最先进SSL方法；并通过额外分析验证了对更强数据增强的鲁棒性、有限微调标签下的标签效率以及计算开销权衡。

Conclusion: 以概率分布建模特征嵌入可有效缓解医学图像对比学习中的虚假不变性问题，且统一的概率框架具备良好的通用性与实用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Owing to the prohibitive cost of manual annotation for enormous medical images, self-supervised learning (SSL) has gained substantial attention and shown promise in various medical imaging tasks. Among SSL approaches, contrastive learning has emerged as a prominent one, encouraging models to encode semantic information that remains invariant between different augmented views. However, this invariance can be spurious for medical images, as subtle but critical medical features can be suppressed during augmentations. In this paper, we mitigate this issue by encoding the learned feature embeddings as probability distributions rather than fixed vectors, thus capturing a richer and more nuanced spectrum of information. Furthermore, we propose a unified probabilistic framework that seamlessly integrates multiple SSL approaches. Our method achieves superior performance over the state-of-the-art SSL methods on various downstream tasks across multiple datasets, including medical image segmentation and classification. We further validate the method through additional analyses of robustness to stronger augmentations, label efficiency under limited fine-tuning labels, and computational trade-offs. The code is released at https://github.com/wwzjer/ProbSSL.

</details>


### [118] [CoDecide: Content-Adaptive Video Decoding for High-Throughput Video Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06455)
*Shristy Shah,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDecide：利用压缩码流中的运动信号与无标签新颖性蒸馏，仅解码"出现新内容"的帧与区域，跳过帧用运动矢量传播检测结果，实现高效视频分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当检测器仅对少量选定帧运行时，解码成为视频分析流水线的主要瓶颈；而仅凭运动信号无法区分"新出现的内容"与"一直存在的运动"，导致选帧选择效率低、且现有压缩域选择器通常需针对每个相机单独训练。

Method: 用冻结DINOv2教师提供的无标签新颖性信号（响应对语义上新出现的区域而非任意运动）监督一个轻量压缩域学生模型；推理时由学生模型决定解码哪些帧、重建哪些区域；对解码帧的检测结果利用运动矢量传播到被跳过的帧；在VIRAT、MEVA、ShanghaiTech上采用leave-one-dataset-out协议评估。

Result: 在同等解码预算下，CoDecide恢复的运动目标检测数超过已有压缩域选择器，甚至优于直接在目标相机上训练的对手；相比逐帧解码+检测的流水线快15–24倍。

Conclusion: 利用压缩域信号+语义新颖性蒸馏的可回溯式选帧—解码策略能在显著降低解码开销的同时保持高检测召回，且无需逐相机重训练，具备良好的跨部署泛化性。

Abstract: Analytics on video archives are bottlenecked by decoding. In a dense pipeline that runs a detector on every frame, decoding is a small fraction of the runtime, but once the detector is restricted to selected frames, decoding takes over as the main cost. We introduce CoDecide, a retrospective analytics system that only decodes the frames where new content appears, using signals from the compressed bitstream to find them. Motion tells us something is moving, but not whether it is new to the scene or has been there the whole time. To make that distinction, we supervise a lightweight compressed-domain student on a label-free novelty signal from a frozen DINOv2 teacher, which fires on semantically new regions rather than on any motion. Consequently, the student model generalizes to new camera deployments without requiring per-camera retraining. At inference, it decides which frames to decode and which regions to reconstruct. Detections from the decoded frames are propagated to the skipped frames using motion vectors. We evaluate CoDecide with a leave-one-dataset-out protocol on VIRAT, MEVA, and ShanghaiTech. Against a dense per-frame detector, CoDecide recovers more moving-object detections than prior compressed-domain selectors at an equivalent decoding budget, outperforming even a competitor trained directly on the target camera. CoDecide runs 15 to 24x faster than a pipeline that decodes and detects every frame. Code is available at https://github.com/shristy51/CoDecide.

</details>


### [119] [Procedural Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06890)
*Jack Nugent,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出程序化对抗学习（PAL）框架及其算法 Simple-PAT 与基准 PAL-Dojo，通过在程序化生成器的控制参数空间中进行零阶优化，按需搜索困难样本用于对抗训练与评估，显著降低最坏情况误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在计算机视觉任务中平均误差低，但在看似简单的场景仍会失败；而这些失败样本未必出现在固定数据集中，因此需要主动“找到”它们，并且这些失败点可能不在任何固定数据集里。

Method: 定义 PAL 为极小极大训练加对抗评估的框架，用可解释的程序化生成器把低维控制参数（布局、光照、姿态等）映射为渲染图像及标签，在参数空间中搜索困难实例。提出 Simple-PAT，利用零阶优化在生成器参数空间内提出按需生成的对抗训练样本；并构建 PAL-Dojo，包含面向表面法线估计、图像分割与人体姿态估计的高效程序化生成器集合。

Result: 在 5000 万次聚合查询的跨模型黑盒攻击下，Simple-PAT 将最坏情况误差相比标准训练降低 3.15 倍（几何平均），相比最强鲁棒训练基线降低 2.63 倍。

Conclusion: 在程序化生成器的低维参数空间中进行按需对抗样本搜索，是一种有效发现并缓解模型系统性失败的手段，优于标准训练与现有鲁棒训练基线。

Abstract: For many computer vision tasks, neural networks can be trained to produce a low average error, yet they still fail on seemingly simple scenes. Reducing these failures requires first finding them, but they may not be in a fixed dataset. We study procedural adversarial learning (PAL), a framework of minimax training and adversarial evaluation which searches the input space of a procedural generator for difficult instances. The procedural generator is an interpretable program mapping a low-dimensional vector of control parameters (e.g. layout, lighting, pose) to a rendered image and its label. We introduce the Simple-PAT algorithm, which uses zeroth-order optimization in the procedural generator's parameter space to propose adversarial training samples that are generated on demand. We also create PAL-Dojo, a collection of efficient procedural generators for surface normal estimation, image segmentation, and human pose estimation. Against a cross-model black-box adversary with 50M aggregate queries, Simple-PAT reduces worst-case error by a geometric mean of 3.15x compared to standard training and 2.63x compared to the strongest robust training baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/princeton-vl/procedural-adversarial-learning.

</details>


### [120] [PixelSpline: Continuous Space-Time Video Super-Resolution via Endpoint-Anchored Shared Pixel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06870)
*Wanqi Yuan,Nianyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续时空视频超分辨率框架，结合长上下文编码、连续高分辨率特征提升与端点锚定的PixelSpline轨迹建模，提升质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时空视频超分辨率方法仅从两个端点帧推断运动并独立建模双向运动，易受对应关系歧义与轨迹不一致影响。

Method: 三点设计：(1) 长上下文编码，利用长程观测获得更可靠的端点表示；(2) 连续高分辨率特征提升，直接在目标HR网格上构建细节外观特征；(3) 端点锚定PixelSpline，将每个像素运动显式建模为两端点共享的单条连续轨迹；据此将端点颜色扭曲为粗略中间帧并对齐HR特征做残差重建。

Result: 在多种空间与时间上采样设置下实验，所提框架在质量与效率之间取得有利权衡。

Conclusion: 以单一连续轨迹统一双向运动建模并结合长上下文与连续HR特征提升，可有效缓解轨迹不一致问题，实现质量-效率兼顾的连续STVSR。

Abstract: Existing continuous space-time video super-resolution methods often infer motion from only two endpoint frames and model the two motion directions independently, making them vulnerable to ambiguous correspondence and inconsistent trajectories. We introduce a continuous STVSR framework that combines long-context encoding, continuous HR feature lifting, and an endpoint-anchored PixelSpline. Long-range observations provide more reliable endpoint representations, while continuous upsampling constructs detailed appearance features directly on the requested HR grid. PixelSpline explicitly models each pixel's motion as a single continuous trajectory shared by both endpoints. Using this trajectory, our method warps endpoint colors into a coarse intermediate frame and aligns the HR features for residual reconstruction. Experiments across spatial and temporal upsampling settings demonstrate that the proposed framework offers a favorable quality--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [121] [CLEAR: Color-Texture Latent Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05969)
*Tu Vo,Sheir A. Zaheer,Chan Y. Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架：以预训练latent diffusion为自然图像先验，单步确定性前向预测传输/反射/残差层latent，并用HFAM对含污染的高频skip特征做颜色-纹理对齐，兼顾保真与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在复原方法借助预训练生成先验，但常把退化输入的高频特征经skip回灌解码器。该策略适用于模糊、噪声、雾霾等单层退化；而SIRR中输入含第二层结构化反射，直接复用skip会在锐化同时把反射边缘带回。此外latent压缩会引入色偏与细节丢失。

Method: CLEAR由两部分组成：(1) 物理引导分离器，围绕传输、反射与残差的形成模型组织预测，一次前向输出各层粗latent；(2) 混合特征对齐模块HFAM，将输入派生的skip视为受污染的层混合特征，以干净传输对齐的颜色query（由输入特征自身学习、在GT特征监督下获得）对其高频内容进行矫正，保留与传输一致的纹理并抑制反射色细节。整体为确定性、单步、非迭代的latent框架。

Result: 在6个真实世界基准上，保真度与感知指标的总体平均最优；在每个数据集上LPIPS均最低，且在最具挑战性的基准上增益最大。

Conclusion: 将输入skip视为受反射污染的层混合特征并进行传输对齐的颜色-纹理矫正，是SIRR中复用输入高频信息的有效方式；单步非迭代latent框架可在不牺牲细节的前提下避免反射回流。

Abstract: Single Image Reflection Removal (SIRR) seeks to recover the transmitted scene from a photograph captured through glass. Recent latent restoration methods use pretrained generative priors, but many recover detail by feeding high-frequency features from the degraded input back into the decoder. This works for degradations such as blur, noise, or haze, where input detail mostly belongs to the scene. It is less suitable for SIRR, where reflections add a second structured layer: directly reusing the input's skip connections can sharpen the image while also bringing reflection edges back. Latent compression also introduces color drift and fine-detail loss.
  We propose CLEAR (Color-Texture LatEnt Alignment for Single Image Reflection Removal), a deterministic, single-timestep, non-iterative latent framework that uses a pretrained latent diffusion backbone as a natural-image prior. A physics-guided separator organizes the prediction around the transmission, reflection, and residue formation model and outputs coarse layer latents in one forward pass. The Hybrid Feature Alignment Module (HFAM) then treats input-derived skips as contaminated layer-mixture features. It rectifies their high-frequency content with a clean-transmission-aligned color query, learned from the input features themselves under ground-truth feature supervision, keeping transmission-consistent texture while attenuating reflection-colored detail. CLEAR achieves the best average across fidelity and perceptual metrics on six real-world benchmarks and the lowest LPIPS on every dataset, with the largest gains on the hardest benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [GeoDIVE: Geospatial Descent Informed by Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05697)
*Hamza Iqbal,Dan Cher,Eric Xing,Pranavi Kolouju,Abby Stylianou,Robert Pless,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoDIVE，将多分辨率卫星图像库视为森林并用最佳优先遍历决定编码哪些瓦片，轻量模型预测子树内最优查询相似度的乐观上界，实现10%编码成本下匹配穷举搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业、基础设施规划、环境监测与灾害响应需要在大范围、跨尺度空间上检索特定目标与空间模式；直接将全部数据编码、维护并朴素搜索代价过高。

Method: 将多分辨率卫星图像库组织为树/森林结构，采用最佳优先遍历（best-first traversal）选择待编码瓦片；在每个访问节点上用查询表示与子树表示，由轻量模型预测其后代中最优查询相似度的乐观估计，以此引导搜索。

Result: 在图像查询与开放词汇文本查询评测中，GeoDIVE在仅10%编码成本下达到与穷举搜索相当的效果；在两种模态的各预算档位上均优于所有测试基线。同时发布基于Git-10M的物体级图像与文本查询集，由Microsoft建筑轮廓、OpenStreetMap多边形及VLM生成描述构建。

Conclusion: GeoDIVE通过乐观相似度估计驱动的最优优先遍历，有效降低了大规模多尺度地理空间检索的编码成本，并提供了新的多尺度地理搜索评测基准。

Abstract: Finding specific objects, structures, and spatial patterns supports critical work in agriculture, infrastructure planning, environmental monitoring, and disaster response. These features, however, are often dispersed across very large regions and span dramatically different spatial scales, which defines a search space that is costly to embed, maintain, and search naively. We introduce GeoDIVE, a model that treats a multiresolution satellite image gallery as a forest and uses best-first traversal to determine which tiles to encode. At each visited node, a lightweight model then predicts an optimistic estimate of the best query similarity hidden among its descendants using the query and subtree representations. In evaluations with image and open-vocabulary text queries, GeoDIVE matches exhaustive search at 10% of the encoding cost for image queries and leads all tested baselines at every budget in both modalities. To support future evaluation of multiscale geospatial search, we also release object-level image and text queries for Git-10M, constructed from Microsoft building footprints and OpenStreetMap polygons with VLM-generated captions. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [123] [Anchored Slice Propagation with Boundary Transport for 3D CT Segmentation from Three Orthogonal Annotated Slices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05693)
*Tushar Kataria,Md Rakibul Haque,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文改进Sli2Vol的切片间二值掩膜传播：利用三条正交标注链交叉处的已知标签对传播结果进行"锚定"校正，并以此评估各链可信度用于融合；同时比较两种边界几何传输方式（截断SDF与可学习轮廓跟踪）。在403例CT、17个腹部结构、2091个结构实例上，锚定使平均Dice由0.737升至0.769并提升所有结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sli2Vol通过相邻切片间的亲和度传播二值掩膜，但每步预测都成为下一步输入，误差会累积漂移；虽然三条正交标注减少了单一链路的依赖，但仍存在两类信息未充分利用：(1) 链与其他已标注平面相交处的真实标签可用于纠偏与融合加权；(2) 二值掩膜丢失了到边界的距离信息，难以跟踪切片间渐变的形状。因此需要引入锚定校正与边界几何传输。

Method: 基于固定的预训练亲和度骨干网络：①锚定（anchoring）——在链与另一标注平面相交处用已知标签校正预测，并以其与这些标签的一致程度加权融合；②边界几何传输——比较截断符号距离场（SDF，通过稠密亲和度平均传播）与轮廓（顶点沿同一亲和度的运动线索移动）两种方式；轮廓跟踪器从手工规则出发，在506例CT体数据的超体素伪目标上学习运动校正，无需人工器官标注。

Result: 在约403例CT扫描、17种腹部结构、2091个留出结构实例上，锚定将平均Dice从正交掩膜传播的0.737提升至0.769，且对每个结构均有改善。在锚定掩膜之外，学习式轮廓跟踪使食管Dice提升5.7%、后腔静脉提升5.9%；SDF传输使右肾上腺Dice提升2.8%。各锚定方法平均Dice相近（0.769–0.772），但轮廓跟踪在肾脏、胃和肝血管树上表现较差。

Conclusion: 利用正交标注交叉处的已知标签进行锚定纠偏与置信度加权融合，可稳定提升传播精度且对所有结构普适；边界几何传输的收益具有结构特异性——轮廓跟踪适合食管、后腔静脉等细长/形变平滑结构，对肾脏、胃、肝血管树等复杂结构不利，SDF对右肾上腺更有效。

Abstract: Sli2Vol propagates a binary mask through affinities between adjacent slices, but an error can persist because each prediction becomes the input to the next step. Three orthogonal annotations reduce dependence on any one chain, yet they offer more than three starting masks: where a chain crosses another annotated plane, the correct label is already known. Those labels could correct drift during propagation and indicate how much to trust each chain at fusion. Between annotated planes, the information carried by the chain may matter as well. A binary mask records foreground membership but discards distance to the boundary, a cue that could help follow gradual shape changes between slices.We address these two sources of error with a fixed pretrained affinity backbone. We anchor each chain by correcting its prediction where it crosses another annotated plane, then use its agreement with those labels to weight fusion. We compare this anchored mask with two ways of transporting boundary geometry: a truncated signed-distance field (SDF) propagated by dense affinity averaging, and a contour whose vertices follow motion cues from the same affinity. The contour tracker starts from a hand-crafted rule and learns a motion correction from supervoxel pseudo-objects in 506 CT volumes, without manual organ labels. On 2,091 held-out structure instances from approximately 403 CT scans spanning 17 abdominal structures, anchoring raises mean Dice from 0.737 with orthogonal mask propagation to 0.769 and improves every structure. Beyond the anchored mask, learned contour tracking improves Dice by 5.7\% for the esophagus and 5.9\% for the postcava, while SDF transport improves right-adrenal Dice by 2.8\%. The anchored methods have similar mean Dice (0.769--0.772); contour tracking performs worse on the kidneys, stomach, and hepatic vessel tree.

</details>


### [124] [GaussianRenderFormer: Transformer-based Rendering of 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05593)
*Shahaf Valencia Levy,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GaussianRenderFormer，一个无需光栅化器的 Transformer 渲染器：以每个 3D Gaussian 为一个 token 经视图无关编码器编码，再由视图相关解码器将相机射线小片映射为像素。在 Objaverse 数据上对 300 个未见物体达 38.08 dB PSNR，低于光栅化基线 44.02 dB，差距主要来自视图解码器瓶颈而非泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络能否在渲染器中完全去除光栅化器、直接从 3D Gaussian splats 预测图像，从而检验 Transformer 对 Gaussian 场景表示的隐式渲染能力及其瓶颈所在。

Method: 构建 GaussianRenderFormer：视图无关编码器将每个 Gaussian 编码为一个 token；视图相关解码器针对相机射线的小片（patch）通过注意力机制查询这些 token 并输出像素。训练数据为 26,820 个 Objaverse 物体，每个裁剪至 20,000 个 Gaussian。

Result: 在 300 个未见物体上，相对完整未裁剪 splats 的 PSNR 为 38.08 dB，而同一裁剪输入经光栅化可达 44.02 dB。诊断实验表明差距并非泛化问题：向解码器显式提供每个 Gaussian 的投影足迹可提升 1.4 dB，说明视图解码器存在信息/容量瓶颈；同时不排除训练不足的影响。

Conclusion: 神经网络可隐式完成 Gaussian splatting 渲染，但当前精度仍不及光栅化器；性能瓶颈集中在视图解码器对投影足迹等几何线索的推断能力上，显式几何提示与更充分训练是潜在改进方向。

Abstract: Can a neural network learn to render 3D Gaussian splats on its own, with no rasterizer in the renderer? We present GaussianRenderFormer, a transformer that takes a set of Gaussians and a camera and outputs the image directly. Each Gaussian becomes one token in a view-independent encoder, and a view-dependent decoder turns small patches of camera rays into pixels by attending to those tokens. Trained on 26,820 Objaverse objects pruned to 20,000 Gaussians each, GaussianRenderFormer renders 300 unseen objects at 38.08 dB PSNR against the full, unpruned splats, while rasterizing the same pruned input reaches 44.02 dB. The gap is not one of generalization, and our diagnostics are consistent with a bottleneck in the view decoder: telling it the projected footprint of each Gaussian, which it could in principle infer, adds 1.4 dB. Under-training is not ruled out. Code, trained models and videos: https://svlwoof.github.io/gaussianrenderformer

</details>


### [125] [FedXCrop: Privacy-Preserving Crop Disease Detection via Federated Learning with Post-Aggregation Explainability under Non-IID Farm Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06221)
*Kriti Saini,Manikandan P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedXCrop联邦作物病害分类流程，并定量评估聚合全局模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式训练需农场共享原始数据存在隐私问题，且缺乏解释的预测对治疗决策支持有限。

Method: 采用MobileNetV2，5客户端30轮联邦训练，比较FedAvg与FedProx在IID及Dirichlet标签偏斜（α=1,0.5,0.1）下的表现，并用Grad-CAM与SmoothGrad在380张测试图上做定量解释评估。

Result: 集中式测试精度99.38%（3种子标准差0.05，5428图38类）；FedAvg在IID与三种偏斜下为98.81%/98.81%/98.59%/97.78%，FedProx无显著差异（McNemar p>0.3）；两种解释方法对叶片的能量归因（0.70-0.78）优于随机（0.60），联邦与集中模型Grad-CAM的Spearman相关为0.74-0.76；异质性增大时一致性下降，病灶定位仅略高于随机，随机化测试显示SmoothGrad主要依赖图像结构而非学习参数。

Conclusion: 标签偏斜使联邦精度相对集中式下降0.6-1.6个百分点；FedAvg基本保持Grad-CAM行为，FedProx无可见收益。

Abstract: Deep learning can classify crop diseases accurately from benchmark leaf images, but centralized training requires farms to share raw data, and predictions without explanations offer limited support for treatment decisions. We present FedXCrop, a federated crop disease classification pipeline that quantitatively evaluates explanations of the aggregated global model. A MobileNetV2 model trained centrally on PlantVillage achieved 99.38% test accuracy (standard deviation 0.05 over three seeds) on a fixed test set of 5,428 images across 38 classes. Federated models initialized with ImageNet weights were trained for 30 rounds across five clients. FedAvg achieved 98.81% accuracy with identically distributed clients and 98.81%, 98.59%, and 97.78% under Dirichlet label skew (alpha = 1, 0.5, and 0.1, respectively). FedProx was not significantly different from FedAvg at any heterogeneity level (McNemar p > 0.3). Grad-CAM and SmoothGrad explanations were evaluated on 380 test images. Both attributed more energy to the leaf than chance (0.70-0.78 versus 0.60), while Grad-CAM maps from federated and centralized models showed Spearman correlations of 0.74-0.76. Agreement decreased as heterogeneity increased, lesion localization was only modestly above chance, and a model-randomization test indicated that SmoothGrad maps depended substantially on image structure rather than learned parameters. In this setting, label skew reduced federated accuracy by 0.6-1.6 percentage points relative to centralized training. FedAvg largely preserved Grad-CAM behavior, while FedProx provided no measurable benefit.

</details>


### [126] [BiCG-Net: Target-Specific Image Quality Assessment via Observation Modeling and In-Image Differencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06203)
*Yibu Yang,Tingqiong Cui,Liming Lei,Hao Xiong,Jun Xu,Siyuan Liu,Guanghui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出目标级无参考质量评估任务，构建嵌套采样与BiCG-Net学生网络，通过线性观测模型与图像内差分机制蒸馏11教师集成，在CSIQ/LIVEC上PLCC超教师共识并具零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整图NR-IQA在目标仅占画面小部分时评分被背景主导，且缺乏目标级人类MOS基准，需要直接估计特定目标区域（如车体）的质量。

Method: 采用嵌套采样：从目标框向外扩展的序列patch，从该序列的相对结构中推断质量。基于此设计BiCG-Net：以线性观测模型与图像内差分机制蒸馏11教师集成，该机制可证明地抵消目标项与教师特定偏置；学生网络独立在各数据集上训练测试，并进行跨基准零样本迁移评估。

Result: CSIQ/LIVEC上学生对人类MOS的PLCC为0.9009与0.9133，超过最佳单教师与教师共识；自建无MOS铁路数据集上对弱标签PLCC达0.9855；CSIQ→LIVEC迁移下冻结学生PLCC 0.8416，超共识0.1947；残差分析与背景污染、教师离散度、标签极端性显著相关。

Conclusion: 学生超越教师源于学习从教师读数相对结构到真值的映射而非简单模仿，能提取共识之外的信息；39,482参数的紧凑学生可作离线目标级质量审计与标签生成引擎，并作为异构教师读数到统一质量尺度的映射层。

Abstract: We estimate the quality of a specific target region (e.g., a train body) in an image, rather than the whole image. This differs fundamentally from whole-image No-Reference Image Quality Assessment (NR-IQA): when the target occupies a small fraction of the frame, the whole-image score is dominated by background, and no public benchmark provides target-level human Mean Opinion Score (MOS). We estimate target quality via nested sampling - a sequence of patches expanding outward from the target box - and infer quality from the relative structure of this sequence.
  This motivates BiCG-Net, a student network that distills an 11-teacher ensemble through a linear observation model and an in-image differencing mechanism, which provably cancels the target term and teacher-specific bias. We train and test the student independently on each dataset, and evaluate zero-shot transfer across benchmarks. On CSIQ and LIVEC, the student reaches PLCCs of 0.9009 and 0.9133 against human MOS, exceeding the best single teacher and the teacher consensus. On a self-built MOS-free railway dataset, the student reaches a PLCC of 0.9855 against a weak label.
  The student exceeds its teachers because it learns a new mapping from the relative structure of teacher readings to the ground-truth label, rather than mimicking them. Residual analysis on CSIQ and LIVEC shows the prediction residual is significantly correlated with background contamination, teacher dispersion, and label extremity, demonstrating the student extracts information the consensus does not contain. Under CSIQ-to-LIVEC transfer, the frozen student reaches a PLCC of 0.8416, exceeding the consensus by 0.1947. We position BiCG-Net as an offline target-level quality auditing and label-generation engine; its 39,482-parameter student is compact by design, acting as a unifying layer mapping heterogeneous teacher readings onto a common quality scale.

</details>


### [127] [Rohbau3D-Semantics: Dense Semantic and Instance Labels for Point Clouds of Indoor Shell Construction Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06180)
*Lukas Rauch,Thomas Braml*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Rohbau3D-Semantics 数据集，为壳体施工现场（毛坯建筑）的激光扫描点云添加密集语义与实例标签，共 14 个测站、504 个点云场景，含 17 类建筑构件加 1 类背景，并提供全景/立方体贴图派生表示与语义分割基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有室内数据集多为合成数据、由重建网格采样的点云或规模较小的施工专用资源，难以体现真实激光扫描特性（非均匀采样、测量伪影、反射、细部几何），也缺乏复杂建筑环境下的密集语义基准，因此需要连接合成室内数据集、网格派生基准与施工场景资源的桥梁型数据集。

Method: 使用地面激光扫描仪在 14 个壳体施工测站采集 504 个场景，保存于扫描仪中心的局部坐标系；由人工逐点标注并配合专家监督与全景复核进行质量控制；提供脚本从三维数组导出几何对齐的全景图与立方体贴图，涵盖颜色、LiDAR 反射强度、深度、表面法线、语义与实例标签；给出基准划分与语义分割基线实验。

Result: 数据集包含 17 类建筑构件语义标签及 1 个背景类，并提供对象级实例标注；保留真实扫描的非均匀采样、测量伪影与反射等特性；论文给出采集流程、标注协议、质控流程、全景生成方法及基准划分与语义分割基线结果。

Conclusion: Rohbau3D-Semantics 以精确地面激光扫描、密集标注和多样化的壳体施工场景，弥补合成室内数据集、网格派生基准与施工专用资源之间的空白，可支持三维场景理解、施工现场分析与多模态场景分析研究。

Abstract: We present the Rohbau3D-Semantics dataset, which adds dense semantic and instance-level labels to laser scans of the previously released Rohbau3D domain data from shell construction sites. The dataset comprises 504 point cloud scenes from 14 survey sites, each captured with a terrestrial laser scanner and stored in a scanner-centered local coordinate system. Unlike point clouds sampled from reconstructed meshes or synthetic building models, the data preserve the characteristic properties of real laser scanning, including non-uniform sampling, measurement artifacts, reflections, and fine geometric detail. Combined with manual labels, expert oversight during pointwise annotation, and full-scene review, this yields a high-quality benchmark for semantic scene understanding in complex built environments. The dataset includes 17 class labels of building components and one background class, along with instance annotations for object-level analysis. In addition to the 3D point clouds, the release also provides scripts for deriving geometrically aligned panoramic and cube-map representations of color, LiDAR reflection intensity, depth, surface normals, semantic labels, and instance labels from the 3D arrays. We describe the acquisition pipeline, annotation protocol, quality-control process, and panorama-generation procedure, and provide benchmark splits and baseline experiments for semantic segmentation. By combining precise terrestrial laser scans, dense annotations, and a wide variety of shell-construction scenes, Rohbau3D-Semantics bridges the gap among synthetic indoor datasets, mesh-derived benchmarks, and smaller construction-specific resources. The dataset is intended to support research on 3D scene understanding, construction-site analytics, and multimodal scene analysis in the built environment.
  Access and download the full dataset via https://github.com/RauchLukas/rohbau3d

</details>


### [128] [MotoVerse: A Benchmark for Predictive Perception of Motorized Two-Wheelers in Dense Unstructured Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06007)
*Varun A. Paturkar,Shankar Gangisetty,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MotoVerse，首个面向机动两轮车（MTW）自视角预测性感知的基准，包含949段密集非结构化交通中的操纵片段与超过31,000个周围交通参与者轨迹标注，定义ERMA与TATP两项任务及MTW专属挑战分类法，并对传统模型、通用/驾驶专用VLM与VLA模型进行系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTW在Global South个人出行中占主导，但在自动驾驶与ADAS的预测性感知研究中长期被忽视；现有基准多基于四轮车视角与结构化交通，缺乏MTW自视角、密集非结构化场景下的预测评测平台。

Method: 构建MotoVerse数据集：949段操纵片段、31,000+交通主体轨迹标注（含16,000+ MTW）；设计两项互补任务——自车骑行者操纵预判（ERMA）与交通主体轨迹预测（TATP）；为每段片段标注MTW专属挑战分类法（动态自运动、弱交通结构、上下文遮挡），支持挑战感知评测；基准测试涵盖传统预测模型、通用VLM、驾驶专用VLM及视觉-语言-动作模型，并开展初步LoRA适配实验。

Result: 三项关键发现：(i) 视频骨干网络在操纵前1/5时段即达到接近全观测的精度，而零样本VLM需观测大部分操纵过程；(ii) 零样本VLM（无论通用或驾驶专用）在两项任务上均接近简单基线，LoRA适配显著提升操纵预判但轨迹预测仍落后于传统模型；(iii) 骑行者自身运动是预测难度主因，所有模型（含静态基线）在左转及动态自运动下误差最高。

Conclusion: MotoVerse为智能两轮车更安全、更鲁棒的预测性AI提供了具有挑战性的基准，数据集与评测代码已公开。

Abstract: Motorized Two-Wheelers (MTWs) dominate personal transport across much of the Global South, yet remain largely overlooked in predictive perception research for autonomous driving and ADAS. We introduce MotoVerse, a benchmark for predictive perception from an MTW's ego-centric perspective. MotoVerse comprises 949 maneuver clips collected in dense, unstructured traffic and trajectory annotations for over 31,000 surrounding road agents, including over 16,000 MTWs, enabling two complementary tasks: Ego-Rider Maneuver Anticipation (ERMA) and Traffic Agent Trajectory Prediction (TATP). Each clip is further annotated with an MTW-specific challenge taxonomy comprising dynamic ego-motion, weak traffic structure and contextual occlusion, enabling challenge-aware evaluation. We benchmark established conventional prediction models, general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs), driving-specific VLMs and vision-language-action models. Our experiments reveal three key insights: (i) video backbones reach most of their full-observation accuracy from the first fifth of a maneuver, whereas zero-shot VLMs need most of the maneuver to be observed; (ii) zero-shot VLMs, whether general-purpose or driving-specific, largely remain close to simple baselines on both tasks; a preliminary LoRA study shows that adaptation lifts anticipation sharply but leaves trajectory prediction behind conventional models; and (iii) the rider's own motion drives forecasting difficulty: for every model, including a static baseline, errors are highest during left turns and rise under dynamic ego-motion. We believe MotoVerse will serve as a challenging benchmark for safer and more robust predictive AI for intelligent two-wheelers. The dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/VarunIIITH/MotoVerse.

</details>


### [129] [Uncertainty-Driven Efficient Communication for UAV-Based Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06635)
*Jiuwu Hao,Yuting Wan,Liguo Sun,Zao Zhang,Yueyang Wu,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出不确定性驱动的UCP框架，用观测不确定性（传感器受限区域）与判别不确定性（决策边界模糊）刻画协同价值，生成请求/供给图实现高效通信，并用U-Fusion聚合，在3D检测与轨迹预测上取得更优性能—带宽权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV协同感知受带宽与算力严格约束，通信效率是瓶颈。现有空间选择方法主要关注前景区域以减少漏检，忽略感知模糊区域，导致误检难以消除。

Method: UCP框架：1) 观测不确定性识别传感器受限区域；2) 判别不确定性捕获决策边界附近的模型级模糊；3) 由两类估计驱动空间请求图与供给图，实现智能体间高效通信；4) 接收消息经U-Fusion不确定性引导的交叉注意力模块聚合。

Result: 在三个基准上、覆盖3D目标检测与轨迹预测任务，表现出更优的性能—带宽权衡，且具备高可解释性与强泛化性。

Conclusion: 以不确定性而非仅前景作为协同价值度量，可同时缓解漏检与误检，为资源受限UAV协同感知提供高效、可解释、可泛化的通信选择方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based collaborative perception enables multiple agents to share complementary perceptual information, overcoming limitations such as occlusion and limited sensing range. Communication efficiency is a critical bottleneck, as UAVs operate under stringent bandwidth and computational constraints. However, existing spatial selection methods focus predominantly on foreground regions to reduce missed detections, neglecting perceptually ambiguous regions and leaving false detections unresolved. We propose UCP, an uncertainty-driven framework that characterizes collaborative value through observational uncertainty, identifying sensor-limited regions, and discriminative uncertainty, capturing model-level ambiguity near the decision boundary. These estimates drive spatial request and supply maps for efficient inter-agent communication, and the received messages are aggregated via U-Fusion, an uncertainty-guided cross-attention module. Experiments on three benchmarks across 3D object detection and trajectory prediction demonstrate a superior performance-bandwidth trade-off with high interpretability and strong generalizability.

</details>


### [130] [Learning to Track Cells from Sparse Annotations in 3D Time-Lapse Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06624)
*Yusuke Uchida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一条细胞追踪流水线，整合稀疏标注下的检测、基于图像特征的候选连接校准与谱系图事件联合选择，在2个斑马鱼胚胎199个视频上5折交叉验证总分0.9762。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从3D延时显微图像重建细胞谱系需区分细胞运动与分裂，并推断时间一致的连接；当仅部分细胞被标注时，把未标注区域当作背景或错误对应进行训练会产生问题。

Method: 1）检测：融合2.5D与3D模型输出，抑制未标注细胞处的错误背景监督；2）校准：利用稠密光流的运动特征、学习式细胞匹配模型的对应特征，以及识别分裂前细胞、分裂母细胞、分裂后子细胞模型的输出，校准普通连接与分裂概率；3）选择：在由评价指标导出的替代目标下联合选择普通连接、以子代对表示的分裂及节点纳入，随后进行间隙填补与时间坐标细化。

Result: 2个斑马鱼胚胎199个视频的5折交叉验证总体得分0.9762；在候选与校准值固定时，联合选择比允许替换已有分裂并重优化普通连接的顺序方法高0.0105，提升主要来自分裂恢复。

Conclusion: 联合选择分裂与普通连接事件优于顺序优化，且稀疏标注下的检测融合与特征校准可有效支撑高精度谱系重建；性能增益主要源于分裂事件的召回。

Abstract: Reconstructing cell lineages from 3D time-lapse microscopy requires distinguishing cell motion from division and inferring temporally consistent connections. When only a subset of cells is annotated, however, training procedures that treat unannotated regions as background or incorrect correspondences can be problematic. We propose a cell tracking pipeline that integrates detection from sparse annotations, calibration of candidate connections using image features, and joint selection of lineage graph events. For detection, we combine the outputs of 2.5D and 3D models trained to suppress erroneous background supervision at unannotated cells. We then calibrate ordinary link and division probabilities using motion features from dense flow, correspondence features from a learned cell-matching model, and features from models that identify cells immediately before division, dividing parents, and daughters immediately after division. Ordinary links, divisions represented as daughter pairs, and node inclusion are selected jointly under a surrogate objective derived from the evaluation metric, followed by gap filling and temporal coordinate refinement. The pipeline achieves an overall score of 0.9762 in 5-fold cross-validation on 199 videos from 2 zebrafish embryos. With candidates and calibrated values held fixed, joint selection outperforms a sequential method that allows replacement of existing divisions and reoptimization of ordinary links by 0.0105. The improvement arises primarily from division recovery.

</details>


### [131] [LungMend: A Healthy-Only Latent Diffusion Shape Prior for Lung Mask Correction in Severe Tuberculosis CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06556)
*Rezkellah Noureddine Khiat,Pierre-Yves Brillet,Catalin Fetita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LungMend：一种仅用健康解剖先验的后处理扩散模型，修复严重结核CT上R231等肺分割模型遗漏的空洞与实变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肺分割模型（如R231）在严重结核CT上失效：空洞和实变在视觉上类似非肺组织，被排除在掩膜之外，导致分割不全。且难以获取带病理标注的数据。

Method: 提出后处理模型LungMend。使用在健康肺上训练的潜在扩散模型，并配以人工合成损坏的掩膜；模型从错误掩膜与骨骼解剖结构出发，学习恢复完整肺轮廓，全程不依赖任何病理数据或CT强度值。

Result: LungMend能够找回R231遗漏的空洞与实变区域，并在专家标注切片上提升了分割准确率。

Conclusion: 表明仅凭健康解剖先验与合成损坏掩膜即可通用地纠正病理CT分割错误，为无需病理监督的鲁棒肺分割提供了可行后处理方案。

Abstract: Lung segmentation models such as R231 are widely used, but they fail on severe tuberculosis (TB) CT: cavities and consolidations look like non-lung tissue and are left out of the mask. We present LungMend, a post-processing model that corrects such masks using only healthy anatomy. A latent diffusion model, trained on healthy lungs with synthetically corrupted masks, learns to recover the complete lung from the faulty mask and the bone anatomy, without any pathological data or CT intensities. LungMend recovers cavities and consolidations that R231 misses and improves accuracy on expert-annotated slices. Code: https://github.com/noureddinekhiati/LungMend

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [When to Ask, When to Answer: Strategic Clarification for Ambiguous Reference Resolution in Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05517)
*Ibrahim Khebour,Nikhil Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究"策略性澄清"问题：在指代博弈中，当澄清问题的答案能缩小候选指代集合时是否提问。基于 SIMMC 2.1 构建可控测试环境并提出 BACE（信念感知、带认知状态跟踪的轻量动作选择器），其选择最优澄清问题数量的稳定性优于 GRPO 与 ArCHer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类指代消歧是协作过程，只在澄清问题能实质改变结果时才提问；而 LLM 存在"过早作答"偏差，偏好单轮确定回答而非多轮澄清。因此需要研究模型何时应"策略性地"发问。

Method: 从 SIMMC 2.1 数据集构建受控测试环境，记录每个歧义指代被成功解析所需的澄清问题数量；对比基线 LLM 行为与常见后训练方法（如 GRPO、ArCHer）在"提问"与"直接作答"间的取舍；提出 BACE——在冻结 LM 主干之上加装轻量动作选择器，并显式跟踪认知/信念状态。

Result: BACE 相比 GRPO、ArCHer 等方法能更稳定地选择最优的澄清问题数量，缓解过早作答偏差。

Conclusion: 策略性澄清可作为独立于语言生成的动作选择问题来建模；冻结主干配合轻量、具备信念与认知跟踪的动作选择器，是实现按需澄清的有效且高效途径。

Abstract: For humans, referential disambiguation is a collaborative process involving clarification questions asked when they will meaningfully change the outcome. However, large language models (LLMs) have been shown to be biased toward premature answering, preferring definite single-turn responses over more clarification exchanges. In this paper, we study the problem of "strategic clarification" -- asking clarification questions in a reference game when their answers will narrow the set of candidate referents. We construct a controlled test environment from the SIMMC 2.1 dataset that records how many questions each ambiguous reference needs to be successfully resolved, and examine how baseline LLM behavior and common post-training methods ask clarifying questions vs. attempt to answer the referent. We also propose BACE (Belief-Aware Critic with Epistemic tracking), a lightweight action chooser method on top of a frozen LM backbone, and show that BACE more consistently chooses the optimal number of clarifying questions than methods such as GRPO and ArCHer.

</details>


### [133] [AI-Driven SaaS Platform Integration: AI Express for Unified Generative AI Services with Pay-As-You-Go Credit Systems and Scalable Microservice Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05389)
*Deepit Sapru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AI Express 是一个按需付费的 SaaS 平台，将文本生成、图像合成、代码辅助等多种生成式 AI 能力整合到单一界面中，以降低订阅成本并消除工作流割裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式 AI 生态高度碎片化：文本生成、图像合成与代码辅助需分别订阅，导致成本高昂且跨工具切换造成工作流中断。

Method: 构建统一的 SaaS 聚合平台，采用 pay-as-you-go 计费模式，在单一界面内集成多类 AI 工具：评估 GitHub 仓库的 Core Code Reviewer、将文本转为可运行网站的 Text-to-Website 生成器、脚本生成、语法检查、数据分析及去水印等，并以零技术门槛的方式提供访问。

Result: 摘要为产品描述性陈述，未给出量化评测结果；其宣称效果为：降低使用成本、消除工作流中断、无需专业技术知识即可使用。

Conclusion: 该工作属于系统/产品类贡献，主张以统一平台与按需计费替代分散订阅，实现生成式 AI 工具的一站式集成。

Abstract: Generative AI is currently fragmented, with separate subscriptions required for text generation, image synthesis, and code assistance, leading to high costs and workflow disruptions. AI Express is a SaaS platform that consolidates these capabilities into a unified pay-as-you-go service. The platform provides a range of AI tools, including a Core Code Reviewer that evaluates GitHub repositories, a Text-to-Website generator that converts text into fully functional websites, script generation, grammar checking, data analysis, and watermark removal, all accessible through a single interface without requiring technical expertise.

</details>


### [134] [The Harness Is the Model: Agent Scaffolding Changes a Fixed LLM's Compliance with Banking Conduct Rules, Unevenly Across Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06116)
*Mehmet Murat Huyut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在模型固定的前提下，四种开源权重模型各配五种harness（流水线、ReAct工具循环、仅检索可达政策的循环、含长期良性客户记忆的循环、同模型合规审查者循环）在模拟零售银行的156个预注册案例上运行，共3120个两轮对话。汇总结论：harness整体影响有限（87.3%–92.1%），但模型内差异可达11.5个百分点，足以改变排名；同模型审查者降低违规率（9.6%→6.4%）且不增加过度拒答；日期算术类规则在所有harness中均失败；26%的违规伴随否认已执行动作的客户消息。验证单元应为"模型–harness对"，合规测试应读动作日志而非对话记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行通常只对语言模型本身进行验证，但实际部署的是"模型+harness"系统，harness决定模型的输入、可调用的工具以及由谁核查其行为。现有研究未在模型固定的条件下检验harness是否改变对银行行为准则的遵守程度，因此需要量化harness效应以指导模型风险管理与合规测试的对象选择。

Method: 设计受控对照实验：4个开源权重模型×5种harness（单次结构化决策流水线、ReAct式工具循环、政策仅通过检索可达的循环、带长期良性客户记忆的循环、同模型合规审查者循环），在模拟零售银行的156个预注册案例上测试，案例覆盖11类规则族（转述欧盟消费信贷、支付、投资者保护、数据保护与反洗钱条款）。每个两轮episode（共3120个）依据实际执行的工具轨迹进行确定性评分，违规与过度拒答分别计数；另做探索性分析，使用两个LLM评判器加盲审人工复核，识别"消息否认已执行动作"的模式。

Result: 模型汇总层面，各harness的正确率仅在87.3%–92.1%之间小幅变动；但模型内部差异显著：某模型最佳与最差harness相差11.5个百分点，足以使其在四模型排名中从第一降至最后，而另一模型的harness效应不可检测；模型×harness交互效应仅为提示性而非确证性。同模型合规审查者将违规率从9.6%降至6.4%，且未提高过度拒答率，与预注册预测相反。需日期算术的规则在所有harness中均失败。探索性分析显示26%的违规伴随客户消息否认刚刚执行的动作，仅基于对话记录的审查会误判为正确拒答。

Conclusion: harness对合规的影响具有强烈的模型特异性，总体平均掩盖了模型内的重大差异。模型风险管理的验证单元应为"模型–harness对"；行为合规测试必须读取实际动作日志而非对话记录，以避免被口头否认所掩盖的违规。同模型审查者是被验证有效的harness级缓解手段，但日期算术等规则形式化能力仍是系统性薄弱点。

Abstract: Banks validate language models, but they deploy systems: a model inside a harness that decides what it sees, which tools it can call and who checks its actions. We test whether that harness changes compliance with banking conduct rules when the model is held fixed. Four open-weight models each ran five harnesses (a pipeline that makes one structured decision, a ReAct-style tool loop, a loop in which the policy is reachable only through search, a loop with a long benign customer memory, and a loop with a same-model compliance reviewer) on 156 pre-registered cases in a simulated retail bank whose 11 rule families paraphrase EU consumer-credit, payments, investor-protection, data-protection and anti-money-laundering provisions. Every one of the 3120 two-turn episodes was scored deterministically from the executed tool trace, with violations and over-refusals counted separately. Pooled across models the harness moved the correct-case rate only between 87.3% and 92.1%, but within a model it mattered a great deal: for one model the spread between its best and worst harness was 11.5 percentage points, enough to move it from first to last place among the four models, while for another the harness made no detectable difference; the model x harness interaction itself was suggestive rather than conclusive. The same-model reviewer cut violations from 9.6% to 6.4% without raising over-refusals, contrary to our pre-registered prediction. Rules that require date arithmetic failed in every harness. In an exploratory analysis with two LLM judges and a blind human check, 26% of violations were accompanied by a customer message that denied the action the agent had just executed, which a transcript-only review would record as a correct refusal. For model-risk management the unit of validation should be the model-harness pair, and conduct testing should read the action log, not the conversation.

</details>


### [135] [ArcRAG: Agentic Compositional Retrieval over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06481)
*Jyotin Goel,Abhinav Arun,Tejas Prakash Agarwal,Bhaskarjit Sarmah,Stefano Pasquali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ArcRAG：在推理时从问题实体可达的节点标签、关系描述符与属性键中归纳出“查询局部图接口”，据此规划多跳遍历并生成/修复 Cypher 执行，从而在无需针对特定构图流水线重设计检索的前提下，实现对不同知识图谱构建方式的跨构建可移植检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 的检索策略通常与其自建图的结构强耦合（依赖特定 schema、关系命名与调参），一旦图的构建流水线改变，检索是否仍有效鲜有检验；论文因此研究“跨构建可移植性”。

Method: 在推理时诱导查询局部图接口（问题所指实体可达的节点标签、关系描述符、属性键），基于该接口规划多跳遍历，并以 Cypher 形式执行与修复；将 MuSiQue、HotpotQA、TriviaQA 三语料分别构建为 schema 引导图（带类型节点、封闭关系集、来源溯源）与开放信息抽取图（无类型节点、自由文本关系），检索器配置在两类图上固定不变；与构建无关的 Direct-Cypher 智能体对比，并在重试与遍历预算下对各阶段做消融，同时与既有 GraphRAG 系统报告结果比较。

Result: 在两种构建方式下，构建无关的检索器借助查询时接口归纳仍保持有效；但构型仍影响绝对准确率，即 schema 引导图与开放 IE 图之间存在精度差距。

Conclusion: 检索无需与图构建协同设计：查询时接口归纳可使构建无关检索器在构建方式变化时依旧有效，而构建本身仍决定绝对性能上限。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) answers questions by retrieving over a knowledge graph built from a corpus. Yet retrieval strategies are designed around the structure their own construction pipeline produces, and whether they survive a change of construction is rarely tested. We study this cross-construction portability: whether one retrieval procedure, with no construction-specific schema, relation names, or retuning, stays effective when the graph is built differently. We introduce ArcRAG, which induces a query-local graph interface at inference time (the node labels, relation descriptors, and property keys reachable from the entities a question names), plans multi-hop traversals against it, and executes and repairs them as Cypher. We build each of the MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and TriviaQA corpora as a schema-guided graph (typed nodes, a closed relation set, source provenance) and as an open-information-extraction graph (untyped nodes, free-text relations), with the retriever's configuration fixed across them.
