*Lingfei Qian,Yan Wang,Xueqing Peng,Yupeng Cao,Yuechen Jiang,Zhuohan Xie,Anke Xu,Wenbo Cao,Jiarui Chen,Zehan Li,Jimin Huang,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yuehua Tang,Sophia Ananiadou,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 FinDendro 基准（2,226 条 1999–2026 年美国事实、每事实 4 种提问），检验 LLM 金融回测中的训练知识泄漏防护（厂商截止日期、自陈知识、指定历史日期）是否可靠。结果显示自陈知识系统性低估实际知识，声称不知道的事实中仍有 61% 能答对，知识声明可超出知识边界达 7 个季度，指定日期仅抑制声明而不改变准确率；因此回测期内无法凭自陈/指令/截止日期证明无泄漏，应改用行为化测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 金融回测的有效性依赖模型"事前不知"其结果，但业界常用的三种防护（信任厂商声明的知识截止、询问模型知道什么、指令其以过去日期回答）均未经检验，需验证其可信性。

Method: 构建 FinDendro：2,226 条 1999–2026 年带日期的美国事实，覆盖月度宏观序列、月末股价与美联储决议。每事实生成四种提示：数值问答、将候选值与"知道/猜测"声明配对的无日期 Recall/Predict 题、以及带历史声明日期的 Predict 题；并设持久性与主导结果对照，使随机准确率约 0.27。

Result: 多数前沿模型的自陈知识低于真实知识：GPT-5 在受控事实上答对 76%，却仅声称知道 38%，且在其自称不知道的事实中答对 61%。知识声明可超出实测知识边界最多 7 个季度；声明历史日期可消除大部分"先前已知"声明，但准确率不变。

Conclusion: 训练窗口内的回测无法通过自陈、指令或厂商截止日期被认证为无泄漏；应改用行为化测量来界定模型的时间知识边界，FinDendro 为据此选择回测期提供了基准与流程。

Abstract: Financial backtests of large language models (LLMs) are meaningful only if the model did not already know the outcomes it is asked to predict. Practitioners guard against this by trusting a vendor's stated knowledge cutoff, by asking the model what it knows, or by instructing it to answer as of a past date. To verify whether any of these safeguards can be trusted, we introduce FinDendro, a benchmark of 2,226 dated U.S. facts from 1999 to 2026, covering monthly macroeconomic series, month-end stock prices and Federal Reserve decisions. Each fact yields four prompts: a value question, Recall and Predict questions that pair each candidate value with a claim of knowing or guessing without a declared date, and a Predict question with a historical declared date. Controls for persistence and dominant outcomes keep chance accuracy near 0.27. Results show that for most frontier models, self-reports understate knowledge. GPT-5 answers 76% of controlled facts correctly but claims to know 38%, and answers 61% of the facts it says it does not know. Knowledge claims can persist beyond the measured knowledge boundary, by up to seven quarters, and a declared date removes most of the claims of prior knowledge while leaving accuracy unchanged. Backtests inside a model's training window therefore cannot be certified leak-free by self-report, instruction or stated cutoff. These findings motivate behavioral measurement of temporal knowledge boundaries, and FinDendro provides a benchmark and procedure for selecting backtest periods based on demonstrated factual knowledge.

</details>


### [172] [Ranking Agreement Does Not Reveal Paragraph-Edit Agreement in Two Released Paper Acceptance Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06789)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文检验"用快速的录用分类器为较慢的评估器筛选修改稿"这一做法是否成立。对ICLR的198篇论文比较了词/文档特征分类器与两个基于30亿参数语言模型微调的纯文本录用预测器，发现原始稿排名一致性尚可，但对"哪些改写会提分"的排序一致性近乎为零，且此前的较小响应被数值精度误差掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常设想以快速录用分类器预筛修改稿、再由更昂贵评估器评审；但该流程的有效性依赖于两种评估器对同一修改的评分排序是否一致，而这一点缺乏实证检验。

Method: 在198篇ICLR论文上，将基于词与文档特征的录用分类器与两个从同一30亿参数语言模型微调得到的纯文本录用预测器进行对比；比较原始稿与段落改写稿的模型排序。改写由自动筛查保证事实、论断强度与归属不变。预先设定的检验数值精度较低，故另作事后检验，以完整32位精度重新对发布权重打分。

Result: 分类器与其中一个预测器对原始稿的排名高度一致，与另一个中等一致；但在"哪些改写会提高得分"上，与两者均无可检测的一致性。低精度下舍入误差主导了微小的分数变化；改用32位精度后，改写一致性仍接近零——每篇论文在五个候选中择优时，平均原始分数比随机选择高不足2分（满分100）。

Conclusion: 证据表明，快速分类器的排序无法可靠迁移到另一评估器对修改稿的判断；修改筛选器必须针对其实际服务的目标评估器、在其所排序的具体修改上进行验证。结论限于表述性改写及同一模型家族的两个公开预测器，向高成本评估器的迁移尚未检验。

Abstract: A fast acceptance classifier could select revisions for a slower evaluator. We ask whether agreement on existing papers supports that use. We compare a classifier of word and document features with two released text-only acceptance predictors fine-tuned from the same language model with three billion parameters. For 198 papers from the International Conference on Learning Representations, we compare model rankings of the original papers and of paragraph rewrites screened automatically for unchanged facts, claim strength, and attribution. The classifier ranks original papers closely with one predictor and moderately with the other, but shows no detectable agreement with either on which rewrites raise scores. The tests specified in advance used reduced numerical precision. Rounding error dominated the small score responses. We therefore rescored the released weights in full 32-bit precision as a post hoc check; edit agreement remained near zero. At this precision, when each predictor chooses among five rewrites per paper, its average raw score is under two points out of 100 higher than with random choice. The evidence covers presentation rewrites and two open predictors from one family; transfer to costly evaluators remains untested. Revision selectors should therefore be checked against the intended evaluator on the revisions they rank.

</details>


### [173] [Dialect Matters: A Dialect-Aware Baseline for Igala-English Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06517)
*Etuh E.,Eli. A. Jiya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个带方言标签的Igala-English平行语料库与方言感知基线机器翻译系统，证明混合方言训练对保证方言公平性是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管研究者持续关注低资源语言的NLP问题，Igala语目前仍为零平行语料。同时，同一英语句子在Igala中可对应20-60种方言形式，方言差异会显著影响翻译质量，因此亟需建立带方言标注的资源与基线系统。

Method: 构建含1137句（训练899句）的Igala-English平行语料，并按方言标注：Idah 459句(40.4%)、Ankpa 297句(26.1%)、Ibaji 160句(14.1%)、Other 187句(16.4%)、Ogugu 32句(2.8%)。基于Helsinki-NLP/opus-mt-en-mul（78M参数）进行微调，开展epoch消融与方言混合/单方言对比实验。

Result: 混合方言训练取得均衡BLEU 11.66；仅用Idah训练的模型在Ibaji上下降4.7 BLEU（Idah 13.2 vs Ibaji 8.5）。收敛于8个epoch，15个epoch出现过拟合。整体BLEU 11.66、chrF 46.69。899句的数据规模与非洲低资源文献的对数线性趋势一致。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个Igala方言语影响分析，证明方言公平性要求混合方言训练，并为未来Igala机器翻译研究建立了开放基线。

Abstract: The massive permeability of intelligent systems in every sphere of human activities have greatly enhanced clarity, ease of work, and increased general global understanding which has significantly awaken the use of intelligent systems by all users. Intelligent systems greatly rely on precise dataset/corpus for the correctness of their output. Despite efforts by researchers to address Low-resource language in the Natural Language Processing (NLP), Igala still has zero parallel corpora despite efforts by researchers. We present a dialect-aware baseline for Igala-English machine translation, among first open systems and first with dialectal variation analysis. Our dataset of 1137 sentences (899 training) is first with dialect tags: Idah 459 (40.4%), Ankpa 297 (26.1%), Ibaji 160 (14.1%), Other 187 (16.4%), Ogugu 32 (2.8%). Same English yields 20-60 Igala forms. Mixed-dialect training achieves balanced BLEU 11.66 versus Idah-only model which drops 4.7 BLEU on Ibaji (13.2 on Idah, 8.5 on Ibaji), proving dialect fairness requires mixing. Fine-tuned from Helsinki-NLP/opus-mt-en-mul (78M), epoch ablation shows convergence at 8 epochs and overfitting at 15. BLEU 11.66, chrF 46.69. Analysis shows 899 sentences on log-linear trend consistent with African low-resource literature. This work provides first dialectal impact analysis for Igala and establishes baseline for future work.

</details>


### [174] [When Do Summaries Become Instructions? Unsupported Policy in LLM Agents' Self-Written Handoff Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06545)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究审计长期运行的LLM智能体在上下文压缩时生成的交接摘要，发现其中存在记录不支持的权限限制与授权（约1.2%–2.4%、0.1%–1.3%），并证明这类句子会改变后继实例行为；通过改写系统提示可显著降低此类虚假限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行LLM智能体压缩上下文时用摘要替代大部分历史，可能引入原记录不支持的权限要求或授权，从而影响后继实例（successor）的行为，但此前缺乏对这一"交接摘要"忠实性的量化审计。

Method: 构建带真实权限账本（ground-truth permission ledger）的合成工具调用任务；采用同快照重放（same-snapshot replay），将可疑整句替换为中性填充语，比较后继行为；两名AI读者仅阅读被规则标记的留出摘要进行判定；预注册重放检验，并对系统提示中"Some actions need approval."进行改写以指明要求来源。

Result: 两个开源权重模型的虚假限制占所有可读摘要的1.2%与2.4%，虚假授权占0.1%与1.3%；限制的预注册重放检验不显著（p=0.58），事后追加的检验出现脆弱效应（p=0.027）；中和虚假授权后，未授权动作从120次留出运行中的24次降至7次（事后；后续集合48次中17降至11）；多数开发期限制源于智能体先前"某动作需批准"的推理（事后）；将系统提示改为说明要求来源后，虚假限制从5.0%降至1.8%（开发摘要）、1.8%降至0.3%（留出模板），两者p<10^-9，而账本所要求的批准请求几乎不变。

Conclusion: 交接摘要确实会写入缺乏记录支持的权限陈述，并对后继智能体的越权行为产生可测影响；针对系统提示来源说明的干预可有效减少虚假限制而不损害必要的批准请求，提示上下文压缩环节需要忠实性审计与提示设计改进。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents compact their context, replacing most history with a summary. We ask whether handoff summaries state permissions or requirements the record does not support, whether such sentences change what the next instance (the successor) does, and where they come from. Synthetic tool-use tasks carry a ground-truth permission ledger; same-snapshot replay reruns the successor with those whole sentences replaced by neutral filler. Two AI readers, reading only rule-flagged held-out summaries, confirmed unsupported restrictions in 1.2% and 2.4% of all readable summaries from two open-weight models, and unsupported authorizations in 0.1% and 1.3%. For restrictions, a pre-specified replay test was not significant (p = 0.58); a follow-up, planned after it, found a fragile effect (p = 0.027). Neutralizing false approvals cut unauthorized actions from 24 to 7 of 120 held-out runs (post hoc; later set: 17 to 11 of 48). Most development restrictions followed the agent's earlier reasoning that an action needs approval (post hoc). In pre-specified tests, replacing the system prompt's "Some actions need approval." with one saying where requirements come from cut unsupported restrictions from 5.0% to 1.8% of development summaries and, on held-out templates, from 1.8% to 0.3% (both p < 10^-9), while requests for approvals the ledger requires barely changed. Code and data: https://github.com/Chuw1/handoff-audit

</details>


### [175] [SAUCE: Summary Analysis Using Conversation Entailment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06812)
*Man-Ling Sung,Hemanth Kandula,Jeff Ma,William Hartmann,Matthew Snover*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAUCE：一种无参考、基于事实的跨语种对话语音摘要评估流水线，通过对话与摘要之间的蕴含关系衡量摘要准确性与事实覆盖率，并可解释、与人工判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM及其语音应用的发展，缺乏明确终止目标的对话摘要在总结与评估上仍存在挑战；同时黑盒LLM评估器与稠密向量指标缺乏可解释性，难以定位具体错误（如转写/翻译导致的信息损失、幻觉或遗漏）。

Method: 构建SAUCE无参考、事实驱动的评估流程：以对话与摘要文本之间的蕴含（entailment）关系为依据，度量摘要的准确性与事实覆盖率；将摘要得分映射到离散、可验证的事实单元，从而定位确切的幻觉与遗漏；并与多种主流摘要指标进行对比。

Result: 实验表明SAUCE能有效捕捉由转写与翻译错误造成的信息损失，并识别出断裂/失效的摘要；相较黑盒LLM评估器与稠密嵌入指标具备内在可解释性；初步研究显示其结果与人工判断高度一致。

Conclusion: SAUCE为跨语种对话语音摘要提供了一种可解释、基于事实的评估方案，既帮助开发者系统刻画LLM行为，也为终端用户提供在嘈杂真实场景下核验摘要准确性的可操作工具。

Abstract: With the growing need for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) and their applications to speech, challenges persist in summarizing and evaluating conversations that lack a clear end goal. We introduce SAUCE -- a reference-free, fact-based evaluation pipeline for cross-lingual conversational speech summarization. It measures the accuracy and the fact coverage of a summary through the entailment between conversation and text. We compare SAUCE against several popular summarization metrics and demonstrate the effectiveness of capturing information loss due to transcription and translation error and identifying broken summaries. Crucially, unlike black-box LLM evaluators or dense embedding metrics, SAUCE is inherently explainable: it maps summary scores to discrete, verifiable facts, allowing users to pinpoint exact hallucinations or omissions. We illustrate how this interpretability helps developers systematically profile LLM behaviors and gives end-users an actionable tool to verify summary accuracy in noisy, real-world conditions. Preliminary investigations show SAUCE strongly align with human judgment.

</details>


### [176] [Zuse: A Decision Model for European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06238)
*Umair Mehmood Imam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者提出 Zuse——三个（4B/9B/27B）决策模型，在冻结的开源基座上加低秩适配器与指针头，覆盖27种欧洲语言；经后验分析给出三条结论：误校准按任务而非语言结构化、可自动化比例更贴近机会校正技能、跨语言泛化上限由基座决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策模型（读文档并对固定选项集输出概率）虽在2026年出现多个，但均无英语以外的公开结果；作者希望在多语言、多任务条件下检验该设计的行为表现。

Method: 在冻结的开源基座上训练低秩适配器（low-rank adapter）与指针头（pointer head），构建4B、9B、27B三个规模、面向27种欧洲语言的决策模型。在11个训练中排除的来源（9个任务组、27种语言）的开发集上评测准确率与池化/按来源加权的期望校准误差（ECE），并对单一种子结果做事后分析（置信差随语言/来源的变化、5%错误预算下的自动化比例与机会校正技能和原始准确率的相关、210种语言上的跨语言迁移）。同时报告多源训练数据构建中的两个陷阱：主题方案间的标签词冲突、以及注入式增广诱导捷径学习。

Result: 27B模型达到87.5%准确率，池化ECE为0.016，按来源加权为0.035。置信差在26种语言的平行阅读与主题题项上大致与采样噪声同量级，而跨来源从−0.07到+0.09，知识类任务过度自信、可接受性判断在所有规模上均欠自信，单一拟合温度无法同时纠正。5%错误预算下可自动化比例与机会校正技能的相关（Spearman 0.97，33个来源-模型对）高于与原始准确率的相关（0.91）。同规模内更换训练运行至多带来约2分变化，而基座文档语言列表之外的语言在所有规模上落后23–28分。在英文公开套件上Zuse落后于Jev与Kev（含同基座的Kev）。

Conclusion: 该设计的多语言行为主要受任务类型与基座模型的语言覆盖制约而非训练语言；误校准需按任务分别处理，自动化决策价值更应依据机会校正技能而非原始准确率评估；同时需警惕多源数据构建中的标签词冲突与捷径型增广。

Abstract: Decision models read a document and return a probability for each of a fixed set of options instead of generating text. Several appeared in 2026, but none has published results outside English. We describe Zuse, three decision models (4B, 9B and 27B) that train a low-rank adapter and a pointer head on frozen open-weight bases, targeting 27 European languages, and use them to study how this design behaves across languages and tasks. On a broad European suite of 11 sources excluded from training, covering 9 task groups and 27 languages, a development set that also guided model selection, the 27B model reaches 87.5% accuracy with a pooled expected calibration error of 0.016, and 0.035 when weighted per source. Post-hoc analyses of these single-seed results give three findings. First, miscalibration is structured by task rather than language: on parallel reading and topic items in 26 languages the confidence gap varies about as much as sampling noise, while across sources it spans -0.07 to +0.09, with knowledge tasks overconfident and acceptability judgements underconfident at all three sizes, so one fitted temperature cannot correct both. Second, the share of decisions that can be automated at a 5% error budget follows chance-corrected skill (Spearman 0.97 over 33 source and model pairs) more closely than raw accuracy (0.91). Third, reach beyond the training languages is set by the base model: across 210 languages, changing the training run moved scores by at most about 2 points within a size, while languages outside the base's documented list trail by 23 to 28 points at every size. On English public suites Zuse is behind Jev and Kev, including Kev models on the same bases. We also report two pitfalls in building multi-source training data: label-word collision between topic schemes, and an injection augmentation that taught a shortcut. Zuse can be tried at https://bineric.com/zuse.

</details>


### [177] [Overview of the NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval Task: Cross-modal Claim Verification in Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06090)
*Xanh Ho,André Greiner-Petter,Florian Boudin,Yun-Ang Wu,Sunisth Kumar,Tian Cheng Xia,Hiba Akbi,Tim Schopf,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文概述了NTCIR-19试点任务SciClaimEval：构建将论文真实声明与其表格/图像（含最小修改的对比版本）配对的数据集，设置"判断证据是否支持声明"与"在两条证据中选出支持者"两个子任务；22支队伍注册、14支提交177个run，系统多基于多模态大模型提示与多阶段推理。最佳系统在子任务一达98.1%配对准确率，但依赖对比对，单对独立判断的最佳系统低24.3分；开源模型经精心流水线设计可媲美专有模型，而实际场景中的单条证据核验仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文中的声明往往依赖文中表格与图像作为证据，验证声明与图表证据是否一致是科研可信性评估的关键环节，但缺少标准化基准与评测平台，故设立该试点任务以推动研究。

Method: 构建SciClaimEval数据集：取自机器学习、自然语言处理与医学领域180篇论文的真实声明，各自与表格/图像的对比版本配对（一版支持声明，一版经最小修改使其不支持）。任务分两个子任务：(1) 判断给定证据是否支持声明；(2) 在两条证据版本中选择支持者。参赛系统主要基于多模态大语言模型，采用提示工程与多阶段推理，而非任务特定微调。

Result: 22支队伍注册（NTCIR-19各任务中最多），14支提交177个run。子任务一最优系统配对准确率98.1%，但前六名均利用对比对信息；对每条声明—证据对独立判断的最佳系统分数低24.3分。经精心设计流水线的开源权重模型可与专有模型持平。

Conclusion: 对比对设置下性能已很高，但结果部分源于可利用的对比结构；在实践中最关键的单条证据核验设定下，性能显著下降，仍属未解决问题，未来需关注无需对比对的鲁棒证据验证方法。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of \emph{SciClaimEval}, a pilot task at NTCIR-19 on verifying scientific claims against the tables and figures of the papers in which they appear. Our dataset pairs authentic claims from 180 papers in machine learning, natural language processing, and medicine with contrastive versions of their tables and figures, one supporting the claim and one minimally modified so that it does not. The task comprises two subtasks: deciding whether a piece of evidence supports a claim, and selecting which of two evidence versions does. Twenty-two teams registered, the highest number among NTCIR-19 tasks, and 14 submitted 177 runs. Most systems built on multimodal large language models, relying on prompting and multi-stage inference rather than task-specific fine-tuning. On the first subtask, the best systems reached 98.1\% pair accuracy, but all six top-ranked systems relied on the contrastive pairs; the best system that judged each claim-evidence pair on its own scored 24.3 points lower. Open-weight models, embedded in carefully designed pipelines, matched proprietary ones. Yet verifying a single claim against its evidence, the setting that matters in practice, remains an open problem.

</details>


### [178] [When Does Better Confidence Improve a Knowledge-Graph Audit?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05877)
*Zehao Lin,Kai Chen,Xixuan Hao,Erenkai Zhu,Chunyu Li,Helan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究“置信度估计改善但审计收益有限”的差距，通过可修复性、选择余量与标签有效性三个角度分析，并将非自适应审计建模为预算化覆盖问题，给出最小决策相关误差统计量与一阶规划遗憾的结构性上界 Δ^cl。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM构建的知识图谱中置信度用于指导人工复核，但更好的误差预测对审计改进甚微，作者希望解释这一“预测-收益”脱节现象并刻画其成因与边界。

Method: 将非自适应审计形式化为预算化覆盖（budgeted covering），刻画最小决策相关误差统计量，并以结构质量 Δ^cl 界定保标量一阶规划的遗憾；在 Re-DocRED 上对五种抽取配置与两个跨文档图进行理想修复模拟（对齐基准标签），在测试集上做冻结确认，并在 500 篇哈希抽样训练文档上复现。

Result: 在留出跨文档图上，误差率×扇出排队策略比同动作的联合模型多留 3.98–4.74 个百分点误差；开放共享动作使联合规划器的残余误差相对其纯事实计划降低 0.89–1.43 个百分点（点估计）；联合模型与独立块模型等价性结论不确定；通用 LLM 验证在每套留出集上改善四种模型-预算配置中的三种，LLM 裁决还识别出基准可能的漏标。

Conclusion: 审计收益受可修复性、选择余量与标签有效性共同约束，单纯改进误差预测作用有限；联合规划与共享动作优于标量启发式排队，但模型等价性仍待厘清，且基准标签质量本身是审计评估的混杂因素。

Abstract: Confidence estimates guide human review in LLM-built knowledge graphs, but better error prediction can yield little improvement in an audit. We study this gap through repairability, selection headroom and label validity. Modelling non-adaptive auditing as budgeted covering, we characterise the minimal decision-relevant error statistics and bound the regret of scalar-preserving first-order planning by a structural mass $Δ^{\mathrm{cl}}$. We simulate ideal repair against benchmark-aligned labels for five extraction configurations and two cross-document graphs on Re-DocRED, followed by frozen confirmation on test and replication on 500 hash-selected training documents. On the held-out cross-document graphs, an error-rate $\times$ fan-out queue leaves 3.98-4.74 percentage points more errors than a joint model with the same actions. By comparison, opening shared actions for that joint planner reduces residual errors by 0.89-1.43 points relative to its own fact-only plans, in point estimates. Equivalence between joint and independent-block models remains inconclusive. Generic LLM verification improves three of four model-budget configurations on each held-out set. LLM adjudication identifies possible benchmark omissions.

</details>


### [179] [Human-like cross-language generalisation in deep neural speaker embeddings and its acoustic foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05384)
*Tianze Xu,Xiyang Li,Xiaoming Jiang,Volker Dellwo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究考察18个先进深度说话人嵌入模型在粤语—英语双语语音上的跨语言泛化性，发现嵌入的跨语言泛化与人类听感大体一致，且依赖共享声学线索并按语言敏感加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言泛化是深度神经网络的核心问题之一，说话人嵌入能否跨语言泛化、以及这种泛化与人类知觉和声学结构的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 采用粤语—英语双语语音，设置语内与跨语言两种条件；从18个SOTA模型提取说话人嵌入相似度，并与人类评分、声学特征进行比较，使用线性混合模型与表征相似性分析（RSA）。

Result: 嵌入表现出跨语言泛化，且与人类行为大体平行，体现准确的身份辨别和对语言切换的敏感性；该泛化依赖基本共享的声学线索，并具有语言敏感加权，与人类模式相呼应。

Conclusion: 结果为跨语言语音相似性加工中的人机对齐提供了汇聚性证据。

Abstract: Generalisability is a central question for deep neural networks, including the cross-language generalisation of speaker embeddings. This study examined whether deep neural speaker embeddings generalise across languages and how such generalisation relates to human perception and acoustic structure. Using Cantonese--English bilingual speech presented in within- and cross-language conditions, we derived similarity from 18 state-of-the-art models and compared it with human ratings and acoustic features using linear mixed models and representational similarity analysis. Embeddings exhibited cross-language generalisation that broadly paralleled human behaviour, reflecting accurate identity discrimination and sensitivity to language switching. This generalisation relied on largely shared acoustic cues with language-sensitive weighting, mirroring human patterns. Together, these findings provide converging evidence for human--machine alignment in cross-language voice similarity processing.

</details>


### [180] [SpecCompact: Accelerating LLM Agent Context Compaction with Slot-Parallel Semantic Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06876)
*Yulu An,Yifei Feng,Xiangyang Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecCompact 通过槽位并行语义推测加速 LLM agent 的上下文压缩，在保持信息的同时实现 2.04–5.63× 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 需要压缩不断增长的交互历史以管理上下文，但用大模型摘要长历史会带来显著延迟，因此压缩需在信息保留与延迟之间权衡。

Method: 基于"结构化摘要槽位可独立生成并保留信息"的观察，提出 SpecCompact：微调小型 drafter 并行生成各槽位；目标模型逐行校验并修复被拒内容；后续起草使用更新后的槽位前缀；并辅以系统优化减少重复上下文处理与模型调用。

Result: 在 AppWorld 与 τ³ 两个基准、两种目标模型上，相较 Hermes 式摘要实现 2.04–5.63× 压缩加速；下游评估显示性能权衡因模型与任务而异；Hermes 用户研究中压缩中位加速达 8.68×。

Conclusion: 槽位并行推测式压缩是一种有效的加速范式，可在近似保持摘要质量的前提下显著降低压缩延迟，但下游收益存在模型与任务依赖性。

Abstract: LLM agents use context compaction to manage growing interaction histories, but summarizing long histories with large models can introduce substantial delays. Compaction therefore requires balancing information retention against latency. We observe that structured summary slots can retain information when generated independently. Based on this observation, we present SpecCompact, a framework that accelerates compaction through slot-parallel semantic speculation. SpecCompact fine-tunes a small drafter and generates slots in parallel. The target model verifies each proposed line and repairs rejected content, while subsequent drafting uses the updated slot prefix. System optimizations further reduce repeated context processing and model calls. Across AppWorld and $τ^3$ benchmarks with two target models, SpecCompact achieves $2.04$--$5.63\times$ compaction speedups over Hermes-style summarization. Separate downstream evaluations reveal model- and task-dependent performance trade-offs. A user study within Hermes further shows an $8.68\times$ median compaction speedup.

</details>


### [181] [DS@GT ARC at CheckThat! 2026: A Verdict-Anchored Pipeline for Generating Fact-Checking Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05382)
*Hoang Thanh Thanh Truong,Shuyu Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 DS@GT ARC 团队参加 CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3（事实核查文章生成）的四阶段流水线系统，平均得分 0.428，在 9 支队伍中排名第 3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假信息传播速度超过人工事实核查员逐条验证并撰写解释文章的产能，因此需要自动化生成事实核查文章。该任务要求仅依据给定 claim、原始 verdict 与抓取到的来源文档，生成完整的事实核查文章。

Method: 系统为四阶段流水线：(1) 清洗噪声证据；(2) 检索并聚类相关来源；(3) 以原始 fact-checker verdict 为条件，驱动 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 生成文章；(4) 通过确定性后处理恢复行内引用（inline citations）。

Result: 平均得分 0.428，在 9 支队伍中位列第 3；基线平均得分为 0.272，相对提升约 57%。

Conclusion: 以判定结论为条件的中等规模指令微调模型配合证据清洗、检索聚类与确定性引用后处理，可有效超越基线完成整篇文章级事实核查生成；代码已开源。

Abstract: Misinformation spreads faster than professional fact-checkers can manually verify it and write articles explaining their verdicts. This creates a need to automate the production of fact-checking articles. This paper details work done by the DS@GT ARC team for CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3 Fact-Checking Article Generation. We seek to generate a full fact-checking article from a claim, its original verdict, and a set of scraped source documents. Our team built a four-stage pipeline that cleans noisy evidence, retrieves and clusters relevant sources, generates the article by conditioning \texttt{Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct} on the original fact-checker verdict, and recovers inline citations through deterministic post-processing. We achieved a mean score of 0.428 and ranked 3rd out of 9 teams for the CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab Task 3, compared to a baseline mean score of 0.272. Our code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/checkthat-2026-task3.

</details>


### [182] [Prospective Memory for Proactive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05731)
*Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Tianxin Wei,Weizhi Zhang,Zhichen Zeng,Philip S. Yu,Hendrik Hamann,Jingrui He,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Pro-Memory，一个面向主动式 LLM 智能体的前瞻性记忆框架，将"维持未来意图"与"决定何时执行"解耦，在延迟意图与开放式主动辅助两类任务上稳定优于代表性记忆方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体记忆系统多为回溯式（retrospective），仅保存和检索过去信息以支持当前决策；而主动式智能体还需要前瞻性记忆（prospective memory）：记住面向未来的意图，并在合适时机无需显式请求即主动实现。核心难点在于"记得正确意图"不等于"在正确时机行动"——同一意图可能因相关观察而应继续等待，也可能因后续线索而变得可执行。

Method: 受人类前瞻性记忆认知理论启发，Pro-Memory 将三件事分离：(1) 维护待办目标及其支撑证据的绑定；(2) 用新观察对照原始目标与激活条件进行评估；(3) 与智能体正在进行的行为协调，决定贡献时机与方式。此外，依据实际执行的动作或已解决的证据更新意图状态，并引入经验记忆，将已完成回合的反馈蒸馏为未来决策指导。

Result: 在两个基准（延迟意图任务与开放式主动辅助）上，跨多个 LLM 主干模型，主动任务表现相对代表性记忆方法取得一致提升，同时抑制了不必要的干预。

Conclusion: 维持意图与触发决策的解耦是构建主动式智能体记忆的关键设计，可同时提升主动性任务的完成质量与干预的精准度。

Abstract: Most memory systems for LLM agents are retrospective, preserving and retrieving past information to inform current decisions. Proactive agents, however, also require prospective memory: retaining future-directed intentions and realizing them when appropriate opportunities arise, even without explicit requests. Crucially, remembering the right intention does not guarantee acting at the right time: a related observation may call for waiting, while a later cue may make the same intention actionable. Inspired by cognitive theories of human prospective memory, we introduce Pro-Memory, a memory framework that separates maintaining future intentions from deciding when and how to realize them. Pro-Memory preserves pending objectives linked to their supporting evidence, assesses new observations against their original goals and activation conditions, and coordinates appropriate contributions with the agent's ongoing actions. It further updates intention states based on actions actually issued or evidence of resolution, while an experience memory distills feedback from completed episodes into guidance for future decisions. Experiments on two benchmarks spanning delayed-intention tasks and open-ended proactive assistance demonstrate consistent improvements in proactive task performance over representative memory approaches across multiple LLM backbones, while limiting unnecessary interventions.

</details>


### [183] [Cognitive Bias Signatures in Search-Equipped LLM Agents: A Stage-Resolved Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05720)
*Yanru Xiao,Yingwei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建受控检索实验框架，考察初始结果顺序、历史失败记录与初始假设如何影响LLM智能体的检索策略与最终答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索能帮助LLM智能体纠错，但其初始条件可能决定其找到并使用哪些证据；现有研究难以区分顺序效应与文档构成差异、失败历史效应与上下文长度效应。

Method: 借鉴认知偏差范式，设计受控检索框架，固定问题与文档集合，操纵结果顺序、先前工作记录与初始假设；用匹配对照组分离顺序效应与文档组成差异、失败工作效应与上下文长度，跨10个模型和1000个合成检索世界追踪查询、检索文档、文档打开行为与最终答案。

Result: 主动检索时最终答案对初始证据顺序比全量呈现证据时更敏感；死胡同历史相较匹配的中性历史会降低策略切换，效应超出上下文长度；错误假设下分阶段测量可区分"检索或打开减少"与"打开反证后仍持续的错误"；探索性干预显示更频繁打开反证可能与准确率下降同时出现，而准确率也可在打开行为几乎不变时提升。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的检索与推理受初始条件系统性影响，且这些影响可通过分阶段行为测量加以定位；提升准确率未必依赖增加反证查阅。

Abstract: Search can help LLM agents correct mistakes, but their starting conditions may shape which evidence they find and use. Drawing on cognitive-bias paradigms, we develop a controlled search harness to examine result order, records of prior work, and initial hypotheses while holding each question and document collection fixed. Matched controls distinguish order effects from differences in document composition and the effects of failed work from context length. Across 10 models and 1,000 synthetic search worlds, we track queries, retrieved documents, document openings, and final answers. Our comparisons suggest that final answers are more sensitive to initial evidence order during active search than when all evidence is supplied in full. Dead-end histories can reduce strategy switching relative to matched neutral histories, indicating an effect beyond context length. Under wrong hypotheses, stage measurements distinguish reduced retrieval or opening from errors that persist after counter-evidence is opened. Exploratory interventions show that opening counter-evidence more often can coincide with lower accuracy, while accuracy can improve with little change in opening.

</details>


### [184] [Twists and Turns: A Graph-Based Benchmark and Framework for Long-Term Memory in Exploration Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06414)
*Chongyuan Dai,Yaling Shen,Zirui Song,Junfeng Liao,Jinpeng Hu,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出长程探索任务(Long-term Exploration)概念，将探索过程建模为图结构，构建基准LExMem（224段对话、2018个问答）并设计图记忆框架LExGraph。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研、软件开发等长期问题求解沿试探-迭代轨迹推进，需要模型不仅记住事实，还要记住方案如何被中间结果检验、早期尝试如何被修订或重启；而现有长时记忆研究仅关注事实回忆与更新，忽略了探索过程的相互关联性。

Method: 观察到探索过程天然形成图（分支方法与修订/回溯相连），据此把探索过程建模为图，并引入演化机制提升结构复杂度以生成数据；同时提出LExGraph框架，将对话增量组织为互联的记忆节点，通过混合检索与邻居扩展进行证据召回。

Result: LExMem对现有基于检索和基于记忆的系统构成显著挑战；LExGraph取得更优性能，并在外部基准上有效泛化。

Conclusion: 以图为统一抽象可同时指导长程探索记忆的评测与建模，为长时记忆研究从事实层面拓展到过程层面提供了基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Long-term problem-solving processes, such as scientific research and software development, proceed along an evolving trial-and-error trajectory toward a central goal. We term these \textit{exploration tasks}, which require models to remember not only facts but also how solutions are tested against intermediate outcomes and early attempts are continually revised or revived. However, existing long-term memory research focuses on factual recall and updates, overlooking the interconnected nature of exploration. We observe that exploration processes naturally form graphs, with branching approaches linked by revisions and reconsiderations. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{LExMem}, a graph-guided benchmark designed to evaluate \underline{\textsc{Mem}}ory in \underline{\textsc{L}}ong-term \underline{\textsc{Ex}}ploration tasks. We model exploration processes as graphs and introduce an evolutionary mechanism to increase their structural complexity, yielding 224 dialogues and 2,018 question-answer instances derived from graph structures. Building on the same insight, we propose \textsc{LExGraph}, a graph-based memory framework that incrementally organizes dialogues into interconnected memory nodes and retrieves evidence through hybrid retrieval and neighbor expansion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{LExMem} poses a significant challenge to existing retrieval- and memory-based systems, whereas \textsc{LExGraph} achieves superior performance and generalizes effectively to external benchmarks. Codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/LExMem.

</details>


### [185] [Persona Perspectives: Adopting Viewpoints Reorganizes Representations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06848)
*Austin C. Kozlowski,Avital Mintz,Madison Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文考察人格（persona）提示是否改变LLM潜在空间的表征几何。作者提取自由派与保守派人格下9,000余词的表示及33条语义轴，发现人格条件会沿意识形态一致方向系统性地改变词的投影和语义轴对齐；且无persona训练的探针、SAE分类器在人格条件下给出偏斜的真伪与道德评分，提示常规表征提取可能削弱监控可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针与稀疏自编码器等特征监控方法普遍假设特征在不同语境下保持一致的表示；但LLM能灵活切换"人格"（不同的概念关系系统）。若人格改变了潜在空间的表征几何，则跨语境迁移的监控器可能失效，这一前提亟待检验。

Method: 对采用自由派与保守派政治人格的模型提取9,000余个常用词的表示与33条语义轴，并与两个中性基线对比；分析词在语义轴上的投影及语义轴之间的对齐关系。随后构建三类无persona训练的分类器（线性探针、SAE分类器、基于人工选取词表示的分类器），测试其在persona条件下对真伪性与道德性的评判。

Result: 人格条件既系统性地改变词在语义轴上的投影，也改变语义轴之间的对齐，且变化方向与指定意识形态一致；三类分类器在persona条件下产生分歧结果，对有争议陈述给出政治上偏斜的真伪与道德评分。

Conclusion: 按常规方式提取的内部表征在模型采用人格时可能呈现不同的几何关系，从而削弱基于探针与SAE的监控有效性；特征监控需考虑人格/语境导致的表征非平稳性。

Abstract: Common methods for monitoring features in large language models (LLMs) such as linear probes and sparse autoencoders (SAEs) assume that features maintain a consistent representation across contexts. LLMs, however, can flexibly adopt different perspectives or "personas" that suggest different underlying systems of conceptual relations. In this paper, we examine whether prompting an LLM to adopt a persona alters the representational geometry of the model's latent space. We extract representations of over 9,000 common words and 33 semantic axes from models adopting liberal and conservative political personas and compare these against two neutral baselines. We find that persona conditioning systematically alters both the projections of words onto semantic axes and the alignments among the axes themselves in directions consistent with the specified ideology. We then test the implications of this reorganization for two safety-relevant features: truthfulness and morality. Linear probes, SAE-based classifiers, and classifiers built from manually selected word representations, all trained without a persona, produce divergent results when applied to models conditioned on political personas, producing politically slanted ratings of truth and morality on controversial statements. These findings suggest that internal representations, as they are conventionally extracted, can exhibit different geometric relations when the model adopts personas, potentially undermining monitoring efforts.

</details>


### [186] [Before You Continue: Evaluating Time-Sensitive Safety Awareness in Conversational Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06839)
*Peidi Dong,Yunze Xiao,Yu-Lin Tsai,Yichen Zheng,Heyuan Huang,Weihao Xuan,Mark Dredze,Jen-tse Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TASC基准，评估LLM助手在对话请求中能否识别并应用隐含的时间约束；13个模型仅在30.5%的违规情形下正确处理时间约束，且62.1%的错误在其已正确计算时间间隔时仍发生，显示"时间推理"与"约束应用"之间存在显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手越来越多地处理含时间依赖约束的日常任务，但用户在后续请求中往往不再重述这些约束，助手需自主识别并调整建议。现有评测缺乏对此能力的针对性考察，作者因此构建专门基准来量化该缺陷。

Method: 构建TASC（Temporal Action Safety in Conversation）基准：由128个模板派生出3,418条输入；另设33个场景，用于对比模型"计算已流逝时间"与"应用该时间信息"的能力。在此基础上对13个模型进行评测，并分析显式提供时间信息后的表现变化。

Result: 在建议动作违反规则的案例中，助手仅在30.5%的情况下考虑时间约束；同时很少出现多余的过度警告（误报率低）。更关键的是，在模型已正确计算时间间隔的情形中，仍有62.1%未能应用该约束；即便显式给出时间信息，也仅能修复约一半的失败。

Conclusion: LLM在时间推理与其在对话建议中的应用之间存在明显鸿沟：正确的时间计算并不保证约束的落地执行，仅补充时间信息不足以弥合该差距。未来需要针对"推理—应用"衔接的建模与训练方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) assistants increasingly help users with everyday activities that involve time-dependent constraints. However, users may not mention these constraints in later requests, requiring assistants to recognize and adjust their advice accordingly. To evaluate such capability, we introduce TASC (Temporal Action Safety in Conversation), a benchmark that evaluates whether LLMs apply time-dependent constraints when responding to conversational requests. TASC consists of 3,418 inputs derived from 128 templates, along with a separate set of 33 scenarios designed to compare models' ability to calculate elapsed time with their ability to apply that information. Across 13 models, assistants account for time-dependent constraints in only 30.5% of cases where the proposed action violates a rule, while rarely warning users unnecessarily. More importantly, models fail to apply these constraints in 62.1% of cases despite correctly calculating the elapsed time. Explicitly providing the timing resolves only about half of these failures, revealing a gap between temporal reasoning and its application in conversational advice.

</details>


### [187] [RAGCompass: Generalizable Retriever Routing via Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05596)
*Valentin Six,Mufei Li,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGCompass 以"阅读检索到的段落集合本身"来为查询路由检索器，通过对证据集答案的对比微调重排器实现，在答案F1上较最佳固定检索器提升28%，并在未见语料/检索器/读者下保持大部分增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 没有任何单一检索器在所有查询上最优，因此需要按查询选择最合适的检索器。现有路由器仅依赖查询特征加固定检索器池，无法处理未见过的检索器，且在新语料上按设计即失效。

Method: RAGCompass 直接读取各检索器返回的段落集合（而非仅编码查询），使来自陌生检索器或语料的段落仍可与其他段落直接比较，从而在 query-only 映射失效处保持路由有效性。方法为：针对每个证据集产生的答案做对比式微调的重排器（贡献主要增益），再加一个小型比较头（带来中等增益）；两个组件均无可检索器专属参数。

Result: 答案F1较最佳固定检索器相对提升28%，优于融合基线、三个 query-only 路由器以及一个经提示的8B语言模型。泛化实验中，在未见语料、未见检索器或未见读者下仍保留大部分增益，而 query-only 基线退化至最佳固定检索器水平或更低。

Conclusion: 以证据集为输入的对比微调重排路由方案是可行且可迁移的，可在检索器与语料变化时保持路由优势，弥补仅依赖查询特征路由器的泛化缺陷。

Abstract: Different retrievers exist for retrieval-augmented generation, yet none performs best for all queries. Therefore, the key question is: given a query and retrieved passages from different retrievers, which one is most suited for that query? Existing routers usually make predictions from query-specific features and a fixed pool of retrievers; such routers have no output for an unseen retriever and are incapable by design on a new corpus. To fill these gaps, RAGCompass reads the retrieved passage sets themselves: passages from an unfamiliar retriever or corpus stay directly comparable to other passages, so routing stays well designed where a query-only map would fail. We contrastively fine-tune a reranker against the answer each evidence set produces, which accounts for most of the gain; a small comparison head adds a moderate improvement, and neither component has retriever-specific parameters. RAGCompass improves answer F1 by 28% relative to the best fixed retriever, and outperforms fusion baselines, three query-only routers, and a prompted 8B language model. Generalization experiments show that RAGCompass keeps most of its gain under an unseen corpus, retriever, or reader, while query-only baselines fall to the best fixed retriever or below.

</details>


### [188] [Accelerating Long-Context Decoding in Large-Scale Hybrid LLMs Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06133)
*João Eduardo Batista,Emil Vatai,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练或改权重的方法，将混合模型残余的全注意力层替换为"滑窗+固定前缀+按预算选取的Top-K相关token"的组合稀疏注意力，并用多目标搜索为每层确定窗口大小与预算，在六个混合模型上保持21个长上下文数据集精度，同时显著加速长上下文解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的全注意力层使每个生成token的成本随序列增长而递增，长上下文任务因此变慢。混合模型用线性注意力替换大部分全注意力层以降低成本，但剩余的全注意力层在长上下文解码时仍占主导，成为瓶颈。

Method: 在推理阶段对剩余全注意力层施加组合稀疏注意力：每层只关注三部分——近期token的滑动窗口、固定的前缀（pinned prefix）、以及上下文中按固定预算选出的最相关token；窗口大小与预算按层设定，通过多目标搜索算法（兼顾精度与速度）优化得到。

Result: 在3个系列共6个混合模型（4B至397B参数）上，所选配置在21个长上下文数据集上保持了基线精度。解码64k/256k token时：Qwen3.5-4B加速最高1.26x/2.22x，Qwen3.5-122B-A10B为1.20x/2.23x，Ornith-1.0-397B为1.16x/2.06x。

Conclusion: 组合稀疏注意力可作为纯推理期的即插即用加速手段，在无需训练与权重修改的前提下消除混合模型长上下文解码的剩余瓶颈，并在精度无损条件下获得随上下文长度增长而放大的加速比。

Abstract: The full-attention layers of large language models (LLMs) make every generated token more expensive than the previous one, slowing models down on long-context tasks. Hybrid models reduce this cost by replacing most full-attention layers with linear-attention layers, but the first still dominate decoding at long contexts. We accelerate these remaining layers without any training or weight changes using combined sparse attention: each layer attends to a sliding window of recent tokens, a pinned prefix, and a fixed budget of most relevant tokens in the context. The window size and budget are found on a per-layer basis using a multi-objective search algorithm. Across six hybrid models from three families, from 4B to 397B parameters, the selected configurations preserve their baseline model's accuracy on 21 long-context datasets. Decoding 64k/256k tokens becomes up to 1.26x/2.22x faster on Qwen3.5-4B, 1.20x/2.23x on Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, and 1.16x/2.06x on Ornith-1.0-397B.

</details>


### [189] [Familiar letters, not related languages, let unseen languages into a shared space of meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06006)
*Yuliang Liu,Xinyu Wang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出无分词器的视觉Transformer模型GlyphMind，将文本作为像素读取，可在任意书写系统间呈现句子，并在27种书写系统上预训练以对齐译文；实验表明，让模型接纳一门未见语言的关键是“熟悉的字形”而非“亲缘语言”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量语言有书面文本但几乎没有译文；模型能否仅从无译文文本学习一门语言、其收益究竟来自“相关语言”还是“熟悉字母”尚不清楚，且二者在现实中通常重合，难以区分。

Method: 构建GlyphMind：无分词器（tokenizer-free）的视觉Transformer，将文本直接作为像素图像读取，使同一句子可被渲染为任意书写系统；在27种书写系统上做译文对齐预训练；随后迁移到无译文的老挝语（语言与文字均未见），并在“改写为不熟悉字母”的条件及桑塔利语（Devanagari与自源文字）上做对照实验。

Result: 在无译文老挝语上适配后，检索英文译文的成功率是最强简单基线的7倍；改写成不熟悉字母后，即便其近亲泰语在预训练中出现，老挝语也无法进入共享语义空间；桑塔利语在Devanagari（因印地语而熟悉）中可进入，而在其自源文字中不能。

Conclusion: 熟悉的字形而非语言亲缘性是未见语言进入共享语义空间的通道；将文本转写（transliteration）为熟悉书写系统，或可让多语言模型覆盖缺乏译文的语言。

Abstract: Hundreds of languages have written text but almost no translations, and whether a model learning one from untranslated text builds on a related language or familiar letters is unclear: they usually coincide. Here we show that familiar letters, not a related language, let an unseen language into a shared space of meaning. GlyphMind, a tokenizer-free vision transformer that reads text as pixels and lets any sentence be shown in any script, was pretrained to align translations across 27 writing systems. Adapted on untranslated Lao, a language and script it had never seen, it retrieved English translations seven times as often as the strongest simple baseline. Rewritten in unfamiliar letters, Lao did not enter although its close relative Thai was in pretraining; Santali, which had none, entered in Devanagari, familiar from Hindi, but not in its own script. Transliteration into familiar scripts may thus open multilingual models to languages without translations.

</details>


### [190] [When Every Choice Is Correct: A Causal Study of Numerical Preference in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06025)
*Hongnian Huang,Hongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文以"1–30之间选数"作为受控任务，通过扰动探针与梯度归因定位神经元并做激活干预，揭示模型对数值偏好的处理如何跨层、跨token持续影响后续计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在合法取值范围内仍会偏爱特定数字，作者希望把这种数值偏好当作可控案例，研究偏好信息在各层与各token间的处理过程，以及早期干预为何会持续影响后续生成。

Method: 在Qwen3-4B上用扰动探针和梯度归因筛选候选神经元做激活干预；采用生成前的编辑，结合选择性缓存替换与注意力输出替换，追踪编辑对第二位数字的持续影响；再通过将后续MLP输出钳制为编辑运行时的值，分析下游计算对局部分支效果的调节；并在18个上下文与多组改写提示上做回溯估计与稳健性检验。

Result: 编辑使答案由17变为15，但两次运行首个数字均为1；替换单个注意力头输出与替换其余头输出产生相近的即时读出贡献，二者都能恢复17，但对7与5的最终对数几率效应不同；将后续MLP输出钳制后，效应差距由0.119 nat降至0.0025 nat。回溯估计能复现大部分概率变化，却漏掉答案切换；提示测试显示影响与答案切换可分离：一个留出提示保留影响但不切换答案，12个新措辞中仅1个满足reader/MLP检验，且此时联合MLP钳制仍缩小效应差距而答案保持17。

Conclusion: 数值选择可作为研究持久状态与条件性下游处理的受控案例；后续计算会改变相似局部贡献对续写的影响，但结果不确立自然数值偏好的来源或完整回路。

Abstract: When a language model chooses a number between 1 and 30, every integer in the range is valid, yet the model can favor particular answers. We use numerical choice to study how preference is processed across layers and tokens. Perturbation probing and gradient attribution nominate neurons for activation interventions. In a selected Qwen3-4B case, a pre-answer edit changes 17 to 15 while both runs first emit 1. After the edit ends, selective cache and attention-output replacements trace part of its continuing influence on the second digit. Replacing one attention head's output or the other heads' outputs jointly produces similar immediate readout contributions; both interventions restore 17 but have different final log-odds effects on 7 versus 5. Holding later multilayer perceptron (MLP) outputs at their values from the edited run without either replacement reduces this effect gap from 0.119 to 0.0025 nat. Later computation therefore changes how similar local contributions affect the continuation. Retrospective estimates across eighteen contexts capture much of the probability movement but miss the selected answer switch. Prompt tests also separate influence from answer changes: one held-out prompt retains influence without switching, and only one of twelve fresh wordings qualifies for the reader/MLP test; there, joint MLP clamping again reduces the gap between the two reader effects while the answer remains 17. These results use numerical choice as a controlled case for studying persistent state and conditional downstream processing; they do not establish the origin or a complete circuit of natural numerical preference.

</details>


### [191] [Replicability, Reproducibility, and Robustness in Political and Social Science Research with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06639)
*Joan C. Timoneda,Sebastián Vallejo Vera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该章针对大语言模型（LLM）研究的核心可复现性问题，区分可复制性、可复现性与稳健性，提出基于结果分布的概率性标准，并通过标准化提示脚本、模型内变异检验与误差结构分析，揭示测量选择可逆转下游回归系数符号；最终以开放权重模型的堆叠集成提升校正步骤的精度同时保持估计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出具有概率性且随模型与版本变化，对下游统计结果产生显著影响；传统要求"输出完全一致"的可复现标准不适用于LLM研究，且现有基于设计的校正方法虽能恢复有效性却牺牲估计精度。

Method: 区分replicability、reproducibility与robustness三个概念；提出以结果分布而非相同输出为基础的概率性标准；构建标准化提示脚本；对编码器与解码器分别检验模型内变异；发展误差结构分析以诊断模型出错位置及误差是否一致；在三个数据集上检验测量选择的效应；采用金标签子样本进行基于设计的校正；并提出开放权重模型的堆叠集成（stacked ensemble）用于校正步骤。

Result: 测量选择可逆转下游回归系数的符号；现有基于设计的校正配合金标签子样本可恢复估计有效性，但降低估计精度；所提出的开放权重模型堆叠集成在校正步骤中提升了精度，同时保持估计有效性。

Conclusion: LLM研究的可复现性应以结果分布的概率性标准来评价而非追求输出同一性；误差结构分析有助于诊断模型偏差；开放权重模型的堆叠集成提供了一种在保持统计有效性的前提下提高校正精度的可行方案。

Abstract: This chapter addresses the central reproducibility problem of research with large language models. LLM output is probabilistic and varies across models and versions, which can have significant effects on downstream statistical results. The chapter distinguishes between replicability, reproducibility, and robustness, and proposes a probabilistic standard based on the distribution of results, not identical outputs. It introduces a standardized prompt script, tests within-model variation for encoders and decoders, and develops error-structure analysis to diagnose where models err and whether their errors align. Using three datasets, the chapter shows that measurement choice can reverse the sign of downstream regression coefficients. Existing design-based corrections with gold-labeled subsamples restore validity, but come at the cost of estimate precision. The main contribution of the chapter is a stacked ensemble of open-weight models that improves precision in the correction step while retaining estimate validity.

</details>


### [192] [CRESiST: Self-Enhancing Explorative Policy Learning for Simultaneous Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06501)
*Chenyang Le,Pei Zhang,Yuxiang Chen,Bing Han,Derek F. Wong,Baosong Yang,Yanmin Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于全模态LLM的端到端交错读写框架，实现全KV缓存复用与免分割无限长流式推理，并用Actor-Critic自增强流水线学习最优读写策略，在句级与文档级基准上刷新质量-延迟帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based SimulST主要依赖前缀增量式生成，存在KV缓存重复计算的问题，且需外部VAD来处理无界语音流，成为质量与延迟平衡的瓶颈。

Method: 将语音块与文本token交错排列，构建端到端交错读写架构以复用全部KV缓存并原生支持免分割无限长推理；引入Actor-Critic自增强流水线：RNN-T Actor动态采样读写轨迹，LLM Critic以内在语义与延迟奖励评估路径，将最优策略蒸馏回Actor，摆脱刚性离线对齐。

Result: 在句级（FLEURS、CoVoST2）与文档级（MuST-C、ACL 60/60）基准上建立新的SOTA质量-延迟帕累托前沿，显著优于级联与静态对齐基线。

Conclusion: 交错读写+全KV复用的全模态LLM架构结合强化学习式流式策略学习，可有效突破前缀增量范式的缓存冗余与分段依赖限制，实现高质量低延迟的无限长流式同传。

Abstract: Simultaneous Speech Translation (SimulST) requires balancing translation quality and latency. While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in offline translation, current LLM-based SimulST systems predominantly rely on prefix-incremental generation. This paradigm suffers from redundant Key-Value cache recomputation and necessitates external Voice Activity Detection to process unbounded streams. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose an end-to-end interleaved read-write framework built upon an omni-modal LLM. By interleaving speech chunks and text tokens, our architecture achieves full KV cache reuse, natively supporting segmentation-free, infinite-length inference. Furthermore, to learn optimal streaming policies without rigid offline alignments, we introduce a novel Actor-Critic self-enhancement pipeline. An RNN-T Actor dynamically samples read-write trajectories, while an LLM-based Critic evaluates these paths via intrinsic semantic and latency rewards, distilling the optimal policy back to the Actor. Extensive experiments on sentence-level (FLEURS, CoVoST2) and document-level (MuST-C, ACL 60/60) benchmarks demonstrate that our model establishes new state-of-the-art quality-latency Pareto frontiers, substantially outperforming existing cascaded and static-alignment baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Grounded but Not Immune: Confirmation Bias in Agent-as-a-judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05662)
*Drake Benjamin Brown,Khushbu Pahwa,Yubin Ge,Salvatore Romeo,Akhil Agnihotri,Yi Zhang,Xiaofei Ma,Daniele Bonadiman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文揭示：带工具的 Agent-as-a-Judge 虽能自行收集证据，却不能免于确认偏误——一条来自权威、与正确结论无证据关联的虚假预筛笔记，可在4个评审模型、5个数据集上翻转其最多40%的正确判定，即便评审已收集到决定性证据。作者将其作为确认偏误的受控诊断，并剖析翻转行为的三类失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式智能体任务的评估日益依赖 Agent-as-a-Judge（给评审模型工具与完整轨迹以对照环境核验结果），其前提是“自采证据即被证据锚定”。作者质疑该前提，探究外部提示线索能否在评审已获决定性证据的情况下仍造成系统性误判，并把这类线索当作研究确认偏误的诊断工具而非对抗攻击。

Method: 构造受控线索：一条虚假预筛笔记，具有权威归属但不含指向正确结论的证据信息；在4个评审模型、5个数据集上注入并观察判定翻转率；对轨迹过程进行行为变化分类，并将有害翻转归因到三类失败模式：过度信任（先核验并得出正确结论，随后仍让步）、幻觉（为翻转虚构支持证据）、调查不足（在首个印证性信号处停止）。

Result: 单一虚假预筛笔记使工具型评审最多40%的正确判定被翻转，且其已在轨迹中收集到决定性证据；失败模式分布随模型规模而异：两个 Claude 评审以过度信任为主（占有害翻转的67%–75%），较小评审的三类错误模式分布更分散；轻量级缓解措施可削弱但无法消除该偏误。

Conclusion: “自采证据”并不构成对确认偏误的免疫；判决可靠性取决于评审如何对待权威性但无证据力的事前线索。作者的核心贡献是给出轨迹层面的行为变化分类与失败模式归因框架，为 Agent-as-a-Judge 的可靠性评估与缓解设计提供可操作诊断，并指出简单缓解不足、需针对过度信任与提前终止调查等机制进行干预。

Abstract: Evaluating open-ended agent tasks increasingly falls to an Agent-as-a-Judge: an evaluator handed tools and the agent's full trajectory so it can verify a result against the environment. The premise is that a judge that gathers its own evidence should be grounded by it. We show that this grounding does not immunize the judge against confirmation bias: a single false pre-screening note, attributed to an authority and carrying no evidential bearing on the correct verdict, overturns up to 40% of a tool-using judge's correct verdicts across four judges and five datasets, even though the judge collects decisive evidence. The shift is driven by specific properties of the cue. We treat this cue as a controlled diagnostic of confirmation bias rather than an adversarial attack. Our central contribution is to explain how confirmation bias changes the judge's behavior in a trajectory. We classify changes in judge behavior and attribute changes to one of three failure modes: over-trust (verify, conclude correctly, then defer), hallucination (fabricate support for the flip), and under-investigation (stop at the first corroborating signal). For the two Claude judges, the failure is predominantly over-trust (67-75% of harmful flips), while smaller judges have error modes spread across all three. Lightweight mitigations blunt but never remove the bias.

</details>


### [194] [Quantifying Trait-State Hierarchy in EEG Foundation Model and Signal Processing Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05652)
*Anusha Madapura,Aditya Kommineni,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文量化了EEG嵌入中"特质(Trait)"与"状态(State)"信息的编码方式，发现多数EEG特征呈"宏观特质层级"结构（特质级聚类内含微观状态），该结构损害状态可分性；通过归一化削弱特质信息可提升状态分类平衡准确率约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号同时包含相对恒定的特质信息（身份、年龄、性别）与响应刺激的状态信息（视觉、听觉、运动）。目前对不同EEG建模特征（信号处理与基础模型）如何编码状态与特质缺乏理解，而这一理解对BCI解码性能至关重要。

Method: 在BCI数据集试次上，使用层级度量与kNN纯度量化EEG嵌入所捕获的特质级与状态级特征；采用线性探针评估状态可分性；探索归一化方法以减少嵌入中的特质特异信息；并比较微调前后嵌入空间层级结构的转变。

Result: 多数EEG特征形成宏观特质层级（特质级聚类，微观状态嵌套其内）；该层级结构导致状态可分性次优，负面影响EEG基础模型的BCI性能；归一化使状态分类平衡准确率提升约10%；微调后嵌入空间由宏观特质层级转为宏观状态层级，从而改善状态解码。

Conclusion: EEG嵌入的结构（特质-状态层级关系）是影响BCI解码性能的关键因素；通过归一化抑制特质信息或将嵌入空间重构为宏观状态层级，可有效提升状态解码表现，为EEG基础模型的设计与训练策略提供指导。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) measures brain activity through recording electrical potentials of the scalp. EEG recordings capture two distinct sets of characteristics in the signal: (1) \emph{Traits} -- information that remains relatively constant over the course of a recording session, such as subject identity, age, and sex; (2) \emph{States} -- signatures that encode responses to external stimuli, including visual, auditory, and motor information. There is limited understanding of how different EEG modeling features (signal processing and foundation models) encode state and trait characteristics. In this work, we quantify state- and trait-level features captured by EEG embeddings using a hierarchy metric and kNN purity on Brain-Computer Interface dataset trials, and find that a majority of EEG features form a macro-trait hierarchy (embeddings form trait-level clusters with micro-states within). Our linear probe results indicate that this hierarchy structure leads to suboptimal state distinguishability, negatively impacting BCI performance of EEG foundation models. To mitigate this, we explore normalization techniques that reduce trait-specific information in embeddings and provide a $\sim$ 10\% improvement in balanced accuracy for state classification. Additionally, we observe that when fine-tuning models for downstream state decoding, embedding spaces transform from macro-trait to macro-state hierarchies, which in turn lead to better state decoding.

</details>


### [195] [Challenge-Aware Knowledge Recruitment with Meta-Agent Supervision for Medical Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05393)
*Riccardo Riccio,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"挑战感知自适应招募"（challenge-aware adaptive recruitment）：由外部 CHALLENGER 检视推理轨迹中的具体事实/专长缺口，触发按需检索或中途招募专家；在 MEDAGENTSBENCH 硬子集上验证了"何时"与"为何"招募外部资源比"从问题本身预先招募"更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在标准医学 QA 表现强，但在更难推理任务上退化，多智能体辩论常打不过强单模型提示。作者认为关键缺陷在于现有系统仅凭问题本身选择专家与检索知识，而非依据推理过程中涌现的缺口。

Method: 引入外部 CHALLENGER 审阅演化中的推理轨迹，识别具体事实或专长缺口，并据此触发两类自适应干预：定向知识检索与讨论中途的专科医生招募。在 MEDAGENTSBENCH 硬子集（9 个生物医学数据集、862 题）上评估，配套消融（检索时机、缺口特定查询信号）、实时网页检索实验及多专家骨干上的招募实验。

Result: 预先检索几乎无收益，挑战感知的过程内检索更优；多专家骨干上 9 个数据集中 8 个提升，较 Challenger Only 提升 6 个百分点。消融显示检索时机与缺口特定查询信号均有贡献，实时网页检索表明时机优势跨检索后端成立。招募方面：自触发自适应不可靠，而 Challenger 触发招募将 GPT-4.1-mini 的 MedQA 准确率由 46% 提至 54%，且在所有泛化数据集上方向为正。简单叠加检索与专家招募会降性能。

Conclusion: 何时以及为何招募外部资源是多智能体医学推理的关键设计选择；不同自适应干预通道需协调而非简单叠加。

Abstract: Large language models perform strongly on standard medical QA but degrade on harder reasoning tasks, where multi-agent debate often fails to beat strong single-model prompting. We argue that a key limitation is that existing systems select experts and retrieve knowledge from the question alone, rather than from gaps that emerge during reasoning. We propose challenge-aware adaptive recruitment: an external CHALLENGER inspects the evolving reasoning trace and identifies specific factual or expertise gaps. These signals trigger targeted knowledge retrieval or mid-discussion specialist recruitment. On the MEDAGENTSBENCH hard subset (862 questions across nine biomedical datasets), upfront retrieval provides little benefit, while challenge-aware in-between retrieval performs better; on a multi-specialist backbone it improves accuracy on 8 of 9 datasets and adds 6 percentage points over Challenger Only. Ablations indicate benefits from both retrieval timing and gap-specific query signals, and a live web-search experiment shows that the timing advantage persists across retrieval backends. For specialist recruitment, self-triggered adaptation is unreliable, whereas Challenger-triggered recruitment raises GPT-4.1-mini MedQA accuracy from 46% to 54%, with positive direction on all tested generalization datasets. Naively combining retrieval and specialist recruitment degrades performance, highlighting the need to coordinate adaptive intervention channels. These results show that when and why external resources are recruited is a key design choice in multi-agent medical reasoning.

</details>


### [196] [Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05391)
*Manu Agrawal,Shrey Nagpal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Agent Harness Awareness"架构，通过主机在执行前提供经过验证的简短状态快照，解决模型"操作盲区"(operational blindness)问题，并以ToolRoute基准实证验证可显著降低策略遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具描述仅说明动作"能做什么"，却不揭示该动作此刻是否健康或适宜；执行框架(harness)通过监控、追踪和近期失败已掌握这些信息，但选择下一动作的模型无法获取，形成"操作盲区"(operational blindness)。

Method: 提出Agent Harness Awareness的可信分离设计：主机在关键动作前提供小而新、经验证的状态快照，同时保留测量、执行、评估与最终调度的权威。以ToolRoute受控隔离基准测试工具-网络实例，固定任务与候选语义，仅改变模型可得的操作信息；包括216次决策消融实验（GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Sonnet 5、Gemini 3.7 Flash）及27次决策的端到端复现（真实本地TCP/HTTP执行、OpenTelemetry客户端span、Toxiproxy注入故障）。

Result: 消融实验中，经验证的工具健康状态使平均可观测策略遗憾较同形态中性对照降低96.9%（4,252 ms→131 ms），单次调度完成率由69.4%升至95.8%。端到端复现中，平均遗憾由8,956 ms降至0 ms（零额外可观测操作成本），真实动作在模型×故障9例中完成9例，中性对照仅5例。

Conclusion: 两项研究确立了Agent Harness Awareness的工具-网络实例：选定的主机遥测能实质提升派发前的操作决策质量。运行时状态、硬件压力、经济性、重试感知恢复与异构选择仍是同一架构的开放实证扩展方向。

Abstract: Tool descriptions tell an agent what an action can do, but they do not necessarily reveal whether that action is healthy or suitable at the moment of use. Execution harnesses often possess this information through monitors, traces, and recent failures while the model choosing the next action does not. We call this gap operational blindness. We introduce Agent Harness Awareness, a trust-separated design in which the host supplies a small, fresh, verified state snapshot before a consequential action while retaining measurement, enforcement, evaluation, and final dispatch authority. We test its tools-and-network instance with ToolRoute, a controlled isolation benchmark that holds the task and candidate semantics constant and varies the operational information available to the model. In a 216-decision ablation across GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Sonnet 5, and Gemini 3.7 Flash, verified tool-health state reduced mean observable policy regret by 96.9% against a same-shape neutral control (4,252 ms to 131 ms) and raised single-dispatch completion from 69.4% to 95.8%. In a separate 27-decision end-to-end replication with live local TCP/HTTP execution, OpenTelemetry client spans, and Toxiproxy-injected faults, verified state reduced mean regret from 8,956 ms to 0 ms (zero excess observable operational cost) and completed the live action in 9/9 model-by-fault cases versus 5/9 for the neutral control. These studies establish the tools-and-network instance of Agent Harness Awareness: selected host telemetry materially improves pre-dispatch operational choice. Runtime state, hardware pressure, economics, retry-aware recovery, and heterogeneous selection remain open empirical extensions of the same architecture.

</details>


### [197] [From Handcrafted to Generative: RLVR-Optimized Icon Generation for Alexa+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06123)
*Rohan Sequeira,Matthew Rosenberg,Amin Mahmoudabadbozchelou,Oleg Poliannikov,Jonathan Rubin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一条已上线的自动化流水线，可由文本描述直接生成矢量动画图标，并依据当前对话内容向用户推荐相关动画；其质量瓶颈在于首个阶段——图像生成。作者用目标检测式验证器（objective verifiers）自动判别违反设计规范的生成结果，并据此以RLVR微调flow matching图像生成模型，使独立VLM评审下的通过率由15.7%提升至58.7%（3.7倍），模型与验证器均已进入生产流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alexa+带屏设备在对话中展示动画图标以强化语义表达，但原始图标由设计师逐一手绘，成品精致却耗时且无法覆盖开放式对话中的广泛话题。因此需要规模化自动生成方案；而流水线的整体质量由第一阶段的图像生成决定，后续向量化、样式化与动画等阶段均依赖其输出。此外图标需在小型画布上按严格设计规范渲染，导致生成结果常不可用于生产；同时缺少符合约束的线稿图标数据集，使监督微调不可行。

Method: 构建自动化流水线，将图标创作分解为图像生成、语义分解、向量化、样式化与动画等串行阶段。针对第一阶段，设计客观验证器以自动标记违反设计规范的生成结果，避免依赖人工标注数据；并以这些可验证奖励对flow matching图像生成模型进行强化学习微调（RLVR）。验证器同时被部署为最终设计评审前的自动质量门。

Result: 在与奖励模型独立的VLM评审下，微调后模型的生成通过率由15.7%提升至58.7%，约为原来的3.7倍；训练后的模型与验证器均已部署于生产流水线中。

Conclusion: 在缺乏合规数据集、难以监督微调的约束下，以客观验证器构造可验证奖励并对flow matching模型做RLVR微调，可有效解决小画布、强设计规范场景下的生成可用性问题，并可与自动质量门结合支撑端到端图标生成流水线的生产化落地。

Abstract: Alexa+ devices with screens display animated icons during conversations to visually reinforce what is being said. The original set of these icons was hand-drawn by designers one at a time, a process that produces polished results but is time-consuming and cannot scale to the range of topics encountered in open-ended conversation. We describe an automated pipeline, deployed in production, that generates these vectorised animated icons from a text description alone and surfaces relevant animations to customers based on their ongoing conversation. The pipeline decomposes icon creation into a sequence of stages (image generation, semantic decomposition, vectorisation, styling, and animation). Its quality is determined at the very first of these, image generation, on which every downstream stage depends. We therefore focus on improving this first and most critical stage. Because the icons are rendered on small display canvases under strict design guidelines, generations are often unsuitable for production. Supervised fine-tuning is not viable, as no dataset of line-art icons exists under these constraints. Instead, we design objective verifiers that automatically flag generations violating the design guidelines, and use them to fine-tune a flow matching image generation model via reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR). The fine-tuned model raises the pass rate from 15.7% to 58.7% (3.7x) under a VLM judge that is independent of the reward model. Both the trained model and the verifier are deployed in the production pipeline, where the verifier also serves as the automated quality gate before final design review.

</details>


### [198] [FPTR: Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement for Deadline-Constrained Joint Beam and Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06491)
*Minghao Kong,Zirong Liu,Jianhao Wu,Muyao Chen,Jianxin Wu,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出保可行性事务式精化（FPTR）框架，将强制性的可行解构造与可选的多阶段精化解耦，在严格时限内始终保有可用解并持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短间隔无线调度器必须在每个执行截止期前输出完整、可行的波束与资源分配，迟到或不可行输出在当前控制周期内无任何效用。延长搜索虽可提升流量，但可能错过截止期，或只留下不可行的中间解。

Method: FPTR维护一个通过验证的可行解（incumbent）。每个精化阶段独立构造候选解，不修改incumbent；候选解仅当其完整、在阶段截止前通过可行性校验器、且严格改进目标函数时才替换incumbent，否则丢弃。共四个精化阶段：缓存感知的资源选择、兼容组（CG）用户共享、基于剩余需求的波束掩码重建、双资源联合重分配。

Result: 在150个固定合成实例上，完整流水线（Full）相对轻量级beam-first参照平均提升流量3.83%，相对其Base构造器提升1.79%。在匹配输入的独立测试集中，Full较六个独立搜索基线（ALNS、禁忌搜索、GA、模拟退火、迭代局部搜索、GRASP）高6.68–8.35%（超过100 ms的输出计零效用）。内部测试集中750次Full运行全部返回可行分配，且均未超过100 ms。

Conclusion: 将强制输出构造与可选精化分离，可使剩余计算时间用于改善分配而不危及incumbent；提供开源实现以支持独立复现。

Abstract: Short-interval wireless schedulers must return a complete, feasible beam-and-resource allocation before each execution deadline, and a late or invalid output gives no utility in the current control cycle. Searching longer can increase transmitted traffic, but it may also miss the deadline or leave only an infeasible intermediate solution. We propose Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement (FPTR), which always keeps a validated feasible allocation, the incumbent. Each refinement stage builds a separate candidate and leaves the incumbent unchanged. A candidate replaces the incumbent only if it is complete, passes the feasibility validator before its stage cutoff, and strictly improves the objective; otherwise it is discarded. Four refinement stages use this rule: buffer-aware resource selection, compatibility-group (CG) user sharing, beam-mask rebuilding from remaining demand, and joint reassignment of two resources. On 150 fixed synthetic instances, the full pipeline (Full) raises traffic by 3.83% on average over a lightweight beam-first reference and by 1.79% over its Base constructor. In a separate suite with matched inputs, Full exceeds six independent search baselines (ALNS, tabu search, GA, simulated annealing, iterated local search, and GRASP) by 6.68-8.35%, where outputs later than 100 ms receive zero utility. All 750 Full runs in the internal suite returned feasible allocations, and none exceeded 100 ms. Separating mandatory output construction from optional refinement lets spare computation time improve the allocation without endangering the incumbent. The open-source implementation supports independent reproduction.

</details>


### [199] [GIST: Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection for Offline RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06395)
*Xuliang Huang,Tianxiang Zhao,Chengwei Qin,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从KL正则化RLVR的Gibbs最优策略出发，用双向KL散度刻画策略与该目标的错配，发现该错配在参考策略处可精确分解：正确回答主要经前向项、集中于其低概率token；错误回答主要经反向项、集中于高频生成回答及其高概率token。据此提出GIST两级数据选择方法，在采样前即可获得的信号上近似上述原则，提升离线训练效果并大幅减少监督token数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RLVR在固定采样回答池上训练，且对所有回答的所有token一视同仁地监督，但回答与token的信息量并不相等；需要理论依据来回答"该监督哪些回答、哪些token"。

Method: 以KL正则化RLVR的Gibbs最优策略为离线训练目标，用双向KL散度度量策略与目标的错配并推导其在参考策略处的精确分解；据此提出GIST（Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection）两级选择：回答级用采样温度在策略上选取正确回答、在策略众数附近选取错误回答；token级用初始策略的token概率仅监督错配所在位置的token。

Result: 在两种离线训练目标上，GIST使三个模型在GSM8K、MATH-500、MBPP上的平均准确率均提升；在Qwen3-0.6B/GSM8K的受控比较中，优于从同一候选集选择的七个基线，且比在同提示全部候选上训练少监督66.6%的token。

Conclusion: 回答与token的信息量不对称，基于Gibbs目标错配分解的两级选择可在训练前信号上高效实现，兼具更高准确率与显著更低的监督成本。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains on a fixed pool of sampled responses and typically supervises every token of every response, although responses and tokens are not equally informative. We study which ones to supervise through the Gibbs-optimal policy of KL-regularized RLVR, which offline training targets, and measure the mismatch between a policy and this target with a bidirectional KL divergence. At the reference policy, this mismatch factorizes exactly and separates the two outcomes: correct responses contribute mainly through the forward term, concentrated on their low-probability tokens, and incorrect responses through the reverse term, concentrated on the responses the policy generates often and on their high-probability tokens. Based on this analysis, we propose GIST (Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection), a two-level selection method that approximates these principles with signals available before training. GIST uses the sampling temperature to select correct responses on-policy and incorrect responses near the policy's mode, and it uses the initial policy's token probabilities to supervise only the tokens where the mismatch lies. Applied to two offline training objectives, GIST raises the average accuracy over GSM8K, MATH-500, and MBPP for all three models under both objectives. In a controlled comparison on Qwen3-0.6B/GSM8K, it outperforms seven selection baselines that choose from the same candidates while supervising 66.6% fewer tokens than training on all candidates of the same prompts.

</details>


### [200] [MA-Mesh: Resilient Multi-Agent Collaboration via Role-based Self-Healing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06389)
*JunYong Lee,Woo Kyung Kim,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MA-Mesh 框架，在协作模式之上构建基于角色的自愈层，将韧性感知的系统规范合成为可执行的多智能体策略代码，并通过跨智能体反思与基于角色的故障转移两种自愈机制提升系统性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统需在动态不确定环境中具备协调与适应能力，但现有系统很少显式建模韧性，多依赖临时性的输出修正，无法在运行时系统化地管理鲁棒性。

Method: MA-Mesh 在既有协作模式之上构建角色化自愈层：生成韧性感知的系统规范并综合为可执行的多智能体策略代码；引入两项自愈机制——跨智能体反思（智能体不仅评估自身结果，还评估同伴表现，从而调整行为并协同精化策略）与基于角色的故障转移（在子任务失败或智能体表现不佳时动态重分配角色、互相替代以保障任务连续性）。

Result: 在软件开发、工具使用、科学推理等多样任务上的实验表明，MA-Mesh 在任务性能与系统不确定性下的鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有多智能体系统。

Conclusion: 显式建模韧性并结合规范合成与自愈机制的 MA-Mesh，为多智能体系统提供了系统化的运行时鲁棒性管理方案，验证了角色化自愈层设计的有效性。

Abstract: Effective collaboration in multi-agent systems requires mechanisms for coordination and adaptability in dynamic and uncertain environments. However, existing multi-agent systems rarely model resilience explicitly, instead relying on ad-hoc output correction that limits systematic, runtime management of robustness. In this work, we propose Multi-Agent Mesh (MA-Mesh), a framework that constructs a role-based self-healing layer on top of a collaboration pattern. Specifically, MA-Mesh generates resilience-aware system specifications and synthesizes them into executable multi-agent policy code. To enhance resilience, we introduce two self-healing mechanisms: Cross-agent reflection, a mechanism that allows agents to evaluate not only their own outcomes but also the performance of their peers, enabling informed behavioral adjustments and collective policy refinement; Role-based failover, a strategy which dynamically reassigns roles and enables agents to substitute for one another when sub-tasks fail or agents underperform, ensuring continuity of task execution. Experimental results across diverse tasks (e.g., software development, tool use, scientific reasoning) demonstrate that MA-Mesh significantly outperforms existing multi-agent systems in both task performance and robustness under system uncertainty.

</details>


### [201] [EnzymeRSI: A dual-loop recursive self-improvement system for enzyme design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06321)
*Genoria AI Team,Yuejie Hou,Lixiang Yang,Yisi Liu,Weiting Tang,Yueyuxiao Yang,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EnzymeRSI双环递归自我改进系统：外环进化流程/工具等"harness资产"而不改模型权重，内环在受控条件下开发预测与选择算法，并规划云实验室闭合物理环路；在ProteinGym、ThermoMPNN、TadA-Bench及三项前瞻湿实验上验证了干湿闭环的自主酶工程范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酶工程改进多局限于在固定模型与固定工作流内优化"测哪些候选"，即只在既定框架下做候选筛选。当瓶颈来自方法不匹配或工作流失效时，这种路线难以突破。作者希望把研究流程的组织方式与算法开发本身作为可优化、可证伪的对象，从而摆脱固定方法的盲区。

Method: 提出EnzymeRSI双环递归自我改进（RSI）系统：外环在不改变模型权重的前提下进化harness资产（流程/工具/预测策略），通过迭代比较更新预测策略；内环在受控条件下开发并改进预测与选择算法（如ThermoMPNN自改进）；并给出云实验室系统设计，以带质量控制的反馈闭合干-湿物理环路。整体将工作流组织与算法开发视作单一可证伪的dry-to-wet回路。

Result: 外环在ProteinGym五个assay上使开发集平均Spearman相关提升0.044，CD19达0.335、OPSD（视紫红质）达0.683（两个第一名中仅CD19由外环自身产生）。内环在ThermoMPNN自改进中PCC达0.849；TadA-Bench上1.13小时内Spearman达0.307。三项前瞻湿实验：荧光素酶RLuc中32个非预设候选中10个活性≥野生型2倍；胞苷脱氨酶A3A推荐变体将5mC转化率提升至90.2%；进化路径选择使DNA聚合酶KOD-E在修饰底物上的催化效率达亲本31.1倍。

Conclusion: 将研究流程的组织与算法开发统一为一个可证伪的干湿闭环，可为突破固定方法的盲区提供进化式范式，并推动自主化酶工程。外环与内环分工明确（流程/工具层进化 vs. 算法层开发），云实验室闭环则提供质量控制反馈，实现从预测到实验决策的自动迭代。

Abstract: Improvement in enzyme engineering is usually pursued by optimising which candidates to assay within a fixed model and workflow; when the bottleneck arises from a mismatched method or a failing workflow, that route is difficult to escape. We present EnzymeRSI, a dual-loop system for recursive self-improvement (RSI). The outer loop evolves harness assets without changing model weights, the inner loop develops prediction and selection algorithms under controlled conditions, and a cloud laboratory system is specified to close the physical loop with quality-controlled feedback. By updating its prediction strategy through iterative comparison, the outer loop raised the mean development-set Spearman correlation on ProteinGym by 0.044 across five assays, reaching 0.335 on CD19 and 0.683 on OPSD (rhodopsin); of the two first places, only CD19 was produced by the outer loop itself. In the self-improvement of the ThermoMPNN algorithm the inner loop reached a PCC of 0.849, and on TadA-Bench it reached a Spearman correlation of 0.307 in 1.13 h. In three prospective wet-lab tests of the system's experimental decision-making, 10 of the 32 candidates that had not been pre-specified for the luciferase RLuc showed at least twice the wild-type activity; a recommended variant of the cytidine deaminase A3A raised 5mC conversion to 90.2%; and path selection in evolution raised the catalytic efficiency of the DNA polymerase KOD-E on a modified substrate to 31.1 times that of the parent. By treating the organisation of the research workflow and the development of algorithms as a single falsifiable dry-to-wet loop, this work offers an evolutionary paradigm for moving beyond the blind spots of a fixed method and for autonomous enzyme engineering.

</details>


### [202] [Split-KV: Efficient Context Parallelism for Training Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05926)
*Geng Zhang,Fangzhao Zhang,Cong Xie,Tiantian Fan,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Split-KV是一个精确的上下文并行方法，利用扩散drafter中"查询远短于上下文"的query-context不对称性，将每层注意力通信限制为查询规模张量、使通信量与上下文长度无关，并用代价感知的LPT策略均衡KV分块负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散drafter用块稀疏掩码对长上下文做cross-attention，训练中经anchor sampling构造紧凑draft查询。作者实证发现：固定查询预算下接受长度随上下文扩展保持稳定，而增大查询预算带来的质量收益递减，从而确立"查询远短于上下文"的实际运行区间。现有CP方法既未利用该不对称性，也无法满足anchor sampling带来的非线性、样本相关的负载均衡需求。

Method: 提出Split-KV：(1) 保持KV分片本地，仅将每层注意力通信限定为与查询等大的张量，使通信开销与上下文长度解耦；(2) 借助全局查询视图实现代价感知的LPT（最长处理时间优先）调度，按各KV块的实测注意力代价进行分块均衡。

Result: CP规模为64时，Split-KV保持85–91%的弱扩展效率，相对最快的现有CP基线最高取得3.67×加速；LPT实现<1%的负载不均衡，且规划速度比最佳负载均衡基线快三个数量级。

Conclusion: query-context不对称性为扩散drafter的上下文并行提供了高效设计空间；Split-KV以查询规模通信与代价感知调度同时实现通信可扩展性与负载均衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion drafters accelerate speculative decoding by denoising multiple draft tokens in parallel. Their training uses anchor sampling to construct compact draft queries and performs cross-attention with a block-sparse mask over a much longer context. We empirically show that acceptance length remains stable as context scales with a fixed query budget, while increasing the query budget yields diminishing quality gains, jointly establishing a practical regime with queries far shorter than the context. No existing context parallelism (CP) method jointly exploits this query--context asymmetry and meets the nonlinear, sample-dependent load-balancing requirement created by anchor sampling. We present Split-KV, an exact CP method that keeps key/value shards local and limits per-layer attention communication to query-sized tensors, making this communication independent of context length. Its global query view further enables a cost-aware longest-processing-time-first (LPT) strategy that balances context key/value chunks by their realized attention cost. At a CP size of 64, Split-KV retains 85--91\% weak-scaling efficiency and achieves up to $3.67\times$ speedup over the fastest available CP baseline. LPT achieves $<$1\% imbalance and plans three orders of magnitude faster than the best load-balancing baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/zxgx/verl.

</details>


### [203] [CHARM+: Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05906)
*Xinhang Zhang,Boning Zhang,Chengchun Liu,Chunpu Li,Lei Sun,Weifeng Zhang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHARM+将序列并行注意力中的通信分层次重组，并通过重合并多流机制解耦跨硬件通信与硬件内通信，从而提升计算-通信重叠度与GPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列长度增长使基于序列并行的分布式注意力（如Ring-Attention）成为LLM主流方案，但其通信依赖限制了计算-通信重叠、降低GPU利用率；在NUMA或跨节点等跨硬件架构中，带宽不均衡导致频繁通信同步拖累整体性能。

Method: 提出CHARM+（Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream）：将顺序通信重组为多个层级，实现层内并行与计算重叠；并将跨硬件通信与硬件内通信解耦，消除带宽不均衡带来的同步开销；给出前向与反向算法设计。

Result: 在多种硬件架构上评估，前向与反向均持续优于最先进方法。相较Ring-Attention，双NUMA PCIe架构上前向/反向平均加速1.3x/1.7x；双节点NVLink架构上分别为2.8x/2.9x。

Conclusion: 通过通信分层与跨/内硬件通信解耦，CHARM+有效改善计算-通信重叠并消除带宽不均衡引起的同步开销，在NUMA与跨节点场景下显著加速分布式注意力。

Abstract: As sequence lengths continue to grow, distributed attention based on sequence parallel (e.g., Ring-Attention) has become a mainstream solution for large language models (LLMs). Nevertheless, inherent communication dependencies limit computation-communication overlap, reducing GPU utilization. Moreover, in cross-hardware (NUMA or node) architectures, due to imbalanced communication bandwidth, frequent communication synchronization will limit overall performance. Based on these insights, we propose CHARM+, Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism. We reorganize sequential communications into several levels and achieve intra-level parallelism and computation overlap. Furthermore, we decouple cross-hardware communication from intra-hardware communication, eliminating synchronization overhead caused by bandwidth imbalance. Forward and backward algorithms were evaluated on multiple hardware architectures and consistently outperformed state-of-the-art methods. Compared to Ring-Attention, our forward and backward algorithms achieve average speedups of 1.3x and 1.7x on a dual-NUMA PCIe architecture, and 2.8x and 2.9x on a dual-node NVLink architecture, respectively.

</details>


### [204] [Autonomy and Coordination in Intelligent Manufacturing: A Recursive Autonomous Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05828)
*Yuning Chen,Hongda Zhang,Linqiang Hu,Ziqing Zhou,Yi Liu,Zhao Huang,Ding Zhou,Haining Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文从复杂适应系统（CAS）视角剖析人类生产组织中的智能，识别出个体自主与集体协调两个耦合机制，提出将目标驱动自主智能体与递归组织结构结合的"递归自主智能体（RAA）"框架，用于下一代智能制造工程化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造要求系统依据生产目标与运行条件进行决策、组织工作并通过反馈持续调整，但缺乏将"智能、开放性、系统级集成"转化为可工程化机制的清晰框架。

Method: 以复杂适应系统（CAS）视角分析人类生产组织中的智能，提炼出个体自主（基于可用信息的局部决策）与集体协调（对齐共享目标与联合约束）两个耦合机制，并由学习与适应持续改进两者；建立生产系统模型，形式化局部决策规则与协调如何共同形成系统决策；据此提出RAA框架：目标契约界定局部目标与运行边界，能力契约支持生产单元的选择与组合，通用接口使协调后的群体可作为单元参与更大组织，实现设备—产线—工厂的递归嵌套。

Result: 提出生产系统形式化模型与RAA框架（目标驱动自主智能体＋递归组织结构＋目标/能力契约＋通用接口），给出设备、产线、工厂多层级递归集成的工程路径。

Conclusion: 将智能、开放性与系统级集成需求转化为自主与协调生产的机制，RAA为下一代智能制造提供了一种工程化方法。

Abstract: Intelligent production requires systems to make decisions and organize work according to production goals and operating conditions. It also requires continuous adjustment through feedback. This paper analyzes intelligence in human production organizations from a complex adaptive systems (CAS) perspective. The analysis identifies two coupled mechanisms: individual autonomy and collective coordination. Individual autonomy enables actors to form local decisions using available information. Collective coordination aligns these decisions with shared goals and joint constraints. Learning and adaptation improve both mechanisms over time. A production-system model formalizes how local decision rules and coordination jointly form system decisions. On this basis, we propose the Recursive Autonomous Agent (RAA) framework, which combines goal-driven autonomous agents with a recursive organizational structure. Goal contracts define local objectives and operating boundaries, while capability contracts support the selection and composition of production units. Common interfaces allow coordinated groups to participate as units in larger organizations, connecting equipment, production lines, and factories. RAA thus provides an engineering approach to next-generation intelligent manufacturing, translating the requirements of intelligence, openness, and system-wide integration into mechanisms for autonomous and coordinated production.

</details>


### [205] [Toward a Compositional Knowledge Architecture for a Shared Organizational Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05753)
*Andrew Miracle,Phyl Georgiou,Christine Okoth,Ese Monday*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四层架构（Signals、Domains、Claims、Dimensions）构建"共享组织皮层"，使解读与其论证依据和置信状态一同持久化，并通过真值维护网络随证据变化修订，实验验证检索性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织积累的是证据（对话、文档、决策、关系），但实际依据的是解读——由多次观察汇集、依赖假设、并随情境修订的判断。现有检索、知识图谱与智能体记忆只保存记录而丢失解读，导致组织缺乏持久可修订的共享判断基础。

Method: 提出四层架构：Signals（原始证据信号）、Domains（领域）、Claims（解读性断言）、Dimensions（经验证、版本化的命名维度，用于在轴向上定位解读）；引入真值维护网络（truth-maintenance network），在证据变化时修订各断言的置信状态，并保留论证依据与standing。采用注册式检索实验与系统级对比进行评测。

Result: 检索实验中Dimension项带来nDCG@10提升0.028（p=0.002）；系统级对比中完整检索配置相对Signals-only提升nDCG@10 0.081、相对图增强的Signals-only提升0.053（均p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 该架构为组织提供持久、可修订的共享判断基础，在保留解读及其论证的同时显著改善检索效果。

Abstract: Organizations accumulate evidence in conversations, documents, decisions and relationships, but they act on interpretations: judgements assembled from several observations, resting on assumptions, and revised as circumstances change. Existing retrieval, knowledge graphs and agent memory keep the records and lose the interpretation. We propose a four-layer architecture, Signals, Domains, Claims and Dimensions, in which interpretations persist with their justifications and standing, a truth-maintenance network revises that standing as evidence changes, and validated, versioned Dimensions position interpretations on named axes. We call the result a shared organizational cortex. In a registered retrieval experiment, the Dimension term gains 0.028 nDCG@10 (p = 0.002). In system-level comparisons, the full retrieval configuration improves nDCG@10 by 0.081 over Signals-only and 0.053 over graph-augmented Signals-only retrieval (both reported p < 0.001). The architecture gives organizations a persistent, revisable basis for shared judgement.

</details>


### [206] [Learning to Decode Linear Codes with Generalized Belief Propagation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06874)
*Samira Malek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出广义置信传播网络GBPNet，在保留Tanner图消息传递对称性的同时提升神经译码器学习能力，并引入渐进训练与高SNR训练两种策略，在多种线性码上取得比传统BP及对比的先进神经译码器更低的误码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠错码是通信系统可靠传输的关键，神经译码器已显示出优于经典迭代算法的性能，但如何在保留消息传递结构对称性的前提下增强译码器的学习能力仍待解决。

Method: 设计级联的变量节点更新与可学习的逐元素消息缩放，同时保留Tanner图结构以维持消息传递对称性；并引入渐进训练和高信噪比训练两种训练策略以提升译码精度。

Result: 在多种线性码上的实验表明，GBPNet的误码率低于传统置信传播以及所评估的先进神经译码器。

Conclusion: 通过将结构对称性约束与可学习消息缩放相结合并配合专门训练策略，可在保持消息传递可解释性的同时显著提升译码性能。

Abstract: Error correcting codes are a fundamental component of communication systems, enabling reliable data transmission over noisy channels.
