We present Assurance-Conditioned Continuous Authorization (ACCA), an approach designed to be harness-independent, for maintaining operational authority according to currently justified assurance. It distinguishes evidence validity from contextual applicability, derives assurance claims from eligible evidence, and resolves a multidimensional effective-authority envelope within a principal-declared ceiling and applicable policy. A typed dependency graph supports selective re-evaluation of authorization-relevant changes. An authority epoch changes when effective authority changes; a distinct optional authorization-basis version supports revalidation of action-specific authorization conditions. Protected effect-time enforcement can reject stale actions before a consequential resource change, subject to the available commit mechanism. A compositional Domain Governance Profile expresses domain-specific semantics without granting authority. Controlled scenario tests implemented as a regression suite using a trading profile exercise default-deny rules, evidence-conditioned contraction and recovery, policy divergence, selective non-response, and stale-action rejection at a local gateway before the simulated-broker boundary. The study establishes implementation-level evidence in one domain, not a cross-domain guarantee of harmful-behavior prevention.

</details>


### [240] [Hybrid Spark: Adaptive Speculative Decoding That Lets the Content Choose the Drafter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05611)
*Ekagra Ranjan,Bharat Venkitesh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 \sys 框架，在每一步同时运行基于模型的草稿器与无模型草稿器（如 n-gram），在自适应验证预算下为每个请求选择更优的草稿链，从而在单一部署中同时高效服务代码编辑与对话等异构负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码（SD）的加速收益取决于工作负载的"形状"（batch size、上下文长度）与"内容"：模型草稿器适合任意内容但每 token 成本高，无模型草稿器在输出复述输入（如代码编辑）时可近乎零成本地生成长复制。现有自适应验证仅支持单一模型草稿器；无模型草稿器缺乏逐 token 置信度，无法参与预算分配，部署只能固定一种草稿器或在 GPU 间拆分。

Method: 核心思想是为每个通过验证的草稿 token 设定"价格"——即该槽位在批内别处可获得的 goodput；并通过在通用文本上一次性拟合，为无模型草稿器的 token 赋予校准的接受率估计，使两类草稿链在同一尺度上可比较。\sys 每步同时运行两种草稿器，在自适应验证中按价格分配预算并择优选链，全程在 GPU 上完成，保持异步调度不被破坏。实现基于 vLLM，实例化为 \dspark 加 n-gram 草稿器。

Result: 在 batch size 4 的代码编辑任务上，输出吞吐达 \dspark 的 1.10–1.62×；batch size 128 时为 1.04–1.27×；温度约 0 的对话任务上保持 0.97–1.01×，几乎无损失。

Conclusion: 通过统一价格尺度使模型草稿器与无模型草稿器协同参与自适应验证，\sys 实现了单一部署同时服务对话与代码编辑两类负载，兼顾高吞吐与通用性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) speeds up large language model (LLM) serving, but its gain depends on the workload's \emph{shape}, such as batch size and context length, and on its \emph{content}. A model-based drafter proposes a short block on any content, while a model-free drafter such as n-gram lookup proposes long copies at almost no cost when the output repeats its input, as in code editing. Adaptive verification spends a batch-wide verification budget on the draft tokens most likely to be accepted, but it supports only one model-based drafter. Model-free drafters provide no per-token confidence, so they cannot take part in budget allocation, and deployments must either fix one drafter or split their GPUs between drafters. We present \sys, a framework that runs a model-based and a model-free drafter on every step and gives each request the better of their chains, within adaptive verification and entirely on the GPU, which keeps asynchronous scheduling intact. The key idea is a price per verified draft token: the goodput that slot would earn elsewhere in the batch. Once the model-free drafter's tokens carry calibrated acceptance estimates, fit once on general text, this price puts both drafters' chains on one scale. Instantiated with \dspark and an n-gram drafter in vLLM, \sys achieves 1.10--1.62\x the output throughput of \dspark on code editing at batch size~4 and 1.04--1.27\x at batch size~128, while staying at 0.97--1.01\x of it on chat at temperature~0. A single deployment can therefore serve both workloads.

</details>


### [241] [VirtualTrialHarness: Coordinating Agentic Operationalization of Virtual Trial Protocols Across Complex Clinical Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05551)
*Pengze Li,Jingna Feng,Yue Yu,Yining Wu,Xinyue Hu,Jie Lin,Avanti Bhandarkar,Cui Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出虚拟试验智能体框架 VTHarness，以可复用流程技能与显式契约协调各阶段决策，并发布 VTBenchmark；在三个合成环境工作流中 95.1% 产出与参考一致（检索基线 60.4%），并在 Mayo Clinic 与 MIMIC-IV 场景验证泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟试验依赖常规临床数据评估治疗策略，但将研究方案转化为可执行分析，需确定临床概念与研究要求如何在现有数据中表征并跨流程一致应用；这些决策受本地数据表示与操作惯例制约，涉及数据源选择、患者与就诊身份、链接及时间语义解释，缺乏系统性协调机制。

Method: 提出 VTHarness 智能体框架：为每个工作流阶段选择可复用的流程性技能（procedural skills），并通过显式契约（explicit contracts）在各阶段间保持研究决策一致性；同时提出 VTBenchmark 用于评估数据源选择与队列构建。评估设置包括三个复杂合成环境、三种语言模型、13 个 Mayo Clinic 案例及 8 个 MIMIC-IV 任务，基线为同一流程语料库上的检索方法。

Result: 在需求澄清后，VTHarness 在 95.1% 的工作流中产出全部所需队列输出并与参考一致，检索基线为 60.4%；流程技能在三种语言模型上均提升数据源恢复效果，并在 13 个 Mayo 案例中提高与分析师代码的数据源重叠度；站点特定适配使 8 个 MIMIC-IV 任务实现参考匹配的队列执行；在 Mayo Clinic 虚拟试验项目中支持了研究者对队列构建与终点分析的审阅。

Conclusion: 将流程性指导与工作流决策相协调，可提升复杂临床模式下虚拟试验的实现效果；显式契约与可复用流程技能的联合设计是跨站点、跨模型迁移的关键。

Abstract: Virtual trials use routinely collected clinical data to evaluate treatment strategies, but translating a study protocol into an executable analysis requires determining how clinical concepts and study requirements should be represented in the available data and applied consistently across the workflow. These decisions depend on local data representations and operational conventions and can involve source selection, patient and encounter identity, linkage, and temporal interpretation. Here we introduce VirtualTrialHarness (VTHarness), an agent framework that coordinates virtual-trial operationalization by selecting reusable procedural skills for each workflow stage and uses explicit contracts to maintain consistent study decisions across stages. We also introduce VTBenchmark for evaluating source selection and cohort construction. Across three complex synthetic environments, VTHarness successfully produced all required cohort outputs matching the references in 95.1% of workflows after requirement clarification, compared with 60.4% using retrieval from the same procedural corpus. Procedural skills improved source recovery across three language models and increased source overlap with analyst code in 13 Mayo Clinic cases. Site-specific adaptation enabled reference-matched cohort execution across eight MIMIC-IV tasks. Within the Mayo Clinic Virtual Trials Program, the framework supported investigator review of cohort construction and endpoint analysis. These findings support coordinating procedural guidance with workflow decisions to improve virtual-trial implementation across complex clinical schemas.

</details>


### [242] [SUMONE: A Simple and Efficient Summarizer for Plug-and-Play Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06445)
*Jizhe Zhang,Ang Li,Chenheng Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SumOne：一个独立的、与具体任务与智能体解耦的摘要器，通过掩码重建+长度惩罚的任务无关奖励与GRPO训练，实现长程智能体历史的自监督上下文压缩，推理时仅保留轻量摘要器即可即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言模型智能体的交互历史不断膨胀，上下文压缩对高效执行至关重要；现有学习式压缩方法依赖下游任务结果或求解器行为，导致训练与特定任务/智能体强耦合，缺乏通用性。

Method: SumOne仅用掩码重建作为训练信号：给定对已过期历史的摘要，冻结的重建器恢复原轨迹中被随机掩码的部分；以重建质量叠加长度惩罚构成任务无关奖励，用GRPO优化摘要器。无需参考摘要、无需下游任务执行，自监督来源于原始轨迹本身。推理时只保留轻量摘要器。

Result: 在长程搜索与软件工程任务上系统评测：相较完整上下文运行，DeepSearchQA上估计推理成本降低约78%，SWE-bench Verified上降低约75%，绝对性能下降0.04；同一检查点无需额外微调即可跨下游模型与智能体框架迁移。

Conclusion: 以掩码重建为任务无关奖励训练独立摘要器，可在几乎不损失性能的前提下大幅降低长程智能体的推理成本，并具备良好的即插即用性与跨模型、跨框架泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon language-model agents accumulate increasingly large interaction histories, making context compaction essential for efficient execution. Existing learned compaction methods often rely on downstream task outcomes or solver behavior, coupling training to specific tasks or agents. We introduce SumOne, a standalone summarizer trained to preserve information in agent histories through masked reconstruction. Given a summary of the expired history, a frozen reconstructor recovers randomly masked portions of the original trajectory; reconstruction quality, combined with a length penalty, provides a task-agnostic reward for optimizing the summarizer with GRPO. This yields self-supervision directly from raw trajectories, without reference summaries or downstream task execution. At inference time, only the lightweight summarizer is retained, enabling plug-and-play context compression for otherwise unchanged agents. We conduct a systematic evaluation of SumOne in long-horizon search and software-engineering tasks. SumOne reduces estimated inference cost by approximately 78\% on DeepSearchQA and 75\% on SWE-bench Verified relative to operating with full context, with absolute decreases of 0.04. The same checkpoint also transfers across downstream models and agent harnesses without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [243] [Corralling AI Risks with Grassroots Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06374)
*Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用"草根契约"(grassroots contracts)——Proudhon两百年前理念的数字化身——来约束数字平台面临的三大AI风险：AI控制全球平台、不可问责的AI代理、无法溯源的深度伪造，并给出智能手机应用实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI处于拐点，风险急剧上升。全球数字平台的算法、成员资格与内容控制权正被移交给AI，导致使用平台的大多数人陷入脆弱处境。具体三大风险为：(1) AI掌控社交与经济类全球平台，使多数人易受AI控制；(2) 匿名AI代理与人类并存在平台上，伪装成人、服务于未知主人与目标、不受约束且无可问责；(3) 匿名AI生成的深度伪造难以辨别真伪，制作者逃避责任。现有治理手段缺乏对权力归属与责任追溯的结构性约束，因此需要新的制度设计。

Method: 将Pierre-Joseph Proudhon两百年前提出的契约理念数字化，构建"草根契约"机制。具体提出两类契约：面向社交图谱的契约，用于实现可问责的AI代理（明确代理的身份、归属与责任）；面向社交网络的契约，用于实现数字溯源（provenance），使内容来源与生成者可被验证。并实现两个可运行的智能手机应用以演示这两种契约。

Result: 两个智能手机应用分别落地了"可问责AI代理的社交图谱"与"具备数字溯源的社交网络"，演示草根契约如何针对性地约束三大AI风险：平台被AI控制、AI代理不可问责、深度伪造无法归属。摘要未给出量化评估指标或用户实验数据。

Conclusion: 草根契约可作为约束AI风险的可行制度—技术混合路径：通过契约明确AI代理的责任主体，通过数字溯源解决内容归属问题，从而缓解平台AI化带来的控制权集中与责任真空。其价值在于以去中心化、基层自组织的方式替代自上而下的平台治理，实现可问责与可溯源。

Abstract: AI's inflection point is a fountain of alarming risks. Digital platforms in particular are vulnerable to three major AI risks, as those in control of global platforms cede control to AI, of the algorithms, of membership and of content: (1) AI control of global platforms, social and economic, used by the majority of humanity, resulting in the majority of humanity becoming vulnerable to AI control. (2) Unaccountable AI agents operating anonymously on social and economic digital platforms, side-by-side with humans, possibly masquerading as humans, serving unknown masters with unknown goals, operating under unknown restraints without accountability or culpability. (3) Unattributed deepfakes generated by anonymous AI, with people unable to distinguish them from genuine content and with their producers evading accountability and culpability.
  Here, we propose to corral these AI risks with grassroots contracts, a digital incarnation of a two-centuries old notion of Pierre-Joseph Proudhon. We present grassroots contracts for a social graph with accountable AI agents and for a social network with digital provenance, and working smartphone apps that implement the two and show how they corral these major AI risks: (1) AI control of global platforms. (2) Unaccountable AI agents. (3) Unattributed deepfakes.

</details>


### [244] [STEK: Structural-Terrain-Enhanced Koopman-Inspired Cross-Channel Regularization for Retrospective Battery Multi-Target Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06299)
*Jing Wang,Shiyuan Piao,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STEK框架，将电芯级生命周期(dQ/dV)结构与端点曲线结构代理通过质量代理条件门控融合，实现跨异质电芯的回顾式电池多目标估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回顾性电池表征面临异质性问题：循环记录随化学体系、协议、电芯形态、容量和工况差异巨大。端点充放电曲线只反映当前循环局部状态，而电芯级生命周期地形可概括整段归档轨迹的结构演化，二者需要统一建模。

Method: STEK将cell-level lifecycle (dQ/dV) terrain与端点曲线导出的结构代理经quality-proxy-conditioned gating融合；用Bounded FiLM与validity-gated residual head细化端点曲线表征并保留曲线预测通路；电池类型与可选材料文本表征用于调节identity-centered low-rank linear map，引入Koopman启发的跨通道一致性目标（不建模时序动力学、不做rollout）。

Result: 在固定七输出MIX-battery协议下，完整回顾式设置相对仅端点学习的基线在多种时序骨干上聚合性能更优（该比较同时含额外归档上下文与建模设计贡献）；在匹配辅助输入下优于直接拼接与quality-unaware FiLM；描述符移除测试显示可平滑回退到端点通路。

Conclusion: 方法定位为回顾式归档协调，而非仅凭第t循环的在线估计；可直接恢复的容量汇总仍宜用确定性规则。

Abstract: Retrospective battery characterization across heterogeneous cells is challenging because cycling records vary substantially with chemistry, protocol, cell format, capacity, and operating conditions. An endpoint charge--discharge curve captures the local condition of an evaluated cycle, while a cell-level lifecycle terrain summarizes structural evolution across the archived trajectory. We propose STEK, a structural-terrain-enhanced framework for retrospective battery multi-target estimation. STEK combines a cell-level lifecycle (dQ/dV) terrain with an endpoint-derived structural proxy through quality-proxy-conditioned gating. Bounded FiLM and a validity-gated residual head refine the endpoint-curve representation while preserving a curve-based prediction pathway. Battery-type and optional material-text representations further condition an identity-centered low-rank linear map, providing a Koopman-inspired cross-channel consistency objective without modeling temporal dynamics or performing rollout. Under the fixed seven-output MIX-battery protocol, the full retrospective setting improves aggregate performance over endpoint-only learned baselines across multiple temporal backbones; this comparison reflects both the additional archived context and the modeling design. Under matched auxiliary inputs, STEK outperforms direct concatenation and quality-unaware FiLM, while descriptor-removal tests show graceful fallback to the endpoint pathway. Deterministic rules remain preferable for directly recoverable capacity summaries. The method targets retrospective archive harmonization rather than online estimation from cycle t alone.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-Resident Data Association in Smart Homes via MDP Modeling and Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06296)
*Xi Chen,Julien Cumin,Fano Ramparany,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将多居民智能家居中的数据关联（Data Association）重构为MDP序列决策问题，用行为克隆求解，实例化为轻量Transformer（多标签分类）与微调LLM（条件文本生成）两种策略，在三个数据集上显著优于SOTA，尤其擅长并发多居民事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境传感器虽保护隐私，但多居民场景下存在数据关联难题：需判断匿名传感器事件由哪位居民触发。现有方法缺乏全局一致性，沉默居民常产生冲突关联；且把连续物理运动模型套用于离散逻辑行为，导致轨迹严重碎片化。

Method: 把数据关联形式化为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的序列决策任务；用行为克隆（BC）直接从专家示范学习策略，规避复杂奖励设计；两种策略实现：(1) 轻量Transformer，将关联视为对线性化事件历史的多标签分类；(2) 微调LLM，将问题重构为条件文本生成以利用预训练语义知识。

Result: 在三个多样化数据集上的广泛评估显示，方法显著优于现有最优基线，在并发多居民事件解析上尤为突出；LLM策略在基准设置下展现出初步的跨环境迁移潜力，但真实部署中的鲁棒性仍需进一步验证。

Conclusion: 以MDP+行为克隆统一建模数据关联，可同时缓解全局一致性缺失与轨迹碎片化问题；Transformer与LLM两种实例化均有效，LLM的跨环境泛化具潜力但真实部署鲁棒性待验证。

Abstract: Ambient sensors offer a privacy-preserving solution for activity recognition in smart homes, yet they face a critical challenge in multi-resident environments known as Data Association, which aims to identify the resident triggering a specific anonymous sensor event. Existing approaches fundamentally fail to maintain global consistency by often yielding conflicting associations for silent residents. Additionally, these methods frequently suffer from severe trajectory fragmentation caused by the ontological mismatch of applying continuous physical motion models to discrete logical behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework that reformulates data association as a sequential decision-making task within a Markov Decision Process (MDP). We solve the defined MDP via Behavior Cloning (BC) to avoid complex reward engineering by directly learning from expert demonstrations. We instantiate the policy using two complementary architectures, including a lightweight Transformer that treats association as a multi-label classification task over linearized event histories, and a fine-tuned Large Language Model (LLM) that reformulates the problem as conditional text generation to leverage pre-trained semantic knowledge. Extensive evaluations on three diverse datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, particularly in resolving concurrent multi-resident events. Furthermore, the LLM-based policy shows preliminary cross-environment transfer potential under benchmark settings, while its robustness in real deployments still requires further validation.

</details>


### [246] [DyPSI: Dynamic Physics Sensing via Joint Field and Sensor-Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06280)
*Yizhou Zhang,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Jianfeng Shi,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DyPSI将动态物理感知表述为"未来场+传感器轨迹"的联合生成问题，在Functional Tucker潜空间中用联合扩散模型实现时空一致的重建与布点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中需从稀疏观测重建稠密时空场并决定后续采样位置。现有重建与布点方法依赖静态感知假设：或全程固定单一布局，或逐帧独立优化布局，难以产生时间上连贯的感知轨迹。

Method: 将离散传感器坐标提升为连续Sensor Position Fields；用共享Functional Tucker表示编码场与感知配置；在潜空间中训练联合扩散模型同时生成未来场与传感器轨迹；在扩散训练扰动中引入高斯过程时间核，使去噪器偏向时间平滑解；理论上将EDM训练目标与序列聚合A-最优性准则相联系，并给出核诱导的离散轨迹变化上界。

Result: 在湍流、GLORYS12海表温度与三维汽车气动力学三项任务上，DyPSI稳定优于静态布点与逐帧布点策略，且在紧传感器预算下误差显著降低。

Conclusion: 联合生成式建模配合时间相关扰动可同时获得高精度场重建与时间连贯的传感轨迹，为动态物理感知提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Physics sensing in evolving environments requires reconstructing dense spatiotemporal fields from sparse observations while adapting where future observations should be collected. Existing reconstruction and sensor-placement methods have made substantial progress, but they typically rely on static sensing assumptions: they either use a single layout across all frames or optimize placements independently at each frame, which limits their ability to produce temporally coherent sensing trajectories. We introduce DyPSI, a dynamic physics sensing framework that formulates this problem as the joint generation of future fields and sensor trajectories conditioned on historical observations. DyPSI lifts discrete sensor coordinates into continuous Sensor Position Fields, encodes fields and sensing configurations with a shared Functional Tucker representation, and trains a joint diffusion model to generate future fields and sensing trajectories simultaneously in the resulting latent space. A Gaussian-process temporal kernel correlates the perturbation injected during diffusion training, biasing the denoiser toward temporally smooth recoveries, and we further provide an analysis linking the EDM training objective to a sequence-aggregated A-optimality criterion and a kernel-induced bound on discrete trajectory variation. Experiments on turbulent flow, GLORYS12 sea-surface temperature, and 3D car aerodynamics show that DyPSI consistently outperforms static and frame-wise placement strategies, with substantial error reduction under tight sensor budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/mengqi132/DyPSI.

</details>


### [247] [A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Personalized Reasoning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06259)
*Dohyun Kim,Junyong Lhim,Kwanyong Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MPRBench——一个评估多模态大模型（MLLMs）多模态个性化推理能力的综合基准，含12个子任务并支持可解释性错误分析，实验表明现有模型因个性化概念识别不准与对无关个人信息的敏感性而表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在决策时往往需要对异构形式的个人信息进行整体推理，但多模态大模型在这一"多模态个性化推理"能力上研究较少，缺乏系统性评测手段。

Method: 构建 MPRBench 基准，覆盖多种任务与真实世界挑战，包含12个子任务，并支持对代表性错误案例的可解释分析；对当前个性化 MLLMs 开展大规模实验评估。

Result: 现有面向个性化的 MLLMs 在个性化推理上仍存在明显困难，主要瓶颈为个性化概念识别不准确，以及对无关个人信息的敏感性（易受干扰）。

Conclusion: MPRBench 揭示了 MLLMs 个性化推理的关键挑战，期望推动该方向后续研究，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: People often make decisions by holistically reasoning over heterogeneous forms of personal information. In this paper, we study the relatively underexplored capability of multimodal personalized reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To this end, we introduce \texttt{MPRBench}, a novel and comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate personalized reasoning across a wide range of tasks and real-world challenges. \texttt{MPRBench} consists of 12 sub-tasks and additionally supports interpretable analysis of representative error cases. Through extensive experiments, we find that current personalized MLLMs still struggle with personalized reasoning due to several key challenges, including inaccurate recognition of personalized concepts and sensitivity to irrelevant personal information. We hope that MPRBench will stimulate further research on personalized reasoning in MLLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kkdh15/MPRBench

</details>


### [248] [Graph-Mediated Large Language Model Systems in the Agentic Era: Reasoning, Knowledge, Retrieval, Execution, and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05953)
*Haihui Liu,Yang Chen,Zice Qiu,Yun Zhang,Jialun Zhong,Wenhua Wang,Yanzeng Li,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述提出"操作—角色—位置"（operation-level role–locus）分类法，系统梳理图在大语言模型系统中承担的推理、知识/证据、检索与记忆、执行、协调五类功能，并构建涵盖基准、对比设计、过程诊断与系统开销的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图在LLM系统中日益重要，但已有研究常混淆"局部指标提升"与"下游任务收益"，也缺少对"图操作做什么、由哪个组件计算、输出如何被后续使用"的清晰区分，导致难以判断图机制何时真正有用。

Method: 提出操作级"角色—位置"二维分类法：角色指图操作承担的功能（推理、知识与证据、检索与记忆、执行、协调），位置指执行该计算的组件及输出被下游使用的位置；据此对比图观测与外部计算如何引导决策、记忆表示与更新如何支持后续任务、依赖关系如何约束执行与信息传递。进一步把基准、对比实验设计、过程诊断和系统成本组织为统一评估框架。

Result: 形成覆盖五类功能的分类体系与评估框架，能够在保留原始报告条件的前提下，区分局部指标改进与下游任务表现；并归纳出自适应计算与停止、记忆维护、依赖感知恢复等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为判断图机制是否适用、以及跨组件与跨阶段评估其效果提供了系统基础，有助于避免将局部增益误读为端到端收益。

Abstract: Graphs play an increasingly important role in large language model (LLM) systems, supporting structural reasoning, knowledge grounding, retrieval, and memory. As these systems evolve toward the agentic era, graphs also organize execution dependencies and multi-agent communication, linking intermediate observations and retained information to subsequent decisions. However, understanding their contribution requires distinguishing the function of each graph operation, the component responsible for its computation, and the subsequent use of its output. This survey introduces an operation-level role--locus taxonomy for reasoning, knowledge and evidence, retrieval and memory, execution, and coordination. We compare how graph observations and external computations guide decisions, how memory representations and updates support later tasks, and how dependencies constrain execution and information delivery. These comparisons distinguish improvements in local metrics from downstream task performance while retaining the conditions under which results were reported. We then organize benchmarks, comparison designs, process diagnostics, and system costs into an evaluation framework. Finally, we identify open challenges in adaptive computation and stopping, memory maintenance, and dependency-aware recovery. The survey provides a basis for assessing when graph mechanisms are useful and for evaluating their effects across components and stages.

</details>


### [249] [Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05919)
*Shengjie Wang,Mohsen Fayyaz,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现MoE路由器的logits可作为推理行为（规划/执行/复述/事实/反思/结论）的线性可分表征，据此在推理时编辑专家路由分数即可训练-free地抑制"过度思考"，中位减少30.4%生成token（最高63.3%），准确率下降不超过0.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型常出现"overthinking"：在已得到正确答案后仍持续验证与重复推导，浪费昂贵测试时算力甚至损害性能。现有干预手段（token预算、steering）只调控推理长度，无法针对具体推理行为进行控制。

Method: 1) 将推理轨迹切分为句子级片段，标注六类行为（planning、execution、restatement、fact、reflection、conclusion），使每个token携带其所属片段的行为标签；2) 在MoE路由器logits上训练线性探针预测行为标签，并与残差流探针对比（特征量约为一半）；3) 在六个数据集、五个领域（数学、代码、科学、知识、常识）上验证专家—行为关联的跨数据集泛化性；4) 推理时选择性编辑专家路由分数以抑制过度思考。

Result: 在四个模型、四个基准上，该干预使生成token数中位数减少30.4%（最高63.3%）；模型倾向于在问题解决后及时停止，而非跳过必要推理步骤；准确率下降不超过0.5个百分点且常有所提升。

Conclusion: MoE路由既是推理状态的可解释观察窗口，也是优化测试时算力的轻量机制，提供了无需训练的行为级控制接口。

Abstract: Reasoning models allocate test-time compute across distinct behaviors such as planning, deriving, and verifying. However, models frequently "overthink," continuing to verify and re-derive long after finding a correct answer. This wastes expensive compute and can even degrade performance. Existing interventions, such as token budgets or steering, primarily regulate reasoning duration rather than targeting specific behaviors. In this work, we demonstrate that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers provide a natural, training-free interface for behavioral control. We first split reasoning traces into sentence-level segments and label each with one of six reasoning behaviors (planning, execution, restatement, fact, reflection, and conclusion), so that every token carries the behavior of its segment. Linear probes on router logits predict this label nearly as well as probes on the residual stream, the model's full hidden state and the standard target for probing, while using about half as many features. These signals generalize: experts associated with a reasoning behavior on one dataset predict the same behavior on the others, across six datasets spanning five domains (math, code, science, knowledge, and commonsense). Building on this, we selectively edit expert router scores during inference to curb overthinking. Across four models and four benchmarks, this intervention reduces the number of generated tokens by a median of 30.4% (and up to 63.3%). Crucially, it encourages the model to stop once the problem is solved, rather than skipping essential reasoning steps. Accuracy never drops by more than 0.5 percentage points, and frequently improves. Ultimately, MoE routing offers both an interpretable lens into reasoning states and a lightweight mechanism for optimizing test-time compute.

</details>


### [250] [Autonomous agents for electricity-system planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05911)
*Yoon Pyo Lee,Samrendra Roy,Jay Phil Yoo,Kazuma Kobayashi,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出全自动电力系统规划框架：由智能体自主生成投资上限、储能运行条件与馈线改造方案，解释数值反馈并迭代重设计，在固定验收准则与年度碳预算约束下执行投资-调度联合优化及代表性馈线校验，最终保留最低成本可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统规划需反复迭代地协调区域资源、投资、运行与网络约束，人工流程耗时长且难以规模化探索情景；同时碳排放约束下的规划需要可复现、可审计的自动评估机制。

Method: 构建"提议—数值评估—反馈解释—再设计—方案选择"的自主规划闭环：智能体提出投资边界、储能运行条件与馈线干预措施；框架自动执行年度投资与调度优化，并进行代表性馈线校验；采用固定验收准则与固定年度运行碳预算检查，按最低成本原则保留合格候选。在5个电力系统、4个国家、10个系统-年情景上评估40条碳约束轨迹，使用两种模型部署及单智能体/多智能体架构。

Result: 40条轨迹均保留满足物理校验与固定年度运行碳预算检查的候选方案；40条中28条匹配的参考设计超出新情景碳上限。另建96条轨迹档案（统一1,000 Mt年度上限），提供匹配参考与更广区域覆盖。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于公开区域数据与已发布机构需求展望，可在显式排放约束下实现自主电力系统规划；该框架可作为情景探索与方案筛选的可复现工具。

Abstract: Planning electricity systems requires repeated decisions that connect regional resources, investment, operation and network constraints. Here we present an autonomous planning framework that carries prepared regional scenarios through proposal, numerical evaluation, feedback interpretation, redesign and final plan selection. Agents propose investment bounds, storage operating conditions and feeder interventions, interpret numerical feedback, and decide how to revise designs and when to stop. The framework automatically executes annual investment-and-dispatch optimization and representative-feeder verification, applies fixed acceptance criteria and retains the lowest-cost accepted candidate. We evaluate forty carbon-constrained trajectories across ten system-year scenarios in five electricity systems and four countries, using two model deployments and single- and multi-agent architectures. All forty retain candidates satisfying the implemented physical and fixed annual operational carbon-budget checks. Twenty-eight of forty matched reference designs exceed the new scenario ceilings. A separate archive of ninety-six trajectories under a common 1,000 Mt annual ceiling provides matched references and broader regional coverage. The results demonstrate autonomous planning under explicit emissions constraints using public regional data and published institutional demand outlooks.

</details>


### [251] [EchoKV: Self-Corrective KV Cache Eviction via Iterative Query Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05903)
*JianKun Zou,Tongtong Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EchoKV，将 KV cache 驱逐中"查询—工作区耦合"视为实际失效模式，通过迭代纠错修复工作区来提升证据分散型长上下文任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 query-driven KV cache 驱逐方法把驱逐视为单纯的 token 排序问题，忽视了生成驱逐查询所依赖的工作区（workspace）本身的质量。早期工作区误差会污染后续查询观测并强化错误的最终驱逐决策，在证据分散于长上下文时尤为严重。

Method: 先用未来解码位置上的 proposals 构建与反馈机制兼容的 bootstrap 工作区；再利用早期解码生成的查询在做完剩余 lookahead 步骤之前对工作区进行恢复/纠正；工作区前向次数与 LAQ 持平，仅额外增加一次张量重打分操作实现工作区修复。

Result: 在 LongBench（16 个任务，Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 与 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上，EchoKV 在需要分散证据的任务上明显优于 LAQ，包括多跳跨文档问答与长单文档理解；在不同预算与骨干模型下均选择性提升证据密集型任务，其余子集性能相当。

Conclusion: 查询—工作区耦合是 query-driven KV 驱逐的一个实际失效模式；通过迭代式工作区纠错可在几乎不增加前向开销的情况下改善长上下文证据密集型任务，同时保持整体性能不退化。

Abstract: KV cache eviction is not merely a token-ranking problem: the workspace retained for generating eviction queries also determines the quality of those queries. Consequently, an early workspace error can distort later query observations and reinforce an incorrect final eviction decision, particularly when evidence is dispersed across a long context. We present EchoKV, which discovers and addresses this failure mode with iterative correction. It initializes a feedback-compatible bootstrap workspace using proposals at future decode positions, then uses early decode-generated queries to recover the workspace before the remaining lookahead steps. It uses the same number of workspace forward passes as LAQ while adding a tensor re-scoring operation for workspace repair. On LongBench (16 tasks, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), EchoKV shows notable improvements over LAQ on several tasks requiring dispersed evidence, including multi-hop cross-document QA and long single-document comprehension. Across budgets and backbones, EchoKV selectively improves evidence-intensive tasks while maintaining comparable performance on the remaining LongBench subsets. These results identify query--workspace coupling as a practical failure mode of query-driven KV eviction.

</details>


### [252] [PAYWORTHY: Can LLMs Predict Which Freelance Jobs an AI Agent Will Complete?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05759)
*Mohammad Reza Rezaei,Dingxian Wang,Brett Levert,Celeste Fejzo,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PAYWORTHY 基准，检验前沿语言模型能否在 AI agent 执行付费自由职业任务前预判其成功率；结论是预测能力高度依赖能否获取 agent 的能力边界信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent 承接付费工作前需要有人判断其能否胜任，但现有研究缺乏对"事前预测 agent 任务成功率"这一能力的系统评测。

Method: 构建 PAYWORTHY：从某自由职业平台 2020–2026 年 1880 万条真实任务中采样 1,494 条付费任务帖。agent 仅凭任务帖单次执行，由充当客户的 LLM 评判是否验收。要求 18 个模型在尝试前预测成功概率，用 AUC 衡量预测对"验收 vs. 拒收"的排序能力，并对比两种 agent 描述设定（告知能力边界 vs. 泛化为"带浏览器与代码执行能力的通用 agent"）；另用 4 个其他模型家族（Claude、GLM、Kimi、Qwen）作评判者复核，并将知情预测与两名人类评分者的判定结果对照。

Result: 告知 agent 不能做什么时，所有模型排序表现良好（AUC 0.66–0.86）；改为泛化的通用 agent 描述后，同一批模型对同一批任务的排序接近随机（0.45–0.56）。该结论在其余四个模型家族的 LLM 评判下稳健，知情预测亦能对两名人类评分者的判定进行有效排序。

Conclusion: LLM 的事前可雇性预测并非固有能力，而强烈依赖对 agent 能力边界的显式刻画；不带能力信息的泛化描述会使预测退化为随机，提示实际部署中应提供明确的 agent 能力说明。

Abstract: Before an AI agent takes on a paid job, someone has to judge whether it is likely to succeed. We test whether frontier language models can make this call before any attempt. We propose PAYWORTHY which contains 1{,}494 real freelance job postings, sampled from 18.8M postings made between 2020 and 2026 on a freelancing platform. An agent attempts each job from the posting alone in a single run, and an LLM judge acting as the client decides whether to accept the work. We ask 18 models to forecast how likely the agent is to succeed and measure how well each model's forecasts rank the accepted jobs above the rejected ones (AUC, where 0.5 is a random ranking). The answer depends on how the agent is described. Told what the agent cannot do, every model ranks the jobs well (AUC 0.66--0.86). Told instead that it is a generic agent with a browser and code execution, the same models rank the same jobs about as well as a random ordering (0.45--0.56). These results hold under judges from four other model families (Claude, GLM, Kimi and Qwen), and the informed forecasts also rank the verdicts of two human raters.
  Code is available at https://github.com/upwork/Payworthy

</details>


### [253] [Task-Efficient Agent Evaluation Using Cached Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06803)
*Ben Johnson,Hayden Helm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用 LLM 提取轨迹结构化摘要并嵌入，构建参考系统低维几何表示，结合 IRT 预测新系统基准分数，在 SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上显著降低 MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效基准测试希望用少量任务执行估计系统整体性能，但现有方法仅利用二值正确性，浪费了 agentic 系统每次执行产生的丰富轨迹信息。直接嵌入原始轨迹会被系统身份、模型家族与 harness 信号主导，因此需要一种能保留正确性与任务相关信号、抑制干扰信号的轨迹表示。

Method: 用 LLM 对每条执行轨迹提取结构化摘要，再用标准文本编码器嵌入，得到抑制系统身份/模型家族/harness 信号的表示；基于所有参考系统在任务执行上的该表示，构建系统的低维表示，并依据新系统在参考系统间的位置预测其得分，与 item-response model 结合。

Result: 单次随机任务执行下，SWE-bench Verified 上 MAE 由 0.13 降至 0.08（Wilcoxon p<10^-4），Terminal-Bench 2.0 上由 0.14 降至 0.12（p<10^-3）；在拥有全部参考系统时，任意执行任务数下均优于基线。

Conclusion: 结构化摘要嵌入是有效且抗干扰的轨迹表示，系统级几何预测可显著提升样本高效基准估计，并在两个 agentic 基准上验证了其泛化性与优越性。

Abstract: Efficient benchmarking estimates a system's full benchmark performance from a small number of task executions. Standard approaches rely solely on the binary correctness of executions from a collection of already-evaluated reference systems. An agentic system, however, produces a rich trace for each execution that can reveal substantially more about its capabilities. Leveraging this information requires useful trace representations. We propose representing a trace by extracting a structured summary of it with an LLM and embedding the summary with a standard text encoder. These representations preserve correctness- and task-related signal while minimizing the presence of system identity-, model family- and harness-related signals that dominate raw trace embeddings. Given this representation for each task execution from all reference systems, we construct low-dimensional representations of the systems and predict a new system's score from its position among the reference systems. On SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0, augmenting an item-response model with this geometry-based prediction reduces mean absolute error from 0.13 to 0.08 (Wilcoxon $p < 10^{-4}$) and from 0.14 to 0.12 (Wilcoxon $p < 10^{-3}$), respectively, when provided a single random executed task. Further, given access to the full set of reference systems, our approach dominates baselines for any given number of executed tasks on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [254] [ModalSearcher: Weaving Visual Evidence into Autonomous Cross-Modal Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06131)
*Zhihan Yin,Ruyi Li,Chengyu Fang,Yifeng Yao,Nonghai Zhang,Pengfei Xiong,Terry Zhang,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“自主跨模态搜索”能力定义与ModalSearch框架：用交错多模态知识图谱流水线自动构造多跳训练数据，结合SFT与强化学习在实时Web环境训练ModalSearcher Agent，并构建ModalSearch-Bench；8B模型在五个多模态知识密集型QA基准上平均提升16.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型受限于静态、不完整的参数化知识，在新颖知识与知识密集型任务（尤其需多跳证据获取）上表现不佳。现有多模态智能体搜索把视觉证据固定在输入或预设轨迹位置，无法随信息需求演化选择搜索模态，也难以应对复杂真实查询。

Method: 1）形式化定义“自主跨模态搜索”：智能体在直接推理、文搜文、文搜图、以图搜图间自主选择，并在多跳过程中主动引入视觉证据、切换并整合模态。2）提出ModalSearch-Pipeline：可扩展地构建交错多模态知识图谱，采样不同深度与模态组合的路径，采用反向问题构造法遮蔽会暴露推理捷径的实体名与图像目标，同时保留必要的消歧线索，生成纯文本、图像初始化与任意交错的多跳问题。3）在实时Web环境中通过SFT+RL训练ModalSearcher Agent，并构建ModalSearch-Bench。

Result: 在五个多模态知识密集型QA基准上，采用相同智能体工作流，ModalSearcher-8B较Qwen3-VL-8B平均准确率提升16.2分；在独立构建的主动视觉搜索基准上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 自主选择与交错整合搜索模态是突破多模态智能体知识瓶颈的有效路径；可扩展的多模态知识图谱数据构造配合SFT+RL训练可显著提升多跳知识密集型QA性能，且增益可迁移至未见基准。

Abstract: Static and incomplete parametric knowledge limits the performance of multimodal large language models on novel-knowledge and knowledge-intensive tasks, especially those requiring multi-hop evidence acquisition. Existing multimodal agentic search methods often fix visual evidence at the input or a predefined trajectory position, making it difficult for agents to select search modalities as their information needs evolve and to handle complex real-world queries. We define this capability as autonomous cross-modal search: an agent chooses among direct reasoning, text-to-text search, text-to-image search, and reverse-image search, actively introduces visual evidence when appropriate, and switches between and integrates modalities across multiple hops. We propose ModalSearch-Pipeline, a scalable procedure for constructing an interleaved multimodal knowledge graph. It samples paths with diverse depths and modality compositions and uses backward question construction to obscure entity names and image targets that would expose reasoning shortcuts while retaining necessary disambiguating clues, yielding text-only, image-initialized, and arbitrarily interleaved multi-hop problems. We train the ModalSearcher Agent with supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning in a live Web environment, and construct ModalSearch-Bench. Experiments show that ModalSearcher-8B improves average accuracy by 16.2 points over Qwen3-VL-8B under the same agentic workflow across five multimodal knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks, with consistent gains on independently constructed active visual-search benchmarks.

</details>


### [255] [UniCompress: From Multi-LoRA Experts to One Compact Model for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06075)
*Mingkang Liu,Peipei Jiao,Weiming Meng,Tianyuan Liu,Lichen Pang,Junyan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniCompress将多任务VLM专家合并为一个无适配器的紧凑模型：以BF16 Qwen3-VL-4B教师模型出发，通过GPTQ初始化与教师引导的量化恢复构建非对称INT2学生模型，视觉编码器与语言主干联合量化，权重存储从8.9GB降至2.1GB（减少约76%），四任务宏平均分比GPTQ-INT2高34.3个百分点，与教师差距仅0.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身与边缘系统依赖VLM支撑异构技能，但为每个技能单独部署任务适配器并保留高精度主干会带来显著的内存与管理开销，因此需要将多任务能力整合进单一紧凑模型。

Method: 以合并后的BF16 Qwen3-VL-4B为教师，采用GPTQ初始化构建非对称INT2学生模型，并进行教师引导的量化恢复；对视觉编码器与语言主干中的投影权重联合执行量化与恢复，实现全模型激进压缩并保留多任务行为。

Result: 在姿态识别、家居安全理解、多模态对话路由、移动GUI动作预测四项异构具身任务上，权重大小从BF16的8.9GB降至INT2的2.1GB（约降76%），INT2宏任务分数较GPTQ-INT2提升34.3个百分点，与BF16教师差距在0.9分以内。

Conclusion: 教师引导的视觉-语言联合INT2恢复可有效支撑在资源受限具身平台上部署单一多技能VLM。

Abstract: Embodied and edge systems increasingly rely on vision-language models (VLMs) to support heterogeneous skills, yet deploying a separate task adapter for each skill while retaining a high-precision backbone creates substantial memory and management costs. We present UniCompress, a deployment-oriented framework that converts multi-task expertise into one compact, adapter-free model. Starting from a consolidated BF16 Qwen3-VL-4B teacher, UniCompress constructs an asymmetric INT2 student using GPTQ initialization and teacher-guided quantization recovery. The approach jointly quantizes and recovers the projection weights in the vision encoder and language backbone, enabling aggressive compression of the complete vision-language model while preserving its multi-task behavior. We evaluate UniCompress on four heterogeneous embodied tasks: posture recognition, household-safety understanding, multimodal dialogue routing, and mobile-GUI action prediction. UniCompress reduces the approximate weight storage from 8.9 GB in BF16 to 2.1 GB in INT2, corresponding to a reduction of approximately 76%. It improves the INT2 macro task score by 34.3 percentage points over GPTQ-INT2 while remaining within 0.9 points of the consolidated BF16 teacher. These results demonstrate the potential of teacher-guided joint vision-language INT2 recovery for deploying a single multi-skill VLM on resource-constrained embodied platforms.

</details>


### [256] [Which One Is Sounding? Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06074)
*Haoze Ma,Yidi Li,Yu Ma,Pengyuan Zhao,Yi Shi,Bin Ren,Yanwen Guo,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BiSID-AVS，利用双耳听觉的耳间差异编码声源方向信息，为视听分割中的同类多候选情形提供空间约束，逐步将类别级候选响应细化为真实发声实例的掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视听分割方法多预测与声音相关区域或类别掩码，当多个同类候选共存时难以确定发声实例；近期视听实例分割方法依赖运动与跨帧关联，在观测稀疏或运动不显著时不可靠。

Method: BiSID-AVS三阶段：先通过音频提示定位类别一致的候选区域；再将双耳线索投影为图像平面空间响应图；最后在特征层与解码器层进行双层级空间条件化，细化出真实发声实例掩码。

Result: 在双耳扩展的AVS-Synthetic基准与面向机器人室内感知的Habitat-AVS数据集上，BiSID-AVS在多候选子集上表现最优，实例级mIoU分别提升35.19与41.21个百分点，并显著减少对非发声同类区域的误激活。

Conclusion: 引入双耳音频的空间方向线索可有效消解同类候选歧义，为视听实例分割提供不依赖运动与跨帧关联的实例级判别途径，并适用于机器人室内感知场景。

Abstract: Existing audio-visual segmentation methods mainly predict sound-related regions or category masks and often fail to identify the sounding instance when multiple same-class candidates coexist. Recent audio-visual instance segmentation methods address instance-level prediction but rely largely on motion and cross-frame association, which may become unreliable under sparse observations or inconspicuous motion. To this end, we introduce binaural audio, whose interaural differences encode source-direction information, to provide spatial constraints for distinguishing the sounding instance among same-class visual candidates. We propose Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation (BiSID-AVS), which progressively refines category-level candidate responses into a mask of the true sounding instance. BiSID-AVS first grounds category-consistent candidate regions through audio prompting, then projects binaural cues into an image-plane spatial response map, and finally refines the true sounding instance through dual-level spatial conditioning at the feature and decoder levels. Experiments on the binaurally extended AVS-Synthetic benchmark and Habitat-AVS dataset for robotic indoor perception show that BiSID-AVS achieves the best performance on the multi-candidate subsets, improving instance-level mIoU by 35.19 and 41.21 percentage points, respectively, while substantially reducing false activation on non-sounding same-class regions.

</details>


### [257] [EviSeek: An Evidence-Centric Agentic Framework for Embodied Active Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06434)
*Jintao Li,Zhiyong Cao,Baihua Xiao,Yu Bai,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviSeek：将问题推导的"证据需求"显式作为获取目标的无训练框架，用于主动具身问答


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动具身问答需通过视点变化与物理交互获取证据，核心难题在于避免"无方向交互"——即采取看似合理、却未指明应揭示何种证据的动作。仅靠动作计划与累积历史，会使交互的信息性目的隐式化。

Method: 提出无训练（training-free）框架 EviSeek：将问题推导出的证据需求显式转化为获取目标，把以观测为条件的动作选择与选择性保留的证据，同智能体所寻求的信息直接关联。

Result: 在 3,436 道 ESI-Bench 问题上，Qwen3-VL-32B 下准确率较单模型智能体提升 7.1 个百分点，Gemini 3.1 Pro 下提升 3.7 个百分点；同时提升视觉支持加权准确率并降低平均 token 消耗。

Conclusion: 结果支持将交互的信息性目的显式化：以"智能体为回答问题所需获知的信息"为中心来组织动作选择与证据保留。

Abstract: Active embodied question answering requires agents to acquire evidence through viewpoint changes and physical interaction. The central challenge is to avoid undirected interaction: taking plausible actions without specifying what evidence they should reveal for answering the question. Action plans and accumulated histories alone can leave the informational purpose of an interaction implicit. We introduce EviSeek, a training-free framework that makes question-derived evidence demands explicit acquisition objectives, linking observation-conditioned actions and selectively retained evidence to the information the agent seeks. On 3,436 ESI-Bench questions, EviSeek improves accuracy over the Single-model agent by 7.1 percentage points with Qwen3-VL-32B and 3.7 points with Gemini 3.1 Pro, while improving visual-support-weighted accuracy and reducing mean token usage. These findings support making the informational purpose of interaction explicit, with action selection and evidence retention organized around what the agent needs to learn to answer the question.

</details>


### [258] [Where Search Agents Go Off the Rails: Graph-Grounded Step-Level Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06662)
*Hyeseo Jeon,Seonho Yoo,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GraphRail，一种将需求、证据与推理表示为结构化声明并进行比对的审计方法，可在无生成式评判器、无金标准答案的情况下检测搜索增强推理中的中间步骤错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强推理系统可能答案正确但中间步骤出错，因此可靠审计需检验三点：证据 D 是否覆盖问题 Q、推理 T 是否针对 Q、T 是否扎根于 D。现有生成式验证器依赖金标准答案或生成式评判，难以稳定完成此类细粒度核查。

Method: GraphRail 将需求（question requirements）、证据与推理统一表示为结构化声明（structured claims），通过声明间比对完成三项核查，不使用生成式评判器，也不依赖金标准答案；并在随机抽取条件下评估其稳定性。

Result: 在三个多跳问答基准与三个推理框架上，GraphRail 相较生成式验证器提升了错误召回率，且在随机化抽取下保持稳定；其诊断结果还能用于指导在线推理，实现可追溯的中间错误检测与纠正。

Conclusion: 结构化声明比对是一种有效、可追溯的审计范式，可替代生成式评判用于中间步骤验证，并能反馈至在线推理流程以纠正错误。

Abstract: Search-augmented reasoning systems can reach correct answers while making errors in intermediate steps. Reliable auditing must check whether evidence $D$ covers the question $Q$, reasoning $T$ addresses $Q$, and $T$ is grounded in $D$. We introduce GraphRail, which represents requirements, evidence, and reasoning as structured claims and compares them without a generative judge or gold answer. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks and three reasoning frameworks, GraphRail improves error recall over generative verifiers while remaining stable under stochastic extraction. Its diagnoses can also guide online reasoning, providing a traceable way to detect and correct intermediate errors.

</details>


### [259] [Using Experience Twice: Dynamic Process Rubrics for Privileged Token-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06543)
*Shumao Wu,Kongcheng Zhang,Jianqiang Huang,Baisheng Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Online Rubric Self-Distillation (ORSD)，利用策略自生成的、自然语言的"过程评分细则"（process rubrics）作为特权信息，为token级提供稠密的过程监督，与轨迹级GRPO优势进行加性整形，从而在稀疏奖励的智能体任务中实现细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体轨迹中同时包含成功决策与失败尝试，但仅依赖稀疏的轨迹级奖励进行优化，难以识别哪些关键决策应被强化或抑制，缺乏细粒度的过程信用分配信号。

Method: 观察到同策略轨迹之间的有意义差异可被蒸馏为自然语言过程评分细则。ORSD在每一步由当前策略基于同一组轨迹独立生成多个rubric bundle，从跨轨迹行为差异中提取候选过程细则；再用冻结验证器对每条轨迹逐项评估，并按"细则满足度—任务成功"的bundle级对齐度选出一个bundle。训练时将选中bundle作为特权信息，比较原始token在有无rubric条件下的对数似然差，得到token级过程信用，并以加性优势整形方式叠加到原轨迹级GRPO优势上。

Result: 在ALFWorld与WebShop上的大量实验表明，在相同环境交互预算下，ORSD对Qwen3-1.7B与Qwen3-4B均能加速策略改进并提升最终任务性能。

Conclusion: ORSD在轨迹级结果监督与细粒度过程监督之间架起桥梁，通过过程信号实现token级信用分配，是一种可与结果导向强化学习互补的有效方法。

Abstract: Agent trajectories often contain both successful decisions and failed attempts, yet optimization with sparse trajectory-level rewards struggles to identify which critical decisions should be reinforced or suppressed. We observe that meaningful distinctions among on-policy trajectories can be distilled into natural-language process rubrics and used as process supervision signals. Based on this insight, we propose Online Rubric Self-Distillation (ORSD), which leverages self-derived process rubrics for dense token-level supervision, thereby augmenting outcome-centric reinforcement learning. Specifically, the current policy independently generates multiple rubric bundles at each step according to the same group of trajectories, extracting candidate process rubrics from cross-trajectory behavioral differences. A frozen verifier then evaluates all rubric items against each trajectory in the group, and selects one candidate bundle based on bundle-level alignment between rubric satisfaction and task success. ORSD treats the selected rubric bundle as privileged information during training, then compares the log-likelihoods of the original tokens with and without the rubric. The resulting likelihood gap provides token-specific process credit, which is added to the original trajectory-level GRPO advantage through additive advantage shaping. ORSD thus bridges trajectory-level outcome supervision and fine-grained process supervision, enabling token-level credit assignment using process signals. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that ORSD accelerates policy improvement and boosts final task performance for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B under the same environment-interaction budget. Our code is available at https://github.com/AnnaXChun/ORSD.

</details>


### [260] [Uncertainty-Aware Resource Orchestration for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05392)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体流水线需求在派发时未知、重尾多峰且非平稳的难题，本文将资源预留形式化为不确定性感知编排问题，提出基于保形预测的在线算法CRO，为计算与带宽分配提供有限样本、无分布假设的概率覆盖保证，并在仿真中兼顾覆盖可行性与最低过度预留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统以自主多步流水线运行，动态调用工具、更新记忆并迭代，其聚合计算、带宽与端到端时延在派发时不可知，只能于运行时由中间观测和随机token采样涌现，呈重尾、多峰分布且非平稳。这使基础设施编排陷入两难：低估导致执行中途限流与超时，高估浪费边缘/云容量。现有调度器要求已知完整流水线图，边缘资源管理方法依赖参数化或平稳需求模型，均不适配该场景。

Method: 将问题形式化为不确定性感知的智能体编排问题：在派发时预留计算与带宽以覆盖真实流水线需求，并以规定概率验证流水线可满足截止期。提出Conformal Reservation for Orchestration（CRO），一种在线算法，将过去执行轨迹的滑动窗口转换为派发时资源分配，借助保形预测给出有限样本、无分布假设的覆盖保证。

Result: 在同构任务混合、具有多峰与重尾分布的仿真中，CRO是唯一在所有风险水平下均满足目标概率覆盖的评估方法；在覆盖可行的方法中其过度预留最低，资源浪费约为Chebyshev界基线的一半。

Conclusion: CRO为未知且非平稳的智能体流水线提供了具概率保证的派发时资源预留方案，兼顾覆盖可靠性与资源效率，优于需完整图信息或依赖平稳需求模型的现有方法。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems powered by large language models (LLMs) execute as autonomous multi-step pipelines that dynamically invoke tools, integrate observations into memory, and iterate until a task is resolved or a resource budget is exhausted. Unlike classical workflows with fixed graphs, the aggregate compute, bandwidth, and end-to-end latency demands of such pipelines are unknown at dispatch time: they emerge at runtime from intermediate observations and stochastic token sampling, yielding heavy-tailed, multi-modal random variables. This unpredictability forces infrastructure orchestrators into a dilemma: under-provisioning risks mid-execution throttling and deadline violations, while over-provisioning wastes scarce edge or cloud capacity. Existing schedulers require a fully known pipeline graph, while edge resource-management methods rely on parametric or stationary demand models, and neither fits the unknown, non-stationary profiles of agentic pipelines. We formalize this setting as the uncertainty-aware agentic orchestration problem, where the goal is to reserve, at dispatch time, compute and bandwidth allocations that cover actual pipeline demands, and to verify that the pipeline can meet its deadline, each with a prescribed probability. We then propose Conformal Reservation for Orchestration (CRO), an online algorithm that translates a sliding window of past execution traces into dispatch-time resource allocations with finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees via conformal prediction. Simulations over heterogeneous task mixes with multi-modal and heavy-tailed profiles show that CRO is the only evaluated method that simultaneously satisfies target probabilistic coverage across all risk levels while, among coverage-feasible methods, attaining the lowest over-reservation, roughly halving the resource waste of the Chebyshev-bound baseline.

</details>


### [261] [Consistency Is Not Understanding: Orbit Audits for Neural Field Weight Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05798)
*Sungguk Cha,Mintae Kim,Youngsub Han,Byoung-Ki Jeon,Sangyeob Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套可检视的评测协议，用隐藏神经元置换与正ReLU缩放等保函数变换，来审计“从网络权重读取视觉内容”时功能信息与参数坐标的分离程度，并用联合正确性/一致性指标、语义线索控制、重复采样与精确有理数取整界区分不同失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从神经网络权重中识别视觉内容，需要把功能性信息与任意的参数坐标解耦。已有“权重读取器”是否真正识别功能而非记忆坐标，缺乏严格审计手段。

Method: 构造保持所表示函数不变的隐藏神经元置换与正ReLU缩放变换，将被变换副本按原始来源分组；构建480个独立拟合的shape场与1,000个MNIST场，评估统计型读取器与归一化集合读取器；另用4个小冻结语言模型做next-token打分，并在4种配置、1,920个评分会话的推理实验中用生成答案评测；辅以规范化为等价提示相同、联合正确性与一致性指标、语义线索对照、新鲜重复以及精确有理数取整界。

Result: 形状识别上，统计读取器在联合变换下从\ShapeRawStats\%跌至\ShapeJointStats\%，而归一化集合读取器保持\ShapeNormSet\%且预测不变；四个小冻结语言模型原始准确率为\LMRange\%（next-token评分）；推理实验中原始准确率为\FrontRawRange\%（生成答案），而相同原始提示的新鲜重复在\FrontRawRepeatRange\%的来源上不一致；规范化可使等价提示完全相同，但既不建立识别性，也不保证生成响应一致。

Conclusion: 该协议能区分坐标依赖型统计读取与功能层面的识别，揭示语言模型评分方式与提示重复带来的失效模式；结论仅限所测场、提示与执行配置。

Abstract: Recognizing visual content from neural-network weights requires separating functional information from arbitrary parameter coordinates. We audit this separation with hidden-neuron permutations and positive ReLU rescalings that preserve the represented function. The study contains 480 independently fitted shape fields and 1,000 MNIST fields, with transformed copies grouped by origin. On shape recognition, a statistics reader falls from \ShapeRawStats\% to \ShapeJointStats\% accuracy under joint transformations; a normalized set reader retains \ShapeNormSet\% with unchanged predictions. Four small frozen language models obtain \LMRange\% raw accuracy with next-token scoring. A separate four-configuration reasoning experiment with 1,920 scored sessions obtains \FrontRawRange\% raw accuracy with generated answers, while fresh identical raw prompts disagree on \FrontRawRepeatRange\% of origins. Canonicalization makes equivalent prompts identical, but this neither establishes recognition nor guarantees identical generated responses. Joint correctness/agreement metrics, semantic-cue controls, fresh repeats, and exact-rational rounding bounds separate these failure modes. The result is an inspectable evaluation protocol for visual weight readers, with claims restricted to the tested fields, prompts, and execution configurations.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Risk Cues: A Mechanism-Level Benchmark and Verifiable Debate for Scam Video Detection on the Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06110)
*Yifan Wang,Junyu Lu,Shun Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Fanliang Bu,Rong Yin,Lingbin Bu,Changrong Min,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了覆盖多种诈骗机制与非诈骗类别的视频基准 MechScam（6,614条），并提供基于视频证据的解释、诈骗要素及上下文标注、由真实证据构造的误导性论证；同时提出 ScamCourt 辩论框架（控方、辩方、审判方），实验显示其在区分相似非诈骗内容、降低误报和抵御误导论证方面优于更大规模 MLLM 与同骨干微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诈骗视频检测研究多聚焦二分类，对诈骗运作机理和判定依据缺乏解释；且忽视与广告等表面相似非诈骗内容的区分，以及模型面对误导性论证的鲁棒性，限制了检测的可靠性。

Method: 构建 MechScam 基准：6,614条视频，涵盖多样诈骗机制与非诈骗类别，提供视频证据支撑的解释、潜在诈骗要素及其上下文标注、以及基于真实证据构造的误导性论证；提出 ScamCourt 辩论框架：训练控辩双方辩手提出对立论证，由训练有素的法官基于带引用核验的记录作出裁决。

Result: 实验表明当前SOTA MLLM难以区分诈骗视频与相似非诈骗内容；ScamCourt 优于更大规模MLLM及同骨干微调基线，降低误报率，并对误导性论证表现出更强抵抗力。

Conclusion: MechScam 与 ScamCourt 共同推动更可靠、可解释的诈骗视频检测；数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Online scam videos pose significant threats to individuals and society. Existing studies primarily focus on binary classification, offering limited insights into how scams work and the reasoning behind detection decisions. Moreover, they often overlook the challenge of distinguishing scam videos from superficially similar non-scam content, such as advertisements, and the robustness of detection models against misleading arguments. These limitations hinder reliable scam video detection. To address these limitations, we introduce MechScam, a benchmark comprising 6,614 videos covering diverse scam mechanisms and non-scam categories. MechScam provides explanations supported by video evidence, annotations of potential scam elements and their context, and misleading arguments constructed from genuine evidence. Recognizing that genuine evidence can still be used to justify misleading conclusions, we further propose ScamCourt, a debate framework in which trained prosecution and defense debaters present opposing arguments, while a trained judge makes decisions based on a transcript with verified citations. Extensive experiments show that state-of-the-art MLLMs struggle to distinguish scam videos from similar non-scam content. ScamCourt outperforms larger MLLMs and same-backbone fine-tuned baselines, reducing false positives and showing stronger resistance to misleading arguments. Together, MechScam and ScamCourt contribute to more reliable and interpretable scam video detection. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/SuperYFan/MechScam.

</details>


### [263] [How Do Artificial Agents Make Decisions in Light of Visual Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05671)
*Keyty Loza,Shayan Monadjemi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过两项互补实验，评估视觉语言模型（VLM）驱动的自主智能体在可视化中感知不确定性以及基于图像做决策的能力，发现GPT-4o在饼图与条形图的不确定性感知上精度最高，且在圆形图、图标阵列或文本表示下决策最符合理性决策理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量人类知识以可视化形式存在于科学文献等来源中，VLM在视觉推理上进展显著，但关于AI智能体如何感知可视化中编码的不确定性、并在不确定性下进行决策，仍存在认知空白。

Method: 基于可视化素养与决策理论的既有工作，设计两项互补研究：研究一考察智能体如何感知与量化可视化中的不确定性；研究二考察智能体如何基于图像形式的视觉数据做决策；并跨模型、跨可视化类型分析感知准确率与决策表现。

Result: 在被测模型中，GPT-4o对饼图与条形图中不确定性的感知准确率最高；在决策任务中，当不确定性以圆形图、图标阵列或文本形式呈现时，自主智能体的行为与理性决策理论的对齐度最佳。

Conclusion: VLM智能体的不确定性感知与决策质量显著依赖模型能力与可视化编码形式，可视化类型会影响其理性决策水平，提示需针对图表设计优化人机协同的决策支持。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated significant advances in reasoning with visual data, which is particularly important given that much of human knowledge is encoded visually in scientific articles and other sources. However, a gap remains in understanding how artificial agents, powered by VLMs, perceive and navigate decision-making under uncertainty in the presence of visual data. This study explores this gap by evaluating and analyzing autonomous decision-making processes. Building upon prior work in visualization literacy and decision theory, we conducted two complementary studies: one examining how agents perceive and quantify uncertainty encoded in visualizations, and another investigating how artificial agents make decisions based on visual data in the form of images. We analyzed perception accuracy and decision performance across models and visualization types. Our results show that GPT-4o demonstrates the highest accuracy in perceiving uncertainty in pie and bar charts among the tested models. For decision-making, autonomous agents showed the best alignment with rational decision theory when uncertainty was represented through circle charts, icon arrays, or text.

</details>


### [264] [Combining Relevant Expert Knowledge Using Mixtures of Feedback Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05664)
*Olaoluwa Adigun,Akash Kumar Panda,Bart Kosko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向神经符号系统的"相关性"专家混合方案：将法理学证据理论中的 probative（证明力）与 material（实质性）双条件相关性定义扩展到模糊认知图（FCM）混合中，通过比较各专家FCM与混合FCM的均衡极限环预测差异估计答案的证明力，并给出类贝叶斯反混合定理将知识混合体分解为后验式FCM，从而为每个查询生成一组相关性加权、查询相关的FCM知识网络。方法在由真实13节点缺血性卒中风险专家FCM生成的合成FCM上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着知识网络规模扩大并连接越来越多的知识源，神经符号系统只能组合来自专家代理或其他知识源的相关答案；仅使用与输入查询相关的专家对准确性和可扩展性愈发关键，因此需要一个可操作、可量化的相关性判别与混合机制。

Method: 将法律证据理论中的相关性定义（答案须同时具备 probative 与 material）扩展到反馈模糊认知图（FCM）的混合：各真实或人工专家的FCM有向因果图组合为公共知识网络；以专家FCM的均衡极限环预测与混合FCM答案之差（变异）估计答案的证明力；提出类贝叶斯反混合定理，将知识混合体分解为对应的后验式FCM以支持基于相关性的比较；最终得到一组相关性混合权重，生成针对给定查询的相关性混合FCM。

Result: 不同查询产生不同的相关性混合FCM；反混合得到的后验式FCM可用于相关性比较；以真实13节点缺血性卒中风险专家FCM为原型生成合成FCM，演示了该相关性方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 相关性可作为神经符号系统中专家答案筛选与FCM混合的组织原则：通过证明力—实质性双准则与极限环预测差异度量，并借助类贝叶斯反混合定理，可构建查询自适应、仅纳入相关专家的FCM知识网络，从而提升准确性与可扩展性。

Abstract: A neuro-symbolic system should combine only relevant answers from expert agents or other knowledge sources. Using only relevant experts for a given input query becomes ever more important for accuracy and scalability as the knowledge network grows and connects ever more knowledge sources. We extend the standard definition of relevance from the legal theory of evidence: An answer is relevant if and only if it is both probative and material for the matter at hand given the knowledge available. We apply this relevancy scheme to mixing the feedback fuzzy cognitive maps (FCMs) of real or artificial experts. The expert FCM directed causal graphs mix or combine into a common knowledge network. We compare the equilibrium limit-cycle predictions or answers of the experts to the answers of the mixture FCM and then estimate the probative status of answers by their difference or variation. A new Bayes-like theorem de-mixes the knowledge mixture into corresponding posterior-like FCMs that also allow relevancy-based comparisons. The result is a final set of relevancy mixing weights that produces a relevancy-mixed FCM knowledge network for the given query. So different queries produce different relevancy-mixed FCMs. We demonstrate this relevancy approach using generated synthetic FCMs from an actual 13-node expert FCM that models the causes and feedbacks of ischemic-stroke risk.

</details>


### [265] [BrainBind: Bridging Synchronized EEG and fMRI through Long-Form Naturalistic Video Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05581)
*Shixiao Wang,Rining Wu,Jian K. Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BrainBind 框架，将同步采集的 EEG 与 fMRI 映射到冻结的长视频嵌入空间作为共享视觉-语义参照，在 CineBrain 上系统比较两种代理锚点与四种系统配置的检索表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视觉是动态且场景丰富的，EEG 与 fMRI 在时间与空间尺度上互补；但现有神经解码多在单一模态上、且多对静态图像或短片段做对齐，缺乏对同一长时自然观影下同步多模态信号的系统性研究。

Method: 在同步记录 EEG 与 fMRI 的长时自然观影数据上，把两者映射进冻结的长视频嵌入空间，并将其视为公共视觉-语义参照而非字面上的神经编码；在 CineBrain 上构建五随机种子的 Recall@K 2×4 矩阵（2 种视频锚点 × 4 种系统配置），并补充与剧集不重叠的划分评估。

Result: Omni 配置下 fMRI 对完整候选排序最强（MRR=23.82，nDCG@10=25.31）；Joint-Dual 取得最高平均标签感知 R@1（13.52%），且排序分布轮廓不同；剧集不重叠评估量化了重复刺激身份与叙事上下文对随机划分的贡献。

Conclusion: 在当前同步数据集上，代理选择与系统配置会产生不同但可区分的头部排序与整体排序检索特征，说明多模态配置需按目标排序区段分别评估。

Abstract: Real-world vision is dynamic and scene-rich, while EEG and fMRI resolve it at complementary temporal and spatial scales. Most neural decoding systems, however, align one modality in isolation and mostly against static images or short clips. We study the synchronized case: EEG and fMRI are recorded during the same long-form naturalistic viewing, giving a shared stimulus but also recurring narrative context and many semantically similar candidates. We present BrainBind, a framework that maps synchronized EEG and fMRI into frozen long-form video embedding spaces and treats those spaces as common visual-semantic references, not literal neural codes. On CineBrain, a five-seed Recall@K 2 x 4 matrix across two video anchors and four system configurations shows distinct retrieval profiles. With Omni, fMRI organizes the full candidate list most strongly (MRR = 23.82, nDCG@10 = 25.31), while Joint-Dual gives the highest mean label-aware R@1 (13.52%) with a distinct rank-wide profile. The complementary episode-disjoint evaluation quantifies the contribution of repeated stimulus identity and narrative context to the random split. Across these experiments, proxy choice and system configuration produce distinct top-rank and rank-wide retrieval profiles within the present synchronized dataset. The code is available at https://github.com/Forainest789/Brainbind.

</details>


### [266] [Whose Transparency is it Anyway? Understanding Generative AI Transparency Needs and Gaps Across Stakeholders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05549)
*Genevieve Smith,Sahiba Chopra,Natalia Luka,Min Kyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文基于社会建构技术（SCOT）的"解释柔性"视角，通过全球400名终端用户、AI开发者与政策制定者的问卷调查及终端用户焦点小组，揭示三类主体对生成式AI透明度的理解与实践落差，主张从"一刀切"方案转向多元化透明度基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明度已成为AI治理的核心原则，但对其内涵、服务对象与适用情境缺乏共识；尤其终端用户作为当前透明度实践中最被忽视的群体，其需求未被充分表征，亟需实证考察不同利益相关方如何理解与体验透明度。

Method: 采用混合方法：一是面向400名终端用户、AI构建者与政策制定者的全球问卷调查；二是针对终端用户的结构化焦点小组，用质性洞察为量化结论提供情境化解释，聚焦当前透明度实践服务最不足的群体。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1) 三类群体对透明度含义及其应提供之保障的理解高度一致，聚焦准确性与隐私；(2) 透明度特征"重要性—可获得性"落差在终端用户中显著大于构建者，体现权力对"建造什么、服务谁"的塑造——仅28%终端用户认为现有透明度实践很好地反映其需求，构建者为53%、政策制定者为49%；(3) 透明度具有情境建构性，优先级随情境与任务结构而变，角色特定框架在情境需要时被激活、否则退隐。

Conclusion: 有意义的AI透明度需超越"一刀切"方案（包括当前对AI披露与静态文档的强调），转向能够反映多元用户与利益相关方需求的多元化透明度基础设施。

Abstract: Transparency has become a central principle in AI governance, yet there is little agreement about what transparency means, for whom, and in what contexts. Drawing on interpretive flexibility within the Social Construction of Technology, this paper examines how different stakeholders understand and experience transparency in generative AI. This study draws from a global survey of 400 end-users, AI builders, and policymakers, complemented by a structured focus group with end-users, grounding the quantitative findings in qualitative insights from the group most underserved by current transparency practice. We present three key findings. First, all three groups share similar understandings of what transparency means and what it should afford, centered on ensuring accuracy and privacy. Second, gaps between the importance of transparency features and their perceived availability are substantially larger for end-users than builders, revealing how power shapes what gets built and who it serves. Only 28% of end-users report that current transparency practices reflect their needs very well, compared to 53% of builders and 49% of policymakers. Third, transparency is situationally constructed, priorities shift with context and task structure, with role-specific frames activating when context demands them and receding when it does not. We argue that meaningful AI transparency requires moving beyond a one-size-fits-all solution, including current emphases on AI disclosure and static documentation, toward pluralistic transparency infrastructures that reflect the needs of diverse users and stakeholders.

</details>


### [267] [Iterated creation, grading, and revision of data science projects by language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05548)
*Rohan Alexander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文评估智能体AI模型能否端到端完成数据科学项目，以及反馈迭代带来的提升。7个模型互为writer/grader，共49对、每对3次、147份初始提交中107份可运行渲染，平均分从59%升至第三轮后76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚不清楚智能体模型能否独立完成端到端数据科学项目，以及多轮反馈（含自评）在多大程度上、以何种机制改善产出质量。

Method: 采用writer–grader回路：writer模型借助指定工具完成数据下载、清洗、分析并撰写短论文；grader模型执行并打分；writer据反馈修订，共三轮。覆盖5家开发者的7个模型，构成49个writer–grader组合，每组重复3次（共147次初始提交），并设置"仅修订无反馈""仅增加时间/工具调用预算"等对照条件。

Result: 147份初始提交中107份可运行并成功渲染；平均分从59%提升至最终轮76%。按writer看提升幅度从3个百分点（Opus 5.5）到46个百分点（Haiku 4.5）；按grader看从13个百分点（Gemini 3.8 Flash）到32个百分点（Haiku 4.5）。自评分数平均比他人均分高约9个百分点；轮次间差异约一半来自grader自身噪声。反馈效果最佳，但单纯获得修订机会或增加预算也能为部分模型带来可观提升。

Conclusion: 反馈迭代回路的收益强烈依赖于模型在回路中的角色分配；弱模型通常无法提升强模型，自评存在系统性偏乐观，且评分改进的可观测性受grader噪声显著影响。

Abstract: We evaluate the extent to which agentic AI models can complete an end-to-end data science project, and how this improves with feedback. A 'writer' model with specific tools creates a complete data science project including downloading, cleaning, and analyzing a dataset, and writing a short paper about it. The project is executed and a 'grader' model evaluates it. The writer can then revise its submission, and this repeats for a total of three rounds. We consider seven models from five developers: Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, DeepSeek, and Z.AI. Each model is both a writer and a grader, so there are 49 writer-grader pairs and we run each pair three times. Of the 147 initial submissions, 107 run and render, and the average score increases from 59 per cent to 76 per cent after the final round of feedback. By writer the extent of the increase ranges from 3 percentage points for Anthropic's Opus 5.5 to 46 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5, and by grader it ranges from 13 percentage points for Google's Gemini 3.8 Flash to 32 percentage points for Anthropic's Haiku 4.5. When a model grades its own writing the score tends to be about 9 percentage points higher than the average of what the other models give it. About half of the difference that we see from round to round is within the grader's own noise. Feedback generally results in the highest marks, but even just having the chance to revise, or having the additional budget, in terms of time or tool calls, without any feedback, results in considerable increases for some of the models but not for others. We find that how much such a loop helps depends on which model plays which role, but weaker models generally do not improve stronger models.

</details>


### [268] [Addition by Subtraction: Toward Mitigating Fragile Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06553)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Alexandra Dmitrienko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Addition by Subtraction"方法，通过在微调中逐步移除安全关键神经元并用偏好优化在其他组件上重建安全对齐，使模型对剪枝、拒绝方向消融及多种黑盒越狱攻击更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐存在稀疏性结构弱点：删除少量安全关键神经元或消融单一拒绝方向即可显著破坏对齐行为，因此亟需降低安全行为对稀疏组件的依赖。

Method: 先识别安全关键神经元，在微调过程中逐步移除这些神经元，并借助偏好优化（preference optimization）让模型其余部分重新建立安全对齐行为。

Result: 所得模型对剪枝攻击显著更鲁棒；鲁棒性可泛化至拒绝方向消融等稀疏性干预及广泛黑盒越狱攻击；真理性提升且基本保持通用能力；在3家厂商4个模型上攻击成功率最高降低90%。

Conclusion: 安全对齐可通过"诱导稀疏依赖的去除+再优化"变得分布式、冗余化，从而在不牺牲通用能力的前提下大幅提升安全鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) undergo safety alignment to prevent harmful generations, yet they remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Recent work reveals a deeper structural weakness: safety alignment can be highly sparse, such that pruning a small number of safety-critical neurons or ablating a single refusal direction can substantially undermine aligned behavior. In this work, we take the first steps toward strengthening safety alignment such that it is less dependent on sparse model components. We propose Addition by Subtraction, which gradually removes identified safety-critical neurons during finetuning and uses preference optimization to re-establish safety-aligned behavior through other parts of the model. Our results show that the resulting models are substantially more robust to pruning-based attacks. Remarkably, these benefits generalize beyond the white-box attacks motivating our design: Addition by Subtraction improves robustness against other sparsity-based interventions, including refusal-direction ablation, as well as a broad range of black-box jailbreak attacks. The finetuned models also exhibit improved truthfulness while largely preserving general capabilities. Comprehensive experiments across four models from three vendors demonstrate consistent improvements in safety robustness, reducing attack success rates by up to 90% for some attacks.

</details>


### [269] [D3D: Decoupled Diffusion Drafting for Asynchronous Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06425)
*Baoyou Chen,Ruiqiao Mei,Guancong Lin,Tao Song,Weihao Yuan,Qun Liu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D3D 提出一种独立的扩散式草稿模型，去除对目标模型隐藏状态的依赖，从而实现异步投机解码，平均加速 5.00×/5.71×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标条件扩散草稿器虽能并行生成候选 token，但每个新块都需等待目标模型更新的隐藏状态，导致草稿与验证只能串行执行，即使单块单次前向生成，加速比仍受限。

Method: D3D 将分块去噪与辅助自回归监督相结合，在无目标隐藏状态下获得较长可接受前缀；据此草稿模型可在验证期间一次性打包前向草拟后续块，实现异步投机解码。此外按各层对接受长度的影响渐进剪枝，降低打包草拟的计算开销。

Result: 以 Qwen3-8B 为目标时，剪掉 36 层中的 5 层，在三个基准上仍保留超过 99% 的平均接受长度。以 Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-32B 为目标，七个基准上平均解码加速分别为 5.00× 和 5.71×，而 DFlash 为 4.03× 和 3.67×；Qwen3-32B 在 GSM8K 上达到 7.48×。

Conclusion: 解除草稿-验证的跨轮依赖是提升投机解码加速的关键，D3D 通过独立扩散草稿器与层剪枝，在保持接受长度的同时显著优于目标条件基线。

Abstract: Target-conditioned diffusion drafters predict candidate tokens in parallel, yet each new block must wait for updated target-model hidden states. This forces drafting and verification to run sequentially, limiting speedup even though each block is generated in one pass. We introduce D3D, a standalone diffusion drafter that removes this inter-round dependency. D3D combines blockwise denoising with auxiliary autoregressive supervision to achieve long accepted prefixes without target hidden states. This independence lets D3D draft successor blocks during verification in one packed pass, enabling asynchronous speculative decoding. We progressively prune layers according to their effect on acceptance length to reduce the cost of packed drafting. Removing five of 36 layers retains over 99\% of mean acceptance length across three benchmarks with Qwen3-8B as the target. Across seven benchmarks, D3D averages 5.00$\times$ and 5.71$\times$ decoding speedup with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B targets, compared with 4.03$\times$ and 3.67$\times$ for DFlash. On GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, speedup reaches 7.48$\times$. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/fudan-generative-vision/D3D.

</details>


### [270] [Does Context Compression Preserve Refusal Alignment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06380)
*Anthony Nguyen,Wenjun Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 上下文压缩虽保持高重构保真度，却系统性削弱指令微调模型的拒答行为；机理上表现为解码器拒答方向激活被衰减，Memory Steering 可在压缩表示空间内无训练地恢复拒答率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文压缩通常以语义保持（重构保真度）为优化目标，但"语义保持是否足以维持拒答对齐等下游行为"仍是开放问题，作者旨在检验语义保真与行为保真之间是否存在缺口。

Method: 在多种模型家族与压缩架构上对比压缩前后的拒答率与重构保真度；通过机制分析考察解码器学得的拒答方向上的激活幅度；并引入 Memory Steering——一种完全在压缩表示空间中运行的轻量推理时干预方法。

Result: 编码器式压缩在重构保真度很高的前提下仍系统性降低拒答行为，且该效应跨模型家族与压缩架构稳定存在；机制上压缩衰减了解码器拒答方向的激活；Memory Steering 无需重训练即可将拒答率恢复至接近基线水平。

Conclusion: 语义保持不保证压缩下的行为保持，压缩感知系统需显式保留与对齐相关的特征；推理时表示空间干预是可行且低成本的补救手段。

Abstract: Context compression reduces inference cost by encoding inputs into compact representations while preserving semantic content. An open question is whether semantic preservation alone is sufficient to maintain downstream behaviours such as refusal alignment. We investigate this question and find that encoder-based compression systematically weakens refusal behaviour in instruction-tuned language models, despite high reconstruction fidelity. This effect persists across model families and compression architectures. Mechanistic analysis shows that compression attenuates activation along the decoder's learned refusal direction. We further explore Memory Steering, a lightweight inference-time intervention that restores refusal rates to near-baseline levels without retraining and operates entirely in compressed representation space. These results demonstrate that semantic preservation does not guarantee behavioural preservation under compression, highlighting the need to explicitly preserve alignment-relevant features in compression-aware systems.

</details>


### [271] [Can Large Language Models Review the Scientific Literature? A Test Against a 102-Article Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05459)
*Abdullah Al Kalbani,David M. Schultz,Alexandra K. Anderson-Frey,Zhonghua Zheng,Catherine McGuire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究以一篇关于西班牙羽流天气模式的已发表综述及其102篇文献语料为基准，跨"检索—解读—综合—提出未来研究方向"四个阶段评估六款AI工具，发现无单一工具全能，LLM可作为文献综述的辅助手段但无法替代专家判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已越来越多用于支撑科学文献综述，但现有评估多聚焦单一任务，缺乏对"完整综述流程"的整体性考察；同时机构图书馆访问权限这一现实因素对工具表现的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 构建以已发表西班牙羽流综述及其102篇PDF语料为金标准的基准；分四阶段评估六款AI工具：(1)检索相关文献，(2)解读文献，(3)跨文献综合，(4)提出未来研究方向。量化指标包括检索到的文献数与机构访问前后的对比、对18篇"实质性讨论"文献与84篇"顺带提及"文献的分类准确率；质性指标为由两位领域专家对三款工具的综合结果进行盲评（准确性、洞见、无依据论断、引文错误、共识夸大等）。

Result: 初次检索中五款工具共找到57篇，单款最佳42篇；启用机构图书馆访问后，Codex由42篇增至82篇，Claude Code由15篇增至79篇。在拥有全部102篇PDF条件下，三款工具均正确识别出全部18篇实质性讨论文献，但将84篇顺带提及文献中的40%–79%误判为实质性讨论。三款工具生成的综合结果总体准确但洞见有限，存在无依据论断、引文错误与共识夸大。三款工具提出的研究方向均比西班牙羽流文献更宽泛，其中两款接近基准综述提出的问题。

Conclusion: 没有任何一款工具在所有任务上表现最佳。作者提出八条面向研究者的AI工具使用建议，并指出LLM可辅助文献发现与解读（机构访问权限尤为关键），但在证据评估与研究综合上仍不能取代专家判断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific literature reviews, yet evaluations often examine individual tasks rather than the review process as a whole. We assess six AI tools across four stages: finding relevant articles, interpreting them, synthesizing across them, and proposing future research. The benchmark is a published review of the Spanish plume weather pattern and its 102-article corpus.
  In the initial searches, five tools collectively identified 57 articles, with the best individual tool finding 42. Institutional library access increased discovery from 42 to 82 articles for Codex and from 15 to 79 for Claude Code. Given the complete corpus of 102 PDFs, three tools correctly identified all 18 articles discussing the Spanish plume substantially, but incorrectly classified 40% to 79% of the 84 passing mentions as substantial. The same three tools produced syntheses evaluated blindly by two domain experts. Although largely accurate, the syntheses provided limited insight and sometimes contained unsupported claims, quotation errors, and exaggerated statements of consensus. Finally, all three tools proposed research directions broader than the Spanish plume literature, although two approached questions identified in the benchmark review.
  No single tool performed best across all tasks. We provide eight recommendations for researchers using AI tools in literature reviews. Our findings suggest that LLMs can assist with discovering and interpreting scientific literature, particularly when institutional access is available, but cannot replace expert judgment in evaluating evidence or synthesizing research.

</details>


### [272] [A Feasibility Condition for Confidence-Gated Tiered LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05394)
*Sumit P. Ahuja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文给出一个闭式条件，判定在何种情况下任何置信度阈值都无法使分层推理级联（cascade）优于将全部查询送往后备大模型，并揭示该条件沿架构成本比κ轴发生“反转”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层推理（端侧/边缘/数据中心）被视为更低成本、更低延迟、更私密的推理路径，但主流系统基于冻结检查点、依赖前向模型的置信度信号做本地/升级路由决策，缺乏对“级联是否根本可行”的理论判据。

Method: 将路由决策建模为二元假设检验，用架构成本比κ将本地计算成本与升级成本分离，推导出可行性的闭式条件，并以“无信号下界”和“完美信号上界”界定可行区间。

Result: 得到可行性条件：低于无信号下界时任意信号质量下均可行，高于完美信号上界时均不可行；沿κ轴条件发生反转——本地计算计费时成本为主要约束（与近期实证一致），本地计算为沉没成本（去中心化硬件）时可行性无条件成立，信号质量转而决定级联能达到的效果。

Conclusion: 将该条件转化为三项设计需求，现有训练式原语仅能各自孤立满足；作者提出协同设计架构与协议作为未来工作，且该条件与反转结论不依赖具体架构，构成论文的核心贡献。

Abstract: Tiered inference distributes large language model computation across a hierarchy of devices, from on-device and edge hardware to the datacenter, and is increasingly offered as the route to cheaper, lower-latency, and more private serving. Most such systems orchestrate frozen model checkpoints, and most decide what to resolve locally and what to escalate using a confidence signal produced by a forward-deployed model. We give a closed-form condition for when no threshold on that signal makes a tiered cascade worthwhile relative to sending every query to the backstop. Modeling the routing decision as a binary test and separating local from escalation cost through an architecture cost ratio $κ$, we derive a feasibility condition bracketed by a signal-free floor, below which a cascade is feasible at any signal quality, and a perfect-signal ceiling, above which none is. Read along the $κ$ axis the condition inverts: where local compute is billed, cost binds, matching a recent empirical finding; where it is sunk, on decentralized hardware, feasibility becomes unconditional and signal quality instead sets what the cascade achieves. We turn the condition into three design requirements that existing trained-in primitives satisfy only in isolation, and we propose, as future work, a co-designed architecture and a protocol to test it. The condition and the inversion hold independently of that architecture; they are the paper's contribution.

</details>


### [273] [Risk Control Certifies Rates, Not Magnitudes: An Audit of Selective Fetal Biometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05851)
*Zhengshuai Yang,Linna Jiang,Changyun Li,Guihu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 产前超声头围测量管线在选择性预测的风险控制下仍会输出271.3 mm级别的严重误差：完整性分数检测不到该失败，而收紧解剖学上定义的可容许区间（下限50→55 mm）可在几乎不损失覆盖率的前提下把最大误差降到约11 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床要求头围（HC）测量必须给出结果、在图像不支持时拒答。选择性预测只能在有限样本下界定"粗错误"的发生率，却无法约束错误幅度；现有基于轮廓完整性的阈值校准会给出与人工设定阈值无法区分的风险证书，却仍容许巨大误差通过，因此需要审计该失败模式并寻找更有效的防护手段。

Method: 在HC18数据集999张标注图像上做两个独立预注册的四折划分，每折重训分割器三次；用精确Clopper-Pearson风险控制校准轮廓完整性阈值；并对比第二种干预——收紧可容许区间（measurand有定义的解剖学范围）的下界。

Result: 完整性阈值校准得到的证书与人工设定下限无异，管线仍输出271.3 mm误差（真实HC 324.6 mm，被接受的候选完整性得分0.94）；约150个答案中单个离群点不破坏α=0.10的认证风险界。独立划分复现机制（233.3 mm）。将可容许区间下界由50 mm提高到55 mm，仅损失0.4覆盖率点，两划分最坏误差降至11.4与11.1 mm；而通过完整性阈值抑制同样误差分别损失19.0和12.1覆盖率点。在解剖学定义的70 mm处，冻结测试给出1.91与1.81 mm MAE。

Conclusion: 边际风险控制（发生率约束）无法排除误差幅度极端离群的失败模式；约束可容许区间这一解剖学先验比调节分数阈值更高效地消除该误差族，是更合适的防线。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound biometry must return a head circumference (HC) and refuse when the image cannot support one. Selective prediction trades coverage against error, but its finite-sample guarantees bound the rate of gross errors, not their magnitude. We audit a tool-grounded biometry pipeline on two separately pre-registered four-way splits of the 999 labelled HC18 images, retraining the segmenter three times on each. Calibrating the contour-completeness threshold by exact Clopper-Pearson risk control returns a certificate indistinguishable from a hand-set floor, yet the pipeline still emits a 271.3 mm error: a small, geometrically complete ring fitted to an image whose true HC is 324.6 mm. The accepted candidate scores 0.94 completeness, so the score cannot see the failure, and one outlier among $\approx 150$ answers leaves the certified risk bound ($α= 0.10$) intact. A second, untouched split reproduces the mechanism on a different image (233.3 mm). Constraining the admissibility region---the anatomical range in which the measurand is defined---removes the family instead: raising the lower bound from 50 to 55 mm costs 0.4 coverage points and cuts the worst-case error on both splits to 11.4 and 11.1 mm, whereas suppressing it through the completeness threshold costs 19.0 and 12.1 points. At an anatomically defined 70 mm the frozen tests give 1.91 and 1.81 mm MAE.

</details>


### [274] [Vision Paper: Why Distributed LLM Post-Training and Decentralized Alignment Are Now Essential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05841)
*Zhanhong Jiang,Qisai Liu,Soumik Sarkar*
